### [35] [Acting by Intent, Reacting by Sight: Visual Conditioned Reflexes for Real-Time VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18723)
*Qiancheng Zhou,Wenhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViCoR提出"视觉条件反射"机制，将VLM智能体拆分为负责审慎决策与规则塑造的主规划器和基于无思考模式VLM的快速视觉反射分支，在17款2D/3D实时游戏中实现SOTA性能与显著延迟、token开销降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时环境（如视频游戏）既需要审慎的探索控制，又需要对快速移动危险做出秒级反应。现有方案存在矛盾：纯依赖VLM直接行动太慢，难以应对突发事件；而把执行交给固定脚本则使智能体在行动瞬间"失明"，场景变化时频繁失误。人类通过"意识控制+条件反射"结合解决该问题——心智负责审慎行动并塑造反射规则，反射本身仍依赖实时视觉做最终瞬时判断。

Method: 提出ViCoR：主VLM在正常游戏动作之外，为反复出现的事件定义并更新轻量级情境指引；同时设置快速视觉反射分支（以no-thinking模式调用VLM），结合运动线索检查实时局部视野，做出亚秒级最终动作决策。将67.8%的高频反应卸载给反射分支（每次检查680 ms）。

Result: 单动作平均决策延迟降低2.6倍（14.0 s→5.4 s），token消耗减少36.9%。在GameWorld基准17款2D/3D游戏中达到SOTA：任务成功率+11.8%（52.9%→64.7%），平均归一化进度+13.9%（66.5%→80.4%）。快节奏游戏中反射经跨回合精炼使进度提升1.67倍；即使使用低投入主规划器，仍以三分之一token成本超越高投入审慎基线+8.1%。

Conclusion: 模拟人类"审慎控制+条件反射"的分工是解决VLM智能体实时性与决策质量矛盾的有效途径；反射分支可承担大部分高频反应且保持实时视觉闭环，同时主规划器可持续塑造反射规则以实现跨回合改进。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong capabilities as generalist agents that perceive, reason, and act in interactive environments. Yet real-time environments such as video games demand both deliberate control for exploration and split-second reactions to fast-moving hazards. Existing agents struggle to handle both: relying solely on direct VLM actions is too slow for sudden events, whereas delegating execution to fixed scripts leaves the agent blind at the moment of action, leading to frequent misfires whenever the scene shifts. Humans overcome this by pairing normal conscious control with conditioned reflexes-while the mind handles deliberate actions and shapes reflex rules, the reflex itself still relies on live vision to make the final instant decision. Emulating this mechanism, we propose ViCoR (Visual Conditioned Reflex). Alongside normal gameplay actions, the main VLM defines and updates lightweight situational guidance for recurring events, while a fast visual reflex branch (invoking the VLM in no-thinking mode) inspects live local views with motion cues to make the final sub-second action decisions. By offloading 67.8% of high-frequency reactions to the reflex branch (680 ms per check), ViCoR reduces average per-action decision latency by 2.6x (from 14.0 s to 5.4 s) and cuts token consumption by 36.9%. Across 17 diverse 2D and 3D games in the GameWorld benchmark, ViCoR achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving task success rate by +11.8% (from 52.9% to 64.7%) and average normalized progress by +13.9% (from 66.5% to 80.4%) over the strongest deliberative VLM and script baselines. In fast-paced real-time games, ViCoR refines its reflexes across episodes to boost progress by 1.67x, and even with a low-effort main planner, surpasses high-effort deliberative baselines by +8.1% at one-third of the token cost.

</details>


### [36] [Detection Is Not the Finish Line: Multiscale Decoding and Constraint-Aware Global Association for Multi-Camera 3D Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18725)
*Abdullah Naeem,Anav Katwal,Ayon Day,Md Tamjidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在冻结Sparse4D检测器的前提下，通过检测后处理（多尺度源选择、几何-外观重识别桥接、约束感知轨迹整合）将碎片化轨迹整合，AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1 上取得62.3947 3D HOTA（DetA 57.4501, AssA 56.8131, LocA 80.1272），排名第一（截至写作时第二），较官方冠军复现高5.63 HOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于query的多视角3D检测器跨标定相机网络仍产生严重碎片化轨迹：在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1验证集上，官方冠军所用冻结Sparse4D将每个Person身份切成中位8段，且一半片段间隙超出因果跟踪器的视野范围。作者旨在论证此类系统剩余提升空间主要在检测之后，而非检测本身。

Method: 保持公开检测器冻结，附加三模块：(i) 类别级源选择，在0.75x与原始两输入尺度间选择，恢复小而远行人同时让大目标保持训练尺度；(ii) 几何到外观桥接，将每个3D框投影到所有标定视角，与2D行人检测匹配，聚合鲁棒的轨迹级ReID描述子；(iii) 约束感知整合，将因果短间隙拼接与离线质心连接聚类结合，施加时间互斥与度量3D可达性两个硬约束，并对刚性目标做类别感知长间隙拼接。所有关联阶段仅改变目标身份，不改检测框。

Result: 挑战赛评测服务器得分62.3947 3D HOTA（DetA 57.4501、AssA 56.8131、LocA 80.1272），在2026年10月30日总榜快照中第一（写作时因对手更新检测器而第二），比官方冠军复现高5.63 HOTA。仅约束关联阶段在DetA不变情况下通过+5.49 AssA带来+3.73 HOTA。固定框对照验证、约束消融、敏感性曲线及负结果清单支撑设计。

Conclusion: 多视角3D检测系统的剩余性能瓶颈主要在检测后的身份关联环节；在检测器冻结条件下，纯关联层面的几何-外观融合与约束感知整合可显著提升AssA与HOTA，且各模块有效性经消融与敏感性实验验证。

Abstract: A query-based multi-view 3D detector can transfer well to a new calibrated camera network and still produce badly fragmented tracks: on the AI City Challenge 2026 Track 1 validation set, the frozen Sparse4D detector behind the official challenge winner splits each Person identity into a median of eight fragments, and half of the gaps between fragments exceed the horizon of a causal tracker. We show that most of the remaining headroom of such a system lies after detection. Keeping the public detector frozen, we add (i) class-wise source selection between two input scales, which recovers small and distant people from a 0.75x pass while leaving large objects at the scale the detector was trained for; (ii) a geometry-to-appearance bridge that projects every 3D box into all calibrated views, matches it to 2D person detections, and aggregates robust track-level re-identification descriptors; and (iii) constraint-aware consolidation, which combines a causal short-gap stitch with offline centroid-linkage clustering under two hard constraints, temporal exclusion and metric 3D reachability, plus class-aware long-gap stitching for rigid objects. Every association stage changes object identities only. On the challenge evaluation server the system scores 62.3947 3D HOTA (DetA 57.4501, AssA 56.8131, LocA 80.1272), first on the general leaderboard snapshot of 30 October 2026 (second at the time of writing, after a competitor's detector update) and 5.63 points above our reproduction of the official winner; the constrained association stage alone contributes +3.73 HOTA through a +5.49 AssA gain at unchanged DetA. Controlled fixed-box validation studies, a constraint ablation, sensitivity curves, and a catalogue of negative results support the design.

</details>


### [37] [Latent Fusion: Channel Stacking for Structure-Preserving Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18727)
*Mohit Dulani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文探索通道级拼接作为低成本指令图像编辑方案，在1.4K对数据、4B骨干、2K分辨率的生产设定下与序列拼接对比，通道拼接保持结构对齐且更省算力，但模型主要学到全局调色而非选择性编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLUX.1 Kontext报告称通道级拼接在1K、12B模型、大规模语料下劣于序列拼接，但缺乏对"保结构、改外观"这一常见生产场景（小数据、中等模型、高分辨率）下的系统评估。作者希望验证在该低成本区间，通道拼接是否更优。

Method: 以FLUX.2 Klein 4B base为起点，仅加宽输入投影层，用相同trainer、数据和超参分别训练通道拼接与序列拼接两种方案；数据为约1,400对结构保持型编辑（天空、曝光、植被、清理），2K帧；通道拼接保持序列长度不变并仅需单尺度CFG（每步两次前向）。另用相同改造训练SD3.5-Medium做对比。

Result: 在29张留出图上，编辑器对人编辑结果达到20.3 dB PSNR、0.198 LPIPS（未编辑源为15.0 dB）；通道拼接使输出与源像素对齐，而序列拼接需从不足1,500对中学到10,880目标token与10,880源token的对应关系，出现结构位移；反事实条件测试显示模型主要学到对源的全局调色，而非选择性编辑。

Conclusion: 在低成本生产设定下通道拼接是可用的省算力选择并保持结构对齐，但选择性编辑能力不足（退化为全局分级）为该配方的主要开放问题；作者仅报告观察，不主张通用排序，并公开训练配方与失败模式。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing models must condition on the source image, either by sequence concatenation, which appends source tokens to the target sequence (FLUX.1 Kontext), or by channel-wise concatenation, which stacks the source latent onto the noisy latent and widens the input projection (InstructPix2Pix, FLUX.1 Fill). The FLUX.1 Kontext report found channel-wise concatenation worse in initial experiments at 1K resolution with a 12B model and a large corpus. We explore channel-wise concatenation as the lower-cost option for a common production regime: structure-preserving edits that change appearance (sky, exposure, foliage, cleanup) but not layout, about 1,400 training pairs, a 4B non-distilled backbone, and 2K frames. It keeps the sequence at its original length, where sequence concatenation doubles it and roughly quadruples attention cost, and needs only single-scale classifier-free guidance (two forward passes per step instead of three). Starting from FLUX.2 Klein 4B base, we widen only the input projection and train both schemes with the same trainer, data, and hyperparameters. Channel-wise concatenation keeps the output pixel-aligned with the source; in our runs sequence concatenation, which must learn the correspondence between 10,880 target and 10,880 source tokens from fewer than 1,500 pairs, displaced structure, an observation we report without claiming a general ranking. On 29 held-out images the editor reaches 20.3 dB PSNR and 0.198 LPIPS against the human edit (unedited source: 15.0 dB), and counterfactual conditioning tests show it largely learned a global grade of the source rather than a selective edit, which we report as the open problem of this recipe. We also compare against an SD3.5-Medium editor trained with the same surgery and report the full training recipe, including failure modes. Code: https://github.com/complete-dope/LatentFusion

</details>


### [38] [SCALE: Towards whole-slide counterfactual explanations in pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18729)
*Weiyi Qin,Yingci Liu-Swetz,Shiwei Tan,Yi Wang,Mohammad Alexanderani,Dimitrios Korentzelos,Peizi Li,Soo Yeon Sohn,Xintong Wang,Hao Peng,Yue Guo,Yuzhe Yang,Han Zhao,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCALE框架，为组织病理学深度学习模型生成全切片反事实解释，预测向目标类别定向转移且保持生物可行性，91.7%胜率、96.7%目标一致性获病理学家认可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织病理图像呈纹理型，相关纹理遍布整张图像而非集中于小区域，传统定位式解释方法难以说明DL预测依据；反事实解释通过"何种图像改变会改变预测"提供替代思路。

Method: SCALE框架，针对多癌种构建九种反事实方向，生成全切片反事实解释（whole-slide counterfactual explanations）。

Result: 在九个反事实方向上，SCALE稳定将预测移向既定目标并保持生物学可行性；6位认证病理学家盲评显示形态学合理性胜率91.7%（相对基线），反事实有效性目标一致性96.7%。

Conclusion: SCALE为纹理型病理图像提供可解释、形态学合理且生物学可行的全切片反事实解释，弥补定位类方法在病理场景中的不足。

Abstract: Cancer diagnosis relies on histopathological evaluation, where tumor morphology guides disease classification and informs treatment decisions. Deep learning (DL) models excel at predicting diagnostic properties from histopathology images. Unlike typical medical images featuring small critical areas, histopathological images are textural, with relevant texture spanning the entire image; this makes it challenging to explain DL predictions by locating relevant image regions. Counterfactual explanations offer a promising alternative by asking "what changes in the image could have altered the model prediction." To achieve this, we propose SCALE, a framework for generating such counterfactual explanations for histopathological DL models. Across nine counterfactual directions spanning multiple cancer types, SCALE generates whole-slide counterfactual explanations that consistently shift predictions toward the intended target while maintaining biological feasibility. Blinded assessment by six board-certified pathologists showed higher morphological plausibility for SCALE (with a win rate of 91.7% over baselines) and promising counterfactual effectiveness (with a target consistency of 96.7%).

</details>


### [39] [ShiftGuard: Evaluating Confidence and Feature Distance for Risk-Controlled Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18732)
*Mitra Varun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文检验在 MSP 与熵之外加入特征距离信号是否能改善错误检测与带风险认证的接受率。实验表明置信度类分数最优，特征距离信号未带来增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的分类器需要决定何时作答、何时弃权。MSP 与预测熵是标准的事后分数，特征空间距离是流行替代方案，将多信号融合是自然思路。作者希望验证：加入特征距离信号能否提升错误检测，以及在仅有少量标注校准集时能否提高在认证错误率下可接受的预测比例。

Method: 在 CIFAR-10 上从零训练适配的 ResNet-18（92.92% 测试准确率，单一种子），比较五种可靠性分数：MSP、熵、到预测类质心的距离、MSP 与熵的逻辑回归融合、三信号逻辑回归融合。在接受阈值选择上，于留出校准集上用 Clopper-Pearson 上界，并在预设的 315 条规则族上做 Bonferroni 校正，达到 95% 同时置信水平。

Result: 置信度类分数表现最佳。错误检测 AUROC：MSP 0.9230，熵 0.9233，MSP+熵 0.9235，三信号融合 0.8814，仅距离 0.8224。在 5% 风险目标、1000 张校准图像下，熵接受 84.82% 测试样本且观测错误率 2.05%；三信号融合接受 80.61%，错误率 2.21%。校准集仅 250 张时，距离与三信号融合均无法认证 5% 目标。

Conclusion: 结果仅基于单一种子、单一数据集与单一架构，且无腐蚀实验，因此无法说明分布偏移检测能力。在此范围内，特征距离信号未能证明其额外复杂度是值得的。

Abstract: A classifier that is usually right still makes confident mistakes, and a deployed system needs some way to decide when to answer and when to abstain. Cheap post-hoc scores such as maximum softmax probability (MSP) and predictive entropy are the standard answer. Distances in feature space are a popular alternative, and fusing several signals into one score is a natural next step. We ask whether adding a feature distance signal to MSP and entropy improves error detection, and whether it increases the share of predictions that can be accepted at a certified error rate when only a small labelled calibration set is available. We train a CIFAR-adapted ResNet-18 from scratch on CIFAR-10 (92.92% test accuracy, one seed) and compare five reliability scores: MSP, entropy, distance to the predicted-class centroid, a logistic fusion of MSP and entropy, and a logistic fusion of all three signals. Acceptance thresholds are chosen on a held out calibration set with Clopper Pearson upper bounds, Bonferroni corrected over a prespecified family of 315 rules at 95% simultaneous confidence. Confidence based scores were best. Error AUROC was 0.9230 for MSP, 0.9233 for entropy and 0.9235 for MSP plus entropy, against 0.8814 for the three signal fusion and 0.8224 for distance alone. At a 5% risk target with 1,000 calibration images, entropy accepted 84.82% of test images with 2.05% observed error, while three signal fusion accepted 80.61% with 2.21%. With 250 calibration images, neither distance nor the three-signal fusion could certify the 5% target at all. The results come from one seed, one dataset and one architecture, with no corruption experiments, so they say nothing about distribution shift detection. Within these limits, the distance signal did not earn its added complexity.

</details>


### [40] [LiNO: Lightweight Network for Occupancy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18733)
*Ruan Bispo,Tim Brophy,Reenu Mohandas,Anthony Scanlan,Ciaran Eising*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种实时、轻量的相机-雷达融合占用栅格估计架构，通过残差多阶段头与任务特定数据增强，在Occ3D和OpenOcc上达到SOTA，同时实现17.2 FPS与5.3 GB显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶占用栅格估计需构建3D地图以识别可行驶区域与障碍物类别，涉及深度估计、分类、分割及多传感器高维数据融合，计算与内存开销大；现有方法多依赖庞大且计算密集的架构，难以满足实时性与实际部署需求。

Method: 设计轻量级实时架构：采用相机与雷达融合而非纯相机重块；引入残差多阶段头（residual multi-stage head）进行特征提取与预测；并提出任务特定的数据增强策略以提升数据多样性与泛化能力。

Result: 在Occ3D上取得45.7 mIoU，在OpenOcc上取得41.9 RayIoU，达到SOTA；推理速度17.2 FPS，仅占用5.3 GB内存；相比STCOcc的4.1 FPS和7.7 GB，效率显著提升。

Conclusion: 相机-雷达融合配合轻量组件与多阶段头，可在保证SOTA精度的同时大幅降低计算与内存开销，验证了实时、可部署占用估计方案的可行性。

Abstract: Occupancy estimation for automated vehicles is a crucial task that builds a 3D map using vehicle sensors. This map allows the vehicle to identify drivable areas and various obstacle classes. The task involves complex subtasks such as depth estimation, object classification, and segmentation to align semantic information with the 3D grid. Moreover, producing these estimations from multiple sensors demands significant computational power and memory, as high-dimensional data fusion requires complex feature extraction and processing. Consequently, most existing methods rely on large and computationally intensive architectures that are not suitable for real-time or real-world applications. We present a real-time, lightweight architecture that leverages camera and radar sensors through a residual multi-stage head. Unlike prior methods that rely on heavy camera-only blocks, our architecture employs camera-radar fusion and lightweight components. Additionally, we introduce a task-specific data augmentation strategy that enhances data diversity and improves generalisation. Our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, attaining 45.7 mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) on Occ3D and 41.9 Ray-level Intersection over Union (RayIoU) (41.9) on OpenOcc datasets, running at 17.2 frames per second (FPS) and using only 5.3 GB of memory, compared with 4.1 FPS and 7.7 GB for STCOcc.

</details>


### [41] [Is It Still There? Place-Registered Verification Makes VLMs Reliable Scene-Change Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18734)
*Jinjie Xie,Baihua Li,Qinggang Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STILL 将场景变化检测重构为"已配准位置的目标验证"任务：先用 3D 几何把每个变化假设的位置配准到另一次采集，再由单个多图像 VLM 结合场景视图与局部位置视图判断该目标是否仍在原处，在 SceneDiff 协议上把主要指标 obj/sc AP 从 24.0 提升到 37.8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人重访同一空间时需判断哪些物体被新增、移除或移动，而两次采集的轨迹、视角与光照均不同。现有多图像 VLM 主要失败在"找不到物体原来所在的位置"：它们能识别每帧内容，却无法在其他采集中核查该物体是否仍位于原处，因而只报告单次采集可见的物体，并漏检大部分被移除或移动的物体。

Method: 将变化检测重构为基于配准位置的目标验证：利用 3D 几何把每个变化假设的位置配准到另一次采集，再由一个多图像 VLM 在场景视图与局部位置视图输入下回答"该物体是否仍在该位置"，并以同形式监督进行训练。

Result: 在官方 SceneDiff 协议下，主要指标 obj/sc AP 由 24.0 提升至 37.8；在无任何厨房训练数据的自我中心厨房子集上由 11.6 提升至 20.6；F1 相较在同一标注上微调的 VLM 提升超过 21 点；运行时间仅增加约 11%。

Conclusion: 显式的位置配准与目标验证范式能有效弥补多图像 VLM 在跨采集空间对应上的缺陷，且具备良好的跨域泛化性与较低的计算开销。

Abstract: A robot that returns to a space must tell which objects were added, removed or moved since its last visit, while its trajectory, viewpoints and lighting differ between the two visits. We find that multi-image vision-language models (VLMs) fail at this task mainly because they cannot find the place where an object was. A VLM recognises what each frame shows but cannot check whether an object is still at its place in the other capture. It therefore reports objects that only one capture shows and misses most removed and moved objects. We propose STILL, which reformulates change detection as object verification at a registered place. 3D geometry registers the place of each change hypothesis in the other capture, and one VLM answers, from a scene view and a local place view, whether the object is still at that place, trained with supervision of the same form. Under the official SceneDiff protocol, STILL raises the main metric obj/sc AP from 24.0 to 37.8, and from 11.6 to 20.6 on the egocentric kitchen subset without any kitchen training data. It improves F1 by more than 21 points over a VLM fine-tuned on the same annotations and adds only about 11% to the runtime.

</details>


### [42] [TyphoonWorld: A Residual Token World Model for Probabilistic Forecasting of Typhoon Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18740)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Tianyi Li,Xuanyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TyphoonWorld：一个离散token世界模型，在回归预报中心之上生成残差场，实现低误差且扩散度–误差一致性的台风卫星预报集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 台风卫星预报需要兼具低误差的两天确定性预报，以及成员为相干场、扩散度能随具体风暴响应的集合预报。小规模直接回归网络已取得最低平方误差，生成模型的额外成本必须由回归平滑掉的高频/残差部分来证明价值。

Method: 提出TyphoonWorld，仅生成回归预报平滑掉的那部分：以回归预报token、最新帧与预报时刻最佳路径状态为条件，逐lead采样残差场，集合成员将残差叠加到回归中心。在Digital Typhoon的46个留出风暴、276个预报窗口上，采用统一协议与逐像素高斯网络、同残差扩散模型、其他风暴残差、无条件token世界模型对比。

Result: TyphoonWorld平方误差与回归模型持平；对220 K以下冷区（深对流直接度量）的CRPS较所有其他集合降低14%–39%；扩散度与预报误差的相关性达0.78，与高斯网络相当；参数量仅为无条件世界模型的56%。

Conclusion: 将生成建模限定于残差、并以回归中心为锚，可在不牺牲确定性精度的情况下获得更优的深对流概率预报与良校准的集合扩散度，且参数更省。

Abstract: A satellite forecast of a typhoon should give a low-error picture of the next two days, together with an ensemble whose members are coherent fields and whose spread responds to the storm at hand. A small direct regression network already gives the lowest squared error on held-out storms, so a generative model has to earn its cost through the part of the future that the regression smooths away. We present TyphoonWorld, a discrete-token world model that generates only that part. Conditioned on tokens of the regression forecast, the latest frames and the best-track state at forecast time, it samples residual fields lead by lead, and members add these residuals to the regression center. We compare it under one protocol with per-pixel Gaussian networks, a diffusion model of the same residuals, residuals from other storms and an unconditional token world model, on 276 forecast windows from 46 held-out storms of Digital Typhoon. TyphoonWorld matches the squared error of the regression, lowers the CRPS of the area colder than 220 K, a direct measure of deep convection, by 14 to 39% against every other ensemble, and ties its spread to forecast error as closely as the Gaussian network, with a correlation of 0.78 across windows. It uses 56% of the parameters of the unconditional world model.

</details>


### [43] [Target-Free Autocalibration of Stereo PIV Camera Systems via Persistent-State Motion Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18741)
*Nabil Madali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需标定板的立体PIV外参标定方法：基于CUT3R循环Transformer积累粒子运动状态，解码为每相机光流并经跨视角解码器回归双向点对应，再用五点RANSAC求解相对位姿；在合成与真实数据上流场误差与位姿精度均优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体PIV需精确外参标定，现有方法依赖向流场中插入物理标定板，且设备移动后须重复标定，操作繁琐、侵入流场且不适用于难以插入标定板的场景。

Method: 利用示踪粒子外观不可区分的特点，改以运动而非外观匹配：每条相机流经循环Transformer处理，持久状态随时间积累粒子运动并解码为单相机光流；跨视角解码器查询该状态，回归双向点对应；最后用五点RANSAC求解相对位姿。训练使用带真值位姿的新合成立体PIV数据集。

Result: 在标准PIV光流基准与200组真实架设位上评估：光流误差低于现有PIV网络，跨视角对应精度优于COTR，位姿精度在合成与真实数据上均超过最强检测-匹配流水线，且无需任何标定靶。

Conclusion: 验证了从粒子图像序列直接进行无靶标立体PIV外参标定的可行性，为免标定板、可快速重新标定的立体PIV系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Stereo Particle Image Velocimetry (PIV) recovers three-component velocity fields by triangulating tracer particles observed by synchronized cameras. It requires an accurate extrinsic calibration, which is still obtained by inserting a physical target into the flow and must be repeated whenever the rig is moved. We present a target-free method that calibrates a stereo PIV rig directly from particle image sequences. Because tracer particles are visually indistinguishable, we match motion rather than appearance: building on CUT3R, each camera stream is processed by a recurrent transformer whose persistent state accumulates particle motion over time. The state is decoded into per-camera optical flow and queried by a cross-view decoder that regresses bidirectional point correspondences, from which the relative pose is recovered with a five-point RANSAC solver. We train on a new synthetic stereo PIV dataset with ground-truth poses and evaluate on standard PIV optical flow benchmarks and on 200 real rig placements. Our model achieves lower flow error than existing PIV networks, more accurate cross-view correspondences than COTR, and higher pose accuracy than the strongest detector-matcher pipelines on both synthetic and real data, without any calibration target.

</details>


### [44] [Recognizing Events Is Not Enough: Why Streaming VLMs Struggle in Compositional Instruction Triggering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18743)
*Yehua Huang,Penglei Sun,Xiang Li,Jinhao Zhou,Jiawen Wen,Qingqing Xiao,Zihan Yang,Wenqi Wang,Haolan Chen,Yu Xu,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoTriggerBench基准与实时VLM智能体TEMPI，通过事件分解与场景记忆处理流视频中的时序约束指令，指令级成功率44.4%，并优化端侧流式推理实现4.90×加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型虽能识别单个事件，但无法在流式视频中正确跟踪时序条件随时间被满足的过程，导致组合式指令执行过早通知或错过目标时刻，且缺乏针对该能力的评测基准。

Method: 构建CoTriggerBench：将标注视频事件组合为时序约束指令，并通过可执行事件计划与源事件回放验证通知时机与静默性；提出实时VLM智能体TEMPI，将指令分解为原子事件查询与时序约束，利用Scenario Memory在事件证据到达时持续维护任务进度，使早期观测可支持超出检测器视觉窗口的后续决策，无通知需求时也持续更新场景状态；同时优化流式推理管线，将部分视觉编码与LLM前缀预填充与帧到达重叠执行。

Result: 在CoTriggerBench上TEMPI取得44.4%的指令级成功率，分别比最强闭源基线Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash-Realtime和微调开源基线MOSS-VL-Realtime高出15.6和18.1个百分点；在8GB RTX 5060上，优化后的检测器实现4.90倍加速。

Conclusion: 时序组合指令的正确执行需依赖跨时间的状态维护而非单纯事件识别，Scenario Memory机制与流式推理优化可有效提升准确率与端侧实时性。

Abstract: Recognizing individual events does not guarantee correct execution of compositional instructions in streaming video. For example, given the request "Notify me when the pot boils after someone turns on the stove and leaves the kitchen", existing models may recognize the individual events yet notify too early or miss the intended moment, failing to track how their temporal conditions are satisfied over time. To evaluate this gap, we introduce CoTriggerBench, which composes annotated video events into temporally constrained instructions and verifies notification timing and silence through executable event plans and source-event replay. To address this challenge, we propose a real-time VLM agent, TEMPI. TEMPI decomposes instructions into atomic event queries and temporal constraints, and uses Scenario Memory to continuously maintain task progress as event evidence arrives, allowing earlier observations to support subsequent decisions beyond the detector's visual window. Even when no notification is required, relevant events continue to update the scenario state. On CoTriggerBench, TEMPI achieves 44.4% instruction-level success, outperforming the strongest closed-source and fine-tuned open-source VLM baselines by 15.6 (Qwen3.8-Omni-Flash-Realtime) and 18.1 (MOSS-VL-Realtime) percentage points, respectively. To support efficient on-device streaming inference, we further optimize the streaming inference pipeline by overlapping partial visual encoding and LLM prefix prefill with frame arrival. On an 8 GB RTX 5060, our optimized detector implementation achieves a 4.90$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [45] [Neural Latent Gaussian Process for 3D reconstruction of Mouse Brain Lipids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18744)
*Daniel Trejo Banos,Artiom Casapu,Luca Fusar Bassini,Halima Hannah Schede,Giovanni D'Angelo,Gioele La Manno,Ekaterina Krymova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出神经潜空间高斯过程（LGP），从稀疏二维切片MALDI-MSI数据插值重建小鼠脑完整三维脂质分布，在173种脂质上优于线性回归、XGBoost、MLP、Neural Transcriptomic Field、SPA3D、DEEPSPATIAL及GPLFR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脂质测量仅在稀疏的二维组织切片上获得，需要插值到参考脑（Allen CCF）的未观测体素以重建连续三维脂质分布，而现有方法缺乏针对该稀疏空间组学插值任务的有效方案。

Method: 对低维脂质剖面施加高斯过程先验（Matérn核或黎曼流形核），将三维坐标编码为少量潜在三维体量，再经MLP映射到具体脂质；使用2个密集采样与6个稀疏采样小鼠脑的冠状切片MALDI-MSI数据并配准至Allen CCF，以留出切片评估泛化，并与线性回归、XGBoost、MLP、Neural Transcriptomic Field、图方法SPA3D、DEEPSPATIAL及GPLFR做全面对比。

Result: 在173种脂质上，LGP优于所有基线（含近期空间组学模型与GPLFR），平均相关系数达0.613。

Conclusion: 高斯过程潜空间先验结合MLP解码能有效完成稀疏三维脂质插值，验证了该架构在空间脂质重建任务上的有效性。

Abstract: We address the problem of reconstructing continuous three-dimensional lipid profiles of the mouse brain from lipid measurements acquired only on a sparse set of two-dimensional tissue sections. Using MALDI-MSI measurements from coronal sections of two densely sampled and six sparsely sampled mouse brains, registered to the Allen Mouse Brain Common Coordinate Framework (CCF), we seek to interpolate the measured hundreds of lipid profiles across the unobserved voxels of the reference brain. We introduce a Neural Latent-Space Gaussian Process (LGP) architecture for this task and critically evaluate its ability to reconstruct spatial lipid distributions, using held-out mouse brain sections to assess generalization.
  We propose a Gaussian Process prior for the distribution of low-dimensional lipid profiles across the mouse brain (with Matérn or a Riemann manifold kernel). This allows us to encode 3D coordinates in a small number of latent 3D volumes that are then mapped to particular lipids using a multilayer perceptron (MLP). We performed an exhaustive analysis over a range of algorithms from simple linear regression, XGBoost, MLP to state of the art models such as Neural Transcriptomic Field, graph-based SPA3D for spatial omics reconstruction and DEEPSPATIAL. We additionally evaluate GPLFR as an alternative Gaussian-process-based approach.
  Across 173 measured lipids, LGP outperforms all evaluated baselines, including recent spatial-omics models and GPLFR, achieving a mean correlation of 0.613 and demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach for sparse 3D lipid interpolation.

</details>


### [46] [VTG-RAG: Long-Video Reasoning via Query-Adaptive Coarse-to-Fine Sampling and Temporal Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18746)
*Yao Lu,Yunhao Wang,Yaxin Gao,Feifei Teng,Chenxiao Liu,Zhe Tang,Jiaheng Wei,Zhaowei Zhu,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VTG-RAG通过查询自适应由粗到细采样与时序图检索提升长视频问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频推理需在长时序中定位稀疏证据、保持时序结构，并抵抗大量多模态干扰；现有视频RAG方法基于均匀采样片段建索引，易引入无关上下文，对含显式时序约束的问题不可靠。

Method: 两阶段采样选取"查询相关性均值高"或"片段内相关性方差高"的核心片段；将字幕、语音转写、视觉描述、实体与关系整合为时序有序的多模态图；在帧、文本与图证据空间上进行带时序过滤的两遍检索与推理。

Result: 在Video-MME与LongVideoBench上验证；LongVideoBench上Qwen2.5-VL-32B由48.16%提升至50.56%，72B由52.13%提升至53.32%，分别提升2.40与1.19个百分点（24帧输入）；消融实验支持各组件均有重要贡献。

Conclusion: 查询自适应采样结合时序图检索可有效缓解均匀采样带来的无关上下文问题，提升长视频问答的准确性与时序约束处理能力。

Abstract: Long-video reasoning requires a system to localize sparse evidence over extended temporal sequences, preserve temporal structure, and generate answers despite substantial multimodal distractors. Existing video retrieval-augmented generation methods typically build indexes from uniformly sampled video segments. In long-video settings, this design readily introduces irrelevant context and is unreliable for questions with explicit temporal constraints. We propose VTG-RAG, a framework for long-video question answering that combines query-adaptive coarse-to-fine sampling with temporal graph retrieval. First, a two-stage sampling strategy selects core segments with high mean query relevance or high intra-segment relevance variance. The framework then integrates subtitles, speech transcripts, visual descriptions, entities, and relations from these segments into a temporally ordered multimodal graph. Finally, it performs temporally filtered, two-pass retrieval and reasoning across frame, text, and graph evidence spaces. A lot of experiments have proven the effectiveness of the VTG-RAG. Specifically, we evaluate the framework on Video-MME and LongVideoBench. On LongVideoBench, the reported Qwen2.5-VL-32B and 72B results improve from 48.16% to 50.56% and from 52.13% to 53.32%, respectively, with 24 answer-input frames. These correspond to gains of 2.40 and 1.19 percentage points. The ablation experiments of VTG-RAG further support that the contributions of each component are very important.

</details>


### [47] [Feature-Space Latent Forecasting for Endoscopic Surgical Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18750)
*Songtao Hu,Hang Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种特征空间语义预测框架，在冻结视觉基础模型(VFM)的表示空间内直接建模手术流程的时间动态，将流程演化建模为掩码未来特征预测，优于RGB像素级预测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术工作流辅助本质上是预测性的，需要推理流程如何演变而非仅识别当前帧。但以往方法多在像素空间合成未来帧，侧重于外观保真度，而非下游推理所需的语义表征。

Method: 以冻结的VFM为基础，构建由多层VFM特征组成的层级目标表示；用可学习的[MASK] token替换未来帧特征token；训练掩码Transformer从观测上下文预测对应的未来潜特征，从而将流程演化形式化为掩码未来特征预测。

Result: 在公开腹腔镜基准上，相比基于RGB的预测基线，在阶段预判、多标签器械存在预测和剩余时间估计三项任务上均取得一致提升，并为轻量下游头提供即插即用的表示接口。

Conclusion: 在语义特征空间而非像素空间进行未来预测，更适合下游手术工作流推理任务，且模块化设计便于下游头集成。

Abstract: Surgical workflow assistance is inherently anticipatory: systems must reason about how procedures evolve, not just recognize the current frame. However, many prior approaches perform prediction in pixel space by synthesizing future frames, which primarily focuses on appearance fidelity rather than the semantic representations required for downstream reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose a feature-space semantic forecasting framework that models temporal dynamics directly within the representations of a frozen Vision Foundation Model (VFM). Specifically, we build a hierarchical target representation from multi-layer VFM features, replace future-frame feature tokens with learnable \texttt{[MASK]} tokens, and train a masked transformer to forecast the corresponding future latent features from observed context, thereby formulating procedural evolution as masked future feature prediction. Experiments on public laparoscopic benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in phase anticipation, multi-label tool presence prediction, and remaining-time estimation over RGB-based forecasting baselines, while enabling a plug-and-play representation interface for lightweight downstream heads.

</details>


### [48] [From Hosted Service to Field-Owned Infrastructure: Open, Self-Hosted Real-Time Camera-Trap Alerting with Regional Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18751)
*Paul Fergus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开源自托管蜂窝相机陷阱系统 Trap Tracker RT，并评估区域专用模型 DeepFaune-SSA v1.2，实现本地化物种识别与实时预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜂窝相机陷阱系统依赖中心服务器，将人员及敏感物种位置的图像传至采集机构之外甚至跨境，存在数据主权问题；作者探索该能力可否在野外机构自有的基础设施上运行。

Method: 开发开源自托管应用 Trap Tracker RT（通过邮件或 FTP/SFTP 接收图像，用项目专用模型分类，经机构自选邮件服务器发送警报）；将 MegaDetector v6 与基于 DINOv3 的分类器组合为 DeepFaune-SSA v1.2（覆盖 33 个撒哈拉以南类群），在来自肯尼亚、纳米比亚、马拉维的 31,131 张分布外图像上与三个开放替代方案对比，并做全本地路径的基准测试。

Result: DeepFaune-SSA 图像级物种准确率 0.901（95% CI 0.894–0.908），显著高于 SpeciesNet（0.530）、YOLOv10x（0.556）与 YOLO26x（0.547）；在全部 26 个评估物种上召回率最高。单阶段检测器约漏检四分之一的动物，SpeciesNet 仅 57% 图像给出物种标签且常退化为粗粒度标签。全本地路径警报在上传完成后约 6 秒发出（含限速与中断上传情形）；两阶段流水线在 8 个 CPU 线程、无 GPU 下每分钟处理 19 张图像。系统已被英国与肯尼亚的五个机构使用，包括用于人象冲突缓解。

Conclusion: 区域定制两阶段模型在分布外数据上远优于通用分类器和区域训练的单阶段检测器；决定数据主权的关键在数据路径而非推理位置。作者进一步讨论了自托管的运维责任与实时监测的伦理问题。

Abstract: Cellular camera traps can alert field teams within minutes, supporting anti-poaching, threatened-species protection and human-wildlife conflict mitigation. Most systems use central servers, sending images of people and sensitive species locations outside the collecting organisation and often across national borders. We ask whether this capability can run on infrastructure owned by field organisations. Trap Tracker RT is an open-source, self-hosted application that receives images by email or locally via FTP/SFTP, classifies them with a project-specific model and sends alerts through a mail server chosen by the organisation. We evaluated DeepFaune-SSA v1.2, pairing MegaDetector v6 with a DINOv3-based classifier for 33 sub-Saharan taxa, against three open alternatives on 31,131 out-of-distribution images from Kenya, Namibia and Malawi, independent of its training data. DeepFaune-SSA achieved image-level species accuracy of 0.901 (95% CI 0.894-0.908), versus 0.530 for the global SpeciesNet classifier and 0.556 and 0.547 for YOLOv10x and YOLO26x detectors trained on the same regional data. DeepFaune-SSA had the highest recall for all 26 species evaluated. The single-stage detectors missed animals in about a quarter of images; SpeciesNet assigned species labels in only 57%, usually falling back to coarser labels otherwise. In bench tests of the fully local path, alerts were issued about 6 s after upload completion, including with slowed and interrupted uploads. The two-stage pipeline processed 19 images per minute on eight CPU threads without a graphics processor. Trap Tracker RT is used by five organisations in the United Kingdom and Kenya, including for human-elephant conflict mitigation. We discuss how data paths, rather than inference location, determine data sovereignty, the responsibilities of self-hosting and the ethics of real-time monitoring.

</details>


### [49] [Conv-Reluplex: A Verification Framework For Convolution Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18753)
*Jin Xu,Zishan Li,Miaomiao Zhang,Bowen Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Conv-Reluplex 框架，将 Reluplex 从全连接 DNN 扩展到 CNN 的卷积层与池化层，用于安全性验证并在误分类时生成对抗样本，实验表明生成的对抗样本可用于增强 CNN、显著提升分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在视觉与自然语言处理等任务上表现优异，但在安全关键系统中部署需对神经网络输出提供形式化保证。现有 Reluplex 工具只能处理全连接 DNN，无法直接处理图像数据所依赖的卷积结构，因此需要扩展以支持 CNN。

Method: 设计 Conv-Reluplex 框架，对 CNN 中的卷积层与池化层进行建模与验证；当网络分类错误时，通过该框架搜索并生成对抗样本。

Result: 在 MNIST 上进行多组实验评估该方法；利用工具生成的对抗样本对原 CNN 进行改进后，分类精度得到显著提升。

Conclusion: Conv-Reluplex 可将 Reluplex 的验证能力延伸至卷积神经网络，同时具备对抗样本生成功能，生成的样本能有效增强模型鲁棒性与分类性能。

Abstract: In recent years, machine learning has demonstrated impressive performance in many real-world tasks, especially in computer vision and natural language processing. However, to apply them in safety-critical systems one needs formal guarantees on the neural network outputs. The Reluplex tool is proposed to verify the safety of deep neural networks (DNNs), and in case the DNN fails to give a correct output, can generate adversarial examples. Since the tool can only handle DNNs, it is necessary to extend the tool to process image data. Therefore, in this paper, we propose the Conv-Reluplex framework, which is designed to verify the convolutional layer and pooling layer in convolutional neural networks (CNNs), and generate adversarial examples when classification is misguided. We conduct several experiments on MNIST to evaluate our approaches. The results show that the original CNN is improved using the adversarial examples generated by our tool, and the precision of classification can be increased significantly.

</details>


### [50] [Frame First, Decode Once: Object-Centric Coordinates and Single-Pass Decoding for System-1 World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18761)
*Abu Anas Ibn Samad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究单一前向的原始几何预测器作为System-1世界模型时，哪些因素真正影响决策质量：SE(2)物体坐标系表达与单步解码是主导因素；软token期望配合端到端2D Kabsch投影取得最低轨迹误差与最低延迟下的低后悔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关心的是：当把快速的动作条件预测器当作System-1世界模型（为候选推挤动作排序）时，模型架构中的哪些设计选择真正决定决策绩效，而非仅关注轨迹精度。

Method: 在ManiSkill3 PushT-v1（512个留出测试族、8192次rollout）与lerobot/pusht人类演示（2384个测试窗口）上，使用匹配的checkpoint、分层bootstrap置信区间和Holm校正，比较解码器类型（单步vs DDIM扩散、KV缓存自回归）、训练损失、刚体投影与坐标系；并检验预先注册的假设。

Result: 两个量级相近的因素主导：在物体初始位姿的SE(2)坐标系下表达输入输出，使五种解码器的决策后悔降低1.9–2.2倍，且128族训练的物体系模型与512族训练的世界系模型相当；物体系下单步头达0.020–0.021后悔、每决策1.6–1.8 ms，而10步DDIM为0.046（10.3 ms）、KV缓存自回归为0.059（12.9 ms）。单步头之间的选择无可测影响，且"坐标系效应大于解码器差异"的注册假设未获支持。软token期望+端到端2D Kabsch投影给出最低轨迹误差（11.35 px / 3.78 px）、精确刚体一致性，并在4–50步DDIM各预算下后悔均更低，延迟低3–29倍。

Conclusion: 对System-1世界模型而言，坐标系规范化与单步解码是关键的可控因素，解码器家族内部差异不显著；软token期望配端到端可微刚体投影是精度与效率俱佳的综合方案。

Abstract: Single-pass primitive decoders such as LocateAnything and VFlash predict all quantized coordinate tokens of a geometric primitive in one forward pass, enabling fast, System-1 visual planning. We study what matters when such a decoder serves as a System-1 world model: a fast action-conditioned predictor that ranks candidate pushes by their predicted outcome. On ManiSkill3 PushT-v1 (512 held-out test families, 8,192 rollouts) and on human demonstrations from lerobot/pusht (2,384 test windows), we compare decoders, training losses, rigid-body projections and coordinate frames, using matched checkpoints, hierarchical bootstrap confidence intervals and Holm correction. Two factors of similar size dominate. First, expressing inputs and outputs in the SE(2) frame of the object's initial pose lowers decision regret by 1.9-2.2x for each of five decoders, and the object-frame model trained on 128 families matches the world-frame model trained on 512. Second, single-pass decoding outperforms iterative decoding: in the object frame, single-pass heads reach 0.020-0.021 regret at 1.6-1.8 ms per decision, against 0.046 for 10-step DDIM diffusion and 0.059 for KV-cached autoregression at 10.3 and 12.9 ms. The choice among single-pass heads has no measurable effect on regret, and our registered hypothesis that the frame effect exceeds the decoder spread is not supported. Soft-token expectation with an end-to-end 2D Kabsch projection gives the lowest trajectory error (11.35 px on ManiSkill3, 3.78 px on lerobot/pusht), exact rigid-body consistency, and lower regret than diffusion at every budget from 4 to 50 DDIM steps, at 3-29x lower latency.

</details>


### [51] [AerialOcc: Unified 3D Aerial Perception without Task-Specific Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18770)
*Fan Liu,Xiang Gu,Baoyu Lv,Chuanyi Zhang,Liang Yao,Yuye Ma,Yuhui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 AerialOcc，一个基于占用（occupancy）的统一无人机感知模型，用单一推理过程支持多种感知任务，无需任务专用预测头，并构建了 AerialOcc-20K 大规模多类航空占用数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有航空感知方案本质上与任务专用输出绑定：检测、分割等目标要么用独立模型，要么共享 backbone 加多任务头，模型本身无法感知这些任务背后的共同结构。作者由此提出核心问题：能否用一个未针对特定任务设计的单一感知表示，支撑不同层次的理解？

Method: 作者认为 occupancy 是理想的 task-agnostic 表示：通过建模环境的空间存在性与语义，天然连接从粗粒度场景理解到细粒度目标定位的不同感知粒度。据此设计 AerialOcc——一个统一的 occupancy-based 感知模型，单次推理即可完成多种无人机感知任务，且不保留任何任务专用预测头；同时构建 AerialOcc-20K 大规模多类航空占用数据集以支撑该方向。

Result: 大量实验表明，AerialOcc 在保持统一架构的前提下，在多种感知任务上取得具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: occupancy 可作为通用感知接口（universal perception interface），为自主无人机提供一种基于共同 3D 场景表示的灵活、可扩展视觉智能新视角。模型与数据已开源。

Abstract: Aerial perception systems are increasingly expected to support diverse tasks, yet existing solutions remain fundamentally tied to task-specific outputs. Detection, segmentation, and other perception objectives are usually learned either by separate models or by sharing a backbone with multiple task heads, leaving the model itself unaware of the common structure underlying these tasks. We are interested in asking: can a single perception representation support different levels of understanding without being designed for any specific task? We notice that occupancy is a promising task-agnostic representation for drone perception. By modeling the spatial existence and semantics of the environment, it naturally bridges different perception granularities, from coarse scene understanding to fine-grained object localization. Therefore, we introduce AerialOcc, a unified occupancy-based perception model that enables multiple drone perception tasks from a single inference process, without maintaining task-specific prediction heads. To facilitate this direction, we construct AerialOcc-20K, a large-scale multi-class occupancy dataset for aerial environments. Extensive experiments show that AerialOcc achieves competitive performance across diverse perception tasks while preserving a unified architecture. Our findings demonstrate that occupancy can serve as a universal perception interface, offering a new perspective toward flexible and scalable visual intelligence for autonomous drones from a common 3D scene representation. Models and data are publicly available at https://github.com/guxiang2002/AerialOcc

</details>


### [52] [Agri-MRAG: Parameter-Enhanced Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Agricultural Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18771)
*Fuyong Liu,Xiantao Lei,Xining Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Agri-MRAG：面向农业视觉问答的多模态检索增强框架，通过语义一致性聚类选知、LoRA 参数化注入与困惑度路由，在 PlantVillageVQA 与新建 PlantVQA 上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MRAG 用于农业 VQA 时存在两点不足：检索相关知识时难以兼顾相关性与冗余控制；对检索证据的利用仍停留在简单的提示拼接层面，未深入影响生成过程。

Method: 1) 语义一致性引导的聚类机制：结合多模态聚类与加权相关性打分，筛选高置信知识子集并自适应确定检索规模 k；2) 参数化知识注入：将检索到的农业知识编码为 LoRA 适配器并合并进 MLLM 的前馈网络层，以参数级适配影响生成；3) PPL 引导路由：依据模型不确定性在隐式参数化推理与显式检索辅助生成之间切换，兼顾效率与可靠性；4) 评测使用 PlantVillageVQA，并构建区域特定林业与果树 VQA 数据集 PlantVQA（7,514 图、15,028 专家核验问答对）。

Result: 在 PlantVillageVQA 与 PlantVQA 上 ROUGE-L 分别为 41.21 与 46.58，较 mRAG 分别提升 11.70 与 4.51 分，较 VisRAG 分别提升 8.80 与 3.39 分。

Conclusion: Agri-MRAG 通过自适应检索规模、参数级知识注入与不确定性驱动的推理路由，有效缓解了冗余检索与浅层证据融合问题，在农业 VQA 上显著优于现有 MRAG 基线，并贡献了新的区域特定基准数据集。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MRAG) has shown promise for Agricultural Visual Question Answering (Agri-VQA), but existing methods still face challenges in retrieving relevant knowledge while avoiding redundancy and in integrating retrieved evidence beyond simple prompt concatenation. To address these issues, we propose Agri-MRAG, a multimodal retrieval-augmented framework for agricultural VQA. Specifically, we first introduce a semantic-consistency-guided clustering mechanism that combines multimodal clustering and weighted relevance scoring to select high-confidence knowledge subsets and adaptively determine the retrieval size $k$. We then design a parameterized knowledge injection strategy, where retrieved agricultural knowledge is encoded into LoRA adapters and merged into the feed-forward network (FFN) layers of the MLLM, allowing external knowledge to influence generation through parameter-level adaptation. In addition, a perplexity-guided (PPL-guided) routing mechanism is employed to switch between implicit parametric reasoning and explicit retrieval-assisted generation according to model uncertainty, balancing inference efficiency and answer reliability. For evaluation, we use PlantVillageVQA as a broad agricultural benchmark and construct PlantVQA, a region-specific forestry and fruit crop VQA dataset containing 7,514 images and 15,028 expert-verified question-answer pairs. Experiments show that Agri-MRAG achieves ROUGE-L scores of 41.21 and 46.58 on the two datasets, outperforming mRAG by 11.70 and 4.51 points, and VisRAG by 8.80 and 3.39 points, respectively.

</details>


### [53] [Quantum-inspired orthogonal adapters for certifiably robust medical image classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18775)
*Olga Tsurkan,Laia Domingo,Victoria Orlova,Asel Sagingalieva,Iris Zalaudek,Raffaele Dante Caposiena Caro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于量子启发正交蝶形层（butterfly layers）的微调方法，在仅用O(n log n)可训练参数的前提下保持预训练认证鲁棒网络的Lipschitz界，实现医学图像分类器的参数高效适应并保留认证鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类器需在输入小扰动下保持可靠预测，认证鲁棒性可提供数学保证，但将预训练模型适配到新任务时这些保证往往难以维持；同时需要参数高效的微调方案。

Method: 设计受可重构分束器量子电路启发的正交蝶形层，构造上保持正交性，仅含O(n log n)可训练参数，可高效经典实现，并在合适连通性假设下具备对数深度量子实现潜力。将其用于微调认证预训练网络，在MNIST、MedMNIST及毛发镜图像（脱发诊断与斑秃活动度评估）上开展对照实验。

Result: 对比常规微调与LoRA，蝶形微调在DermaMNIST迁移中对所测攻击更具抵抗性；在毛发镜分类任务上认证准确率约为常规微调和LoRA的两倍，而分类准确率与经验鲁棒性相当；以准确率下降为代价可获得更大鲁棒性保证，包括覆盖部分图像处理变换的认证。

Conclusion: 量子启发蝶形层在保持认证鲁棒性数学保证的同时实现医学图像分类器的参数高效适配，具备应用与向量子实现扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Medical image classifiers require not only high accuracy but also reliable predictions under small input perturbations. Certified robustness provides mathematical guarantees of prediction stability, but preserving these guarantees when adapting pretrained models to new tasks remains challenging. We propose a fine-tuning approach based on quantum-inspired orthogonal butterfly layers that preserves the Lipschitz bounds of pretrained certified networks. Inspired by quantum circuits of reconfigurable beam splitters, these layers preserve orthogonality by construction using only $\mathcal{O}(n\log n)$ trainable parameters. Their structured design enables efficient classical implementation and offers a path toward future quantum implementations with logarithmic circuit depth under suitable connectivity assumptions. Controlled experiments on MNIST and MedMNIST demonstrate improved certified robustness compared with conventional alternatives and, in the DermaMNIST transfer, greater resistance to the tested attacks than low-rank adaptation (LoRA). We then apply it to trichoscopic image classification for alopecia diagnosis and alopecia areata activity assessment. By adapting a certified ImageNet-pretrained network, butterfly fine-tuning achieves approximately twice the certified accuracy of conventional fine-tuning and LoRA, with comparable classification accuracy on the same backbone and data split and similar empirical resistance to the tested adversarial attacks. Larger robustness guarantees, including certificates covering certain image-processing transformations, can be obtained at the cost of reduced accuracy. These results highlight the potential of quantum-inspired butterfly layers for parameter-efficient adaptation of medical image classifiers while preserving the mathematical guarantees underlying certified robustness.

</details>


### [54] [Third-Place Solution for the UCF UrbanTwin V2X-Real Track at ECCV 2026: Synthetic Point Cloud Generation and Sim-to-Real 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18778)
*Antong Deng,Yuxin Zhang,Zhongxiang Xing,Zhenzhen Lin,Jiong Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 面向 UrbanTwin V2X-Real Sim2Real LiDAR 挑战赛，报告给出合成点云生成与 3D 检测的完整方案：用逐轴分位数校准生成 50 帧×2000 点云，用 RangeLDM 增强数据训练 VoxelRCNN，最终官方综合得分 0.6512。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实与仿真点云间存在域差异（Sim2Real gap），赛题同时要求高质量合成点云生成与鲁棒的 3D 目标检测，需要一套从数据构建到模型训练推理的完整技术方案。

Method: 生成端：以公开 V2X-Real 训练数据构造真实参考分布，对基础合成点云 x/y/z 三轴独立估计一维分位数映射进行逐轴分位数校准，再用固定随机种子重采样并裁剪至有效空间范围，每帧 2000 个 float32 XYZ 点、共 50 帧。检测端：采用两阶段 Voxel R-CNN，训练数据为 RangeLDM 增强的合成点云、每帧降采样至 50k 点，并加入重建的行人数据；4 张 RTX 4090、全局 batch 16、训练 40 epoch，测试时使用原版 VoxelRCNN 单模型直接推理。

Result: 官方评测服务器：归一化 Realism 0.7905，归一化 Detection 0.5583；mAP@0.5 为 0.3908，BEV mAP@0.5 为 0.4219，mAP@0.7 为 0.1047。按官方加权规则 0.6×0.5583 + 0.4×0.7905，综合得分 0.6512。

Conclusion: 逐轴分位数校准可有效缩小合成点云与真实分布差异，显著提升 Realism 指标；结合 RangeLDM 数据增强的 VoxelRCNN 在检测任务上取得可用性能。报告完整覆盖数据构建、模型训练、推理、提交格式与官方评测流程，可作为该赛题的基线技术参考。

Abstract: This report presents a complete technical solution for the UrbanTwin V2X-Real Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge, covering both synthetic point cloud generation and 3D object detection. For data generation, we adopt per-axis quantile calibration. Public V2X-Real training data are used to construct the real reference distribution, and independent one-dimensional quantile mappings are estimated for the x, y, and z axes of the base synthetic point clouds. The calibrated point clouds are then resampled using a fixed random seed and clipped to the valid spatial range, producing 50 frames with 2,000 float32 XYZ points per frame. For detection, we use a two-stage Voxel R-CNN (VoxelRCNN) detector trained with RangeLDM-enhanced synthetic point-cloud data, point clouds downsampled to 50k points per frame, and reconstructed pedestrian data. Training is performed on four NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090 GPUs with a global batch size of 16 for 40 epochs. Final testing uses direct single-model inference with the original VoxelRCNN model. On the official evaluation server, the normalized Realism score is 0.7905 and the normalized Detection score is 0.5583, with mAP@0.5 of 0.3908, BEV mAP@0.5 of 0.4219, and mAP@0.7 of 0.1047. According to the official weighting rule, the final combined score is 0.6 x 0.5583 + 0.4 x 0.7905 = 0.6512. This report documents the complete implementation details of data construction, model training, inference, submission formatting, and official evaluation.

</details>


### [55] [Same Evidence, Different Verdicts: Delivery of CT Images and DICOM Metadata Changes VLM Quality-Control Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18790)
*Nicolas Avelar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型（VLM）作为影像输入合规检查时，其判断结果严重依赖信息送达顺序：后到的信息几乎总能覆盖先前的正确答案（"后到者胜"）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床中扫描影像与DICOM元数据可能相互矛盾（如截断的CT序列被误纳入体积随访，影响肺结节增长测量），VLM被提议用作自动化输入检查。直觉上的安全设���是"让模型先看图、再看元数据"，但作者发现这种设计反而会失效，需要系统性验证送达顺序的影响。

Method: 在60对NLST胸部CT（含覆盖缺陷，ground truth由扫描几何计算）上测试；对比"图像与错误表头同时输入"vs"错误表头在模型给出答案之后到达"两种顺序；跨Claude Haiku 5.5、Nemotron-3.5-VL等多个VLM；并设置对照实验排除泛化谄媚（sycophancy）解释：中性"请确认"提示、表头以工具返回结果形式送达（模拟agentic流水线）、加入一句警告语，以及在24对真实不匹配数据上复验。

Result: 同时呈现时Claude Haiku 5.5仅有2%检测被错误表头推翻；而表头后到时，91%的检测被推翻（Nemotron-3.5-VL从43%升至100%）。顺序反转后，图像可推翻表头错误98–100%（Claude系列）。中性"请确认"提示在1,072次正确拒绝中推翻数为0；表头以工具结果形式送达时效应依旧存在；一句警告语将顺序性反转降至≤9%；24对真实不匹配数据上模式复现。

Conclusion: VLM输入检查决策在证据完全相同时取决于"送达方式"，构成临床VLM输入检查的重大可靠性隐患。论文贡献：(i) 证明送达顺序决定判断的证据；(ii) 带精确几何ground truth的测试平台；(iii) 面向临床VLM输入检查的验证协议；并公开代码、提示与全部调用日志。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are being proposed as automated checks on clinical imaging inputs, where a scan and its DICOM metadata can disagree; a truncated CT series wrongly admitted to volumetric follow-up, such as lung-nodule growth assessment in screening, biases the measurement. A natural safeguard is to let the model judge the image before it sees the metadata. We find this backfires. On 60 NLST chest CT pairs with coverage defects whose ground truth is computed from scan geometry, a false header that Claude Haiku 5.5 resisted when shown with the image (2% of its detections overruled) overturned 91% of them when it arrived after the model's answer (Nemotron-3.5-VL 43% to 100%). With the order reversed, the image overturned 98-100% of the header's errors (Claude models): whichever source came second won. This is not generic sycophancy: a neutral "please confirm" reversed 0 of 1,072 correct refusals, and the effect persisted when the header arrived as a tool result, as in agentic pipelines. One warning sentence cut sequential reversal to at most 9%, and the pattern held on 24 real mismatched pairs. Contributions: (i) evidence that VLM input-check decisions on identical evidence depend on delivery; (ii) a testbed with exact geometric ground truth; (iii) a validation protocol for clinical VLM input checks. Code, prompts and all call logs: https://github.com/nav92061/ct-vlm-delivery-order

</details>


### [56] [From Retouched Images to Paired Videos: A Synthetic Benchmark for Face Video Retouching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18791)
*Peiyuan Zeng,Fengchuang Xing,Shiming Wu,Hongwei Yue,Chao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeepVFR 是一个用于成对人脸视频修图（unretouched/retouched）的受控合成基准，而非新的修图架构，包含 1,024 对 150 帧、30 fps 的片段，并采用身份不相交协议与显式帧对应进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的成对人脸视频（未修图/已修图）制作成本高：专业编辑需在时间上保持一致性；同时发布可识别的面部视频会引发隐私、知情同意与治理方面的问题。因此需要一个受控的合成基准来提供成对监督与可控评测的起点。

Method: 以成对合成图像 DeepFRR 为起点，用 Wan2.2 生成轻度运动的源片段；再由后端特定的人脸迁移方法构建同步的代理修图目标；随后进行自动有效性筛选和人工目视检查。基准含 1,024 对 150 帧、30 fps 片段，评测采用身份不相交协议并显式给定帧对应关系，发布元数据将记录构建路径。评测手段包括基准内基线、在 DeepVFR 上应用的外部检查点、定性分析，以及 17 名参与者的排序研究。

Result: 基准内基线与外部检查点建立了受控域下的参考行为；人工排序研究显示，构建的目标存在明显的低偏好长尾。两条视频流均继承合成内容，目标流还额外继承商业 API 或人脸迁移的伪影。

Conclusion: DeepVFR 为成对监督与受控评测提供了起点；但它不能替代由艺术家修图的真实拍摄视频，也不建立从合成到真实的泛化结论。更广的运动覆盖与真实视频验证留待未来工作。数据、个体排序结果与训练日志将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Paired unretouched/retouched face videos are costly to produce because professional editing must remain consistent over time, while releasing identifiable face footage raises privacy, consent, and governance concerns. We present DeepVFR, an initial controlled synthetic benchmark-not a new retouching architecture-for paired face video retouching. Starting from paired synthetic DeepFRR images, Wan2.2 generates mild-motion source clips, and backend-specific face transfer constructs synchronized proxy retouching targets, followed by automatic validity screening and manual visual inspection. DeepVFR contains 1,024 pairs of 150-frame clips at 30 fps, evaluated under an identity-disjoint protocol with explicit frame correspondence; release metadata will record the construction route. Within-benchmark baselines, external checkpoints applied to DeepVFR, qualitative analysis, and a 17-participant ranking study establish reference behavior in the controlled domain. The constructed targets exhibit a substantial low-preference tail in the human study. Because both streams inherit synthetic content and the targets additionally inherit commercial-API or face-transfer artifacts, the benchmark neither replaces artist-retouched captured video nor establishes synthetic-to-real generalization. DeepVFR provides a starting point for paired supervision and controlled evaluation. Data, individual study rankings, and training logs will be made publicly available upon acceptance. Broader motion coverage and real-video validation remain future work.

</details>


### [57] [CARET: Clinical Assertion-Aware Residual Calibration for Medical Image-Text Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18811)
*Yifan Jin,Tao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARET: 一种参数高效的图像侧适配方法，用配对正负临床陈述构建低维语义校正空间，并按断言状态区分报告语义，提升胸片零样本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉-语言模型将胸片与自由文本报告对齐，但放射学语言区分显式所见、否定、不确定与未提及等状态。若在特征适配中把这些状态视为可互换，会在缺乏视觉证据支持的情况下改变图像预测，因此需要保留这种语义差异。

Method: 保留预训练的图像与文本编码器，利用配对的正、负临床陈述定义低维语义校正空间；以有界残差沿这些方向调整图像特征；采用断言感知监督区分明确存在、明确缺失与不确定/未提及的所见。推理阶段适配器仅需图像输入，文本参考保持固定。

Result: 零样本分类在 CheXpert 5×200 上达到 67.80%，在 RSNA 上达到 65.20%，较已发表的 CLEFT-LoRA 分别高 1.06 和 0.27 个百分点；作者指出与文献基线的比较未控制预训练和评测协议差异。八项消融实验表明，四状态区分与断言目标贡献最大，未提及保留与配对对齐呈任务依赖效应。

Conclusion: 对报告断言状态的显式建模是图像侧参数高效适配的关键，可在推理仅依赖图像的前提下提升零样本胸片分类，并保持文本侧固定。

Abstract: Medical vision--language models align radiographs with free-text reports, but radiology language distinguishes explicit findings, negation, uncertainty, and omission. Treating these report states as interchangeable during feature adaptation can change image predictions without supporting visual evidence. We introduce CARET, a parameter-efficient image-side adaptation method that retains pretrained image and text encoders and uses paired positive and negative clinical statements to define a low-dimensional semantic correction space. A bounded residual adjusts image features along these directions, while assertion-aware supervision distinguishes explicitly present and absent findings from uncertain or unmentioned ones. At inference, the adapter requires only an image; textual references remain fixed. In zero-shot classification, CARET achieves 67.80\% accuracy on CheXpert 5$\times$200 and 65.20\% on RSNA. These scores exceed the published CLEFT-LoRA results by 1.06 and 0.27 percentage points, respectively. Comparisons to literature baselines are informative but not controlled for differences in pretraining and evaluation protocols. Ablations across eight components identify the four-state distinction and assertion objective as major contributors, with task-dependent effects for nonmention preservation and pair alignment.

</details>


### [58] [RescueVLM: Model-Agnostic Vision-Language Framework for Rescue Drone Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18813)
*Niklas Voigt,Tim Sibum,Julien Meine,Niklas Digakis,Hartmut Surmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RescueVLM：开源、免训练、模型无关的无人机灾情影像分诊工具，基于SketchVLM，用本地开源VLM+提示词与输出契约替代云端服务与微调检测器，将整场测绘任务压缩为少量带标注帧供指挥员快速审阅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次无人机任务产生数百张影像，远超指挥员在时间压力下的审阅能力，人工筛查毁伤影像还有心理代价；而云端VLM受GDPR与德国BOS民防规定限制（影像不得离开应急网络），微调专用检测器又老化快、重训练与再认证成本超出多数救援组织资源。

Method: 构建RescueVLM：模型无关的免训练分诊工具，任务知识全部放在提示词与输出契约中而非训练权重；通用开源权重VLM全本地部署，通过下拉菜单即可换用更新模型；单条自然语言查询遍历任务文件夹，输出排序短名单、逐图理由、可编辑SVG叠加标注与命中点GPS地图。

Result: 在来自三次真实部署与一次实火演练的德国消防队现场数据上做定性评估：单条查询即可将整场勘察任务压缩为少量带标注帧，指挥员可一眼审阅。

Conclusion: 以免训练、模型无关、本地部署的设计实现合规且可持续升级的SAR影像分诊；评测仅为定性、样本为德国消防队现场数据，尚缺定量基准与跨场景泛化验证。

Abstract: A single unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) mission over an incident site produces hundreds of images, far more than a rescue commander can review under time pressure, and human screening of destruction imagery carries a documented psychological cost. The two obvious remedies both fail in search and rescue (SAR) practice: cloud vision-language model (VLM) services are excluded because the imagery must not leave the incident network under GDPR and German civil-protection (BOS) rules, and fine-tuned specialist detectors age quickly while retraining and re-certifying them exceeds most SAR organizations resources. We present RescueVLM, an open-source, training-free and model-agnostic triage tool built on SketchVLM: a general-purpose open-weight VLM runs fully on-premise and is swapped for a newer model through a simple dropdown selection, so triage quality grows with the underlying models because all task knowledge lives in prompts and an output contract rather than in trained weights. One natural-language query over a mission folder returns a ranked shortlist with per-image justifications, editable SVG overlay annotations, and a GPS map of the hits. In a qualitative evaluation on German fire-brigade field data from three real deployments and a live fire exercise, a single query condenses an entire survey mission into a handful of annotated frames that a commander can review at a glance.

</details>


### [59] [LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.6 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18822)
*Meituan LongCat Team,Xunliang Cai,Yiqi Chen,Feng Gao,Xiaoyu Kong,Le Li,Hongyu Liu,Xiaoming Wei,Tianyu Yang,Yong Zhang,Sijie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.6 通过改进后训练流程提升音频驱动虚拟人视频的表情自然度、唇音同步与长时一致性，可生成超 10 分钟视频并在 Arena 评测中超越前代与商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动虚拟人视频生成虽日益逼真，但仍存在唇音精确同步不足、动作不够自然拟人、长序列视觉一致性差等问题；1.5 版本还存在夸张唇动、唇色过红且时序不稳定等误差累积现象。

Method: 在 LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5 基础上精炼后训练流水线：引入强化学习、面向 DMD 少步蒸馏的改进无分类器引导（CFG）策略、长视频 rollout 以及 on-policy 蒸馏。

Result: 提升表情表达与头部/身体协同运动的自然性，抑制夸张唇动与唇色偏红等不稳定现象，减少长时生成的误差累积，实现超过 10 分钟视频的生成并保持视觉质量与时序一致性。

Conclusion: Arena 评测显示 1.6 优于其前代及竞争性商业音频驱动虚拟人模型，验证了改进后训练流水线的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in audio-driven avatar video generation have enabled increasingly realistic synthesis, yet achieving precise lip-audio synchronization, natural, human-like motion, and visual consistency over long sequences remains challenging. We present LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.6, which builds on LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5 to improve expressive naturalness and reduce error accumulation during extended generation, e.g., exaggerated lip movements and overly red, temporally unstable lip coloration, enhance the naturalness of facial expressions and coordinated head and body movements. These improvements are driven by a refined post-training pipeline that integrates reinforcement learning, improved classifier-free guidance strategies for DMD-based few-step distillation, long-video rollouts, and on-policy distillation. Together, these refinements improve expressive realism and enable the generation of videos exceeding 10 minutes while maintaining visual quality and temporal consistency. Arena evaluations show that LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.6 outperforms its predecessor and competing commercial audio-driven avatar models, demonstrating the effectiveness of the improved post-training pipeline.

</details>


### [60] [Scaling Channel-Adaptive Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18849)
*Alice V. De Lorenci,Seungeun Yi,Theo Moutakanni,Piotr Bojanowski,Camille Couprie,Juan C. Caicedo,Wolfgang M. Pernice*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现：在大规模数据集上训练时，通道独立编码（independent-encoding）显著优于联合编码（joint-encoding）的自监督策略，由此提出 DINO BoC 方法，在细胞显微与地理空间影像等多项基准上取得 SOTA，并提供开源的荧光显微通用特征提取器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像基础模型的自监督预训练已很成熟，但在生物、医学、卫星等非 RGB 科学影像领域，数据集的通道数量与语义高度异构（通道数目不同、各通道揭示的对象/场景信息不同），阻碍了自监督策略的广泛应用。已有通道自适应方法虽在不牺牲联合编码能力的前提下处理可变通道组合并报告了优势，但其在大规模下的表现仍不清楚。

Method: 系统性对比通道独立编码与联合编码两类通道自适应策略，并在从细胞显微到地理空间影像的多个大规模数据集上进行大量实验验证；基于"独立编码更优"的发现构建 DINO BoC 方法。

Result: 出人意料的是，大规模训练下通道独立编码以显著幅度优于联合编码；DINO BoC 在多项高难度基准（包括分布外任务泛化与未见通道组合）上刷新 SOTA。

Conclusion: 通道独立编码在大规模非 RGB 科学影像自监督预训练中更具优势，打破了对联合编码的直觉预期；作者开源代码与模型权重，提供面向荧光显微的通用特征提取器。

Abstract: Recent advances in self-supervised pre-training of foundation models for natural images have made them a popular choice for various visual systems and applications. Self-supervised strategies are also promising in non-RGB scientific imaging domains such as in biology, medical and satellite imagery, but their broader application is hampered by heterogeneity in channel composition and semantics between relevant datasets: two datasets may contain different numbers of channels, and these may reveal distinct aspects of an object or scene. Recent works on channel adaptive strategies report substantial advantages for those that account for variable channel compositions without sacrificing the ability to jointly encode channels; yet, how these strategies behave at scale remains unclear. We here show that, surprisingly, trained across large-scale datasets, independent-encoding of channels outperforms joint-encoding methods by a substantial margin. We validate this result along an extensive set of experiments on various datasets from cell microscopy to geospatial imagery. Our DINO BoC approach sets a new state-of-the-art across challenging benchmarks, including generalization to out-of-distribution tasks and unseen channel combinations. We open-source code and model weights for a new general-purpose feature extractor for fluorescent microscopy.

</details>


### [61] [Guided Group Featurizers (GGF): Improving Block-Sparse Featurizers with Object-Guided Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18861)
*Waldemar Chang,Andrii Krutsylo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GGF通过引入物体标注引导BSF的初始化与训练，在不改变BSF架构的前提下使特征组语义更凝聚，单组即可聚焦目标，并在COCO/VOC上优于SLiCS、Concept-SAE等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块稀疏特征器（BSF）用成组的学习方向刻画视觉概念的多维结构，但仅靠分组并不能保证一个概念的信息被整合进单一组内，因此需要一种机制提升组内的语义凝聚性。

Method: 提出引导式分组特征器（GGF）：一种以物体标注进行初始化与训练的物体引导扩展，保持BSF原有架构不变；通过盲评判断每组最强响应区域是否在跨图像上共享同一物体类型、部件或材质，并评估单组覆盖目标裁剪的比例、假阳性率、定位性能与参数/训练成本。

Result: 盲评语义一致性得分GGF为94.1±0.9%，SLiCS为83.0±1.9%，监督版Vanilla BSF为65.3±2.2%；在未受引导的类别上，每类选取一组可覆盖65.6±2.4%的含目标裁剪（Vanilla BSF为58.2±2.2%），假阳性率均约5%。在COCO与VOC上，GGF的单组目标捕获与定位均优于Vanilla BSF、SLiCS与Concept-SAE；其组响应更聚焦、可复现，并在旋转、尺度与亮度变化下保持关联；参数少于Concept-SAE，VOC训练轮次也少于两种监督基线。

Conclusion: 物体引导有助于组织出更具可解释性的BSF分组，且响应集中效应可泛化到未参与引导的类别，说明引导带来的是结构层面的改进而非单纯类别记忆。

Abstract: Block-sparse featurizers (BSFs) use groups of learned directions to capture the multidimensional structure of visual concepts. However, grouping alone does not ensure that information about a concept is consolidated within a single group. We introduce Guided Group Featurizers (GGF), a novel object-guided extension that uses object annotations for initialization and training while preserving the BSF architecture. We assess whether each group's strongest response regions share an object type, part or material across images. Blinded reviews give GGF groups an average semantic coherence score of $94.1\pm0.9$%, versus $83.0\pm1.9$% for SLiCS and $65.3\pm2.2$% for supervised Vanilla BSF. GGF's consolidation advantage extends to unguided categories: one selected group per category covers $65.6\pm2.4$% of target-containing crops, versus $58.2\pm2.2$% for supervised Vanilla BSF, at approximately 5% false-positive rates for both. Across COCO and VOC, GGF also improves single-group object Capture and localization over Vanilla BSFs, SLiCS and Concept-SAE. Its groups produce more focused and reproducible object responses while supporting recognition and retaining associations under rotation, scale and brightness changes. GGF also uses fewer learned parameters than Concept-SAE and shorter recorded VOC training loops than both supervised alternatives. These findings support object guidance for organizing more interpretable BSF groups and show that response concentration extends to categories excluded from guidance.

</details>


### [62] [FiReIQ: Field-Level Confidence Estimation with Reasoning and Image Quality in Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18865)
*Xiao Liu,Yage Wang,Qi Yu,Giri Iyengar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FiReIQ字段级置信度估计框架，融合单次VLM生成的字段对齐推理信号与图像质量特征，并构建FiReIQ-Bench基准；实验证明其在置信度判别、选择性预测与概率准确性上优于现有估计器，在私有金融服务数据上AUROC提升6.8%、95%精度下覆盖率提升10.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在结构化文档抽取中表现良好，但高风险场景需可靠的字段级置信度。现有不确定性估计器多聚合整段生成的信号，掩盖字段级推理不确定性，且忽略可能导致"自信错误"的图像质量退化。

Method: FiReIQ：字段级置信度估计框架，通过质量条件化视觉融合（quality-conditioned visual fusion），将单次VLM生成中字段对齐的推理信号与局部、全局图像质量特征相结合；同时构建覆盖金融票据与法律合同的FiReIQ-Bench基准用于系统评估。

Result: 在两个推理型VLM上，FiReIQ在置信度判别、选择性预测与概率准确性上持续优于现有估计器；在私有金融服务数据上，AUROC较基线提升6.8%，95%精度下覆盖率提升10.6%。

Conclusion: FiReIQ可减少人工复核并支撑更可靠的文档自动化；字段级推理信号与图像质量的联合建模是提升文档抽取置信度可靠性的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in structured document extraction, yet their deployment in high-stakes applications requires reliable confidence estimates for individual fields. Existing uncertainty estimators often aggregate signals across an entire generation, obscuring field-specific reasoning uncertainty while overlooking visual degradations that may lead to confident extraction errors. To address these limitations, we introduce FiReIQ, a field-level confidence estimation framework that combines field-aligned reasoning signals from a single VLM generation with local and global image-quality features through quality-conditioned visual fusion. To facilitate systematic evaluation, we further introduce FiReIQ-Bench, a benchmark spanning financial receipts and legal contracts. Experiments with two reasoning VLMs show that FiReIQ consistently improves confidence discrimination, selective prediction, and probabilistic accuracy over existing estimators. These gains extend to private financial service data, where FiReIQ improves AUROC by 6.8% and coverage at 95% precision by 10.6% over an existing baseline. Together, these results highlight the potential of FiReIQ to reduce manual review while enabling more reliable document automation.

</details>


### [63] [UnSPAD: Uncertainty-aware Single Photon Burst Photography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18869)
*Sachin Shah,Kalyan Bhetwal,Levi Burner,Christopher A. Metzler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UnSPAD：不确定性感知的SPAD二值突发摄影框架，可融合去噪任意长度序列


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有方法需固定突发长度或依赖强生成先验，易产生幻觉

Method: 先用光流对齐二值帧求均值得到初始场景估计，再利用逐像素不确定性图（反映运动/遮挡/对齐误差导致的观测不足与散粒噪声）引导去噪；除光流结构外初始估计无先验

Result: 在真实与仿真SPAD及QIS基准上达到SOTA重建质量，时间一致性与色彩保真度优于现有方法

Conclusion: 学习式运动估计+不确定性融合去噪可在无生成先验下实现无幻觉、时间一致的重建

Abstract: We introduce UnSPAD, an uncertainty-aware burst photography framework capable of fusing and denoising arbitrarily long sequences of binary single photon avalanche diode (SPAD) measurements. Unlike prior methods that require fixed burst lengths or rely on powerful generative priors, UnSPAD combines learned motion estimation with uncertainty-informed measurement fusion and denoising to produce temporally consistent, hallucination-free reconstructions. UnSPAD first forms an initial scene estimate by averaging flow-aligned binary frames. UnSPAD then uses per-pixel uncertainty maps to perform uncertainty-aware denoising; the denoiser is informed about which regions accumulated the fewest observations (and thus contain the most relative shot noise) due to motion, occlusion, or alignment errors. This two-step reconstruction pipeline helps mitigate hallucinations---outside of the structure implicitly introduced by the flow estimates, the initial scenes estimates are entirely prior-free. Experiments on real and simulated SPAD and quantum image sensor (QIS) benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art reconstruction quality, improved temporal consistency, and superior color fidelity relative to the state of the art.

</details>


### [64] [RAVEN-Bench: A Paired EO-IR Video QA Benchmark for Aerial Multimodal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18880)
*Yu Hu,Jianyang Gu,Yue Cao,Hao Liu,Kangnan Wang,Jozsef Hamari,Zheng Liu,Mohsen Zardadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RAVEN-Bench，一个配对的航空光电-红外（EO-IR）视频问答基准，用于诊断 MLLM 的跨光谱推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在 RGB 图像/视频理解上进展迅速，但其在配对航空 EO-IR 视频上的推理能力尚缺乏系统评测；这类视频相比标准 RGB 视频 QA 更难：目标小、证据短暂、视角随平台变化、有效线索可能只出现在单一模态。

Method: 构建 RAVEN-Bench：100 对时间对齐的 EO-IR 视频与 1,200 道人工撰写四选一题目，逐题审核答案键、模态需求、证据时间区间与分组有效性；支持仅 EO、仅 IR、EO+IR 三种受控模态条件，采用分层能力等级与结构化问题组进行诊断。

Result: 评测前沿、开源及遥感类 MLLM，可诊断相对更强单模态条件的配对输入增益、单模态依赖、目标一致性与证据链连贯性；结果显示总体准确率会高估具备证据支撑的 EO-IR 推理能力。

Conclusion: RAVEN-Bench 提供受控模态条件与组级诊断，用于审计 RGB 视频基准上的进展是否能迁移到航空跨光谱理解；代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced rapidly in image and video understanding, yet their ability to reason over paired aerial electro-optical and infrared (EO-IR) videos remains poorly understood. Such video poses a harder test than standard RGB video QA: targets are often small, evidence may appear only briefly, viewpoints change with the platform, and useful cues may appear in only one stream. We introduce RAVEN-Bench (Reasoning over Aerial Videos with EO-IR aligNment), a paired EO-IR video QA benchmark for low-altitude aerial multimodal understanding. RAVEN-Bench contains 100 temporally aligned EO-IR video pairs and 1,200 human-authored four-way multiple-choice questions, each independently audited for answer keys, modality requirements, evidence intervals, and group validity. It supports EO-only, IR-only, and paired EO+IR evaluation, and uses hierarchical capability levels and structured question groups to diagnose paired-input gain beyond the stronger unimodal condition, unimodal reliance, target consistency, and evidence-chain coherence. We evaluate frontier, open-weight, and remote-sensing MLLMs, showing that aggregate accuracy can overstate evidence-supported EO-IR reasoning. RAVEN-Bench provides controlled modality conditions and group-level diagnostics for auditing whether progress on RGB video benchmarks transfers to aerial cross-spectral understanding. Code and data are available at https://github.com/yuhu990424/RAVEN-Bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/TerraSenseAnalytics/RAVEN-Bench.

</details>


### [65] [CISyn: Human Mesh Recovery in Close Interactions with Physics-Simulated Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18894)
*Yufan Deng,Lorenza Prospero,Christian Rupprecht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CISyn——一个基于物理仿真与Blender渲染构建的大规模合成数据集，面向近距离人体交互场景的3D人体网格恢复（HMR），包含2.5万条带精确3D标注的序列，并提出不确定性感知损失以提升对重遮挡样本的学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近距离交互场景下的3D人体网格恢复受限于缺乏大规模、精确标注的训练数据：真实采集成本高昂，而现有合成数据集极少包含物理接触，因而缺少接触、相互遮挡与姿态纠缠等关键特性。

Method: 通过SMPL-X骨骼化人体之间的碰撞动力学仿真，结合Blender渲染自动生成具备接触、互遮挡与姿态纠缠特性的数据，无需人工标注；同时设计不确定性感知损失（uncertainty-aware loss），使模型能更有效地从重遮挡样本中学习。

Result: 将CISyn用于增强当前最优HMR模型的训练，并以不确定性感知损失替代原回归损失后，在近距离交互基准上取得一致性能提升。

Conclusion: CISyn可作为真实数据可扩展的补充，为近距离交互HMR提供低成本、高精度标注的数据来源；数据集、生成流程与代码已开源。

Abstract: 3D human mesh recovery (HMR) in close-interaction scenarios is hindered by the lack of large-scale, accurately annotated training data: real capture is expensive, and existing synthetic datasets rarely include physical contact. We introduce CISyn, a large-scale synthetic dataset purpose-built for close human-human interactions, generated via physics simulation and Blender-based rendering. By simulating collision dynamics between SMPL-X-rigged humanoids, CISyn naturally produces the contact, mutual occlusion, and pose entanglement that characterize close-interaction scenes, with no manual annotation effort. The released set provides 25,000 sequences with accurate 3D annotations. We further propose an uncertainty-aware loss that allows models to learn more effectively from the heavily occluded examples in our data. Augmenting state-of-the-art HMR training with CISyn and replacing the regression loss with our uncertainty-aware variant yields consistent improvements on close-interaction benchmarks, validating CISyn as a scalable complement to real-world data. The dataset, generation pipeline, and code are available at https://github.com/yfde/CISyn.

</details>


### [66] [Effects of Conventional, Adaptive, and AI-Powered Upscaling on the Legibility of License Plates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18949)
*Hany Farid,Joshua D. Bucci,Emily A. Cooper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 两项在线实验测量255块美国车牌（原高128–8像素）在8种放大方法下的可读性：传统插值仅小幅增益（5–9个百分点），最近邻有害，SwinIR最多提升38个百分点，而某自适应去噪先验与商业AI放大反而降低可读性；未发现稳定的字符幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在人类读者可读性这一目标下，传统插值与AI生成式放大孰优，以及AI"合成细节"究竟是帮助还是误导读取。

Method: 两项在线人类行为实验；以美国车牌为刺激材料，分辨率从128像素降至8像素高；对比"不放大"与8种放大方法（双线性、双三次、Lanczos、最近邻、SwinIR、自适应去噪图像先验、商业AI放大等）；测量可读性（legibility），并将任务分解为字符计数与字符识别子任务。

Result: 未放大车牌的可读性在32→10像素的狭窄区间内从天花板跌至地板；双线性/双三次/Lanczos在区间内提升约5–9个百分点；最近邻有害，使原本可读的许多车牌变为不可读；SwinIR相对不放大最高提升38个百分点；自适应去噪先验与商业AI放大倾向损害可读性；各方法均无稳定的字符幻觉证据。

Conclusion: 放大方法的收益取决于其是否保留而非臆造信息：传统插值仅助计数、增益有限，SwinIR因使字符本身更可辨而收益最大，生成式/去噪类方法可能引入误导性细节而适得其反。

Abstract: Small details in digital images -- a face, a street sign, a license plate -- are routinely upscaled in hopes of making them interpretable. Upscaling options now range from conventional interpolation, whose operations are spatially invariant and fully explainable, to AI-powered methods that synthesize detail potentially absent from the source. Here, we ask which of these techniques most reliably helps human readers. In two online experiments, we measured legibility of 255 US license plates ranging in resolution from 128 to 8 pixels tall, as well as their legibility after upscaling by eight different methods. Legibility of the un-upscaled plates collapsed from ceiling to floor across a narrow band of plate heights (32 to 10 pixels). Conventional upscaling techniques (bilinear, bicubic, and Lanczos interpolation) gave modest gains of about 5-9 percentage points within this range. Nearest-neighbor interpolation, however, was harmful, rendering unreadable many plates that were readable without any upscaling. Advanced methods diverged: SwinIR, an open-source AI enhancer, raised performance by up to 38 points relative to no upscaling, while an adaptive denoiser-based image prior and commercial AI upscaler tended to impair legibility. We found no strong evidence of consistent character hallucinations across methods. Decomposing the task, conventional interpolators mainly helped readers count characters, whereas SwinIR alone made the characters themselves more identifiable.

</details>


### [67] [Probing Where Surprise Happens: Localized Violation-of-Expectation Evaluation of Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18979)
*Yanbin Xu,Ming bo Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 传统VOE基准将预测误差在时空上全局聚合，无法确定成功判别是否来自违反发生处的理解；本文提出局部化VOE评估框架，被发现全局与局部判断可双向背离，表明全局预测误差不必然反映物理违反的时空证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有违反预期（VOE）基准通过比较物理不可能事件与匹配可能事件的预测误差来评估视频世界模型的直觉物理能力，但误差在空间与时间上的全局聚合掩盖了判别依据的来源：无法确定模型是否真正在物理违反发生的具体位置与时刻表现出理解，因此需要一种能够定位证据的评估方式。

Method: 提出局部化VOE评估框架，仅在与物理违反相关的空间区域和时间区间内计算可能与不可能事件之间的预测误差差异；据此对在发展性自我中心视频和大规模自然视频上预训练的V-JEPA模型，分别进行全局、时间局部化和时空局部化三种评估的对比。

Result: 全局与局部判断可在两个方向上出现分歧：全局聚合既可能掩盖正确的局部判别，也可能在缺乏相应局部证据的情况下呈现出表面上的成功判别；在发展性自我中心视频上继续预训练时，全局与局部判断的一致性呈非单调变化，而局部判断的置信度上升，尽管准确率提升有限。

Conclusion: 局部化VOE评估可作为评估视频世界模型直觉物理能力的补充性诊断工具，并说明全局预测误差并不必然反映物理违反的时空证据。

Abstract: Violation-of-expectation (VOE) benchmarks evaluate intuitive physics in video world models by testing whether physically impossible events produce greater prediction errors than matched possible events. However, conventional VOE evaluations aggregate prediction errors across space and time, making it unclear whether successful discrimination reflects model's understanding of physical violations at the locations and time where they occur. To address this limitation, we introduce localized VOE evaluation, a framework that measures prediction error differences between possible and impossible events within spatial regions and temporal intervals associated with physical violations. We compare global, temporally localized, and spatiotemporally localized evaluations of V-JEPA models pretrained on developmental egocentric videos or large-scale natural video datasets. Our results reveal that global and localized judgments can diverge in both directions: global aggregation can either obscure correct localized discrimination or produce apparent discrimination without corresponding localized evidence. During continued pretraining on developmental egocentric videos, agreement between global and localized judgments changes non-monotonically, while confidence in localized judgments increases despite modest improvements in accuracy. Together, these findings establish localized VOE evaluation as a complementary diagnostic for assessing intuitive physics in video world models and demonstrate that global prediction errors do not necessarily reflect the spatiotemporal evidence of physical violations.

</details>


### [68] [Role-Separated Diffusion Transformers: Global Rule Isolation and Deterministic Sampling Manifold Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19009)
*Zhiguang Liu,Yi Shang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoSeDiT 是一种角色分离扩散 Transformer，通过 adaLN 聆听机制将控制器通道与图像 token 的角色分离引入潜空间扩散，在相同参数与训练协议下全面超越 SiT，并提出 DSMC 两阶段训练策略进一步降低 FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoSeViT 在视觉推理中表明，普通 ViT 把共享规则摊布到全部 L 个 token 上，熵为 O(Ld)，而强制控制器路径可将其降至 O(d)。但在类条件 DiT/SiT 中，类别与噪声水平已由 adaLN 显式条件化，仅在 ViT 侧加控制器通道无法实现完整的角色分离，因此需要把角色分离机制迁移到潜空间扩散领域。

Method: 提出 RoSeDiT：将全局规则与图像 token 隔离，并引入 adaLN 聆听机制（adaLN listening），使 adaLN 在控制器的指令下进行计算，从而把熵定理与角色分离带入潜扩散。此外，观察到相同去噪损失下 FID 差异巨大，形式化为确定性采样流形压缩（DSMC），并采用两阶段训练调度。

Result: 在同参数量的 SiT 训练/评估协议下，400K 步时四个模型尺度全部优于 SiT；RoSeDiT-L/2 以少 32% 参数达到 15.34 FID，超过 SiT-XL/2 的 17.2。RoSeDiT-XL/2 在 1.4M 步达到 7.98，超过 SiT-XL/2 在 7M 步的 8.3，仅用五分之一步数。结合 DSMC 后，RoSeDiT-XL/2 在 2.4M 步达到 5.84，超过 SiT + REPA 在 4M 步的 5.9。

Conclusion: 角色分离与 adaLN 聆听机制可有效提升扩散 Transformer 的采样质量与训练效率；而去噪目标与图像流形之间存在错位，DSMC 训练调度能缓解该问题并进一步提升性能。

Abstract: We present Role-Separated Diffusion Transformers (RoSeDiT), a new diffusion transformer architecture that isolates the global rule from the image tokens. RoSeViT showed in visual reasoning that a vanilla ViT spreads a shared rule over all $L$ tokens at an entropy of order $Ld$, whereas an enforced controller route reduces it to order $d$. In class-conditional DiT and SiT, the class and the noise level are explicitly conditioned by adaLN, so a RoSeViT-style controller channel on the ViT side is insufficient to establish the full role separation. By introducing the adaLN listening mechanism, which makes adaLN compute under the controller's command, RoSeDiT brings the entropy theorem and role separation into the latent diffusion domain. Under the same SiT training and evaluation protocol at the same level of parameter count, RoSeDiT surpasses SiT at all four model scales at 400K steps, and RoSeDiT-L/2 even surpasses SiT-XL/2's 17.2 FID with 15.34 and 32% fewer parameters. RoSeDiT-XL/2 surpasses SiT-XL/2's 7M-step FID of 8.3 at 1.4M steps with 7.98, using one-fifth as many training steps.
