We further observe that models with the same denoising loss can have a large FID gap, which indicates a misalignment between the denoising objective and the image manifold. We formalize this phenomenon into Deterministic Sampling Manifold Compression (DSMC), a two-stage training schedule with which RoSeDiT-XL/2 surpasses SiT + REPA's 4M-step FID of 5.9 at 2.4M steps with 5.84.

</details>


### [69] [Hard or Mislabeled? Failure Screens Enrich Vision Benchmarks for Label Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19044)
*Haolin Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 难样本视觉基准（如ImageNet-D/Hard）常用强分类器失败图，而失败中大量源于标签错误；本文量化该污染并给出可预测的经验曲线与审计工具ScreenAudit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难样本基准以"强分类器都错"筛图，但分类器出错既可能因图像难，也可能因标签错，导致筛选器系统性收集标签错误，虚高基准难度、误导模型评估与训练。

Method: 在ImageNet两次重标注验证集上用103个分类器测量；统计66个监督分类器全错的图像中错误标签比例，并考察"通过筛选的图像越少—误标比例越高"的曲线在模型族间与ImageNet-D/ImageNet-Hard上的可迁移性；在ImageNet-V2、CIFAR-10及ImageNet-A（限类别筛选）上检验；用ReImageNet做人工重标注核验。

Result: 66个监督分类器全错的1,892张图中61%标签错误，整体仅11.1%；曲线跨模型族预测平均误差2.4个百分点，复现ImageNet-D与ImageNet-Hard分别为0.1与6.3点；ImageNet-V2最严筛选实测67%误标（预测46%），CIFAR-10富集更强；限类别筛选（ImageNet-A 200/1000）低于曲线；按ReImageNet，ImageNet-Hard的ImageNet图像中32%无可正确标签。

Conclusion: 难样本筛选的"失败即难"假设不成立，失败屏幕是标签错误的富集放大器；误标比例随严格度呈近似统一曲线且可跨模型族预测，但新数据池与限类别筛选偏离。作者将发布ScreenAudit审计工具、ImageNet-Hard 5,464张已判定图的修正答案集重建脚本及1,198张基准图逐图审计。

Abstract: Hard vision benchmarks often keep images that strong classifiers get wrong. Since a classifier is also marked wrong when the label is wrong, such failure screens may collect label errors. We measure this on ImageNet's twice-relabeled validation set with 103 classifiers. Among the 1,892 images that all 66 supervised classifiers get wrong, 61% have a wrong label, versus 11.1% overall. The fewer images a screen lets through, the more of them are mislabeled, along approximately one curve. Fit on one model family, this curve predicts the screens of two held-out model families with a mean error of 2.4 percentage points, and replays of ImageNet-D's and ImageNet-Hard's screens within 0.1 and 6.3 points. On new pools, ImageNet-V2 and CIFAR-10, enrichment at the strictest screens is stronger than the curve predicts: on ImageNet-V2, by human relabeling, 67% of the images that all 66 classifiers get wrong are mislabeled, against 46% predicted. Screens that let models choose among only some of the classes, as ImageNet-A's does (200 of 1,000), fall below the curve. By ReImageNet, 32% of ImageNet-Hard's ImageNet images have no correct label. ScreenAudit, which comprises an audit tool, a script that rebuilds corrected answer sets for the 5,464 decided ImageNet-Hard images (of 5,646) from ReImageNet's gated release, and per-image audits of 1,198 benchmark images, will be released later.

</details>


### [70] [SearchChord: Prompt Preselection and Field Recomposition for One-Step Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19076)
*Boyang Deng,Yuzhen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 SearchChord，通过候选池（prompt-seed 对）、文本相似度预选、图像验证器打分与潜空间重组，在单步图像编辑中权衡保真度、原始内容保持与计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为一步式图像编辑器寻找有效 prompt 与 seed 需同时平衡编辑保真度、源图保持与计算开销，逐一大规模生成验证代价高；且均匀平均式重组缺乏质量区分度。

Method: 构建由唯一 prompt-seed 对定义的大规模候选编辑池并按需生成图像；SearchChord 先做基于文本的预选（TextSim 从 100 个候选中筛出 10 个），再用图像验证器打分作为重组权重，在潜空间中做加权重组而非均匀平均；在 PIE-Bench 与自建 CropWeed-EditBench 上开展消融，池规模至 200。

Result: 池规模 100 为性价比最佳工作点；候选选择提升目标文本对齐，重组更好保持未修改源内容；oracle 验证器在保持与文本对齐上均最优；实际验证器仅部分恢复语义对齐，重组仍损害编辑保真度（含农业图像编辑场景）。

Conclusion: 存在保真度与保持度的权衡，据此倡导“文本优先预选 + 更强验证”的路线，以在保真度、保持性与成本间取得平衡；代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Finding effective prompts and seeds for one-step image editors requires balancing edit fidelity, source preservation, and computational cost. To address this challenge, we explore a large collection of candidate edits, where each candidate is defined by a unique prompt-seed pair, and generate corresponding images on demand. We present SearchChord, a training-free framework that leverages a large pool of such candidates, combining text-based preselection, image verification, and latent-field recomposition. Our ablation studies across pool sizes up to 200 candidates show that a pool of 100 offers a cost-effective operating point. Compared with feeding all 100 candidates into recomposition, TextSim preselects a subset of 10 candidates. Rather than uniform averaging, we incorporate image-verifier scores to weight each candidate during recomposition. Experiments on PIE-Bench and the proposed CropWeed-EditBench reveal a trade-off: candidate selection improves target-text alignment, while recomposition better preserves unmodified source content. The oracle verifier achieves the best performance in both preservation and target-text alignment. Practical verification partially recovers semantic alignment, but recomposition still compromises edit fidelity, including in agricultural image edits. These results motivate text-first preselection and stronger verification to balance preservation, fidelity, and cost. Code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [71] [FDAM: A Frequency-Direction-Aware Multi-Task Framework for Pavement Crack Grid Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19089)
*Haiyang Xu,Bin Pan,Mengze Wang,Qiaoying Qu,Zhenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FDAM多任务框架，结合框检测与网格分类分支，通过频域增强与方向特征细化实现细小微裂缝的网格定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细小微裂缝视觉线索弱、背景干扰强，框检测易漏检，而监督式语义分割依赖有限像素级标注且标注成本高。

Method: FDAM含框检测与网格分类双分支，核心网格分类分支由频域增强(FDE，自适应加权谱带并将重建特征与空间特征融合)与方向特征细化(DFR，多尺度条带卷积、方向敏感调制、特征重校准)构成；采用自适应网格监督，即自适应重要性重加权关注不确定预测与局部标签跃迁，并对所有网格采用focal监督；两分支通过共享特征学习耦合。

Result: 在自建RoadDistress2026细小微裂缝数据集上验证，并在公开数据集Crack500与DeepCrack上进一步评估。

Conclusion: 将框检测与网格分类分支通过共享特征学习耦合，可在降低标注成本的同时提升路面裂缝网格定位性能。

Abstract: Pavement crack grid localization remains challenging for thin and tiny cracks due to weak visual cues and background interference. Box detection often misses these cracks, while supervised semantic segmentation faces limited pixel-wise annotations and high labeling costs. In this paper, we propose a Frequency-Direction-Aware Multi-Task framework (FDAM) with box detection and grid classification branches. The core grid classification branch comprises Frequency-Domain Enhancement (FDE) and Directional Feature Refinement (DFR). FDE adaptively weights spectral bands and fuses reconstructed features with spatial features. DFR captures thin crack patterns through multi-scale strip convolutions, direction-sensitive modulation, and feature recalibration. Sparse positive cells and ambiguous local predictions motivate adaptive grid supervision. Adaptive Importance Reweighting emphasizes uncertain predictions and local label transitions, with focal supervision for all cells. Our contribution is to couple the box detection and grid classification branches through shared feature learning for pavement crack grid localization. We validate FDAM on our RoadDistress2026 dataset of thin and tiny cracks and further evaluate it on public datasets Crack500 and DeepCrack. The code is available at https://github.com/Lab-PANbin/FDAM

</details>


### [72] [DEFDVO: Deformable Deep Visual Odometry for Equirectangular Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19095)
*Burak Sevsay,Egor Bondarev,Erkut Akdag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DEFDVO：一种直接在ERP（等距柱状投影）影像上运行的patch级深度VO方法，通过球面初始化可变形特征提取、端到端接缝感知估计和球面梯度patch选择，解决360°相机ERP投影的纬度畸变、接缝和极区采样偏置问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 360°相机每帧捕获全场景，视野优于有限视场的针孔传感器，利于视觉里程计（VO）。但ERP投影带来随纬度变化的畸变、图像边界的伪接缝以及对两极的采样偏置，现有360° VO方法仅部分应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出patch级深度VO方法DEFDVO，直接作用于ERP图像，包含三部分：(1) 球面初始化可变形特征提取器，将球面几何先验与可学习采样偏移结合；(2) 端到端接缝感知处理，保持ERP连续性；(3) 球面梯度patch选择，降低极区采样偏置。仅在合成数据TartanAirV2 ERP影像上训练。

Result: 在360DVO定义的TartanAirV2评测集上，最佳平均绝对轨迹误差（ATE）由0.201 m降至0.042 m，提升4.8倍；在真实世界360DVO基准的两个划分上均取得最低平均ATE。

Conclusion: 球面几何先验与接缝、极区偏置的针对性设计可有效释放ERP 360° VO的潜力，且仅用合成数据训练即可在真实基准上达到领先精度。

Abstract: Omnidirectional ($360^\circ$) cameras capture the full surrounding scene in every frame, providing a strong advantage for visual odometry (VO) over pinhole sensors with a limited field of view. However, their equirectangular projection (ERP) introduces latitude-dependent distortion, an artificial seam at the image border, and a sampling bias toward the poles. Existing $360^\circ$ VO methods address these challenges only partially. DEFDVO closes this gap with a patch-based deep VO method that operates directly on ERP imagery, combining spherically initialized deformable feature extraction, end-to-end seam-aware estimation, and spherical-gradient patch selection. The deformable feature extractor combines spherical geometric priors with learned sampling offsets, while seam-aware processing preserves ERP continuity, and spherical-gradient selection reduces polar sampling bias. Trained exclusively on synthetic TartanAirV2 ERP imagery, DEFDVO improves the best prior average absolute trajectory error (ATE) by $4.8\times$ (0.201 m to 0.042 m) on the TartanAirV2 evaluation set defined by 360DVO and achieves the lowest average ATE on both splits of the real-world 360DVO benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/BurakSevsay/DEFDVO

</details>


### [73] [DecoEdit: Beyond Flat Control for 3D-Aware Unified Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19121)
*Xiaotang Gai,Jinbo Xing,Zijie Meng,Jiayi Yi,Jimin Xiao,Yixuan Qian,Yu Liu,Chaojie Mao,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DecoEdit统一图像编辑框架，融合1D文本、2D空间与3D体素控制，配套构建1.6M样本的DecoDataset与DecoBench，通过SAM-3D结构引导与细节保持双路径解耦，在3D位姿/朝向控制上超越Seedream 5.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图像编辑局限于"扁平"2D控制（文本提示、包围框、掩码），缺乏精确的3D几何操控能力；在协调目标位置、物理尺度与3D朝向的同时严格保持细粒度身份，仍面临细节严重退化或需昂贵逐实例微调的问题。

Method: 1) 建立数据策划流程，构建含160万样本的大规模数据集DecoDataset，覆盖1D/2D/3D任务及其复杂组合；2) 建立综合评测基准DecoBench；3) 提出解耦式流程DecoEdit：以SAM-3D提供的结构引导锚定目标几何、尺度与空间朝向，同时并行使用细节保持路径锁定高保真纹理，统一架构下集成三类输入。

Result: 大量实验显示DecoEdit达到SOTA；在具有挑战性的3D空间位姿与朝向控制任务上，其几何精度与身份一致性显著优于Seedream 5.0等领先闭源模型。

Conclusion: 通过统一多维控制架构与结构引导/细节保持的解耦设计，可在无需逐实例微调的前提下实现精确3D感知的几何操控与身份保真，配合配套数据集与基准推动该方向系统化研究。

Abstract: While recent advances in multimodal image editing have achieved impressive results, current frameworks remain largely confined to ``flat" 2D controls (e.g., text prompts, bounding boxes, or masks). Precise 3D-aware geometric manipulation -- such as coordinating an object's location, physical scale, and 3D orientation while strictly preserving its fine-grained identity -- remains an elusive challenge, typically suffering from severe detail degradation or requiring expensive per-instance fine-tuning. In this paper, we present DecoEdit, an all-in-one image editing framework that goes beyond flat control by seamlessly integrating 1D textual, 2D spatial, and 3D volumetric inputs under a unified architecture. First, to power this unified model, we establish a data curation pipeline and curate a large-scale dataset, namely DecoDataset, containing 1.6 million samples, encompassing diverse multi-dimensional (1D/2D/3D) tasks and their complex compositions. Furthermore, we establish a comprehensive image editing benchmark named DecoBench to systematically evaluate multi-task editing performance. Finally, to address the fundamental trade-off between complex spatial transformations and identity preservation, we propose a novel decoupled pipeline: DecoEdit factorizes the editing process by utilizing structural guidance from SAM-3D to anchor the target geometry, scale, and spatial orientation, while concurrently leveraging a detail-preservation path to lock in high-fidelity textures. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DecoEdit achieves state-of-the-art results. Notably, on the challenging 3D spatial pose and orientation control tasks, DecoEdit significantly outperforms leading closed-source models such as Seedream 5.0 in both geometric accuracy and identity consistency.

</details>


### [74] [Puzzle: One Pass Is Not Enough for Open-Vocabulary Remote Sensing Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19146)
*Wensong Xu,Siqi Lu,Jibao Yuan,Yongbin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的迭代重推理框架 Puzzle，利用预测历史与视觉相似性先验逐步修正 patch 级注意力，在不训练分割模型的前提下提升开放词汇遥感图像分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OVRSIS 方法的预测中，同一图像内常同时存在误分类 patch 与视觉相似但分类正确的同类别 patch；然而多数方法仅做单次推理，未利用已有预测引导后续推理。

Method: Puzzle 由轻量级历史条件控制器构成：结合预测历史与视觉相似性先验，调节每个 patch 作为信息源的贡献度及其对视觉相似 patch 的依赖度，并配以自适应停止规则决定迭代终止；分割模型保持冻结，控制器跨模型、跨数据集复用且无需重训练。

Result: 在多种 OVRSIS 模型与数据集上取得一致的性能提升，体现出强迁移性，并能进一步提升 SOTA 方法的表现。

Conclusion: 迭代重推理结合相似性先验是提升 OVRSIS 的有效且通用途径，Puzzle 可作为通用插件集成到现有框架中。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary remote sensing image segmentation (OVRSIS) enables the segmentation of unseen classes without additional annotation or retraining. By analyzing the predictions of existing OVRSIS methods, we find that many misclassified patches coexist with visually similar, correctly classified patches of the same class within the same image. Unfortunately, most of these methods stop after a single inference rather than using their predictions to guide further inference. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Puzzle}, a plug-and-play iterative re-inference framework that combines prediction history with a visual-similarity prior to progressively refine patch-level attention. Puzzle employs a lightweight history-conditioned controller to regulate each patch's contribution as an information source and its reliance on visually similar patches, while an adaptive stopping rule determines when to stop. It keeps the segmentation model frozen and reuses the same controller across different models and datasets without retraining. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent performance improvements across diverse OVRSIS models and datasets, highlighting the strong transferability of Puzzle. Moreover, Puzzle can further improve the performance of SOTA methods.

</details>


### [75] [From zero-target transfer to target-site personalization: multi-source training across fall-detection models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19156)
*Jaeseung Ryu,Jae-Hun Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在八个OmniFall数据集与一个AI-Hub数据集上（2,697片段）以留一数据集方式做联合跨数据集训练，比较三种VLM（Qwen、LLaVA、InternVL）与三种视觉基线（CLIP-temporal、VideoMAE、Pose-TCN），并以"目标站点个性化"协议检验源训练对目标域迁移和后续本地微调的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视频的跌倒检测可在人无法呼救时提供及时援助，但部署时面临陌生摄像头域偏移与本地标注稀缺的问题。作者希望验证联合跨数据集训练能否提升跨数据集检测性能，并进一步有利于目标域上的本地训练。

Method: 使用8个OmniFall数据集加1个AI-Hub数据集共2,697个片段，正样本含跌倒或倒地转折，负样本不含。逐一留出每个数据集，用其余八个训练；评估3个VLM与3个视觉基线。先在不接触目标数据（不用于训练或选择）的情况下评估源模型，再对每个标签预算用留出目标的已标注片段更新独立副本（target-site personalization），并将源初始化模型与Target-only（跳过源训练、使用相同目标片段和更新次数）对比。

Result: 跨九个目标，源训练使平均AUROC从冻结模型值提升：Qwen 0.821→0.912，LLaVA 0.883→0.947，InternVL 0.939→0.962。在每类5个目标样本、每次运行26次更新时，相对Target-only的AUROC增益的95%置信区间在Qwen、CLIP-temporal、VideoMAE、Pose-TCN上均高于零；全量目标训练时仅Qwen和LLaVA的置信区间高于零。单独的VLM分析揭示阈值敏感性与跌倒后误检问题。

Conclusion: 源训练带来的收益因模型与标签预算而异：在少样本目标个性化下多数模型获益显著，而全量目标训练下仅部分VLM仍保持增益；VLM存在阈值敏感性及跌倒后误检，部署需针对模型与标注预算分别评估。

Abstract: Video-based fall detection could support timely assistance when a person cannot call for help. Unfamiliar cameras and scarce local labels challenge deployment. We examine whether joint training improves cross-dataset detection and subsequent local training. We analyze 2,697 clips from eight OmniFall datasets and one AI-Hub dataset. Positive clips contain a fall or collapse transition, while negative clips do not. We hold out each dataset in turn, training on the other eight. We evaluate three vision-language models (VLMs), Qwen, LLaVA, and InternVL, and three visual baselines, CLIP-temporal, VideoMAE, and Pose-TCN. We evaluate the stored source model without target data for training or selection. For each label budget, we update a separate copy using labeled clips from the held-out target dataset. We call this target-site personalization. We compare the Source-initialized model with Target-only, which skips source training but uses identical target clips and updates. Across nine targets, source training raises mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) from frozen-model values of 0.821 to 0.912 for Qwen, 0.883 to 0.947 for LLaVA, and 0.939 to 0.962 for InternVL. With five target examples per class and 26 updates per run, 95% confidence intervals for the AUROC benefit over Target-only lie above zero for Qwen, CLIP-temporal, VideoMAE, and Pose-TCN. With full-target training, they lie above zero only for Qwen and LLaVA. Separate VLM analyses reveal threshold sensitivity and post-fall false detections. The benefit varies by model and label budget.

</details>


### [76] [EviRef: Evidence-Specific Memories for Logical Anomaly Detection across Capture Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19172)
*Yaoting Huang,Wenqi Cai,Yijin Wei,Jianjian Qin,Chunzhi Gu,Haoran Xie,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法 EviRef，将逻辑异常检测建模为可分解约束，为每类证据建立独立参考记忆，在 VID-AD 上取得 92.1% 图像级 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑异常表现为各组件本身正常、但其组合违反场景规则，且实际检测需在背景、光照、成像质量多变、每场景仅数十张正常图像条件下成立。现有方法将正常性与外观耦合，并把不同约束合并到单一参考中，难以适应变化条件。

Method: 将逻辑正常性建模为可分解约束。每个场景仅构建一次组件原型，把任意拍摄条件下的图像解析到同一标签空间，并在该空间上校验占位约束与关系约束；语义约束用与解析无关的整图嵌入独立校验；文本等额外证据通过任务级门控接入，无需修改既有记忆。整个流程免训练。

Result: 在 VID-AD 上平均图像级 AUROC 达 92.1%（仅视觉证据为 88.9%），在五种拍摄条件下均为对比方法中最高，标准差 0.5；每任务仅用 20 张正常图像构建记忆时，仍优于所有使用 50 张正常图像的对比方法。

Conclusion: 解耦且可分解的证据记忆设计能在免训练、少样本条件下稳健实现逻辑异常检测，并支持证据的模块化扩展。

Abstract: Logical anomalies arise when every component looks normal but their combination violates the scene rules. In practical inspection, these rules must hold under varying backgrounds, illumination, and imaging quality, with only a few dozen normal images per scene, whereas existing methods couple normality with appearance and merge different constraints into one reference. We model logical normality as decomposable constraints and propose EviRef, a training-free method that builds a separate reference memory for each family of evidence. Component prototypes built once per scene parse images under any capture condition into one label space, on which occupancy and relation constraints are verified. Semantic constraints are verified with whole-image embeddings independent of parsing, and additional evidence such as text is admitted through a task-level gate without modifying existing memories. On VID-AD, EviRef attains a mean image-level area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 92.1% (88.9% with visual evidence alone), the highest among the compared methods under each of five capture conditions, with a standard deviation of 0.5. Even with memories built from only 20 normal images per task, it surpasses all compared methods that use 50 normal images per task.

</details>


### [77] [WA-JEPA: Goal-Aware Predictive Feedback Action Refinement for a World-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19183)
*Tao An,Weihang Liang,Tianqi Wang,Chuqing Cao,Wenming Tan,Kai Zhang,Haibo Lei,Tao Xie,Lijun Zhao,Ruifeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WA-JEPA 世界-动作模型，用目标感知的潜在预测反馈直接生成并迭代精化动作，无需候选动作搜索，在保持紧凑模型与低在线算力的同时取得有竞争力的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 类世界模型在潜空间预测动作后果，但代表性规划方法依赖候选动作搜索，需反复评估动作序列，在线计算开销大；而直接动作生成虽避免搜索，却难以利用潜在预测在给定任务目标下引导动作生成，学到的动力学难以转化为有效决策。

Method: WA-JEPA 将动作生成、动作条件潜在预测与目标感知反馈耦合：先生成动作提案，预测未来场景与本体感受状态，再以任务目标为参考经两条互补反馈路径精化提案——目标差异残差反馈（GDRF）用预测场景与任务目标潜在表示之差指导分块动作精化；未来场景上下文反馈（FSCF）保留细粒度预测场景表示引导后续精化。整体由离线示范联合学习，执行前对同一计划迭代更新。

Result: 在短时域目标到达、长时域任务与完整任务执行三类评测中，目标感知预测反馈在保持紧凑模型规模和低在线计算成本的前提下达到有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: 未来预测可直接参与动作生成而无需候选动作搜索，目标感知的预测反馈是连接潜空间世界模型与高效决策的有效途径。

Abstract: JEPA-based world models predict action consequences in latent space, but representative planning approaches rely on candidate-action search, requiring repeated evaluation of action sequences and incurring substantial online computational cost. Direct action generation avoids this search, yet how to leverage latent predictions to guide action generation given task goals remains a key challenge in translating learned physical dynamics into effective decisions. We introduce WA-JEPA, a world-action model that couples action generation, action-conditioned latent prediction, and goal-aware feedback. The model first generates an action proposal and predicts the resulting future scenes and proprioceptive states, then refines the proposal through two complementary feedback paths, using the task goal as a reference. Goal-discrepancy residual feedback (GDRF) uses differences between the latent representations of predicted scenes and the task goal to guide chunk-wise action refinement. Future-scene context feedback (FSCF) preserves fine-grained predicted scene representations to guide subsequent action refinement. Jointly learned from offline demonstrations, this process iteratively updates the same plan before execution, making future predictions directly participate in action generation without candidate-action search. We evaluate short-horizon goal reaching, long-horizon tasks, and full-task execution. The results show that goal-aware predictive feedback supports competitive task performance while maintaining a compact model and low online computational cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Dovisya/WA-JEPA.

</details>


### [78] [Do VLMs Really Look at the Road? A Diagnostic Evaluation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19184)
*Shankar Gangisetty,Muskan Singh,C V Jawahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出诊断式评测框架，系统检验开源视觉语言模型在自动驾驶决策中是否真正依赖视觉证据，发现高基准准确率掩盖了肯定偏置、对反事实场景变化不敏感、否定题接近随机等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶视觉语言模型多以问答基准准确率衡量，但高准确率未必代表视觉 grounded 推理；模型可能依赖语言先验、答案偏差与数据集规律，因此在安全关键场景下需要超越聚合准确率的诊断性评测。

Method: 构建诊断评测基准，含 Adverse Weather 与 Junctions & Intersections 两类任务，并加入 nuScenes 空间推理扩展；覆盖通用、驾驶专用与空间推理类开源 VLM；采用四项诊断协议：有图 vs 无图评测、反事实天气敏感性、改写与否定下的语言鲁棒性、空间推理。真值标签由 BDD100K 与 DriveLM 标注程序化生成，免人工标注、可复现。

Result: 所有模型虽有具竞争力的总体准确率，却暴露系统性失效模式：强肯定偏置；对反事实场景变化敏感度有限；否定题表现接近随机，尽管改写一致性较高；甚至在无视觉输入时仍给出高置信度驾驶决策。

Conclusion: 当前开源 VLM 在安全关键自动驾驶中视觉 grounding 不足，聚合准确率不足以反映真实能力，需采用诊断式评测范式。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly being evaluated for autonomous driving through question-answering benchmarks, yet high benchmark accuracy does not necessarily reflect visually grounded reasoning. We present a diagnostic evaluation framework that systematically probes whether VLMs base safety-critical driving decisions on visual evidence or instead rely on language priors, answer biases, and dataset regularities. Our benchmark comprises two task categories, Adverse Weather and Junctions \& Intersections, with a nuScenes spatial reasoning extension, and evaluates open-source (general, driving, and spatial-reasoning) VLMs along four diagnostic protocols: image versus no-image evaluation, counterfactual weather sensitivity, linguistic robustness under paraphrasing and negation, and spatial reasoning. All ground-truth labels are derived programmatically from BDD100K and DriveLM annotations, enabling reproducible evaluation without manual labeling. Across all models, we find that competitive overall accuracy conceals systematic failure modes, including strong affirmation bias, limited sensitivity to counterfactual scene changes, near-chance performance on negated questions despite high paraphrase consistency, and confident driving decisions even without visual input. These results demonstrate that current open-source VLMs remain insufficiently grounded for safety-critical autonomous driving and underscore the need for diagnostic evaluation beyond aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [79] [Calibrating the Uncertainty of Self-Supervised Monocular Depth without Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19188)
*Jun Seok Kim,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出自监督单目深度估计网络预测的不确定度在数值幅度上存在错误校准问题，并提出无需真值深度即可同时校正尺度与形状的MonoCal方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估不确定度的指标（如排序相关性）对不确定度的绝对幅度不敏感：一个只有真实误差一半大的不确定度仍能获得相同分数。因此作者质疑预测不确定度是否在数值上真正等于深度误差，包括平均意义上（尺度）以及在不同置信度、远近像素上的分布（形状）。

Method: 作者先在KITTI上检验尺度与形状的校准情况，发现所有评估的不确定度估计尺度均未校准（如MonoProb误差约为其预测不确定度的两倍）。为此提出MonoCal：尺度校正通过从双目图像对中测得的单一常数放大不确定度，无需重训练，且该常数与用真值拟合的常数高度吻合；由于常数无法改变形状，进一步重训练不确定度，使其预测主网络与第二个自监督网络深度不一致的位置。

Result: 在KITTI上的实验表明，MonoCal在不同不确定度水平和不同深度范围内均取得比使用真值重标定更好的校准效果，同时推理时仍只需单张图像。

Conclusion: 自监督单目深度网络的不确定度存在系统性错误校准，仅有排序指标不足；MonoCal可在无真值深度条件下同时校正尺度与形状，实现更优的不确定度校准，且保持单图像推理。

Abstract: Self-supervised monocular depth networks can predict a per-pixel uncertainty, which is mainly evaluated by how well it ranks pixels according to their error. Such metrics are insensitive to the magnitude of the uncertainty, so an uncertainty half as large as the actual error obtains the same score. In this paper, we investigate whether the predicted uncertainty is as large as the depth error, both on average (scale) and across confident and uncertain, near and far pixels (shape). On KITTI, the scale is miscalibrated for every uncertainty estimate we evaluate, and the error of MonoProb is nearly twice its predicted uncertainty. We propose MonoCal, which corrects both the scale and the shape without ground-truth depth. Scale calibration multiplies the uncertainty by a single constant measured from stereo pairs, requiring no retraining, and this constant closely matches the one fitted with ground truth. Since a constant cannot change the shape, we further retrain the uncertainty to predict where the depth of the network disagrees with that of a second self-supervised network. Experiments on KITTI show that MonoCal achieves better calibration across uncertainty levels and depth ranges than rescaling with ground truth, while still using a single image at inference. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [80] [Ref-MIL: Plug-and-Play Posterior Refinement for Multiple Instance Learning in QSM-Based Brain Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19193)
*Ang Gaoa,Rui Jina,Yin Liu,Pengfei Rong,Feng Liu,G. Bruce Pike,Hongfu Sun,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PoRef-MIL弱监督多实例学习框架，结合迭代切片重标注与即插即用后验细化模块，实现无需切片级标注的QSM图像疾病分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSM是研究帕金森病（PD）和多发性硬化（MS）等脑疾病组织磁化率变化的新型定量MRI对比，但小样本队列及缺乏局部（切片级）标注使深度学习诊断建模困难。

Method: 提出PoRef-MIL：1）训练中交替训练2D双分支诊断网络与受试者级分类器共五轮，由分类器更新切片伪标签以重训诊断网络（迭代切片重标注）；2）推理时使用即插即用的后验细化模块（PRM），通过双向后验推断细化切片概率，并由细化概率计算熵置信度，结合滑窗打分得到受试者级预测。

Result: 在287例自建PD队列与150例公开MS队列上评估，与8种分类方法比较，PD与MS分类的平均AUC分别达0.942和0.872，准确率分别达0.926和0.833，均为最高；消融实验验证迭代重标注与PRM有效性，并在四种先进MIL模型上验证PRM的即插即用性。

Conclusion: PoRef-MIL为小样本、无人工切片标注条件下的QSM疾病分类提供了有效且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Quantitative susceptibility mapping (QSM) is a novel quantitative MRI contrast for studying tissue susceptibility changes in brain disorders, including Parkinson's disease (PD) and multiple sclerosis (MS). However, small cohorts and the lack of local annotations make developing deep learning models for QSM-based diagnosis challenging. In this work, we propose PoRef-MIL, a weakly supervised multiple instance learning (MIL) framework combining iterative slice relabeling and network retraining with a plug-and-play posterior refinement module (PRM). During training, a 2D dual-branch diagnosis network and a subject-level classifier were alternately trained for five rounds. The classifier was used to update slice pseudo-labels for retraining the diagnosis network. The PRM was applied during inference to refine slice probabilities through bidirectional posterior inference and calculate entropy-derived confidence from the refined probabilities, which was then used in sliding-window scoring to obtain subject-level predictions. PoRef-MIL was evaluated on an in-house PD cohort of 287 participants and a public MS cohort of 150 participants. Compared with eight classification methods, PoRef-MIL achieved the highest mean areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.942 and 0.872, and accuracies of 0.926 and 0.833, for PD and MS classification, respectively. Ablation experiments were conducted to validate the effectiveness of iterative relabeling and PRM, and the plug-and-play feature of the proposed PRM was also evaluated using four state-of-the-art MIL models. Overall, PoRef-MIL offers a promising approach to QSM-based disease classification in small cohorts without manual slice annotations.

</details>


### [81] [Incremental Learning of Vision-Language Models via Task Subspace Projection and Dynamic LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19213)
*Hao Fu,Qian Feng,Hanbin Zhao,Jiahua Dong,Xu Tan,Lina Wei,Chao Zhang,Hui Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TSP-DLoRA方法解决预训练视觉-语言模型在多域任务增量学习(MTIL)中的遗忘、零样本迁移退化与新任务欠拟合问题，由任务子空间投影(TSP)与动态低秩适配器(DLoRA)两模块构成，在11个数据集、三项指标上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉-语言模型在实际中常面临多域任务增量学习(MTIL)：多模态任务的类别集合逐步到达。受隐私与内存限制无法保存旧数据，导致旧任务知识遗忘、零样本迁移能力下降、新任务知识欠拟合。现有MTIL方法中，跨任务识别(CTI)模块存在严重的已见/未见任务混淆，新任务适配(NTA)模块难以在融入任务特定知识的同时自适应平衡性能与参数开销。

Method: TSP-DLoRA包含两个模块：(1) 任务子空间投影(TSP)：由基于任务特定子空间的任务标识分类器，以及一个特征投影策略组成，可为来自已见与未见任务的样本确定对应标识，缓解任务混淆；(2) 动态低秩适配器(DLoRA)：依据任务分布在各Transformer层动态分配LoRA，以改善新任务知识适配，兼顾性能与参数效率。

Result: 在11个数据集上使用三项性能指标进行实验评估，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: TSP与DLoRA的组合可有效且高效地缓解MTIL中的任务混淆与适配失衡问题，提升增量学习场景下预训练视觉-语言模型的综合表现；代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent pre-trained vision-language models often encounter a Multi-Domain Task-Incremental Learning (MTIL) scenario in practice, where a set of classes of multi-modal tasks arrive incrementally. Due to privacy concerns and memory constraints, MTIL with pre-trained models encounters forgetting of knowledge from old tasks, degradation of zero-shot transfer capability, and underfitting of new-task knowledge. To overcome these challenges, previous MTIL methods attempt to learn a discriminative cross-task identification (CTI) module and an effective new-task adaptation (NTA) module. However, current CTI modules suffer from severe task confusion between seen and unseen tasks, and NTA modules cannot adaptively balance the performance and parameter cost while incorporating task-specific knowledge. To alleviate the above dilemmas, we propose an effective and efficient TSP-DLoRA method for MTIL, which consists of Task Subspace Projection (TSP) and Dynamic Low Rank Adapter (DLoRA) modules. Specifically, our TSP module includes a task identifier classifier based on task-specific subspaces and a feature projection strategy that can determine the identifier associated with samples from both seen and unseen tasks. Our DLoRA improves the knowledge adaptation from new tasks by dynamically assigning Low Rank Adapter (LoRA) across transformer layers based on the task distributions. Experimental evaluations across 11 datasets, using three performance metrics, demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method. Code is available at https://github.com/FerdinandZJU/TSP-DLoRA.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Alignment: Supervising the Generator for EEG-to-Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19232)
*Sangmin Jo,Wootaek Jeong,Heung-Il Suk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出EEG到图像重建中"更强跨模态对齐≠更好重建质量"，提出生成派生监督（GDS）：用图像条件教师把静态视觉编码器特征映射到扩散Transformer的内部表示空间，作为动态监督目标训练EEG条件学生生成器，推理时丢弃教师，在THINGS-EEG与THINGS-MEG上取得SOTA重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG-to-image方法依赖将EEG与预训练视觉嵌入对齐以条件化图像生成器，但作者实证发现更强的跨模态对齐并不能带来相应的重建质量提升，说明仅改进输入条件不足，生成过程本身需要显式监督。此外，REPA式用固定视觉编码器特征引导扩散Transformer的方案，与EEG条件生成器之间存在目标不匹配问题。

Method: 提出生成派生监督（GDS）：构建一个图像条件教师模型，将静态图像编码器特征转译到扩散Transformer的内部表示空间中，形成随去噪过程变化的动态监督目标；EEG条件的学生生成器在去噪过程中紧密跟踪这些动态目标，教师仅在训练阶段使用，推理时丢弃。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG和THINGS-MEG两个数据集上，该方法一致优于以条件对齐为中心的基线，达到当前最优的重建保真度。

Conclusion: 重建性能的瓶颈不仅在于跨模态对齐质量，更在于生成过程的显式监督；通过生成派生的动态监督对齐学生生成器内部表示，可有效缓解EEG条件与固定视觉特征目标间的失配，显著提升脑解码重建效果。

Abstract: Visual brain decoding translates neural activity into human-interpretable images. While recent EEG-to-image reconstruction methods rely on aligning EEG signals with pretrained visual embeddings to condition an image generator, we empirically demonstrate that stronger cross-modal alignment do not translate into comparable gains in reconstruction quality. This indicates that improving the input condition alone is insufficient; the generative process itself requires explicit supervision. Although Representation Alignment (REPA) offers a solution by guiding diffusion transformers with pretrained vision encoder features, we find that EEG-conditioned generators struggle to follow these fixed targets. To address this mismatch, we introduce generation-derived supervision (GDS): an image-conditioned teacher that translates static image-encoder features into the internal representation space of a diffusion transformer. Our EEG-conditioned student generator closely tracks these dynamic targets during denoising, discarding the teacher at inference. Evaluated on THINGS-EEG and THINGS-MEG, our approach consistently outperforms conditioning-centric baselines, achieving state-of-the-art reconstruction fidelity.

</details>


### [83] [GameWM: Toward Next-Generation Video Games with Interaction-Centric World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19247)
*Bing He,Runze Wang,Fei Zha,Zhongyuan Wu,Ke Xu,Tao Zhuang,Yuxuan Xie,Zhongyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GameWM，一种将游戏引擎与生成式视频模型明确分工的下一代游戏设计：引擎负责规则、状态、相机与世界布局等必须精确的部分，视频模型负责世界外观与交互细节等难以手工创作的生成部分，并配套构建了数据引擎、创作接口与 5B–33B 参数（含实时因果变体）的模型族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏虽有神经渲染，但把视频生成模型仅当作重绘引擎已编排运动的后处理渲染器，浪费了其大部分生成能力，并限制了游戏可提供的多样性；同时人工创作美术与交互细节成本高昂。

Method: 确立引擎与视频模型的职责划分：GameWM-engine 保留规则、状态、相机和世界布局的精确性，使 VM 无需记忆世界结构；GameWM-VM 生成世界外观与玩家交互细节。系统层面构建以交互为中心（而非探索）的数据引擎、输入角色设定稿/场景美术/技能描述等创作素材的接口，以及 5B 至 33B 参数、含实时因果推理变体的模型族。

Result: 同一游戏机制可在视觉与物理上以多种不同方式呈现，而结果严格由引擎计算；每位玩家可在同一引擎机制上获得由自身角色、世界与技能构成的全新体验，事件时序与结果保持引擎计算的一致。

Conclusion: GameWM 通过引擎保证确定性与可玩性、视频模型负责内容生成的分工范式，实现了"同一机制、千人千面"的游戏体验，为生成式世界模型作为游戏创作基础设施提供了完整系统方案。

Abstract: For more than half a century, video games have pursued better graphics, more natural interaction, and more engaging gameplay. Generative video models (VMs) now bear on all three. Neural networks already draw most of the pixels in shipped games, and recent world models let a lightweight game engine keep the world state while a VM turns the engine's coarse proxy video into final frames. These systems, however, treat the VM as a renderer that only restyles motion the engine has already authored, which leaves most of its generative capacity unused and limits the diversity a game can offer. We present GameWM, a design for next-generation games built on a clear division of labor. The GameWM-engine keeps what must be exact: rules, state, camera, and the layout of the world, which also spares the video model from having to remember it. The GameWM-VM generates what is expensive to author by hand: the look of the world and the detail of the player's interactions. One game mechanic can then be realized in many visually and physically distinct ways, while its outcome stays exactly as the engine computed it. To realize this division of labor, we build GameWM as a complete system: a data engine centered on interactions rather than exploration, an interface through which authored character sheets, scene art, and skill descriptions enter the model, and a model family from 5B to 33B parameters with a real-time causal variant for play. Together they give every player a fresh version of the same game: each player's own character, world, and skills turn the same engine mechanics into a new experience, while every event keeps the timing and outcome the GameWM-engine computed.

</details>


### [84] [Size-Matched Nulls Reveal What Block Ablation Measures in MambaVision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19248)
*Tahmina Kousar,Tehseen Zia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在MambaVision上，逐块消融带来的性能损失与其尺寸匹配的随机零假设扰动几乎无法区分，单块损害很小且主要是位置性的；但同类型块的整体移除存在强非线性叠加效应，输入依赖选择贡献Mamba效应的20–32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合视觉主干网络（Mamba状态空间混合器与自注意力交错）常通过"移除某块并测量损害"来判断该块的作用，但该因果测量指标本身是否可信尚缺乏检验，本文旨在验证这种消融归因的有效性并给出规范协议。

Method: 在全部50,000张ImageNet验证图像与三种模型规模上，对每个块的消融都配以尺寸匹配的零假设对照（通道打乱或符号翻转）；以top-1准确率与真实类别秩为指标，用DeiT-Small与Swin-T作为仅含注意力的阳性对照；并按单个损害量归一化比较内容占比，进一步考察同类型块全部移除的复合效应及对齐零假设，最后冻结Mamba混合器中的输入依赖选择以分离其贡献来源（B、C投影）。

Result: MambaVision-T各块的内容效应在[-0.044,+0.016]个百分点内（90%区间）；阳性对照可检出内容效应（DeiT-Small达4.2点、Swin-T达1.0点）。MambaVision单块平均损害仅0.11–0.34点（对照为1.7与2.1点），归一化后其内容占比在准确率上与Swin-T相当、在秩上更低、远低于DeiT-Small。移除同类型全部块时损害为单块效应之和的3.7–8.4倍，远超零假设（包括保持扰动对齐的零假设）；相邻块对则可由对齐零假设复现大部分复合效应。冻结输入依赖选择使Mamba贡献下降20–32%，主要经由B、C投影。

Conclusion: 单块消融在MambaVision上几乎无法提供内容层面的因果证据，损害小且偏位置性；块间存在强烈超加性协同，逐块分析的结论不可外推至组合。作者据此提出面向混合网络的因果归因规范协议。

Abstract: Hybrid vision backbones interleave Mamba state-space mixers with self-attention, and a block's role is usually probed by removing it and measuring the damage. We test what that measurement means in MambaVision. On all 50,000 ImageNet validation images and three model sizes, every block ablation is paired with a size-matched null, the same perturbation with its channels shuffled or their signs flipped. In top-1 accuracy and in true-class rank, real and null damage are nearly indistinguishable in absolute terms: pooled over the blocks of MambaVision-T, the content effect lies within [-0.044,+0.016] percentage points (90% interval). Two attention-only controls show that the test detects content, with real ablations worse than their nulls by up to 4.2 points in DeiT-Small and 1.0 point in Swin-T. Part of MambaVision's near-equivalence reflects how little damage its blocks do (0.11--0.34 points on average, against 1.7 and 2.1). Scaled by damage, its content share is comparable to Swin-T's in accuracy, lower in rank, and far below DeiT-Small's: single-block damage in MambaVision is small and mostly positional. Ablation also hides what blocks carry together: removing all blocks of one type compounds damage to 3.7--8.4 times the sum of single effects, far more than size-matched nulls, including nulls that keep the alignment between the blocks' perturbations; for adjacent pairs, aligned nulls reproduce most of it. Freezing input-dependent selection in all Mamba mixers shows that it accounts for 20--32% of the Mamba contribution, mainly through the B and C projections. We turn these results into a protocol for causal attribution in hybrid networks.

</details>


### [85] [dit.xpu : Taming DiT Sparse Inference on Mobile SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19261)
*Jiayu Chen,Jinye Hu,Maoliang Li,Anqian Cen,Hailong Zou,Zihao Zheng,Xinming Wei,Guojie Luo,Jie Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiT.XPU：面向异构移动SoC的稀疏DiT推理框架，通过tile对齐稀疏注意力、锚点误差补偿与NPU/GPU异构协作，实现高效设备端扩散生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer（DiT）支持设备端图像生成与编辑，但在高分辨率下注意力开销高；现有稀疏注意力虽降低计算，但动态稀疏引入的预处理与不规则访存与移动NPU不匹配。

Method: 提出DiT.XPU框架：1）tile对齐的稀疏注意力，适配NPU规则化计算；2）基于去噪步间稠密-稀疏输出残差缓慢变化的特性，采用锚点式误差补偿；3）异构XPU协作，将NPU稠密注意力与GPU稀疏校准重叠执行；4）掩码引导的数据流，使数据传输与计算重叠，减少搬运与布局转换。

Result: 在两款骁龙智能手机上测试五个DiT模型：相比Hexagon-NPU后端端到端去噪最高加速1.56倍，注意力核相比最快替代方案加速11.5倍，同时生成质量接近稠密注意力。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将稀疏注意力与移动NPU硬件特性对齐，并结合异构计算与误差补偿，可在保持生成质量的前提下显著提升设备端DiT推理效率。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) enable on-device image generation and editing but incur high attention costs at high resolutions. Existing sparse attention reduces computation, yet dynamic sparsity introduces preprocessing and irregular data accesses that poorly fit mobile NPUs. We present DiT.XPU, a sparse DiT inference framework for heterogeneous mobile SoCs. DiT.XPU combines tile-aligned sparse attention with anchor-based error compensation by exploiting slowly varying dense-sparse output residuals across denoising steps. Heterogeneous XPU collaboration overlaps NPU dense attention with GPU sparse calibration, while mask-guided dataflow reduces data transfers and layout conversion by overlapping data movement with computation. Across five DiT models on two Snapdragon smartphones, DiT.XPU achieves up to 1.56x end-to-end denoising speedup over the Hexagon-NPU backend and 11.5x attention-kernel speedup over the fastest alternative, while preserving generation quality close to dense attention. Code is available at https://github.com/IF-LAB-PKU/dit.xpu.

</details>


### [86] [LC-ISP Benchmark: Learning Advanced ISP with Live and Capture Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19284)
*Kai Chen,Junyu Lou,Jinhua Zhang,Weiyi You,Shuhang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LC-ISP大规模RAW-to-sRGB基准数据集，利用相机实时预览流与离线拍摄流之间的内在差异构建53.9k空间对齐数据组，并提出紧凑两阶段基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAW-to-sRGB配对数据集多依赖跨设备采集，导致空间错位，且无法真实反映实际拍摄条件，制约了数据驱动ISP的研究。

Method: 从相机实时预览（live）与离线拍摄（capture）两条流之间的内在差异出发构建数据集：LC-ISP含53.9k空间对齐数据组，每组包括live RAW、live sRGB、多帧融合RGB与capture sRGB，覆盖白天、夜间、室内、室外及HDR场景；同时提供拍摄流中间结果用于模块化监督。基线采用紧凑两阶段结构，以这些中间结果为辅助监督目标。

Result: 在448×448 RAW输入、2.5-GFLOP计算预算下，基线相比最强单帧基线PSNR提升超过2 dB，并在纹理、去噪与色彩还原方面均有改善。

Conclusion: LC-ISP可支撑更贴近真实场景的基准评测、监督策略设计与轻量级ISP模型开发。

Abstract: High-quality paired RAW-to-sRGB datasets are essential for data-driven ISP, yet existing datasets often rely on cross-device collection, causing misalignment and failing to faithfully reflect real capture conditions. We therefore introduce LC-ISP, a large-scale benchmark built from the intrinsic gap between a camera's live preview and offline capture streams. LC-ISP contains 53.9k spatially aligned groups, each comprising live RAW/live sRGB and multi-frame fused RGB/capture sRGB, and covers day, night, indoor, outdoor, and HDR scenes. It also provides capture-stream intermediates for modular supervision. We further develop a compact two-stage baseline that uses these intermediates as auxiliary targets. Under a 2.5-GFLOP budget for 448$\times$448 RAW inputs, it outperforms the strongest single-frame baseline by over 2 dB PSNR while improving texture, denoising, and color reproduction. LC-ISP supports realistic benchmarking, the design of supervision strategies, and the development of lightweight ISP models.

</details>


### [87] [Generate, Align, and Restore: MLLM-Empowered Diffusion for Generalizable Low-light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19285)
*Kaisheng Pang,Feng Li,Chen Zhang,Junjun Jiang,Haoang Li,Sam Kwong,Runmin Cong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MrLIE：以多模态大模型（MLLM）作为参考合成器与诊断批评器，结合条件扩散实现免配对的无监督低光照图像增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实夜间增强面临严格对齐的昼夜图像对稀缺、极端黑暗与异构人工光源导致退化复杂，现有方法泛化性脆弱。

Method: 提出基于参考的无监督框架：MLLM 直接从退化夜间观测迭代生成、诊断、修正正常光照参考；引入置信度偏移模块校正空间几何不一致以提升参考可靠性；采用结构锚定注入方案，在去噪过程中融合对齐参考线索、低光结构约束与 ControlNet 加性锚定。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，方法在感知质量、自然外观与泛化鲁棒性上均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 将 MLLM 的生成与推理能力与条件扩散结合，可有效绕开配对数据依赖，为真实夜间低光照增强提供可靠参考引导范式。

Abstract: Low-light image enhancement (LLIE) is essential to numerous vision applications operating under adverse illumination. Despite substantial advances, real-world nighttime enhancement remains challenging due to the scarcity of strictly aligned day-night pairs and complex degradations involving extreme darkness and heterogeneous artificial light sources, leading to fragile generalization. To overcome these challenges, we propose MrLIE (MLLM-empowered reference Low-light Image Enhancement), a novel reference-based unsupervised LLIE framework that synergizes the generative and reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with conditional diffusion. MrLIE employs the MLLM as a high-fidelity reference synthesizer and diagnostic critic, iteratively generating, diagnosing, and correcting normal-light references directly from degraded nighttime observations. Based on this, our method governs the enhancement process through two complementary mechanisms, in which a confidence offset module rectifies spatial-geometric inconsistencies for reference reliability and a structure-anchored injection scheme harmonizes aligned reference cues, low-light structural constraints, and ControlNet additive anchoring during the denoising process. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that MrLIE achieves superior perceptual quality, natural appearance, and robust generalization.

</details>


### [88] [SAR-TopoGuard: Training-Free, Speckle-Aware Topological Verification for Structure-Preserving SAR-to-Optical Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19316)
*Satabarto Sarkar,Debojyoti Ghosh,Subhranil Biswas,Kaustav Mandal,D. Sobya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAR-TopoGuard——一种面向冻结生成器的免训练推理期层，用随机采样+斑点感知验证器+SAR引导频率门抑制SAR到光学翻译中的幻觉细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR到光学的翻译模型常合成雷达测量不支持的细节（幻觉），而像素级指标无法揭示这一问题。

Method: 免训练推理层：随机采样器生成K个候选（GAN用MC dropout，扩散用种子方差）；斑点感知验证器依据与SAR输入的梯度幅值相关性和幻觉频率指数对候选排序；SAR引导频率门仅在SAR边缘支持处放行高频细节。五个超参数全部固定，三个训练种子。

Result: 在500个场景不相交SEN1-2测试块、500个留出夏季场景块和200个跨数据集SEN12MS冬季对上评估：Pix2Pix提升PSNR +0.060/+0.012/+0.077 dB、SSIM +0.0034/+0.0057/+0.0135（Wilcoxon p<1e-6）；CycleGAN与CUT在三组上均提升（+0.013~+0.076 dB）；边缘精度上升但召回下降（边缘F1 -1.3%）；COP-GEN上加+0.33 dB（base）和+0.16 dB（Beta）。消融显示：MC-dropout GAN候选近乎相同，增益来自频率门；扩散样本差异大，验证器选择贡献达+0.26 dB。

Conclusion: 该层零可训练参数，K=5次生成器前向外每块仅增加0.4 ms，可有效抑制幻觉并提升多类生成器的翻译质量。

Abstract: SAR-to-optical translators often synthesize detail that the radar measurement does not support, and pixel metrics alone cannot reveal it. We propose SAR-TopoGuard, a training-free inference-time layer for frozen generators: a stochastic sampler draws K candidates (Monte Carlo dropout for GANs, seed variance for diffusion), a speckle-aware verifier ranks them by gradient-magnitude correlation with the SAR input and a hallucination-frequency index, and a SAR-guided frequency gate admits high-frequency detail only where SAR edges support it. With all five hyperparameters frozen and three training seeds, we evaluate on 500 scene-disjoint SEN1-2 test patches, 500 patches of a held-out summer scene, and 200 cross-dataset SEN12MS winter pairs. The layer improves Pix2Pix by +0.060, +0.012, and +0.077 dB PSNR and by +0.0034, +0.0057, and +0.0135 SSIM, respectively (Wilcoxon p < 1e-6), and it improves CycleGAN and CUT on all three sets (+0.013 to +0.076 dB). It raises edge precision at the cost of edge recall (edge F1 -1.3% on the test set). On the latent diffusion model COP-GEN, it adds +0.33 dB (base) and +0.16 dB (Beta) on the winter set, the only set with dual-polarization SAR. An ablation shows that the gate produces the gain for MC-dropout GANs, whose candidates are nearly identical, whereas verifier selection contributes up to +0.26 dB for diffusion, whose samples differ. The layer has zero trainable parameters and adds 0.4 ms per patch beyond the K=5 generator passes.

</details>


### [89] [Unlocking Fine-Grained Cell Representations via Soft-Interior Rim Dense-Grid Pooling in Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19325)
*Wenwen Lou,Mingyi He,Xinyi Guo,Yining Li,Weiming Chen,Jiawen Li,Lianghui Zhu,Zhiwei Yang,Xitong Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRDP（Soft-interior Rim Dense-grid Pooling），一种无需训练、几何感知的细胞级特征读出方法，将冻结的病理基础模型（PFM）的粗粒度特征网格转换为掩膜对齐的精细细胞表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型提供强大的可迁移特征，但细胞级精细表征仍具挑战：HoVer-Net需任务特定训练；CellViT++对检测核分配的原生网格token取平均，忽略了核的部分覆盖，且易将核内容与邻近组织混合。

Method: SRDP为无训练读出：以半步长应用冻结的patch投影，交错四个偏移格点，将细胞掩膜映射为分数覆盖权重，并将整细胞池化与两个内边界池化拼接。

Result: 在4个数据集、6个冻结编码器、24种设置下，SRDP较原生token平均将平均平衡准确率提升5.68个百分点；在CoNIC与Lizard上平均提升11.22个百分点。

Conclusion: SRDP在不更新编码器的前提下，把冻结PFM的粗粒度格点转化为掩膜对齐的细粒度细胞表征，验证了几何感知池化的有效性。

Abstract: Fine-grained cell representations are essential for characterizing cellular heterogeneity and tissue organization in computational pathology. Pathology foundation models (PFMs) provide powerful pretrained features for transferable tissue analysis. HoVer-Net uses task-trained segmentation and typing, whereas CellViT++ averages native-grid tokens assigned to detected nuclei. However, the former requires cell-specific training; the latter ignores fractional nuclear coverage and can mix nuclear content with neighbouring tissue. Therefore, we propose SRDP, short for Soft-interior Rim Dense-grid Pooling, a training-free geometry-aware readout. We apply the frozen patch projection at half stride, interleave four offset lattices, map the cell mask to fractional coverage weights, and concatenate whole-cell pooling with two inner boundary pools. Across four datasets and six frozen encoders, SRDP improves mean balanced accuracy by 5.68 percentage points over native token averaging across 24 settings, including 11.22 points on average for CoNIC and Lizard. SRDP converts a coarse frozen PFM lattice into a mask-aligned, fine-grained cell representation without updating the encoder.

</details>


### [90] [A Think Block Is Conditioning, Not a Plan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19341)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过"替换"而非"消融"的方式检验生成模型think block的作用：该文本的价值在于明确"画什么"（指称规格），而非提供被执行推理计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在绘图前先输出思维链并声称带来大幅增益，但该文本究竟贡献了什么尚不清楚；作者希望以受控替换的方式分离并定位其价值来源。

Method: 固定生成器、采样器与随机种子，将think block替换为等长的无内容散文、来自无关8B模型的一句指称性描述、截断/改写计划等对照；在统一模型（带think模式）、强化学习推理生成器、以及驱动冻结扩散模型的规划器三类系统上重复；并施加字段级干预、将同样句子写入用户提示作为通道对照，辅以两名评判者与两名标注者复核。

Result: 等长无内容散文收益为−0.05至−0.15；无关8B模型的一句指称（主体+若干识别细节）以少3–6倍的conditioning token达到与模型自身计划相当的效果；写出正确名称收益+0.33，错误时仅+0.05；在生成器本就成功的样本上效应为−0.07±0.07；按文本编码器窗口截断计划后286张图有272张字节级一致；改写以适配窗口的计划与裸指称持平（+0.003±0.044）；字段级干预中仅颜色被可靠执行。

Conclusion: think block的价值是"绘制内容的规格说明"而非"被执行计划"；计划中不含指称未提供的信息，仅颜色等可执行字段可靠落地，且该局限属于生成器而非规划通道。

Abstract: Generative models increasingly write a chain of thought before drawing, and report large gains from doing so. We ask what that text contributes, by substitution rather than ablation: holding the generator, sampler and seeds fixed, we replace the think block with controlled alternatives and measure the image. Across a unified model with a think mode, a reinforcement-learned reasoning generator, and a planner driving a frozen diffusion model, the same picture emerges. Content-free prose of matched length is worth nothing or less ($-0.05$ to $-0.15$). One sentence from an unrelated 8B model, naming the subject and a few identifying details, matches the generator's own plan in three to six times fewer conditioning tokens. The value sits where the referent is in doubt: writing down a name is worth $+0.33$ when it is right and $+0.05$ when it is wrong, and where the generator already succeeds the whole effect is $-0.07 \pm 0.07$. Cutting a plan at the text encoder's window leaves 272 of 286 images byte-identical, and a plan rewritten to fit that window ties a bare identification ($+0.003 \pm 0.044$), so a plan contains nothing the identification does not already supply. Field-level interventions find only colour executed reliably, and a control writing the same sentences into the user prompt shows that limit belongs to the generator rather than to the planning channel. Two judges and two annotators replicate the central contrasts. A think block earns its keep as a specification of what to draw, not as a plan that is carried out.

</details>


### [91] [RETR: A Structure-Preserving RGB-Event Transformer for Robust 3D Lane Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19358)
*Jingtao Dong,Hao Zhuang,Hao Yang,Liyuan Pan,Wei Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将事件相机引入3D车道线检测，构建DSEC-3DLD与Ev-OpenLane两个带度量标注的多模态基准，并提出结构保持Transformer（RETR），将RGB与事件观测融合为一致的3D车道几何，在两个基准上取得SOTA，尤其在恶劣光照与复杂道路几何下提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RGB的3D车道检测在不利光照、运动模糊和远距离透视压缩下，车道证据脆弱，度量几何恢复不稳定；事件相机具备高时间分辨率、高动态范围的结构线索，但这些线索稀疏、依赖运动，难以直接融合为一致的3D几何。因此需要系统性地研究事件相机在3D车道检测中的作用。

Method: 1) 构建两个带度量3D车道标注的多模态基准：DSEC-3DLD（真实序列）与Ev-OpenLane（大规模仿真序列）；2) 提出RETR结构保持Transformer，包含：Reciprocal Context Flow Fusion（互上下文流融合）实现模态一致证据对齐；Uncertainty Aware Structural Consolidation（不确定性感知结构巩固）保持细薄与不确定车道结构；Geometry State Decoder（几何状态解码器）以提案条件初始化、参考条件查询演化和多样化几何查询解码有序车道假设。

Result: 在DSEC-3DLD与Ev-OpenLane两个基准上均达到SOTA性能，在挑战性光照和复杂道路几何场景中提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 事件相机可作为RGB的有效补充用于3D车道检测；所建基准与RETR框架为多模态事件-视觉3D车道感知提供了系统化研究基础，代码与数据集将开源。

Abstract: Robust 3D lane detection requires accurate metric road geometry recovery from visual inputs, yet conventional RGB-based approaches suffer from fragile lane evidence under adverse illumination, motion blur, and long-range perspective compression. Event cameras offer complementary high-temporal-resolution, high dynamic-range structural cues that are robust to these challenges, but their sparse, motion-dependent responses cannot be directly fused into consistent 3D geometry. In this paper, we present the first attempt to introduce event cameras into 3D lane detection. To enable systematic research, we first build two multimodal benchmarks with metric 3D lane annotations: DSEC-3DLD (real-world sequences) and Ev-OpenLane (large-scale simulated sequences). We further propose RETR, a structure preserving transformer that converts complementary RGB and event observations into coherent 3D lane geometry. RETR first aligns modality consistent evidence through Reciprocal Context Flow Fusion, then preserves thin and uncertain lane structures with Uncertainty Aware Structural Consolidation, and finally decodes ordered lane hypotheses using a Geometry State Decoder with proposal conditioned initialization, reference conditioned query evolution, and diversified geometric inquiry. Extensive experiments show that RETR achieves state-of-the-art performance on both benchmarks, especially showing strong improvements in challenging lighting and complex road geometry scenarios. Code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/duskswell/RETR.

</details>


### [92] [AcousticBEV: Acoustic BEV Object Localization for Intelligent Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19381)
*Ziang Liu,Shiming Wang,Joseph Adeola,Julian F. P. Kooij*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了车载水平麦克风阵列（车辆“耳朵”）与真实驾驶声学数据集AcousticScenes，并提出AcousticBEV方法，从阵列信号估计移动车辆在BEV下的中心位置，F1达47.7%（Easy）与45.1%（Difficult），超越传统信号处理与学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶感知主要依赖相机、雷达与LiDAR，但相机受光照限制、LiDAR受恶劣天气影响；声学感知（麦克风阵列）可提供互补信息，却长期缺乏探索。

Method: 设计水平布置的车载麦克风阵列采集空间声信号；构建AcousticScenes真实驾驶数据集（2,117静态样本、2,863动态样本，含同步空间声信号、LiDAR点云与3D框标注）；提出AcousticBEV学习式方法，以自车速度为条件，从阵列信号回归移动交通参与者在BEV下的中心位置（方位+距离）。

Result: AcousticBEV在Easy场景F1为47.7±1.2%，Difficult场景为45.1±1.4%，优于经典信号处理基线与其它学习方法。

Conclusion: 低成本车载麦克风阵列的声学信号可用于在方位与距离上定位邻近移动车辆，声学感知是自动驾驶感知的有效互补模态。

Abstract: Modern autonomous driving and driver assistance systems rely predominantly on cameras, radars and LiDARs for environmental perception. However, these modalities face different environmental limitations, including poor illumination for cameras and adverse weather for LiDAR. Acoustic sensing, particularly using microphone arrays, could provide complementary perception capabilities for autonomous driving, but has remained largely underexplored. In this work, we design a horizontally arranged microphone array that functions as the "ears" of a vehicle, enabling localization of nearby moving vehicles in a bird's-eye view (BEV) representation that captures both their relative direction and distance. Using this array, we collect a real-world driving dataset, AcousticScenes, which contains 2,117 static and 2,863 dynamic samples with synchronized spatial acoustic signals, LiDAR point clouds and 3D bounding box annotations. Building on this dataset, we propose AcousticBEV, a learning-based method for acoustic BEV object localization, which estimates the center locations of moving traffic participants in BEV from the microphone-array signals, conditioned on the ego vehicle's own speed. Experimental results demonstrate that AcousticBEV outperforms both classical signal-processing baselines and other learning-based methods, achieving F1 scores of $47.7 \pm 1.2$% on "Easy" scenarios and $45.1 \pm 1.4$% on "Difficult" cases. Our findings demonstrate that acoustic signals from a low-cost, vehicle-mounted microphone array can localize nearby moving vehicles in both azimuth and distance.

</details>


### [93] [Detecting Small Space Objects for Space Situational Awareness with Collaborative Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19394)
*Xingyu Qu,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向多视角空间目标检测的协同边缘AI框架，并系统评测深度学习模型与融合策略，发现图像级融合在多卫星链路带宽约束下综合时延最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近地轨道日益拥挤，航天器碰撞与空间碎片风险上升，亟需低成本、高效的在轨空间目标检测（SOD）与避碰系统；尽管基于低成本视觉传感器的星载边缘AI深度学习方案已有前景，但仍面临数据集有限、小目标检测困难及缺乏全面性能评估等挑战。

Method: 构建多视角SOD的协同边缘AI框架，对多种深度学习模型与多视角融合策略（图像级、特征级、基于注意力的融合）进行系统基准测试，并在不同星间链路（ISL）带宽下权衡检测精度与"感知—通信—推理"流水线总时延。

Result: 更高图像分辨率显著提升小目标检测性能；在评估的ISL带宽范围内，图像级融合相比特征级与注意力融合，平均降低通信与推理总时延30.13%与32.80%；视角数较多时，特征级与注意力融合精度更优，但会增加感知—通信流水线的分析开销；在未超出接收容量时，多视角协同引入的时延开销极小。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来协同卫星网络设计提供设计指导，支撑新兴空间态势感知与空间交通管理需求。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of space activities, near-Earth space is becoming increasingly congested, leading to a higher risk of spacecraft collisions and space debris generation. Recent close-proximity encounters and collision incidents necessitate efficient and cost-effective onboard systems for space object detection (SOD) and collision avoidance. While deep learning (DL) enabled Edge AI using low-cost vision sensors on single or multiple satellites has shown promises for SOD, significant challenges remain, including limited datasets, small SOD, and the lack of comprehensive performance assessments. This paper presents a collaborative Edge AI framework for multiview SOD and systematically benchmarks DL models and fusion strategies. Results show that higher image resolution significantly improves small SOD. For multiple views, image-level fusion reduces the combined communication and inference latency by 30.13% and 32.80% on average compared with feature-level and attention-based fusion across the evaluated inter-satellite link (ISL) bandwidth. For larger view sets, feature-level and attention-based fusion achieve better detection accuracy at the cost of analysis in the sensing-communication pipeline, revealing that multiview collaboration introduces minimal latency overhead under ISL constraints when reception capacity is not exceeded. Our work provides valuable design insights for future collaborative satellite network design, supporting the emerging space situational awareness and space traffic management requirements.

</details>


### [94] [SplatFlow3D: Learning Scene Flow via 3D Motion Feature Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19409)
*Bo Wang,Dekang Zhu,Jian Li,Zhipeng Meng,Bo Wang,Dewen Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双帧骨干SimpleFlow3D与权重共享多帧框架SplatFlow3D，通过层次化3D运动特征构建与伪输入统一首帧/非首帧推理，以更少参数在KITTI、Spring上达到SOTA并改善遮挡处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用3D物体在短帧间隔内的运动连续性与稳定性，引入多帧设置以缓解场景流估计中的遮挡问题。

Method: 1) 双帧骨干SimpleFlow3D：以"先解耦后耦合"方式层次化构建3D运动特征，融合图像平面的稠密信息与深度维度的稀疏信息；2) 权重共享多帧框架：将前一帧3D运动特征splat到当前估计过程，并通过构造伪输入统一首帧与非首帧推理；3) 二者结合得到SplatFlow3D。

Result: SimpleFlow3D以极小参数量取得SOTA；SplatFlow3D误差低于双帧骨干，且参数量少于现有方法；在KITTI与Spring上于所有已发表及提交方法中排名第一。

Conclusion: 实验表明SplatFlow3D改善了遮挡处理、非刚性运动估计与真实场景泛化能力。

Abstract: Considering the motion continuity and stability of 3D objects over short inter-frame intervals, introducing multi-frame settings in scene flow estimation can potentially address occlusion. In this paper, we propose a two-frame backbone, SimpleFlow3D, which integrates dense information from the image plane and sparse information from the depth dimension in a decoupling-followed-by-coupling manner by hierarchically constructing the 3D motion feature. SimpleFlow3D achieves SOTA results with minimal parameters. We also propose a weight-sharing multi-frame framework to splat the previous 3D motion feature to the current estimation process. The framework unifies the first and non-first frame inference by constructing pseudo inputs. After combining the proposed framework and backbone, the newly born multi-frame method SplatFlow3D further advances the state of the art. It achieves lower errors than the two-frame backbone, with fewer parameters than current methods. On both KITTI and Spring, SplatFlow3D ranked 1st over all published and submitted methods. Experiments show that SplatFlow3D improves occlusion handling, non-rigid motion estimation, and generalization to real-world scenes. Code is available at https://github.com/wwsource/SplatFlow3D.

</details>


### [95] [Memorization Lives in a Few Attention Heads: Mitigating Memorization in Diffusion Models via Projection-Based Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19427)
*Puwei Lian,Yujun Cai,Songze Li,Yuezu Lv,Guanghui Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文从交叉注意力头视角重新审视扩散模型的记忆现象，发现记忆主要由少数浅层、语义控制异常强的注意力头驱动，并提出基于投影的Top-k注意力头抑制方法（Projection-based Top-k Attention Head Suppression），在推理阶段有选择性地削弱记忆相关特征，从而在降低记忆与保持图像质量间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在推理时可能记忆并复现训练样本，带来隐私与版权风险。现有推理期缓解方法往往全局压制条件信息，虽能减少记忆，但会损害语义对齐与图像质量，缺乏精细化定位记忆来源的手段。

Method: 通过系统分析与针对性消融，定性记忆来源为少量浅层交叉注意力头；提出语义控制分数（semantic-control score）识别与记忆最相关的Top-k注意力头，再借助条件表征与无条件表征之间的方向投影，去除这些头中条件特定分量，从而选择性削弱记忆特征并保留有用语义信息。

Result: 在多种扩散模型与数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法有效降低记忆程度，并在记忆缓解与图像质量之间取得优于现有方法的权衡。

Conclusion: 记忆并非由整个交叉注意力机制均匀驱动，而是集中于少数浅层高语义控制头；据此实现的推理期定向抑制是一种兼顾隐私保护与生成质量的有效策略。

Abstract: Diffusion models can memorize and reproduce training samples during inference, raising serious privacy and copyright concerns. Existing inference-time mitigation methods often suppress conditional information globally, which may reduce memorization but can also harm semantic alignment and image quality. In this paper, we revisit memorization from the perspective of cross-attention heads. Through systematic analysis and targeted ablation, we find that memorization is mainly driven by a small subset of shallow-layer attention heads with abnormally strong semantic control, rather than by the entire cross-attention mechanism. Motivated by this finding, we propose Projection-based Top-k Attention Head Suppression, a targeted inference-time mitigation method. Our method first identifies the top-k attention heads most related to memorization using a semantic-control score, and then removes their condition-specific components through directional projection between conditional and unconditional representations. This design selectively weakens memorization-related features while preserving useful semantic information. Extensive experiments on different diffusion models and datasets show that our method effectively reduces memorization and achieves a better trade-off between memorization mitigation and image quality than existing approaches.

</details>


### [96] [Prepare Lighter and Faster for Open-Vocabulary Queries: A Query-Wise 3D Segmenter for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19429)
*Nhat-Quynh Le-Pham,Khoi Nguyen,Binh-Son Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量、免训练的Gaussian Splatting查询驱动3D实例分割框架，用稠密像素级CLIP特征与视频分割器跨视角传播实现开放词汇3D理解，无需逐场景训练即可达到SOTA并显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Gaussian Splatting的开放词汇3D方法依赖昂贵的预处理与冗长训练，难以满足快速、轻量化的应用需求；同时基于mask级CLIP嵌入的做法会丢失细粒度语义信息，并受限于预定义实例提案。

Method: 1) 采用稠密像素级CLIP特征替代mask级嵌入，保留细粒度语义并支持灵活的文本驱动定位，无需预定义提案；2) 给定文本查询，在精选的pivot视角中定位高质量目标区域，利用视频分割器跨视角传播，获得一致且完整的2D tracklets；3) 通过新颖的可见性投票策略将2D tracklets提升至3D高斯，得到边界清晰、准确的3D分割掩码。整体流程免训练、轻量。

Result: 在LERF-OVS、ScanNet、ScanNet++上取得SOTA性能，在3D-OVS上表现具有竞争力，且相较现有开放词汇3D分割方法有显著加速。

Conclusion: 该框架无需逐场景训练即可高效实现开放词汇3D理解，验证了稠密特征+跨视角传播+可见性投票路线的有效性，为轻量化、快速的开放词汇3D分割提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D understanding aims to localize and segment arbitrary objects beyond predefined categories. However, existing approaches based on Gaussian Splatting often rely on expensive preprocessing and lengthy training, limiting their applicability when fast and lightweight solutions are desired. We propose a lightweight, training-free framework for query-driven 3D instance segmentation on Gaussian Splatting. Instead of conventional mask-wise CLIP embeddings, we use dense pixel-level CLIP features to preserve fine-grained semantic information and enable flexible text-driven localization without relying on predefined instance proposals. Given a text query, we identify a high-quality target region in a carefully selected pivot view and propagate it across views using a video segmentor, providing consistent and comprehensive coverage of the target instance. We then lift the resulting 2D tracklets to 3D Gaussians through a novel visibility voting strategy, yielding accurate 3D segmentation masks with clean object boundaries. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on LERF-OVS, ScanNet, and ScanNet++, competitive results on 3D-OVS, and significant speedups over existing open-vocabulary 3D segmentation methods. These results highlight the effectiveness of our framework for efficient open-vocabulary 3D understanding without per-scene training.

</details>


### [97] [Few-Shot Visual Concept Extraction for Steering Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19440)
*Nabyl Quignon,Antitza Dantcheva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种从扩散Transformer（DiT）图像侧激活中提取视觉概念方向的免训练方法，仅用少量正负提示对即可实现细粒度、连续、可组合的图像概念操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型引导方法主要作用于文本条件空间，或依赖昂贵的辅助训练，难以在细粒度上控制图像概念且不破坏整体生成过程。作者希望直接从图像侧激活中提取概念方向，实现免训练、精确的操控。

Method: 从DiT中间残差激活中用差均值估计器做少样本概念提取。观察到（a）场景级概念（光照、天气、风格）分布于图像token上；（b）局部概念（物体颜色、纹理、材质、面部属性）集中于特定区域。据此提出统一免训练框架：对分布式概念用全局差均值，对局部概念用掩码差均值，避免局部信号被全局平均稀释；提取的可复用概念方向可在推理时注入，并支持patch级应用。

Result: 定量上优于文本编码器引导与免训练激活编辑基线，与专门编辑模型相当；patch级应用实现精确定向与组合式操控，可在同一图像中同时操纵多个概念。

Conclusion: 验证了图像侧激活空间是提取视觉概念方向的有效场所，差均值+掩码策略能兼顾分布式与局部概念，为免训练、细粒度、可组合的图像引导提供了新范式。

Abstract: Image steering aims to provide fine-grained control over concepts in generated images, enabling targeted and continuous manipulation without broadly altering the generation process. Recent steering methods for diffusion models primarily focus on the text-conditioning space or rely on costly auxiliary training. Deviating from that, we here propose a novel steering approach that extracts visual concept directions directly from the image-side activations of Diffusion Transformers. Specifically, we showcase few-shot concept extraction in the activation space using a simple difference-of-means estimator over intermediate residuals. We observe (a) scene-level concepts such as lighting, weather, and style are distributed across image tokens, as well as (b) local concepts such as object colors, textures, materials, and facial attributes are concentrated in specific regions. Towards controlling both concepts, we introduce a new unified training-free framework that uses global difference-of-means for (a) distributed concepts and masked difference-of-means for (b) local concepts, preventing local signals from being diluted by global averaging. Notably, using only a handful of positive and negative prompt pairs, our method identifies and extracts reusable concept directions that can be injected at inference for continuous steering. Quantitatively, our method outperforms text-encoder steering and training-free activation-editing baselines, as well as is competitive with specialized editing models. Our results suggest that as concept directions can be applied at patch level, our method enables precise targeted and compositional steering, manipulating multiple concepts in the same image.

</details>


### [98] [InvarOrd: Self-Supervised Ordinal Knowledge Distillation with Rank-Invariant Alignment for Label-Efficient Cross-Species Hyperspectral Fish Freshness Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19472)
*Kazi Nabiul Alam,Omenuko Joseph Nwebonyi,Pooneh Bagheri Zadeh,Akbar Sheikh-Akbari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InvarOrd：以每等级5个标注立方体（共30个）实现跨鱼种高光谱鲜度序数迁移，通过秩内收缩协方差差异对齐保住序数结构，蒸馏出423K参数学生模型，MAE 1.476天、1日内准确率56.1%，优于仅目标域训练0.29天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱鱼类鲜度模型跨物种迁移性差，标注新物种耗时且具破坏性；鲜度本身是序数标签，常规域对齐易抹除序数顺序（源模型跨物种QWK低于0）。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 在无标注源域立方体上做自监督光谱预训练；(2) 仅在匹配秩内计算收缩协方差差异作为跨物种对齐项，理论上该对齐项对秩均值不变，故不会激励坍缩序数轴；(3) 将序数知识蒸馏至紧凑学生模型。新物种仅需30个标注立方体（每秩5个）。

Result: 三文鱼-鳕鱼配对数据（各800立方体、16天）：源域教师跨物种MAE 1.6–1.9天、QWK<0；423K参数学生（比教师小2.54倍）在14种配置中MAE最低（1.476天）、1日内准确率最高（56.1%），与6个ImageNet预训练骨干中最佳的ConvNeXt-T（1.503天）持平，参数少66倍，GPU单立方体1.44ms，重适应5s；较仅目标域训练优0.29天（p=0.031）。秩不变对齐保留序数结构最多（嵌入秩相关0.183、QWK 0.112），全局差异对齐降至秩相关0.049、QWK≈0，MAE仅高0.014天。

Conclusion: 跨物种鲜度迁移的关键在于对齐时保持序数轴不变；秩内收缩协方差对齐配合序数蒸馏，可在极少标注与极小模型下取得与大骨干相当甚至更优的序数预测性能，并显著优于目标域单训。

Abstract: Hyperspectral models of fish freshness rarely transfer between species, and labelling a new species is slow and destructive. Freshness is also ordinal, and standard domain alignment can erase that order. We present InvarOrd, which adapts to a new species from 30 labelled cubes (5 per rank) in three stages: self-supervised spectral pretraining on unlabelled source cubes, cross-species alignment by a shrinkage covariance discrepancy computed only within matched ranks, and ordinal distillation into a compact student. The alignment term is provably invariant to the rank means, so it gives no incentive to collapse the ordinal axis. On paired salmon and cod data (800 cubes each, 16 days), a source-trained teacher reaches 1.6 to 1.9 days mean absolute error (MAE) on the other species, with quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) below zero. The 423K-parameter student, 2.54 times smaller than its teacher, attains the lowest MAE (1.476 days) and highest within 1-day accuracy (56.1%) among 14 configurations, matching the best of six ImageNet-pretrained backbones (ConvNeXt-T, 1.503 days) at 66 times fewer parameters, 1.44ms per cube on a GPU and 5s re-adaptation. InvarOrd beats target-only training by 0.29 days (p = 0.031). Among alignment objectives, rank-invariant alignment retains the most ordinal structure (embedding rank correlation 0.183, QWK 0.112), whereas global discrepancy lowers the rank correlation to 0.049 and QWK to zero at only 0.014 days higher MAE.

</details>


### [99] [StyleJudge: A Human-Validated, In-Context-Learning Vision-Language Evaluator for Artistic Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19475)
*Lokesh,Pramukto Mandal,Somanshu Juneja,Jashnoor Singh,Amit Kumar,Pranav Adlinge,Richa Thakur,Kunal Swami,Aditya Nigam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 StyleJudge：一个以少量参考图像对为条件、从 72B 视觉语言教师蒸馏而来的 0.8B 紧凑评估器，用于自动评价艺术风格迁移并与人类评分对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风格迁移自动指标在孤立图像对上打分，未参考"预期风格变换"的示例，且与人类判断相关性差（Gram 矩阵风格损失 r=0.041、CLIP 相似度 r=0.168、LPIPS 反向 r=-0.404）。

Method: StyleJudge 先以少量源图→风格化图参考对进行条件校准，再评价查询对，模拟人类先校准风格再打分的过程；训练知识来自仅在训练期使用的 72B 视觉语言教师（蒸馏）。训练数据涵盖 12 种风格，通过 LoRA 适配扩散模型生成源—风格化对，并用高分辨率图像变形（morphing）管线补充渐进中间样本，使模型能识别部分与完整风格化。

Result: 在 878 对人类评分基准（12 种风格；728 来自 10 个训练/验证风格，150 来自 2 个未见风格）上，0.8B 模型在已见风格上 Pearson r=0.291（Spearman 0.325），未见风格 r=0.381，为所测指标中最高；教师模型 r=0.327。两个留出验证风格上，与教师结构化分数的精确匹配率 28.2%（未调优基座仅 1.1%），推理轨迹缩短 67.8%；单张 NVIDIA T4 上中位 39 秒评价一对，显存 2.5 GB。

Conclusion: 紧凑蒸馏可保留教师大部分人类对齐能力，条件化参考对是关键设计；作者公开人类评分基准、标注指南、SDXL 适配 FreeMorph 代码与模型权重。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation of artistic style transfer remains an open problem: existing metrics score an image pair in isolation, without seeing an example of the intended style transformation, and correlate poorly with human judgment. We introduce StyleJudge, a compact vision-language evaluator conditioned on a small set of reference source-to-stylized pairs before scoring a query pair, mirroring how a human rater calibrates to a style before judging a candidate. On a human-annotated benchmark of 878 rated pairs across 12 artistic styles (728 from 10 training/validation styles, 150 from 2 unseen styles), our efficient 0.8B-parameter StyleJudge reaches a Pearson correlation of 0.291 (Spearman 0.325) with human ratings on seen styles and 0.381 on unseen ones. On seen styles, it achieves the highest positive correlation among tested metrics: Gram-matrix style loss and CLIP similarity correlate weakly ($r=0.041$, $r=0.168$), while LPIPS (scored higher when closer to the source) correlates negatively ($r=-0.404$). We achieve this by distilling evaluation knowledge from a 72B-parameter vision-language teacher used only during training, which reaches $r=0.327$, confirming that most of its human alignment survives compact distillation. For training, we build a dataset of source-to-stylized pairs across the 12 styles via LoRA-adapted diffusion, densified with graded intermediate examples from a high-resolution image-morphing pipeline so the evaluator recognizes partial and complete stylization. On two held-out validation styles, StyleJudge reaches 28.2% exact-match agreement with its teacher's structured scores versus 1.1% for the untuned base model, with 67.8% shorter reasoning traces, and scores a pair in 39 s (median) using 2.5 GB of GPU memory on one NVIDIA T4. We release the human-rating benchmark, annotation guideline, SDXL-adapted FreeMorph code, and model weights.

</details>


### [100] [PALIN: Pixel-Aligned LiDAR-NIR Multimodal 3D Object Detection with Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19485)
*Yanan Zhang,Amit Vishnia,Chengbo Ai,Eleni Christofa,Runan Cheng,Siyuan Meng,Yuanchang Xie,Yu-Min,Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出内参像素对齐的LiDAR-NIR多模态数据集PALIN与融合框架CMambaFusion，实现3D目标检测精度与效率的更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯LiDAR几何表征在稀疏观测与遮挡下存在歧义；跨传感器多模态融合又受标定与同步误差困扰，需一种天然像素对齐、无需复杂配准的几何+强度多模态方案。

Method: 利用Ouster LiDAR构建PALIN数据集，天然实现像素级对齐并同时采集几何与NIR强度信息，含约8000帧标注数据（城市路口静态场景与多样移动郊区场景）。在此基础上提出CMambaFusion：四方向Cross-Mamba模块实现双向跨模态交互，门控Concat-Mamba模块进行联合特征精炼。

Result: 大量实验表明PALIN能有效支撑多模态学习；CMambaFusion在精度-效率权衡上优于纯LiDAR方案与代表性融合基线。

Conclusion: 内参像素对齐的LiDAR-NIR多模态数据可规避标定/同步误差，结合基于Mamba的双向交互与门控融合，可高效提升3D目标检测性能。

Abstract: 3D object detection is critical for intelligent transportation systems. LiDAR has been widely adopted for accurate geometric measurements; however, purely geometric representations remain ambiguous under sparse observations and occlusions, while cross-sensor multimodal fusion suffers from calibration and synchronization errors. To overcome these challenges, we leverage an Ouster LiDAR to construct PALIN (Pixel-Aligned LiDAR-NIR), a large-scale intrinsically pixel-aligned multimodal dataset jointly capturing geometry and NIR intensity, comprising approximately 8,000 annotated frames from static urban intersections and diverse mobile suburban scenes. Building upon this dataset, we propose CMambaFusion, a novel fusion framework consisting of a four-directional Cross-Mamba module for bidirectional cross-modal interaction and a Gated Concat-Mamba module for joint feature refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PALIN enables effective multimodal learning, and CMambaFusion achieves superior accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over both LiDAR-only and representative fusion baselines.

</details>


### [101] [Vegetation Health Classification for Low-Data and Edge Hardware Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19495)
*Yifei Cheng,Haoyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量可解释的植被分类算法，仅需每类5张参考图、无需GPU与重型框架；采用2D色调-饱和度直方图特征配EMD/QFD距离与对数似然评分，低数据下Top-1准确率81–85%，推理<1ms，并可在ESP32上以82.8%准确率、45KB堆内存运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分类方法依赖大数据集、GPU硬件、难以灵活重训且可解释性低，不适合偏远、资源受限环境下独立从业者使用无人机进行植被与环境健康监测。

Method: 特征表示采用2D色调-饱和度（hue-saturation）直方图；距离度量使用Earth Mover's Distance或Quadratic Form Distance，并结合对数似然概率评分，将图像分为三类植被健康等级；支持增量式重训练而无需重建全模型；在ESP32微控制器上部署QFD变体验证边缘可行性。评测含三套自建流程与三个成熟模型，覆盖n=5/10/25低数据场景及2,172张、来自28个南美地点的全数据场景。

Result: 自建流程在低数据约束下取得具竞争力的Top-1准确率（81–85%），推理时间低于1ms，可支持实时分类及后续播种、灌溉等任务；ESP32部署的QFD变体达到82.8%准确率，堆内存占用率1.01%（约45KB），无需蜂窝网络连接。

Conclusion: 有效的植被分类可在低成本、低功耗嵌入式硬件上实现，无需GPU、大数据集与云/蜂窝连接，适用于资源受限的远程场景且具备可解释性与可维护性。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) equipped with cameras are popular, viable tools for vegetation and environmental health monitoring. However, existing image classification methods are incompatible with UAV implementation for independent practitioners in remote, resource-constrained environments due to large dataset requirements, GPU hardware, inflexible retraining, and low interpretability. This paper presents a lightweight, interpretable vegetation classification algorithm operating with as few as five reference images per class, without GPUs or heavy machine learning frameworks. It uses a 2D hue-saturation histogram feature representation and Earth Mover's Distance or Quadratic Form Distance, paired with a log-likelihood probability score, to classify images into three vegetation health categories. We evaluated six systems: three custom pipelines and three established models across low-data scenarios ($n = 5, 10, 25$) and a full-data regime of 2,172 drone images from 28 South American locations. Our custom pipelines achieve competitive Top-1 accuracy (81--85\%) under low-data constraints, maintaining sub-1ms inference times that enable real-time classification and subsequently tasks such as seeding and watering. The algorithms also support incremental retraining without full model reconstruction. We validate edge feasibility by deploying the QFD variant on an ESP32 microcontroller, achieving 82.8\% accuracy with 1.01\% heap memory utilization (45kb), demonstrating that effective vegetation classification is achievable on low-cost, low-power embedded hardware without cellular connectivity.

</details>


### [102] [DBHiCo: Dual-Branch Hierarchical Contrastive Learning for MRI Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19511)
*Chengsheng Zhanga,Wei Li,Chenghao Sun,Jian Gong,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双分支层次对比学习框架DBHiCo，利用原始与去颅骨MRI构成解剖多视图对，并以边界排序相似度层次替代刚性二元对比约束，实现结构一致性与语义判别性的协同强化，在三个神经数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI自动分类是神经诊断的重要无创手段，但受采集噪声和受试者间高结构相似性影响，细微病理变化被掩盖，限制深度网络性能。现有对比学习范式存在两点不足：一是采用刚性二元正负划分，忽略负样本异质性；二是缺乏跨视图结构一致性建模，难以实现细粒度解剖对齐。

Method: 构建自然解剖多视图对（原始MRI与去颅骨MRI），采用双分支架构融合整体结构鲁棒性与颅内局部细节；核心是层次对比损失，抛弃传统二元约束，改用边界排序的相似度层次，按结构与语义难度对正负样本对排序，从而放大表征的细粒度语义判别力。

Result: 在三个不同的神经科学数据集上进行大量实验，DBHiCo均取得state-of-the-art性能，稳定优于现有多视图MRI表征学习方法与先进对比微调方法。

Conclusion: 以解剖多视图双分支+层次化排序对比替代二元对比，可有效缓解噪声与跨受试者相似性带来的判别瓶颈，为MRI细粒度诊断表征学习提供可行范式。

Abstract: Automated Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) image classification provides a crucial non-invasive approach for neurological diagnosis, yet it is fundamentally challenged by inherent acquisition noise and high inter-subject structural similarity, which obscure subtle pathological variations, thereby limiting the performance of deep neural networks. While contrastive learning (CL) offers a promising mitigation strategy, existing paradigms rely on rigid binary partitions that ignore negative sample heterogeneity, and they fail to capture the cross-view structural consistency vital for fine-grained anatomical alignment. To address these barriers, we propose Dual-Branch Hierarchical Contrastive Learning (DBHiCo), a novel framework explicitly designed to collaboratively enforce structural consistency and semantic discrimination. Specifically, DBHiCo leverages a natural anatomical multi-view pair (original and skull-stripped MRI scans), processing them through a dual-branch architecture to seamlessly integrate holistic structural robustness with localized intracranial details. Crucially, we introduce a hierarchical contrastive loss that discards conventional binary constraints in favor of a margin-ranked similarity hierarchy. This mechanism explicitly orders positive and negative pairs based on their structural and semantic difficulty, thereby amplifying the fine-grained semantic discriminability of the learned representations. Extensive experiments across three diverse neurological datasets demonstrate that DBHiCo achieves state-of-the-art performance, consistently outperforming existing MRI multi-view representation learning and advanced contrastive fine-tuning methods.

</details>


### [103] [PathLoop: Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Reasoning for Evidence-Grounded Whole-Slide Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19527)
*Jincheng Li,Zhengyi Liu,Zelin Liu,Yihui Zhan,Yifei Sun,Xiangyu Li,Minye Shao,Lingyao Kong,Tao Hou,Xinheng Lyu,Lanfeng Zhong,Ziquan Zhu,Rongzhao Zhang,Rui Yan,S. Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PathLoop 是一个自我进化的多智能体框架，融合组学数据实现循证式全切片图像分析的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WSI 多智能体系统对分子证据利用有限，倾向于选取视觉显著但代表性不足的区域、忽视全局上下文，且难以复用既往病例经验。

Method: 协调视觉与组学解释器在切片、图块、像素三个层级协作，用分子信号引导区域选择并融合局部与全局特征；判读智能体依据由24个器官系统、48条目构建的 Pathology-Wiki 知识库校验证据一致性并在不一致时触发复查；四级记忆（推理轨迹、实践提示、技能、工具）构成"证据采集—审查—记忆更新"闭环。

Result: 在四个 TCGA 队列上针对生物标志物预测、生存预测与亚型分类任务进行评估，均展现有效性。
