Conclusion: PathLoop 在多任务上验证有效，支持循证式 WSI 分析。

Abstract: Analyzing whole-slide images (WSIs) requires combining evidence across tissue regions and spatial scales. Multi-agent systems offer a promising approach to this task. However, existing systems make limited use of molecular evidence, often select visually prominent but less representative regions while overlooking global context, and struggle to reuse experience from previous cases. To address these challenges, we present PathLoop, a self-evolving multi-agent framework that integrates omics data for evidence-grounded WSI analysis. Specifically, PathLoop coordinates visual and omics interpreters across slide, patch, and pixel levels, using molecular signals to guide region selection and combining local tissue details with global slide features. A judgment agent then checks the consistency of the collected evidence against Pathology-Wiki, a knowledge base built from 48 entries across 24 organ systems, and triggers re-examination when the evidence is inconsistent. To reuse past experience, a four-level memory stores reasoning trajectories, practical tips, skills, and tools to guide future cases, forming a loop of evidence collection, review, and memory update. We evaluate PathLoop on four TCGA cohorts with biomarker prediction, survival prediction, and subtype classification. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of PathLoop across these tasks and support its use for evidence-grounded WSI analysis.

</details>


### [104] [Lesion-Aware Explainable AI for Diabetic Retinopathy Grading from Fundus Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19550)
*Truong Thinh Nguyen,Hai Phong Nguyen,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Giang Son Tran,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CCEM，用无标注可靠性线索为Grad-CAM++、Ada-SISE、SG-IG三种归因图做逐图加权融合，得到更忠实、更贴合病灶的解释图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习DR分级的事后解释难以信任：不同归因方法对同一图像高亮不同区域，且视觉上合理的热图并不能证明高亮像素真正驱动预测。

Method: CCEM融合Grad-CAM++、Ada-SISE、SmoothGrad-IG三种归因，权重由无标注可靠性线索逐图生成（删除敏感度、方法间一致性、紧凑性、视网膜内含量、峰值结构）。解释对象为冻结的序数分级器：EfficientNet-B7+CBAM+标量回归头，EyePACS 2015预训练、APTOS 2019微调并做QWK阈值优化。

Result: 分级器QWK在Kaggle隐藏测试集0.920，外部MAPLES-DR 0.559。在MAPLES-DR 198张含专家像素级病灶标注（不参与训练/阈值拟合/融合）的图像中，193张有病灶标注上：Soft Pointing Game 69.61%（95% CI 63.7–75.3，定义为最强病灶支撑显著性/全局最大值的比值，非病灶检测率），删除AUC最低0.570，删除-插入平衡最佳0.115；各项增益对最强单一解释器配对检验显著。

Conclusion: 无标注的逐图可靠性加权可将三个互相分歧的解释器整合为单一图，其峰值证据更具病灶支撑性、更忠实于模型，优于任一单一解释器。

Abstract: Post-hoc explanations of deep-learning models for diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading are difficult to trust because different attribution methods highlight different retinal regions for the same image, and a visually plausible heatmap does not show that the highlighted pixels drive the prediction. We propose the Consensus-Calibrated Explanation Map (CCEM), which fuses Grad-CAM++, Ada-SISE, and SmoothGrad Integrated Gradients (SG-IG) using image-specific weights derived from annotation-free reliability cues: deletion sensitivity, inter-method agreement, compactness, retinal containment, and peak structure. CCEM explains a frozen ordinal grader, an EfficientNet-B7 with CBAM and a scalar regression head, pre-trained on EyePACS 2015 in Stage 1 and fine-tuned on APTOS 2019 in Stage 2 with quadratic weighted kappa (QWK)-optimized thresholds; it reaches a QWK of 0.920 on the hidden Kaggle test set and 0.559 on the external MAPLES-DR cohort. Explanations are evaluated on MAPLES-DR, 198 MESSIDOR images with expert pixel-level lesion annotations that are never used for training, threshold fitting, or fusion. On the 193 images with annotated lesions, CCEM obtains the highest Soft Pointing Game score (69.61%; 95% CI 63.7-75.3), defined as the ratio of the strongest lesion-supported saliency to the global map maximum rather than a lesion detection rate, together with the lowest deletion AUC (0.570) and the best deletion-insertion balance (0.115); each gain is significant against the strongest single explainer on paired tests. Annotation-free, per-image reliability weighting therefore turns three disagreeing explainers into one map whose peak evidence is more lesion-supported and more faithful to the model than that of any single explainer.

</details>


### [105] [Consistency-Aware Map Generation at Multiple Zoom Levels Using Aerial Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19576)
*Linwei Chen,Zheng Fang,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多级地图瓦片生成框架，从航拍图像自动生成多缩放层级、内容一致的地图，并构建MLM数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多级瓦片地图由矢量数据渲染，而矢量数据更新需专业设备实地逐点核查，成本高、时效性差；航拍图像获取更易更廉价，但缺乏从航拍到多级一致地图的自动生成方法。

Method: 框架包含两个模块：层级感知生成器（level-aware map generator）为每个缩放级别生成细节真实、准确度高的初始地图；一致性感知生成器（consistency-aware map generator）将各级初始地图视为序列并建模层间关联，保证跨层级内容一致性。同时构建大规模高质量数据集MLM。

Result: 在MLM数据集上，综合量化指标与感知质量均优于已有最先进的地图生成方法。

Conclusion: 航拍图像可作为低成本地图更新来源，通过层级感知与一致性建模实现多级地图自动生成，验证了框架的有效性与数据集的价值。

Abstract: The multi-level tiled map service is widely used and serves as a kind of digital infrastructure. These map tiles are usually rendered from vector data, whose update needs to walk or drive with professional equipment to check every points of interest. This leads to inconvenience and expensive cost in timely updating maps. Compared with vector data, aerial images are much easier and cheaper to obtain. In this paper, we propose a novel multi-level map generation framework that can automatically generate accurate and consistent maps with multiple zoom levels from aerial images. It consists of a level-aware map generator and a consistency-aware map generator. The level-aware map generator is able to generate accurate initial maps with realistic details for each zoom level. The consistency-aware map generator regards initial maps at each zoom level as a sequence and builds the connection between them, so as to guarantee content consistency between maps at different zoom levels. Furthermore, we collect a large-scale high-quality dataset called Multi-Level Map (MLM) for map generation at multiple zoom levels. Experiments on our MLM dataset show that our method outperforms previous state-of-the-art map generation methods on both comprehensive quantitative metrics and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [106] [EndoComparer: Comparative Surgical Video Question Answering with Pseudo-Siamese Visual Adaptation and Perceive-then-Compare Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19578)
*Shi Li,Lalith Sharan,Joël L. Lavanchy,Kun Yuan,Lorenzo Arboit,Nabani Banik,Elif Yazici,Maria Vannucci,Vinkle Srivastav,Pietro Mascagni,Nicolas Padoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EndoComparer——面向手术视频配对比较问答的视觉语言模型，包含伪孪生视觉LoRA（PSVL）与"先感知后比较"思维链（PtC-CoT）两个组件，并构建近11K题目的EndoDiffVQA基准，在差异检测与描述上优于通用及手术专用VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术训练依赖于病例间的显式比较（患者状况、技术、技能熟练度），而现有VLM通常一次只处理单个视频，难以胜任比较类任务。

Method: (a) PSVL：在共享冻结视觉编码器上为每个视频槽位配置独立的低秩适配器，以单次编码器前向的代价在视觉通路中分离两段视频；(b) PtC-CoT：训练模型先陈述各视频所见内容，再基于这些陈述推导答案；同时构建EndoDiffVQA基准，覆盖胆囊切除与胃旁路手术的视频对多选题，涉及解剖、器械、动作与技能四个维度。

Result: 在视频差异检测与具体化描述上优于强通用VLM与手术专用VLM；消融实验显示两组件贡献互补；在视频交换对照下模型对两视频混淆更具鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过PSVL与PtC-CoT构建专用视觉推理路径，可提升VLM跨病例手术视频比较的鲁棒性；EndoDiffVQA与EndoComparer为该任务提供基准与强基线。

Abstract: Purpose: Surgical training benefits from explicit comparison between cases: in patient condition, in technique and in skill proficiency. Surgical vision-language models (VLMs) offer a promising and flexible interface for this purpose. Yet current VLMs typically see one video at a time, and they often fall short when asked to compare videos. Methods: We propose EndoComparer, a VLM for comparative surgical video question answering with two components. (a) A Pseudo-Siamese Visual LoRA (PSVL) gives each video slot its own low-rank adapter over a shared frozen vision encoder, separating the two videos in the visual pathway at the cost of a single encoder pass. (b) A Perceive-then-Compare chain of thought (PtC-CoT) trains the model to first state what each video shows, then derive the answer from these statements. We further curate EndoDiffVQA, a benchmark of nearly 11K multiple-choice questions on video-pair over cholecystectomy and gastric bypass procedures, covering anatomy, tool, action and skill. Results: EndoComparer outperforms strong generalist and surgical VLMs on detecting and specifying difference between videos. Ablations show that two proposed components contribute complimentarily, and the model remains more robust under a video-swap control that exposes confusion between the two videos. Conclusion: Providing a dedicated visual reasoning path through PSVL and PtC-CoT makes cross-case comparison of surgical videos more robust for VLMs. EndoDiffVQA and EndoComparer provide a benchmark and a strong baseline for this task. Project page: https://camma-public.github.io/endocomparer

</details>


### [107] [Learning Spatially Aware Audio-Visual Features from Videos with Mono Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19593)
*Mengyu Yang,You Zhang,Vishnu Raj,Olha Townsend,James Hays,Andrea Fanelli,Gautam Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可同时进行语义与空间推理的双耳音频-视觉联合模型；通过自动流水线（定位声源、估计相对3D轨迹、渲染双耳音频）对VGGSound做空间化，实现大规模训练，从而在空间推理任务上提升性能与泛化性，同时保持语义任务竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全面的视听感知需要语义与空间两类推理能力。然而现有空间模型因数据稀缺而泛化性差，仅语义模型又无法完整刻画空间特性，二者难以兼得。

Method: 构建联合建模双耳音频与视觉、同时处理语义与空间任务的模型；为缓解真实数据不足，设计自动化数据生成流水线：在视频中定位声源→估计其相对3D轨迹→渲染双耳音频，并据此将VGGSound空间化以进行大规模训练。

Result: 在空间推理任务上的性能与泛化性均有提升，同时在语义任务上与仅语义模型相比仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 自动化空间化数据流水线可有效突破真实数据瓶颈，使单一模型兼顾语义与空间推理，并具备更优的泛化能力。

Abstract: Comprehensive audio-visual perception requires both semantic and spatial reasoning. Existing spatial models suffer from poor generalization due to data scarcity, while semantic-only models fail to capture spatial characteristics completely. We introduce a model capable of jointly reasoning over binaural audio and vision on semantic and spatial tasks with improved generalization. To overcome the shortage in real-world data, we develop an automatic pipeline that localizes the sound source in a video, estimates its relative 3D trajectory, and renders binaural audio. We then use this to spatialize VGGSound, allowing us to train at a much larger scale. We demonstrate improved performance and generalization on spatial reasoning tasks while still remaining competitive on semantic tasks compared to semantic-only models.

</details>


### [108] [Toward Planning Representations That Separate Task Structure from Visual Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19621)
*Aarav Srivastava,Vishwas Sathish,Rajesh P. N. Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在ProcTHOR房屋中对ObjectNav进行受控视觉泛化评估：固定布局、物体位置、目标与起始状态，仅单独或组合改变视觉因素（表面材质、杂物、光照、目标外观），比较无模型RL、基于模型RL与冻结预训练表征智能体。结果显示外观变化损害导航且因素间差异显著，表面材质影响最大，全局图像统计漂移不能可靠预测性能；基于DINOv2冻结特征规划的智能体在全偏移下成功率最高（0.83）但仍对表面外观敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉泛化评测常同时改变多个因素，难以定位失败来源；因此需要一个能隔离单一视觉因素、同时保持任务几何与物体身份不变的受控评测，以揭示规划表征的真实需求。

Method: 在程序化生成的ProcTHOR房屋中构建受控ObjectNav基准：训练与评测房屋共享平面图、物体位置、目标与起始状态，视觉因素单独或组合扰动。对比三类智能体：无模型RL、基于模型RL，以及使用冻结预训练视觉表征（DINOv2）进行规划的智能体；并分析因子级别的成功率变化与全局图像统计漂移的关系。

Result: 外观变化普遍损害导航，但效应按因素分化：表面材质变化贡献了平均成功率变化的主要部分，杂乱物与光照影响很小；全局图像统计漂移幅度不能可靠预测性能，仅改变目标物体外观即可在图像级变化很小的情况下显著损害端到端智能体。基于冻结DINOv2特征规划的智能体在全偏移条件下取得最高成功率0.83，但仍对表面外观敏感。

Conclusion: 视觉泛化失败需按因素归因，而非以整体分布漂移衡量；规划表征必须将不变的空间任务结构与外观分离，表面材质是当前表征的关键薄弱点。

Abstract: Effective planning requires representations that preserve task-relevant geometry and object identity while ignoring visual variation that does not alter the task. Existing evaluations of visual generalization often change several factors simultaneously, making the source of a failure difficult to identify. We introduce a controlled evaluation of object-goal navigation (ObjectNav), the task of reaching an instance of a named object category, in procedurally generated ProcTHOR houses. Training and evaluation houses share floor plans, object locations, goals, and start states, while visual factors are changed individually or in combination. Across model-free reinforcement learning (RL), model-based RL, and agents using frozen pretrained visual representations, our results show that appearance changes impair navigation, but their effects differ sharply by factor. Surface material changes account for most of the average change in success, whereas clutter and lighting have little effect. The magnitude of a global image statistics shift does not reliably predict performance: changing only the goal object's appearance can substantially impair an end-to-end agent despite a small image-level change. We found that a model based agent that plans over frozen features from the pretrained DINOv2 image encoder achieves the highest success (0.83) under the full shift, but remains sensitive to surface appearance. Our experiments reveal an important requirement for planning representations: they must separate invariant spatial task structure from appearance.

</details>


### [109] [Where slide-level foundation-model classifiers fail on atypical breast lesions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19630)
*Nerea Hernandez,Luis J. Herrera,Cesar Ramirez,Olga Valenzuela,Ignacio Rojas,Francisco M. Ortuño*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在4个病理基础模型上，乳腺非典型增生与低级别原位癌的分类失败源于切片级标签仅取最严重病变（仅39%含ADH标注切片被标为ADH）、注意力排序偏置，以及ADH区域被赋予比非典型更高的恶性概率；改为按决策、用病灶级标签训练可改善全部四个非典型边界，但会使良性切片评分贴近病灶、分诊特异性显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导管型乳腺病变（非典型增生 vs 低级别原位癌）的命名直接决定手术决策，而基于病理基础模型（PFM）的切片级分类器恰在此类病变上表现最差，需定位其失败机制。

Method: 使用4个PFM，在BRACS上做交叉验证并采用预注册假设；结合标注病灶的空间信息分析注意力排序，检验"仅输入病灶区域"的影响，与病灶级标签的按决策分类器对比，并做ADH区域植入良性切片的扰动实验；在病理医师标注单亚型切片的医院数据及HISTAI上检验外部泛化与分诊特异性。

Result: 含ADH标注的切片仅39%按最严重病变规则被标为ADH；注意力将病灶排位靠前但极少列首位；仅展示病灶收益有限；病灶级标签的按决策分类器改善全部四个非典型边界；植入良性切片的ADH区域使恶性概率的升高超过非典型概率；外部数据中良性切片评分贴近病灶，分诊特异性由0.48-0.53降至0.00-0.07（医院数据）和0.22（HISTAI）。

Conclusion: 非典型增生分类失败主要来自切片级弱标签（最严重病变规则）、注意力聚合与恶性概率校准偏差，而非模型表征能力不足；改用病灶级标签与按决策建模是有效方向，但会牺牲良性切片的特异性，需在判读与分诊阈值设计上进一步权衡。

Abstract: Whether a ductal breast lesion is called atypical hyperplasia or low-grade carcinoma in situ increasingly determines surgery. However, slide-level classifiers built on pathology foundation models (PFMs) perform worst on these lesions. Using four PFMs, cross-validation on BRACS and preregistered hypotheses, we studied where the classification of atypia fails. Because slides are labelled with their most severe lesion, only 39% of the slides with annotated atypical ductal hyperplasia (ADH) were labelled ADH. Attention ranked the lesion high but rarely first, and showing the model only the lesion helped little, whereas classifiers trained per decision with lesion-level labels improved all four atypical boundaries. ADH regions inserted into benign slides raised the probability of malignancy more than that of atypia. In a hospital with pathologist-labelled single-subtype slides and in HISTAI, benign slides were scored closer to lesions and triage specificity fell from 0.48-0.53 to 0.00-0.07 and 0.22.

</details>


### [110] [Floorplan-Context-Aware Cross-Environment Human Activity Recognition from mmWave Radar Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19640)
*Zixiong Han,Xilai Wang,Chenzhe Zhao,Amr Mourad,Miodrag Bolic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出平面图上下文感知（FCA）的毫米波雷达点云人体活动识别增强方法，通过引入人与家具的相对位置信息并结合伪地标增强策略，显著提升跨环境性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于毫米波雷达点云训练的HAR模型多在受控空旷空间采集，部署到有家具的房间时性能明显下降，即跨环境泛化能力不足；而现有毫米波点云数据集缺少配准的平面图信息，难以直接引入环境上下文。

Method: FCA方法在输入中增补受试者与家具的相对位置信息以增强跨环境表现；针对缺乏配准平面图的数据，设计伪地标增强策略用于训练FCA模型；部署阶段仅需一次性家具地标配准，无需目标域活动标注或模型权重更新。

Result: 在mm-ADL-Home数据集跨环境评估中，FCA达到77.70%准确率和65.49%宏F1，较仅用点云的直接迁移基线分别提升10.73和10.05个百分点，并超过最强适配基线7.37和6.27个百分点。

Conclusion: 引入家具相对位置的环境上下文可有效缓解毫米波HAR的跨环境性能退化，且部署成本低（仅一次性地标配准），无需目标域标注与再训练。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) models based on mmWave radar point clouds and trained in controlled open spaces often experience performance degradation when deployed in furnished rooms. We propose a Floorplan-Context-Aware (FCA) HAR enhancement method that augments the input with subject-furniture relative position information to improve cross-environment performance. Because existing mmWave point-cloud datasets lack registered floorplans, we introduce a pseudo-landmark augmentation strategy for training the FCA model. In cross-environment evaluation on the mm-ADL-Home dataset, FCA achieves 77.70% accuracy and 65.49% macro-F1, improving upon the point-cloud-only direct-transfer baseline by 10.73 and 10.05 percentage points, respectively, and exceeding the strongest evaluated adaptation baseline by 7.37 and 6.27 percentage points. At deployment, FCA requires only a one-time registration of furniture landmarks, with no target-domain activity annotations or model weight updates.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond the Training Cameras: Coverage-Controlled Probes of Viewing Direction in Frozen Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19642)
*Peize Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 预训练视觉模型（扩散模型、自监督与图文编码器）的隐藏特征以共享的二维平面线性编码相机相对视角α：两物体特征差沿(cosα, sinα)轨迹，且该表示超越可见测量、可外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在2D图像上训练的视觉模型是否、以及以何种形式编码三维场景的相机视角方向，从而判断其内部是否具备对三维几何的抽象理解。

Method: 在渲染场景中固定两物体中心像素、移动相机，使透视、阴影与明暗变化而投影布局不变；在8个预训练模型（扩散、自监督、图文编码器）的隐藏空间中分析两物体特征差与相对视角α的关系，并做跨场景、光照/颜色/形状扰动、路径外推与PixArt-α逐块干预实验。

Result: α被编码为跨场景共享的单一二维平面，特征差呈(cosα, sinα)；平面取向对光照、颜色、地板、形状变化稳健；在一组相机路径上拟合的读出在路径范围外仍准确（8个模型中7个），而同等精度的几何解码器失败；PixArt-α中该平面在单个block处突然形成并向后传递，但其编辑改变物体而非视角。

Conclusion: 视觉模型内部存在简单、共享且可外推的相对视角线性表示，说明该编码不只是对可见测量的汇总；但该表示与生成视角的因果控制相分离。此外，论文还考察了该编码在真实扫描物体上的迁移性。

Abstract: Vision models are trained on 2D images of a 3D world. Do their hidden features encode the direction from which the camera views a scene, and if so, in what form? We study the relative viewing direction $α$ of two objects in rendered scenes where both object centres stay at fixed pixels while the camera moves, so that perspective, shading and shadows change but the projected layout does not. In eight pretrained models, from diffusion models to self-supervised and image-text encoders, $α$ is encoded in a strikingly simple form: a single two-dimensional plane in the hidden space, shared across scenes, in which the difference between the two objects' features traces $(\cosα, \sinα)$. The plane keeps its orientation when lighting, colours, floors and object shapes change. This encoding is more than a summary of a few visible measurements: readouts fitted on one set of camera paths stay accurate at camera positions outside their range in seven of the eight models, whereas decoders of the measured object and shadow geometry, equally accurate within that range, fail there. In the diffusion transformer PixArt-$α$, the plane forms abruptly at a single block and carries direction information to later blocks, yet editing it changes the objects rather than the generated viewpoint. We also examine how this encoding carries over to scanned real objects.

</details>


### [112] [From Cloud to Edge: A Fully Local Pipeline for Pothole Risk Assessment and Driver Warning Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19737)
*Abdalmalek Aburaddaha,Samir A. Rawashdeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将五阶段云端路面坑洞预警流水线全部重建为单卡本地部署：SegSAM半精度+图编译加速2.7倍，ConvNeXt蒸馏分级，8B本地VLM受语法约束生成警告；整体1.27 s/场景、快16倍、完全离线且零边际成本，生成警告在盲评中比云端更安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型虽能把道路图像转化为几何感知的坑洞预警，但依赖商业云服务：原五阶段流水线每场景需两次云端调用，耗时约20 s、成本0.105美元，且无法离线运行。

Method: 用相同数据、提示词与协议，将每个阶段重建为单卡GPU本地运行：(1) 分割沿用原始Segment Anything权重，采用半精度与图编译（graph compilation）加速；(2) 严重度分级用6个ConvNeXt学生模型，从3092条云端标签蒸馏；(3) 警告文本由本地8B参数视觉-语言模型在仅允许车道内机动的语法约束下生成。

Result: 分割提速2.7倍且不改变任何下游风险等级，5个轻量变体在小尺度路面缺陷掩码上均不及原模型；分级学生模型相对人类共识Cohen's kappa=0.268、28 ms/坑洞，接近云端0.321；三位独立盲评者认为本地警告比云端更安全（4.18 vs. 3.65/5，p=0.001，50个场景）且清晰度相当，而自动LLM评判给出相反结论。

Conclusion: 将全流水线本地化后每场景1.27 s，较云系统快16倍，可离线运行且无按场景计费成本；表明本地部署可在性能与安全感知上不逊甚至优于云端方案，但自动LLM评判与人类盲评结论相悖。

Abstract: Vision-language models can turn road images into clear, geometry-aware pothole warnings, but current systems depend on commercial cloud services. Our earlier five-stage pipeline needed two cloud calls per scene, taking about 20 s and USD 0.105, and could not run offline. We rebuild every stage to run on a single local GPU and evaluate it with the same data, prompts, and protocol. Segmentation keeps the original Segment Anything weights but runs them in half precision with graph compilation, which makes it 2.7x faster without changing any downstream risk class; five lightweight variants could not match its masks on small road defects. Severity is graded by six ConvNeXt students distilled from 3092 cloud labels. They reach Cohen's kappa = 0.268 against human consensus at 28 ms per pothole, close to the cloud model's 0.321, within the camera setup they were trained for. A local 8-billion-parameter vision-language model writes the warnings under a grammar that admits only in-lane maneuvers. Three blind, independent raters scored these warnings safer than the cloud warnings (4.18 vs. 3.65 of 5; p = 0.001; 50 scenes) and equally clear, whereas an automated LLM judge ranked them the other way. The complete pipeline runs at 1.27 s per scene, 16x faster than the cloud system, offline and at no per-scene cost.

</details>


### [113] [TIVS: Mapping the Sensitivity Landscape of Visual Token Transformations in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19745)
*Yikang Qiao,Jianye Zeng,Duan Huang,Ziying Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的TIVS框架，将视觉token变换拆分为序列置换、保留、数值精度与统计替换四类，在受控条件下分别考察其对冻结解码器可用性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM效率研究（视觉token剪枝、量化、特征压缩）缺乏对"变换后的视觉token是否仍可被冻结解码器使用"这一性质的系统刻画，缺少受控的实证依据与可靠的多模态接口设计准则。

Method: TIVS（Token Identity Versus Statistics）为无需训练的正交干预框架，在192道POPE问题、TinyLLaVA上分别施加：(1)三种token序列置换；(2)对角与秩64的高斯统计替换；(3)精确矩约束替换；(4)量化误差；(5)token保留率变化，并扩展到额外视觉任务。

Result: 三种token置换平均仅使准确率变化−0.17个百分点，但改变了4.69%的个体预测；对角与秩64高斯替换分别使准确率下降25.0与28.1个百分点。精确矩约束、量化误差与token保留实验表明，效应强度强烈依赖原始特征值与评测设定。

Conclusion: 视觉token的"身份/顺序"对冻结解码器影响甚微，而"原始特征值统计"关键；效应高度依赖特征数值与评测设定。该结果为评估视觉token变换、设计更可靠的多模态接口提供实证基础。

Abstract: Recent efforts to improve the efficiency of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have explored visual token pruning, quantization, and feature compression. However, the properties that determine whether transformed visual tokens remain usable by a frozen decoder are not well characterized. We introduce \textbf{TIVS (Token Identity Versus Statistics)}, a training-free intervention framework that separately studies sequence permutation, token retention, numerical precision, and statistical replacement under controlled conditions. On 192 POPE questions, three token permutations change TinyLLaVA accuracy by only $-0.17$ percentage points on average, while changing 4.69\% of individual predictions. Diagonal and rank-64 Gaussian replacements decrease accuracy by 25.0 and 28.1 points on the same subset. Further experiments with exact moment constraints, quantization error, token retention, and additional visual tasks reveal how strongly the effects depend on the original feature values and the evaluation setting. These findings provide an empirical basis for assessing visual token transformations in frozen MLLMs and designing more reliable multimodal interfaces.

</details>


### [114] [Event-based object detection under adverse lighting and motion: lessons for construction sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19746)
*Zhuoran Feng,Xiaoshan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在像素对齐的PEOD数据集上比较三种事件相机检测器与RGB检测器，量化事件相机在施工场地低光照与快速运动场景下的检测优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工场地视觉监控在夜间、晨昏、隧道及泛光灯下失效，传统RGB相机易欠曝或过曝；事件相机具有微秒级延迟和超过120 dB动态范围，但其对场地相关目标检测的收益尚未在真实数据上量化。

Method: 使用像素对齐的PEOD数据集，将SMamba、SATE、EvRT-DETR三种事件检测器与同数据训练的RGB检测器对比；进行免训练的传感器晚期融合；对53,091个目标做目标级分析；用activation patching定位丢失事件造成的损伤；开展模型无关的剪枝研究；并在渲染施工场景上做零样本测试。

Result: 低光照帧（占测试集70%）上最佳事件检测器达27.2 AP，RGB为21.3 AP；晚期融合在所有光照条件下最优（33.3 AP）。事件检测器受亮度影响小，但快速运动影响显著：每帧位移超过20像素时，RGB对中等目标检出率11–19%，EvRT-DETR为39–60%。activation patching显示事件丢失的损伤位于1/16分辨率特征，小目标依赖最细尺度；剪枝保留60%输入可维持约95%精度但小目标召回减半；渲染场景中调暗泛光灯使RGB近乎归零，事件检测器保留大部分AP50。

Conclusion: 事件相机在施工场地低光照与快速运动条件下显著优于RGB，晚期融合为最稳健方案，并据此提出施工场地相机布设建议。

Abstract: Vision-based monitoring on construction sites fails at night, at dawn and dusk, in tunnels and under floodlights, when conventional RGB cameras under- or over-expose. Event cameras record per-pixel brightness changes with microsecond latency and over 120 dB of dynamic range, but their benefit for site-relevant object detection has not been quantified on real data. We compare three recent event-based detectors (SMamba, SATE and EvRT-DETR) with an RGB detector trained on the same data, using the pixel-aligned PEOD dataset. On low-light frames (70% of the test set) the best event detector reaches 27.2 AP against 21.3 AP for RGB, and a training-free late fusion of both sensors is the best option in every lighting condition (33.3 AP). An object-level analysis of 53,091 objects shows that brightness has little effect on event detectors, whereas fast motion matters: above 20 pixels per frame RGB finds 11-19% of medium-size objects and EvRT-DETR 39-60%. Activation patching locates the damage caused by losing events at the 1/16-resolution features, with small objects depending on the finest scale, and shows that event detectors fail when few signal events meet added noise. A model-agnostic pruning study keeps about 95% of accuracy with 60% of the input but halves small-object recall. On rendered construction sites, used zero-shot, dimming the floodlights drops RGB to near zero while the event detector keeps most of its AP50. We close with camera-placement guidance for construction sites.

</details>


### [115] [How Far Is Too Far? Object Recognition Declines Monotonically with Semantic Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19747)
*Domenic Bersch,Hai Van Tran,Gemma Roig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ImageNet-OOC1k 基准，沿"共识视觉-语义差异度"连续轴对物体-背景关系进行分级控制，发现35个模型（CNN、ViT、VLM）的识别准确率随差异度增大呈高度一致的单调下降；ViT 内部呈"聚合失败"特征（patch 级存在物体证据但未整合进最终预测），据此用简单空间重排序策略最高提升样本内准确率5.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模型对物体-背景上下文敏感，但语义距离增大时识别性能如何变化尚不清楚；现有基准缺乏对物体-背景关系的连续、分级控制，难以刻画上下文敏感性的规律性与可预测性。

Method: 构建 ImageNet-OOC1k 基准，沿连续"共识视觉-语义差异度"轴分级控制物体-背景关系；在35个模型（CNN、ViT、VLM）上评估；对 ViT 做内部表征分析以定位失败机制；基于该诊断提出简单空间重排序策略用于错误恢复。

Result: 35个模型均表现出高度一致的单调准确率下降；ViT 分析显示物体证据在 patch 级存在但未被整合到最终预测（聚合失败）；空间重排序策略恢复部分错误，样本内准确率最高提升5.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 上下文敏感性是现代视觉系统连续且可预测的属性；ViT 分析表明，即便物体证据可得，聚合环节的局限也可能导致部分失败类型。

Abstract: Vision models are sensitive to object-background context, yet it remains unclear how recognition changes as the semantic distance between objects and backgrounds increases. We introduce ImageNet-OOC1k, a benchmark enabling graded control of object-background relationships along a continuous consensus visual-semantic dissimilarity axis. This formulation reveals a highly consistent monotonic decline in recognition accuracy across 35 models spanning convolutional neural networks, Vision Transformers, and vision-language models. Internal analyses of Vision Transformers reveal patterns consistent with aggregation failure, where object evidence is present at the patch level but not consolidated into the final prediction. Using this characterization as a diagnostic probe, a simple spatial re-ranking strategy recovers a subset of these errors and improves in-sample accuracy by up to 5.7 percentage points. These results establish context sensitivity as a continuous and predictable property of modern vision systems, with Vision Transformer analyses suggesting that aggregation limitations may contribute to some failure types even when object evidence remains available.

</details>


### [116] [SEAT: Sign-Type Evidence-Audited Transport for Multi-Label Tongue Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19748)
*Liuyi Xu,Qing Wang,Zikai Liang,An-Yang Lu,Yining Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SEAT证据审计框架，将舌象不同类型证据（局部形态、全局色度、标签依赖）匹配到专属预测与评测机制，在TongueDx上六种子macro-F1均值72.59、集成达74.15。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动舌象识别可提升非侵入式检查的一致性，避免依赖主观目视判断。单张舌图常同时存在多类异质证据的征象（局部形态、全局色度、标签间依赖），但现有方法多依赖共享表示与统一多标签头，难以验证预测是否来自临床相关证据而非虚假视觉相关。

Method: 基于冻结的DINOv2 patch特征构建SEAT：1) 掩码引导的征象专属注意力建模局部形态；2) 在LAB/HSV颜色原型上采用半松弛最优传输路径增强淡白舌识别；3) 共现感知的传输正则项约束批级预测标签边缘分布；并按证据类型设计对应的审计评估。

Result: 在TongueDx基准上，六个随机种子macro-F1均值72.59（标准差1.04），六模型集成达74.15；集成分别超过主干匹配基线和大规模领域专用视觉语言模型8.06与2.99个百分点。

Conclusion: 将专属预测路径与审计机制对齐到征象特性，可在提升多标签精度的同时提供比统一事后空间解释更有针对性的证据支撑。

Abstract: Automated tongue-sign recognition can improve the consistency of non-invasive examinations that otherwise rely on subjective visual judgment. A single tongue image often exhibits multiple coexisting signs characterized by heterogeneous evidence, including localized morphology, global chromaticity, and inter-label dependence. However, existing methods typically rely on shared representations and uniform multi-label heads, making it difficult to verify whether predictions stem from clinically relevant evidence rather than spurious visual correlations. We propose SEAT, an evidence-audited framework that matches distinct evidence types with dedicated prediction and evaluation mechanisms. Built on frozen DINOv2 patch features, SEAT couples mask-guided sign-specific attention for localized morphology, a semi-relaxed optimal transport pathway over LAB/HSV color prototypes to enhance pale-tongue recognition, and a co-occurrence-aware transport regularizer to shape the batch-level predicted label marginal. These components are evaluated through audits matched to their evidence types. On the TongueDx benchmark, SEAT achieves a mean macro-F1 of 72.59 with a standard deviation of 1.04 across six random seeds, with a six-model ensemble reaching 74.15. The ensemble outperforms a backbone-matched baseline and a larger, domain-specialized vision-language model by 8.06 and 2.99 percentage points, respectively. Our results demonstrate that aligning dedicated prediction pathways and audits with sign-specific characteristics improves multi-label accuracy while offering more targeted evidential grounding than uniform post-hoc spatial explanations. Code is available at https://github.com/NEU-TCM-Lab/Sign-type-Evidence-Audited-Transport.

</details>


### [117] [Input-Space versus Feature-Level Conditioning for Robust Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19754)
*Weihang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在RAF-DB上以匹配的主干、训练预算与条件标注，系统比较"输入空间条件化"（四种确定性、免标签视图共享主干+分支专属BN/线性头并平均logits）与"特征空间条件化"（FiLM与Conditional BatchNorm）对表情识别退化的鲁棒性提升及其代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面部表情识别在光照、曝光或分辨率偏离训练分布时性能急剧下降。实践中主要有两类补救：丰富输入信号，或依据估计的图像质量对中间特征进行条件化。二者很少在匹配主干、训练预算与条件标注的条件下被比较，其计算代价也鲜有报告，因此缺乏可直接指导取舍的对照证据。

Method: 受控对照实验，数据集为RAF-DB。输入空间条件化：四个确定性且免标签的视图（对数拉伸、反对数拉伸、降-升采样、恒等），共享主干，每个分支配专属BatchNorm与线性头，最终对各分支logit取平均。特征空间条件化：FiLM与Conditional BatchNorm，采用完全一致的训练协议。在六种主干架构上评估，并用bootstrap区间、符号检验及固定随机种子的ResNet-18估计进行统计推断，同时报告FLOPs、延迟与训练时间开销。

Result: 六种主干上输入空间变体全部提升top-1精度：平均+0.94个百分点（bootstrap区间[0.51, 1.63]，符号检验p=0.016）；ResNet-18的种子控制估计为+0.42个百分点；而特征级条件化仅得+0.10与+0.03个百分点。作者将差距归因于启发式条件标签的信息含量，而非条件化机制本身。代价为4.00×FLOPs、4.18×延迟与3.79×训练时间，即每额外GFLOP约0.26个百分点，并给出该权衡何时值得的明确判据。

Conclusion: 在匹配条件下，输入空间的多视图条件化显著优于基于质量估计的特征级条件化（FiLM/CBN），但提升幅度有限且计算开销近4倍；差异根源在于启发式条件标签所含信息量。因此该方案更适合精度边际收益高于约4倍算力成本、且难以获得高质量条件标注的场景，并应以每GFLOP增益作为取舍准则。

Abstract: Facial expression recognition (FER) degrades sharply when illumination, exposure or resolution departs from the training distribution. Two remedies dominate practice: enriching the input signal, or conditioning intermediate features on an estimated image quality. The two are rarely compared under matched backbones, training budgets and condition annotations, and their cost is seldom reported. We present a controlled comparison on RAF-DB. Input-space conditioning uses four deterministic, label-free views (logarithmic stretching, inverse-logarithmic stretching, down-up resampling and identity), each processed by a shared backbone with a branch-specific BatchNorm and linear head, with the branch logits averaged. Feature-space conditioning is implemented as FiLM and Conditional BatchNorm under an identical protocol. Across six backbone architectures the input-space variant improves top-1 accuracy on every one (mean +0.94 percentage points; bootstrap interval [0.51, 1.63]; sign test p = 0.016), with a seed-controlled estimate of +0.42 percentage points on ResNet-18, whereas feature-level conditioning yields +0.10 and +0.03 percentage points. We attribute the gap to the information content of the heuristic condition label rather than to the conditioning mechanism. The gain costs 4.00x FLOPs, 4.18x latency and 3.79x training time, i.e. 0.26 percentage points per additional GFLOP, and we state an explicit rule for when the trade is worth making.

</details>


### [118] [Drift-Conditioned Structural Replay for Continual Cardiac Ultrasound Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19756)
*Zahid Ullah,Jaeyoon Seo,Jihie Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出漂移条件结构回放损失（DCSRL），依据源任务参考签名的多维漂移动态调节回放监督强度，用于持续心脏超声分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验回放对历史样本施加固定的保留约束，忽略了顺序训练中表示、预测与解剖结构变化幅度的差异，导致监督强度与样本实际遗忘风险不匹配。

Method: 在可靠性引导的回放框架中，按解剖内容与边界特征筛选信息量高的历史样本；对每个保留样本构建冻结的源任务参考签名，度量特征表示、类概率、边界响应与预测解剖结构四类漂移，聚合并归一化为有界的样本级系数，动态缩放边界与结构回放损失，且不改变记忆选择策略。

Result: 在CAMUS、CardiacNet与EchoNet-Dynamic上进行顺序学习评测，与正则化、蒸馏及回放类持续学习方法比较，整体最终分割性能与解剖边界保持均有提升，在同质化成像条件下尤为明显，但任务间知识迁移仍存在权衡。

Conclusion: 源参考的多维漂移条件化为持续心脏超声分割中的自适应解剖知识保持提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Conventional experience replay typically applies fixed preservation constraints to historical exemplars, overlooking variations in how learned representations, predictions, and anatomical structures change during sequential training. To address this limitation, we propose Drift-Conditioned Structural Replay Loss (DCSRL), an exemplar-adaptive supervision mechanism that regulates anatomical preservation according to source-referenced knowledge drift. DCSRL operates within a reliability-guided replay framework that selects informative historical exemplars using anatomical content and boundary characteristics while adaptively regulating replay during training. For each retained exemplar, DCSRL constructs a frozen source-task reference signature and subsequently measures changes in feature representations, class probabilities, boundary responses, and predicted anatomical structures. These complementary drift measurements are aggregated and normalized to generate bounded, exemplar-specific coefficients that dynamically adjust boundary and structural replay losses without modifying the underlying memory-selection policy. We evaluate DCSRL under sequential learning across CAMUS, CardiacNet, and EchoNet-Dynamic, comparing its performance with regularization-, distillation-, and replay-based continual-learning approaches. Experimental findings indicate that DCSRL improves overall final segmentation performance and anatomical boundary preservation, particularly under heterogeneous imaging conditions, although trade-offs remain in knowledge transfer across tasks. These findings suggest that source-referenced multidimensional drift conditioning offers a promising direction for adaptive anatomical knowledge preservation in continual cardiac ultrasound segmentation.

</details>


### [119] [Spatiotemporal Reconstruction of Auroral Precipitations with Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19757)
*Nazar Misyats,Mizuki Fukizawa,Yoshimasa Tanaka,Satoshi Ikehata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种时间条件神经网络建模动态极光电子沉降场：以逐帧隐码+离散动力学驱动共享解码器，将电子能谱映射为多波长体发射率并渲染成像，实现动态极光层析重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极光提供了对沉降到高层大气的高能电子的远程光学观测，其能量分布编码了磁层大尺度过程的关键信息。但从地面相机图像反演电子沉降场是困难的反问题，现有极光层析方法多局限于简化静态场景、离散空间与能量网格，或对电子能谱有强假设。

Method: 用时间条件神经网络建模动态沉降场：每帧状态由受学习到的离散动力学驱动的逐帧隐码表示；共享解码器将水平位置与隐状态映射为电子能谱；预测通量转换为多波长体发射率，并在考虑延迟发射的情况下渲染为相机图像。

Result: 在合成动态极光序列和真实多视角极光视频上评估，所提表示能够产生时间上连贯的沉降重建，且图像投影与观测一致。

Conclusion: 以隐动力学+共享解码器的神经表示可有效刻画动态极光电子沉降场，突破静态、离散网格与能谱强假设的限制，重建结果在时间一致性与图像一致性上均得到验证。

Abstract: Auroras provide remote optical observations of energetic electrons precipitating into the upper atmosphere, and whose energy distribution encodes key information about large scale magnetospheric processes. Recovering the underlying electron precipitation field from ground-based camera images of auroras remains a challenging inverse problem. Existing auroral tomography techniques are often limited to simplified static settings, discrete spatial and energy grids, or strong assumptions on the electron energy spectrum. We propose to model the dynamic auroral electron precipitation field via a temporally conditioned neural network. The state of the precipitation field at each frame is represented by a per-frame latent code driven by learned discrete dynamics. A shared decoder maps horizontal positions and latent states to electron energy spectra. The predicted flux is converted to multi-wavelength volume emission rates and rendered into camera images accounting for delayed emissions. We evaluate the method on synthetic dynamic auroral sequences and real multi-view auroral videos, showing that the proposed representation produces temporally coherent precipitation reconstructions and image projections consistent with the observations.

</details>


### [120] [SemLite-GS: Semantic-Selective Parallel Gaussian Splatting for Lightweight Building Reconstruction from UAV Oblique Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19773)
*Tengfei Wang,Xin Wang,Zihan Li,Quanjian Ji,Yifei Yu,Dong Lin,Zongqian Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemLite-GS：基于语义选择性并行3DGS的轻量化建筑网格重建框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量化3D建筑模型支撑城市空间分析，但传统流程需稠密场景重建、建筑提取与网格简化。3DGS虽比多视图立体更高效，但现有大规模方法缺乏语义选择与并行的实例级训练能力。

Method: 1) 以正射参考图建立全局一致的建筑身份，并传播至倾斜视图生成相机-实例对与感兴趣区域；2) 语义选择性并行PGSR训练，在相关图像区域内联合处理多个配对，仅更新目标高斯，上下文高斯保持固定以维持可见性；3) 从渲染的深度、不透明度与法向图提取建筑平面，引导SDF优化，实现自适应多边形分配与结构边界清晰的网格。

Result: 在多个大规模无人机数据集上，相较代表性全场景3DGS方法显著缩短建筑重建时间；在相当多边形预算下，相较稠密重建后网格简化流程，更好地保留主导平面结构与锐利建筑过渡。

Conclusion: 语义选择+并行实例训练+PGSR平面引导SDF优化可在保证结构质量的前提下高效产出轻量化建筑网格，代码已开源。

Abstract: Lightweight 3D building models support urban spatial analysis, but their production often requires dense scene reconstruction, building extraction, and mesh simplification. Although 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is more efficient than conventional multi-view stereo, existing large-scale methods lack semantic selection and parallel instance-level training. We present SemLite-GS, a building-centric framework that reconstructs lightweight building meshes from large-scale UAV oblique imagery. First, an orthographic reference map establishes globally consistent building identities, which are propagated to oblique views to form camera-instance pairs and regions of interest. Second, semantic-selective parallel PGSR training jointly processes multiple pairs within relevant image regions while updating only target Gaussians; contextual Gaussians remain fixed to preserve visibility. Third, architectural planes extracted from rendered depth, opacity, and normal maps guide signed distance field optimization, producing meshes with adaptive polygon allocation and well-defined structural boundaries. Experiments on multiple large-scale UAV datasets demonstrate that our SemLite-GS substantially reduces building reconstruction time compared with representative full-scene 3DGS methods. With comparable polygon budgets, SemLite-GS achieves superior preservation of dominant planar structures and sharp architectural transitions compared with conventional dense reconstruction followed by mesh decimation. The source code is available at https://github.com/IRIS-LAB-whu/SemLite-GS.

</details>


### [121] [When Backdoors Can Be Switched Off: Suppression-Aware Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19774)
*Nur Afsa Syeda,Mohamed Elmahallawy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA模型中的"抑制感知后门"，通过合取触发器与指令条件抑制结合，构造三种变体（Cross-Modal Trigger、Self-Suppressing Trigger、Multi-Trigger），在SmolVLA/SO-100上显著降低隔离森林检测率（96–98%→1–18%），动作空间l1散度降低10–14倍，攻击成功率63–71%且干净任务性能完好，并可在OpenVLA/LIBERO上迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉观测、机器人状态与自然语言指令映射为动作，多模态接口引入新安全风险；现有VLA后门攻击多关注触发器激活与隐蔽动作操纵，而对恶意行为的"条件性抑制"（conditional suppression）研究不足。

Method: 提出抑制感知后门设计：合取触发器激活 + 指令条件抑制。三种变体：①Cross-Modal Trigger（机器人状态+空间分离视觉触发）；②Self-Suppressing Trigger（状态触发+[DISRUPT]指令token激活，[RESTORE]token抑制）；③Multi-Trigger（额外需视觉触发，且在物理触发持续存在时仍保持抑制）。在SmolVLA上基于两个SO-100操作数据集，与State Backdoor、BadVLA、TabVLA、SilentDrift四个基线对比评估。

Result: Self-Suppressing与Multi-Trigger将Isolation Forest检测率从96–98%降至1–18%，动作空间l1散度降低10–14倍，攻击成功率达63–71%，干净任务性能完美。OpenVLA/LIBERO实验表明攻击可迁移至7B参数策略，并规避STRIP与动作分布漂移检测。

Conclusion: 条件性抑制是评估VLA策略后门威胁的重要维度，为VLA安全评测提供了新视角。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map visual observations, robot states, and natural-language instructions to robotic actions, but their multimodal interfaces introduce new security risks. Existing VLA backdoor attacks primarily study trigger-based activation and stealthy action manipulation, leaving conditional suppression of malicious behavior underexplored. We investigate suppression-aware backdoors, which combine conjunctive trigger activation with instruction-conditioned suppression. We instantiate this design through three variants: Cross-Modal Trigger, combining robot-state and spatially separated visual triggers; Self-Suppressing Trigger, requiring a state trigger and a [DISRUPT] instruction token for activation, with [RESTORE] token suppressing the attack; and Multi-Trigger, additionally requiring a visual trigger while retaining suppression even when physical triggers persist. We evaluate these attacks using SmolVLA on two SO-100 manipulation datasets against four baselines: State Backdoor, BadVLA, TabVLA, and SilentDrift. Self-Suppressing and Multi-Trigger reduce Isolation Forest detection from 96--98% to 1--18% and action-space l divergence by 10--14X, achieving 63--71% attack success rates with perfect clean-task performance. Experiments with OpenVLA on LIBERO further show that the attacks transfer to a 7B-parameter policy, evading STRIP and action-distribution-shift detection. Our findings establish conditional suppression as an important dimension for evaluating backdoor threats in VLA policies.

</details>


### [122] [A Threshold-Free Visual-Semantic Matching Metric for Evaluating Consistency in Subject-Driven Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19780)
*Bowei Shan,Heng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TF-VSM：一种全连续、无阈值的视觉一致性度量，用置信度加权积分替代阈值门控，并配合特征解耦重训练，在100对基准上显著优于VSM、CLIP、DINO与VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动文生图评测中，局部视觉一致性难以量化：CLIP/DINO/VLM类全局嵌入池化空间表征，丢失细粒度纹理伪影；VSM等标量阈值门控稠密度量存在点对齐丢失、阈值敏感与分数不稳定问题。

Method: 提出TF-VSM：对每个目标点以非线性语义匹配置信度 w_j=ReLU(ŝ_j)^2 加权，并在所有一致性水平 r∈[0,1] 上连续积分视觉相似度，得到闭式置信度加权分数，无需手工超参；同时为双分支ResNet特征聚合网络设计扩展的特征解耦重训练方案。

Result: 在含真值修复oracle的100对受控基准上，TF-VSM取得Pearson 0.638、Spearman 0.674，重训练后较预训练VSM基线（0.454/0.589）提升+0.171/+0.116，显著优于CLIP（−0.052/−0.004）、DINO（0.087/0.121）与VLM（0.096/0.003）。

Conclusion: 六项诊断消融表明：平滑置信度加权是正确机制；硬语义门控脆弱（T_s=0.8时Pearson降至0.533）；无阈值度量对训练损失平衡α基本不变，而阈值化度量仍依赖该参数。

Abstract: In subject-driven text-to-image generation, quantitatively evaluating localized visual consistency across varying poses and environments remains a major challenge. Existing metrics either rely on global image embeddings (CLIP, DINO) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) that pool spatial representations and miss fine-grained textural artifacts, or depend on scalar threshold-gated dense metrics such as Visual-Semantic Matching (VSM) that suffer from point-alignment dropouts, severe threshold sensitivity, and score instability. To overcome these limitations, we propose TF-VSM, a fully continuous, threshold-free visual-consistency metric paired with an extended feature-disentanglement retraining schedule for dual-branch ResNet feature-aggregation networks. Rather than enforcing rigid binary cutoffs $(T_s,T_v)$, TF-VSM weights every object point by a non-linear semantic match confidence $w_j=ReLU(\hat{s}_j)^2$ and continuously integrates visual similarity over all consistency levels $r\in[0,1]$, yielding a closed-form confidence-weighted score without any hand-tuned hyperparameters. On a controlled 100-pair benchmark with ground-truth inpainting oracles, TF-VSM attains Pearson $0.638$ and Spearman $0.674$ oracle correlation, improving over the pretrained VSM baseline ($0.454/0.589$) by $+0.171/+0.116$ after retraining, and drastically outperforming CLIP ($-0.052/-0.004$), DINO ($0.087/0.121$), and VLMs ($0.096/0.003$). Six diagnostic ablations confirm that smooth confidence weighting is the right mechanism, that hard semantic gating is brittle (dropping to $0.533$ Pearson at $T_s{=}0.8$), and that the threshold-free metric is essentially invariant to the training-time loss balance $α$ that the thresholded metric still depends on.

</details>


### [123] [Fine-Grained RLAIF for Visual Safety: Learning to Zoom into Hazards for Multimodal Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19786)
*Chunxue Xu,Yiwei Wang,Yujun Cai,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出细粒度视觉安全对齐任务与1,200样本基准，并设计Fine-Grained RLAIF（FGR）强化学习框架，将图像放大作为可学习的感知策略，通过分解奖励联合对齐"看哪里、何时行动、如何推理"三种安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作假设风险在语言中显式表达或在图像中全局显著，而现实危害常视觉上细微或位于边缘区域；模型依赖粗粒度全局视觉表示而失败。

Method: 构建覆盖四个领域、需局部检视的1,200样本基准；提出FGR强化学习框架，将zoom-in建模为可学习感知策略；采用分解奖励设计：视觉证据奖励（定位）、工具格式约束（行动时机）、视觉接地安全解释（推理）。

Result: FGR准确率达86.71%–93.00%，持续优于强多模态与基于智能体的基线。

Conclusion: 细粒度视觉安全对齐需显式的局部感知策略；将放大操作作为可学习动作并结合分解奖励可有效提升细微/边缘危害的识别与解释能力。

Abstract: Recent multimodal safety alignment heavily relies on a text-centric paradigm, assuming risks are explicit in language or globally prominent in images. However, real-world hazards are often visually subtle or peripheral, causing existing models to fail due to their reliance on coarse, global visual representations. In this work, we study fine-grained visual safety alignment and introduce a challenging 1,200-sample benchmark across four domains, where hazards necessitate localized inspection. To address this, we propose Fine-Grained RLAIF (FGR), a reinforcement learning framework that casts image zoom-in as a learnable perception policy. FGR employs a decomposed reward design to jointly align three core safety behaviors: (i) where to look via a visual evidence reward, (ii) when to act via a tool-formatting constraint, and (iii) how to reason via visually grounded safety explanations. FGR achieves accuracy ranging from 86.71% to 93.00%, consistently outperforming strong multimodal and agent-based baselines.

</details>


### [124] [Pros4D: Modeling Evolving 4D Worlds in Latent Space for Dynamic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19803)
*Rongxin Gao,Dongxuan Liu,Chu Li,Chengyang Ji,Jialing Huang,Runyu Chen,Kairun Wen,Chenxin Li,Jingrui Ye,Haosong Zhen,Xinghao Ding,Yue Huang,Yuzhi Huang,Xiaodong Tu,Jingyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Pros4D 框架，在潜空间中建模演化的 4D 世界，统一"观测动力学理解"与"未来动力学预测"，并配套 Pros4D-Engine 与 Pros4D-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多聚焦已观测动力学理解，而动态环境中的智能体还需超越观测边界的未来预测；同时外部几何处理与文本描述会增加推理复杂度或丢失连续动力学信息，因此需要一个可共享、保留观测动力学且具备外推预测能力的内部状态表示。

Method: Pros4D-Engine 生成几何接地监督并为两类任务构建配对样本；模型将观测动力学压缩为潜观测状态并自回归滚动为潜未来状态；4D Latent Alignment（4DLA）通过视觉-语义与几何互补监督对齐这些状态，并结合语言监督连接任务推理；Pros4D-Bench 覆盖动态相机、物体、场景共八类空间任务。

Result: 在多个基准上，两类能力（观测动力学理解与未来动力学预测）均相较强基线取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 以潜空间 4D 世界状态为核心的统一建模范式，可在无需外部几何流水线与文本中介的情况下同时支撑回顾性理解与前瞻性预测，为 VLM 的动态空间推理提供可行路径。

Abstract: Dynamic spatial reasoning from monocular video enables vision-language models (VLMs) to understand how 3D environments evolve over time, supporting physical world understanding and embodied intelligence. Existing efforts primarily address observed-dynamics understanding, whereas agents in dynamic environments also require future-dynamics prediction beyond the observation boundary. Moreover, external geometric processing and textual descriptions can increase inference complexity or lose information when representing continuous dynamics. These limitations motivate a shared internal state that preserves observed dynamics and remains predictive beyond the input video. Accordingly, we present Pros4D, a framework for modeling evolving 4D worlds in latent space that unifies retrospective understanding of observed-dynamics with prospective reasoning for future-dynamics prediction. Specifically, Pros4D-Engine derives geometry-grounded supervision and constructs paired examples for both tasks, while Pros4D-Bench evaluates them across eight spatial task categories spanning dynamic cameras, objects, and scenes. At the model level, Pros4D compresses observed dynamics into latent observed states and autoregressively rolls them forward into latent future states. 4D Latent Alignment (4DLA) grounds these states through complementary visual-semantic and geometric supervision, while joint language supervision connects them to task reasoning. Experiments across benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong baselines in both capabilities.

</details>


### [125] [The Flip Test: Video World Models Fail to Learn the World State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19822)
*Mi Luo,Lijun Yu,Adams Wei Yu,Ang Cao,Baochen Sun,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Nan Hua,Yuan Hao,Alex Dimakis,Ya Xu,Ed H. Chi,Xuezhi Wang,Yinxiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出反事实测试基准 FlipTest，检验视频世界模型是否真正追踪世界状态；结果显示 11 个视频世界模型全部失败（最佳成对准确率 18.3%，低于随机猜测 25%），而 VLM 与代码智能体在同样帧上表现良好，说明决定性信息存在于图像中，模型未能捕捉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型声称能从当前场景预测未来，但现有模型可能只是生成看似合理的像素，而非追踪决定未来动力学的因果状态。作者希望通过反事实探针（微小改变当前状态即翻转未来）检验模型是否具备真正的世界状态感知。

Method: 构建 FlipTest：基于 10 个模拟任务与 15 个真实任务共 1,680 帧，设计三级递进严格度的测试——(1) 视觉最小对（几乎相同但真实未来不同的两帧），检验预测是否跨越边界改变；(2) 匹配对照，检验改变是否仅发生在未来确实改变之处；(3) 稠密扫描（最严格），精确定位变化发生的位置。评估 11 个视频世界模型，并与 VLM 和测量图像的代码智能体对比。

Result: 所有 11 个视频世界模型均未达标：最佳模型在真实与模拟场景上平均成对准确率仅 18.3%，低于随机猜测的 25%；模型在未来不变的步进与未来改变的步进上翻转频率相近，且在稠密扫描中响应保持平坦。相比之下，VLM 具有选择性并在边界附近呈近 S 形曲线，代码智能体测量图像后逼近真实边界。

Conclusion: 决定性信息已存在于帧中，视频模型只是未捕捉到。作者呼吁社区重新思考将世界建模等同于纯视频预测的做法：下一帧预测不仅要生成合理像素，还必须识别控制未来动力学的因果状态。

Abstract: Video world models promise to predict the future of a scene from its present. Do current models keep this promise? We probe this question counterfactually. When a small change to the present flips the future, such as a box resting a few millimeters before or past a table edge, a model that tracks the state of the world must flip its prediction too. We build FlipTest, a three-level hierarchy of increasingly strict tests over 1,680 initial frames from 10 simulated and 15 real-world tasks. At the base, visual minimal pairs, two nearly identical frames whose true futures differ, test whether predictions change across the boundary; matched controls go further and test whether they change only where the future does; and dense sweeps, the strictest level, locate exactly where they change. All 11 video world models we evaluate fall short. The best averages 18.3% pair accuracy over real and simulated scenes, below the 25% of random guessing. Models flip as often on steps that leave the future unchanged as on steps that change it, and their response stays flat across the sweeps. On the same frames, a vision-language model is selective and traces a near S-shaped curve near the boundary, and a coding agent that measures the image comes close to the true boundary. This implies that the deciding information is in the frame, and video models just miss it. We therefore urge the community to rethink world modeling as pure video prediction. Next-frame prediction must do more than draw plausible pixels; it must also identify the causal states that control future dynamics.

</details>


### [126] [When Does Reusing Layers Help Multimodal Embeddings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19827)
*Zhizhang Hu,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在紧凑通用多模态嵌入模型中重复执行同一批层（循环 Transformer）能否提升检索嵌入？作者在单一视觉-语言架构的三个检查点上系统研究11种循环设计与训练变体，提出诊断协议区分“是否用上额外计算”“图像状态是否响应查询”“更新是否真正提升检索”，并从理论上证明单向信息流下置于查询前的图像在重复层后仍与查询无关，进而通过三种方式打通查询→图像路径。结果表明：标准增加循环在MMEB-V2全部78个数据集上反而下降0.46分，中间生成阶段可转为+0.36，而递归预训练模型相对去除循环的对照提升+4.23分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环 Transformer 以几乎不变的参数量增加计算，被视为替代更大编码器的有吸引力方案，其在语言推理与近期检索任务上的成功让人期待其用于多模态嵌入。但作者指出：更高的检索分数本身不能说明额外一遍前向到底贡献了什么——模型可能并未真正利用额外计算，图像状态可能不随查询变化，或更新并未改善检索。因此需要一套可拆解的诊断协议，厘清循环在多模态嵌入中何时有效、为何有效。

Method: 在同一个视觉-语言架构的三个检查点上对比11种循环设计与训练变体；构建三阶段评估协议，分别检验模型是否使用额外一遍、图像状态是否对查询敏感、这些更新是否提升检索。理论分析方面，证明在信息仅前向传播时，位于查询之前的图像经过重复层仍保持查询无关。据此以三种方式打开查询→图像通路，其中包括顺序交替递归（下一遍将查询置于最前）；并做双向注意力与顺序交替下的冻结后段视觉更新消融。

Result: 三种路径均产生查询敏感的画像状态；在双向注意力与顺序交替设置下，冻结后段视觉更新不降低准确率。MMEB-V2（78个数据集）上：标准新增循环较其训练对照下降0.46分；引入中间生成阶段后效果变为+0.36分；以递归预训练的模型相对自身“删除循环”对照提升+4.23分，且在视觉问答、按文本定位目标等任务中受益于查询条件化的图像更新。

Conclusion: 单纯增加循环并不可靠，检索增益取决于查询信息能否回流到图像表示以及训练方式；仅看检索分数会掩盖额外一遍的实际作用。提出了“查询→图像”通路与递归预训练这一有效设计方向，并用诊断协议将架构改动与训练策略的贡献分离。局限在于仅在单一架构/三个检查点上验证，绝对增益幅度较小，结论的跨模型泛化性有待进一步确认。

Abstract: We ask whether a compact universal multimodal embedding models can produce better search embeddings by running the same layers again. Looped transformers add computation with nearly unchanged model size, making them an appealing alternative to a larger encoder. Their success in language-model reasoning and recent use for retrieval motivate the idea, but higher retrieval scores alone do not reveal what an extra pass contributes. We study eleven loop designs and training variants on three checkpoints of one vision-language architecture. Our protocol distinguishes whether a model uses its extra pass, whether its image states respond to the query, and whether those updates improve retrieval. We prove that an image placed before the query remains query-independent through repeated layers when information only travels forward. We then open a query-to-image path in three ways, including order-alternating recurrence, which places the query first on the next pass. Each path produces query-sensitive image states. For bidirectional attention and order alternation, freezing later visual updates does not lower accuracy. On all 78 datasets of the Massive Multimodal Embedding Benchmark version 2 (MMEB-V2), a standard added loop loses 0.46 points against its trained control; an intermediate generative stage changes the effect to +0.36. A model pretrained with recurrence gains +4.23 points over its own loop-removed control and benefits from query-conditioned image updates when answering visual questions or locating objects from text.

</details>


### [127] [PRISM: Primitive-Integrated Separable Modulation with Priority Visual Memory Queue for Compositional Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19828)
*Jianhua Wang,Zhihang Zheng,Yang Liu,Jiale Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM 是一个在线组合零样本学习（CZSL）框架，将“基元共享”从训练阶段延伸到测试时自适应，通过分层原型迁移与视觉记忆接地，在闭世界与开世界设定下取得 state of the art。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CZSL 方法在部署阶段停止共享证据：固定原型无法利用无标签测试流；组合级细化只能在单个组合内修正，无法把纠正传播到共享基元（属性/物体）的其他组合上。因此需要一种在推断时仍能跨组合共享并更新基元证据的机制。

Method: 提出 PRISM 在线 CZSL 框架。核心为分层知识累积模块（HiKAM），将原型迁移分解为组合级、属性级、物体级三套残差库，实现跨共享基元的纠错传播；优先级视觉记忆队列（PVMQ）并由原型队列初始化（PQI）初始化，用有界视觉证据对文本原型更新进行接地。同一自适应核心提供两种推理：穷举式 Full PRISM 与基于留出排名选择候选预算的召回校准 Adaptive PRISM-Lite。

Result: 在 UT-Zappos、MIT-States、C-GQA 的闭世界与开世界实验中均达 SOTA：Full PRISM 在三个基准上取得最佳开世界 AUC 与调和平均，并在 UT-Zappos、C-GQA 上取得最佳闭世界结果。细粒度长尾与非平稳基元分块评测显示，在低支持概念上有增益且长时间偏斜下适应稳定。Adaptive PRISM-Lite 在完整开世界空间中显著提效，仅有小幅精度损失。

Conclusion: PRISM 将基元共享的在线自适应、多模态接地与候选感知推理三者结合，验证了测试时继续共享基元证据对 CZSL 的有效性；并以轻量变体在效率与精度间提供可调折中。

Abstract: Compositional Zero-Shot Learning (CZSL) recognizes unseen attribute--object pairs by recombining primitives from seen compositions. Existing methods stop sharing evidence at deployment: fixed prototypes cannot exploit unlabeled test streams, while composition-wise refinement cannot propagate corrections across pairs sharing a primitive. We introduce PRISM, an online CZSL framework that extends primitive sharing to test-time adaptation. Its Hierarchical Knowledge Accumulation Module (HiKAM) factorizes prototype migration into composition-, attribute-, and object-level residual banks. A Priority Visual Memory Queue (PVMQ), initialized by Prototype Queue Initialization (PQI), grounds text updates with bounded visual evidence. The same adaptive core supports exhaustive Full PRISM and recall-calibrated Adaptive PRISM-Lite, which selects candidate budgets from held-out ranks. Comprehensive closed- and open-world experiments on UT-Zappos, MIT-States, and C-GQA demonstrate the state of the art, with Full PRISM achieving the best open-world AUC and harmonic mean on all three benchmarks and the best closed-world results on UT-Zappos and C-GQA. Fine-grained long-tail and non-stationary primitive-block evaluations reveal gains on under-supported concepts and stable adaptation under prolonged skew. Adaptive PRISM-Lite further delivers substantial efficiency gains in the full open-world space with only a small accuracy cost. PRISM thus combines primitive-shared online adaptation, multimodal grounding, and candidate-aware inference.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Vehicle-Centric Data: Scaling Autonomous Driving through Roadside-to-Vehicle Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19830)
*Yichen Zhang,Zihan Wang,Hao Chen,Runyi Huang,Ruixue Zong,Jing Wang,Yunong Mao,Lei He,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyR2V将路侧多模态观测转换为七路环视视频，实现多车视角训练数据生成，构建R2V360数据集并在图像保真度与下游检测性能上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶模型训练依赖大规模多视角车载数据，车队的采集、处理与标注成本高昂。路侧基础设施可记录交通流与多车交互，但路侧与车载传感器之间存在视角鸿沟，难以直接用于训练。

Method: 提出AnyR2V框架：将序列化路侧观测聚合为统一场景表示（几何与道路使用者运动），沿目标车辆轨迹渲染外观、深度与布局条件，并在时空对齐的路侧-车载数据对监督下生成结构感知、运动一致的七相机环视视频；同时将既有路侧3D框变换到目标车坐标系，与生成图像配对。构建R2V360数据集（城市交叉口89段真实交通序列，含对齐的多模态路侧数据与车载环视视频）。

Result: 图像保真度及生成与真实车载图像的深度、语义一致性优于六种场景重建方法；将生成帧按真实帧数50%加入训练，在真实车载测试集上使三种3D检测器的mAP提升3.3–3.4 AP。

Conclusion: 路侧交通记录可作为多车辆视角训练数据来源，为自动驾驶提供新的数据扩展途径。

Abstract: Training autonomous driving models requires large and diverse datasets, collected primarily by vehicles. Scaling them entails investment in fleet operation, data processing and annotation. Road infrastructure records traffic and multi-vehicle interactions, yet the viewpoint gap between roadside and onboard sensors limits its direct use for training. Here we introduce AnyR2V, a framework that transforms multimodal roadside observations into seven-camera surround-view videos for different vehicle perspectives. It aggregates sequential observations into a shared scene representation of geometry and road-user motion, then renders appearance, depth and layout conditions along target-vehicle trajectories. Supervised by spatiotemporally aligned roadside-vehicle pairs, AnyR2V generates surround-view videos guided by scene structure and road-user motion. Existing roadside 3D bounding boxes are transformed into target-vehicle coordinates and paired with generated images, allowing one traffic recording and its annotations to yield training data from multiple vehicle perspectives. We construct R2V360, a dataset of 89 real traffic sequences at an urban intersection, with aligned multimodal roadside data and onboard surround-view videos. AnyR2V outperforms six scene reconstruction methods in image fidelity and in depth and semantic agreement between generated and real onboard images. We further evaluate generated videos along other vehicles' trajectories by adding them to real training data. Adding generated frames equal to 50% of the original real training frame count improves mean average precision (mAP) by 3.3-3.4 AP-points across three 3D object detectors on the real onboard test set, relative to real-data-only baselines. These results demonstrate that roadside traffic records can provide training data from multiple vehicle perspectives, offering a new data source for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [129] [AGPF: Dynamic Source Selection for Feature Pyramid Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19836)
*Xu Yang,Gady Agam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究特征金字塔中每个输出位置应跨哪些尺度聚合信息，发现注意力意外集中于更粗的层级，据此提出非对称全局金字塔融合（AGPF）并显著提升检测与分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征金字塔为稠密预测提供多空间分辨率特征，但每个输出位置应从哪些金字塔层级聚合信息仍缺乏研究；作者通过让每个输出位置对所有层级做注意力并进行统计，以揭示尺度注意力的分布规律。

Method: 允许每个输出位置关注所有金字塔层级的特征并测量注意力的跨尺度分布；基于观察提出AGPF颈部结构，在保持原始输出网格不变的前提下，让每个位置与图像坐标对齐的多尺度记忆进行交互，实现灵活的跨尺度聚合而不改变预测分辨率。

Result: 最细输出网格上约75%的注意力分配给粗两级特征，而非当前或相邻层级；同一网格上物体越大越偏向粗特征。相比FPN，AGPF在COCO上改进全部19种目标检测与7种实例分割配置；单个融合块使Cascade Mask R-CNN提升1.7 box AP与2.3 mask AP，且增益可迁移到语义与全景分割。

Conclusion: 尺度注意力分布明显偏向更粗层级且随物体尺寸变化，表明固定的层级分配并非最优；AGPF以非对称全局跨尺度融合有效利用该规律，在检测与各类分割任务上带来一致且可迁移的提升。

Abstract: Feature pyramids provide dense predictors with features at several spatial resolutions. However, it remains largely unexplored which pyramid levels each output location should aggregate information from. We investigate this question by allowing each output location to attend to features from every pyramid level and measuring how attention is distributed across scales. Surprisingly, locations on the finest output grid allocate approximately 75\% of their attention to features two levels coarser, rather than to the current or adjacent levels. Even on the same output grid, the balance shifts toward coarser features as object size increases.
  These observations motivate Asymmetric Global Pyramid Fusion (AGPF), a neck that preserves the original output grids while allowing each location to dynamically select its contextual sources across scales. Each location interacts with a multiscale memory whose features are aligned in image coordinates, enabling flexible cross-scale aggregation without altering prediction resolutions. Compared with FPN, AGPF improves all 19 object detection and all seven instance segmentation configurations evaluated on COCO. A single fusion block improves Cascade Mask R-CNN by 1.7 box AP and 2.3 mask AP, and the gains consistently transfer to semantic and panoptic segmentation.

</details>


### [130] [Passable for Whom? Few-Shot Personal Calibration of Sidewalk Accessibility Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19839)
*Wesley Maia,Angel Martinez-Sanchez,Maitrayee Keskar,Ross Greer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"少样本个性化无障碍感知"任务：仅用一个人对K个场景的回答，预测其对从未见过的人行道场景的可通行性判断。PassCal将冻结VLM的内部状态与审计数据监督的障碍属性（类型、严重度、上下文）构成的共享场景描述，与从个人回答中拟合的个性化解释（响应风格校准+属性权重）分离。在135名辅助器具使用者对50个场景的8,883条判断上（人和场景均留出），8个回答将Brier分数从0.550降至0.479；保形风险控制可输出"可通行/受阻/取决于你"三类陈述并绑定错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可通行性判断本质上是因人而异的：使用同一辅助器具的人对同一图像也会给出不同结论，因此"每场景一个标签"或"每类器具一个标签"的标注方式对很多人是错误的。现有VLM提示方法只能提供群体平均判断，缺乏对个体感知差异的刻画与可验证的不确定性控制。

Method: PassCal将预测分解为共享描述与个人解释两部分。共享描述来自：(1) 冻结VLM的内部状态；(2) 在14.9k条公开审计数据上训练的检测器所预测的障碍类型、严重度与上下文属性。个人解释由该人K个回答拟合，包括其响应风格校准和属性权重。此外引入保形风险控制(conformal risk control)，在人群层面为错误陈述设置期望错误率上限，输出"可通行/受阻/取决于你"。

Result: 在135名经核实的辅助器具使用者对同样50个场景的8,883条判断上，人与场景均留出：8个回答使Brier分数由0.550降至0.479；同等条件下直接提示VLM为0.562，仅做个人校准而无场景信息为0.521。保形风险控制在5%目标下，8个回答将决定性场景比例由27%提升至42%。审计监督的属性在测试的全部16种视觉与VLM编码器上均带来提升，并优于人行道导航VLM产生的属性。

Conclusion: 个性化是行人无障碍感知中不可忽略的维度，单一场景标签会掩盖个体差异。将场景描述与个人解释解耦，可在极少标注（K=8）下显著提升个体判断预测，并借助保形风险控制给出错误率可控、可解释的三类结论。

Abstract: Whether a sidewalk is passable depends on who is trying to pass: people who use the same mobility aid disagree about the same image, so one label per scene, or per aid, is wrong for many of them. We introduce few-shot personal accessibility perception: from K answers of a person never seen before, predict their judgment of sidewalk scenes never seen before. PassCal separates a shared description of the scene from the person's interpretation of it. The description comes from the internal state of a frozen vision-language model (VLM) and from barrier type, severity and context properties predicted by a detector trained on 14.9k public audits; the interpretation, a calibration of the person's response style and a weighting of the properties, is fit from their answers. On 8,883 judgments in which each of 135 verified mobility-aid users rated the same 50 sidewalk scenes, with people and scenes both held out, eight answers lower the Brier score from 0.550 to 0.479. The same VLM prompted with those answers reaches 0.562, and the same calibration without the scene 0.521. Conformal risk control turns predictions into passable, blocked or "depends on you" statements with a bounded expected rate of wrong statements over people; at 5%, eight answers raise the share of decisive scenes from 27% to 42%. Audit-supervised properties improve all sixteen vision and VLM encoders we test and outperform attributes from a sidewalk navigation VLM.

</details>


### [131] [PanDWM: Panoramic 4D Driving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19857)
*Jiajun Sun,Zeyu Zhang,Zeyu Ren,Guangfeng Jiang,Yang Zhao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanDWM 将六路环视相机拼接为单一 360° 全景带，使跨相机一致性转化为单图连续性，实现 4 步因果流式生成并快速重建 4D 场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶世界模型常用六台光心分离、重叠很小的针孔相机生成视频，需额外学习跨视角一致性，边界处一致性约束困难且效率低。

Method: 把六相机视图缝合成 360° 全景带，相机边界落在图像内部，一致性即图像连续性，且每像素保留相机射线；以布局为条件，4 步因果学生窗口式逐段生成，由因果教师蒸馏并在自身 rollout 上训练；前馈解码器沿射线将每帧提升为 3D 高斯，与相邻帧静态高斯补全为 4D 场景。

Result: 在 nuScenes 上以单一拼接 360° 视角评测，FID 与 FVD 均低于多相机基线，速度为其 8–46 倍；保持 50 步教师的 FID，FVD 降低 22–25%。

Conclusion: 全景带表示把多相机一致性问题简化为图像连续性问题，配合少步蒸馏与高斯重建，可在保证生成质量的同时大幅提速。

Abstract: Driving world models usually generate videos from six pinhole cameras with separate optical centres and small overlaps, and must learn to keep these views consistent. We present PanDWM, which stitches them into one $360^\circ$ panoramic band. Camera boundaries then fall inside one image, so keeping the views consistent means keeping the image continuous, and each pixel still records its camera ray. Conditioned on layouts, a causal student generates the band window by window in four steps; it is distilled from a causal teacher and trained on its own rollouts. A feed-forward decoder lifts each frame along these rays into 3D Gaussians, and static Gaussians from neighbouring frames complete them into 4D scenes. On nuScenes, scored in one stitched $360^\circ$ view, PanDWM has lower FID and FVD than the multi-camera baselines at $8$-$46\times$ their speed, and keeps its 50-step teacher's FID while lowering FVD by 22-25%.

</details>


### [132] [Illusion of Explainability: A Sanity Check for Spectral Band Attribution in Satellite Time-Series Crop Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19889)
*Sumit Tahiliani,Avi Mathur,Sandarbh Agarwal,Rakshith Sathish,Ashwathi Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文检验了多光谱遥感作物分类中带状特征归因的有效性，发现归因排序虽与领域知识一致且通过忠诚度测试，却不能可靠预测重训练性能，揭示谱段冗余输入空间中归因的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归因方法通常以"跨方法收敛"和"与领域知识一致"作为验证标准，但两者都无法证明被识别特征对任务是否必要。多光谱遥感中谱段信息高度重叠、单波段可被替代，使这一验证缺口尤为突出。

Method: 在PASTIS基准的Sentinel-2时间序列上训练U-TAE分割模型，用GradientSHAP、Shapley采样和Occlusion三种归因方法分析作物类型分类；进行删除/插入忠诚度测试；用归因排名高低的前五/后五波段重训练模型；分析全部252个五波段子集的有效维度。

Result: 三种方法均识别出符合领域知识的植被敏感谱区，Shapley与Occlusion在删除/插入测试中呈现强排序敏感性，但这些性质无法预测重训练表现；聚合归因掩盖了显著的类间分歧（成对平均Spearman相关0.09–0.79）；用排名最低五波段重训练的效果等于或优于最高五波段；同配置同种子独立训练的模型给出不同的前五子集且表现更优；全部252个五波段子集有效维度仅1.35–2.62，表明谱段冗余严重。

Conclusion: 合理且看似忠诚的归因排序并不必然识别出能提升重训练性能的任务相关波段，凸显了谱段冗余输入空间中波段级特征归因的固有局限。

Abstract: Attribution methods are commonly validated through convergence across methods and consistency with domain knowledge, yet neither criterion establishes whether identified features are necessary for a task. This limitation is particularly relevant in multispectral remote sensing, where overlapping spectral information makes individual bands potentially substitutable. We investigate this gap in crop-type classification using GradientSHAP, Shapley sampling, and Occlusion on a U-TAE segmentation model trained on Sentinel-2 time series from the PASTIS benchmark. All three methods identify vegetation-sensitive spectral regions consistent with domain knowledge, while Shapley and Occlusion exhibit strong ordering sensitivity in deletion and insertion tests. However, these properties do not reliably predict retraining performance. Aggregate attribution consensus masks substantial per-class disagreement, with pairwise mean Spearman correlations ranging from 0.09 to 0.79. Models retrained from scratch using the five lowest-ranked bands perform comparably to or better than those using the five highest-ranked bands. Furthermore, attribution rankings from an independently trained model with identical configuration and seed identify a different top-five subset that yields better retraining performance. Analysis of all 252 five-band subsets reveals consistently low effective dimensionality (1.35 to 2.62), suggesting substantial spectral redundancy. These findings demonstrate that plausible and apparently faithful attribution rankings do not necessarily identify task-relevant bands for improved retrained performance, highlighting the limitations of band-level feature attribution in spectrally redundant input spaces.

</details>


### [133] [HIVE-ECIA: Identity-Aware Historical Evidence Acquisition for Bandwidth-Efficient Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19897)
*Hai Nguyen,Tarik Taleb,Qize Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HIVE-ECIA：通过选择性检索历史高分辨率证据来恢复全景多目标跟踪中的碎片化身份，并提出DualPano-MOT数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景多目标跟踪需360°视野下的可靠身份关联，但带宽受限导致低分辨率身份歧义与高分辨率传输开销之间的权衡难题。

Method: 提出身份感知框架HIVE-ECIA：ECIA Base-Guided查询策略判定何时需额外HR证据；HIVE对历史LR观测排序选择信息量高的检索时间戳；ECIA Full通过门控融合与保守身份保护机制整合检索到的HR裁剪；并构建DualPano-MOT双鱼眼全景数据集（模型辅助+人工核验标注）。

Result: 在QuadTrack留出序列与零样本DualPano-MOT上，四类MOT跟踪器无需重训即实现身份切换最多下降21.8%和23.4%，IDF1最大提升3.81和6.74个百分点；离线JPEG采集模型下编码数据量较连续Full-HR采集减少89.2%和79.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法在不改动检测框与跟踪器主体的前提下，以极低带宽代价显著提升跨跟踪器的身份关联性能，具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Panoramic multi-object tracking (MOT) requires reliable identity association across a 360° field of view, yet bandwidth-constrained operation forces a trade-off between low-resolution (LR) identity ambiguity and costly high-resolution (HR) transmission. We propose HIVE-ECIA, an identity-aware framework that selectively retrieves historical HR evidence to recover fragmented identities without modifying detection boxes or the underlying tracker. An ECIA Base-Guided query policy determines when additional HR evidence is warranted; HIVE ranks historical LR observations to select an informative retrieval timestamp; and ECIA Full integrates the retrieved HR crop through learned gated fusion and conservative identity safeguards. We further introduce DualPano-MOT, a real-world dual-fisheye panoramic evaluation dataset featuring controlled pedestrian interactions, panorama-seam challenges, and occlusions, with model-assisted, manually verified tracking annotations. On held-out QuadTrack sequences and zero-shot DualPano-MOT, HIVE-ECIA improves identity association across four MOT trackers without tracker-specific retraining. Across these trackers, identity switches decrease by up to 21.8% and 23.4%, while the largest IDF1 gains reach 3.81 and 6.74 percentage points, respectively. Under an offline JPEG-based acquisition model, our framework reduces encoded data volume by 89.2% and 79.3% relative to continuous Full-HR acquisition.

</details>


### [134] [Remember, Replay, Adapt: Learning from Past Experience in Online Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19901)
*Chenhao Ding,Jizhou Han,Lixin Xiu,Muyao Wang,Songlin Dong,Hideki Nakayama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R2A，一个受人类情景记忆启发的在线VLN自适应框架，通过持续复用历史成功与失败导航经验来提升策略学习，在REVERIE、R2R、R2R-CE三个基准和五个导航骨干上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反馈的在线VLN方法主要仅从当前回合的结果中学习，此前获得的反馈经验未被充分利用，造成信息浪费。

Method: 两大模块：1）Success-Failure Memory (SFM)，保存带有反馈的成功与失败导航回合，并依据输入指令检索任务相关经验，使历史反馈引导后续导航；2）Prior-Anchored Adaptation (PAA)，以冻结的预训练策略为锚点，强化成功轨迹、抑制失败轨迹，同时正则化行为漂移；无需源域数据或专家监督即可实现持续的反馈复用与稳定策略自适应。

Result: 在REVERIE、R2R、R2R-CE三基准与五种导航骨干上实验，性能一致提升；REVERIE验证unseen划分上，DUET的SR由46.98%提升至69.84%，SPL由33.73%提升至50.98%。

Conclusion: 历史反馈的可复用记忆机制结合锚定式自适应策略，可在无源域数据、无专家监督条件下实现稳定有效的在线VLN策略改进，并具良好泛化性。

Abstract: Existing feedback-based methods for online vision-and-language navigation (VLN) primarily learn from current episode outcomes, leaving previously acquired feedback underutilized. Inspired by human episodic memory, we propose R2A, an online VLN adaptation framework that continually reuses successful and failed navigation experiences for policy learning. Specifically, Success-Failure Memory (SFM) retains successful and failed navigation episodes with acquired feedback and retrieves task-relevant experiences based on incoming instructions, enabling historical feedback to guide subsequent navigation. Prior-Anchored Adaptation (PAA) learns from these experiences by reinforcing successful trajectories and suppressing failed ones relative to a frozen pretrained policy, while regularizing behavioral drift. Together, these modules enable continual feedback reuse and stable policy adaptation without source-domain data or expert supervision. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks, REVERIE, R2R, and R2R-CE, and five navigation backbones demonstrate consistent performance gains. On the REVERIE validation unseen split, R2A improves DUET's SR from 46.98% to 69.84% and SPL from 33.73% to 50.98%.

</details>


### [135] [HEmIF-Bench: Benchmarking H&E-to-mIF Models BeyondVirtual-StainFidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19905)
*Fuxian Zhao,Hyokeun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HEmIF-Bench，将 H&E-to-mIF 生成器的"内部编码器特征"与"生成输出"解耦，在分子、细胞、组织、患者四个尺度、八个数据集上评测其下游实用性，并与冻结的病理基础模型 UNI2 比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 H&E→mIF 生成器仅以像素级标志物重建为评价标准，但像素级染色保真度并不等同于下游临床可用性；缺乏一个以临床任务为出口的系统性基准来判断生成模型的真实价值。

Method: 构建 HEmIF-Bench，对五个冻结生成器在八个数据集上评测；将内部编码器特征与生成输出分离，分别与病理基础参考模型 UNI2 对比；设置五项下游任务：外部 CD3 恢复、单细胞分类、密集细胞核分割、TLS/GC 分割、患者生存预测。

Result: 基于基础模型的 MIPHEI-ViT 在生成器中整体领先，非基础模型呈任务特异性优势；内部编码器特征普遍优于生成输出；更大的输出标志物面板并不保证更强下游效用；内部特征与生成输出均未能稳定超越冻结的 UNI2。

Conclusion: 像素级染色保真度不等于下游效用；当前配对数据规模下训练的生成器尚未超越大规模纯 H&E 基础模型。HEmIF-Bench 为追踪 H&E→mIF 生成何时真正超越常规组织学、服务于肿瘤微环境分析与精准肿瘤学提供了确定性基准。

Abstract: Hematoxylin and eosin (H&E)-to-multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF) generators predict spatial protein expression from routine histology, yet pixel-level marker reconstruction does not establish downstream utility. We present HEmIF-Bench, evaluating five frozen generators across eight datasets spanning molecular, cellular, tissue, and patient scales. HEmIF-Bench decouples internal encoder features from generated outputs, benchmarking both against the pathology foundation reference UNI2. Mirroring the clinical workflow where pathologists rely on mIF to define cell phenotypes, delineate tertiary lymphoid structures (TLSs) and germinal centers (GCs), and stratify patient risk, we evaluate external CD3 recovery, single-cell classification, dense nuclear segmentation, TLS/GC segmentation, and patient survival prediction. Across the benchmark, foundation-model-based MIPHEI-ViT leads among the generators, while non-foundation models show task-specific strengths. Comparing the two routes reveals that internal encoder features generally outperform generated outputs, and larger output marker panels do not guarantee stronger downstream utility across datasets. Moreover, neither internal encoder features nor generated outputs consistently surpass frozen UNI2. These findings show that pixel-level stain fidelity does not equate to downstream utility, and that generators trained at current paired-data scales have yet to surpass large-scale pure-H&E foundation models. With the expansion of paired translation cohorts and the advancement of foundation-based generative architectures, HEmIF-Bench establishes a definitive benchmark for tracking when H&E-to-mIF generation crosses the threshold to deliver genuine value beyond routine histology for tumor microenvironment analysis and precision oncology.

</details>


### [136] [SAGE-VQA: A Training-Free, Self-Evolving Agentic Framework with Grounded Evidence for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19933)
*Ruirui Shen,Jianqi Gao,Xiangfeng Luo,Yonggang Zhang,Muran Yu,Barney Haoyun Miao,Hang Yu,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的智能体框架 SAGE-VQA，通过"证据驱动自适应推理"策略将共验证视觉定位与自演化分层记忆结合，缓解视觉感知错误导致幻觉与跨场景泛化受限两大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识型视觉问答方法存在两个瓶颈：视觉感知错误会传播并引发幻觉；跨场景泛化能力有限。

Method: 免训练的智能体框架 SAGE-VQA，核心为证据驱动自适应推理（EDAR）策略：1）共验证视觉定位模块（Co-verified Visual Grounding）对与问题相关的细粒度视觉信息进行获取与核验，为后续推理提供有据可依的证据；2）自演化分层记忆（SHM）机制，按性质将经验组织为陈述性记忆与程序性记忆，可在后续推理中复用并依据反馈持续精化，且无需更新模型参数。

Result: 实验表明，该方法优于基于轻量级开源视觉语言模型（VLM）以及基于闭源 VLM 的对比方法；并可作为一种即插即用推理框架用于现有 VLM，无需修改其架构。

Conclusion: 通过证据核验与经验演化的结合，SAGE-VQA 在免训练条件下提升了 KB-VQA 的推理鲁棒性与泛化性，并具备良好的可移植性。

Abstract: Knowledge-based visual question answering (KB-VQA) aims to combine visual information with external knowledge to answer user questions. Existing methods face two main bottlenecks: visual perception errors can propagate into hallucinations, and generalization across scenarios remains limited. To address these issues, we propose SAGE-VQA (Self-evolving Agent framework with Grounded Evidence), a training-free agentic framework that combines evidence verification with experience evolution. Our key insight is to explore an Evidence-Driven Adaptive Reasoning (EDAR) strategy. It accurately acquires and verifies question-relevant fine-grained visual information. It also extracts and refines reusable experience to support robust reasoning across tasks. Specifically, we design a Co-verified Visual Grounding module that verifies question-relevant visual information and provides grounded evidence for subsequent reasoning. We further introduce a Self-evolving Hierarchical Memory (SHM) mechanism. SHM organizes experiences into declarative and procedural memories based on their nature. These memories can be reused in subsequent reasoning and continually refined through feedback, without updating model parameters. Experimental results show that our method outperforms competing methods based on lightweight open-source vision-language models (VLMs) as well as those based on closed-source VLMs. Moreover, our method can serve as a plug-and-play reasoning framework for existing VLMs without modifying their architectures.

</details>


### [137] [Two-Stage Prompt Learning: A Unified Framework for Person ReID without Clothing Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19939)
*Yuxuan Xiao,Shanshan Zhang,Shengcai Liao,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段提示学习框架TSPL，在VLM中统一处理同衣服、换衣服与通用三种衣服条件下的行人重识别，五个基准均达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReID方法多为SC或CC单独训练专用模型，跨条件泛化差；引入文本提示的VLM方法依赖固定预定义指令，缺乏可学习上下文嵌入，难以应对衣服变化未知的GEN场景。

Method: 统一框架，含两阶段：阶段一在冻结编码器下进行外观解耦提示学习，分离衣服与身份信息，得到可选择性保留或抑制衣服线索的稳定表征；阶段二以阶段一表征为引导，用可学习上下文嵌入训练图像编码器，实现场景感知的任务提示适配SC/CC/GEN。

Result: 在五个基准上全部场景取得SOTA，LTCC（SC）mAP最高提升9.2%，VC-Clothes（GEN）提升6.4%。

Conclusion: 衣服-身份解耦与场景感知提示相结合，可突破固定指令提示的局限，实现单一模型对SC、CC、GEN的统一鲁棒重识别。

Abstract: Person re-identification (ReID) in real-world scenarios involves three clothes conditions: the same clothes (SC), changed clothes (CC), and the unknown (GEN) scenario where clothes changes are not specified. Existing methods typically train separate SC- or CC-specific models, which limits generalization across all conditions. Meanwhile, pre-trained visual-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP have shown strong generalization ability in various vision tasks, motivating recent ReID methods to incorporate textual prompts. However, to distinguish SC and CC, prior approaches rely on fixed predefined instructions, which lack learnable context embeddings in prompts and cannot effectively handle the GEN scenario. To overcome these limitations, we propose Two-Stage Prompt Learning (TSPL), a unified framework that integrates clothes-identity decoupling and scenario-aware task prompts within VLMs. In Stage 1, appearance-decomposed prompt learning disentangles clothes and identity information under frozen encoders, producing stable embeddings where clothes cues can be selectively preserved or suppressed. In Stage 2, task-aware prompt learning trains the image encoder with learnable context embeddings guided by Stage-1 representations, enabling robust adaptation across SC, CC, and GEN conditions.
