Extensive experiments on five benchmarks show that TSPL achieves state-of-the-art performance under all scenarios, improving mAP by up to 9.2% on LTCC (SC) and by 6.4% on VC-Clothes (GEN), respectively. The code will be released at https://github.com/YuSuen/TSPL.

</details>


### [138] [FinVision: Multimodal AI for Visualizing Financial Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19956)
*Yixuan Tang,Hui Wang,Yi Yang,Allen H. Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FinVision——一个金融领域专属的多模态生成模型，将MD&A文本转化为同期股价走势图，实现"文本→视觉"的生成式财务文本分析，并验证了生成图表的经济有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本分析将财务叙述压缩为数值表征（如情感得分、词向量），可能丢失叙述中描述的动态结构信息；需要一种能保留并显式呈现"动态性"的表征方式。

Method: 构建87,940对MD&A披露与同期股价走势图（chart）配对样本，训练领域适配的多模态生成模型，学习"财年经营叙述—同期股价动态"的平均映射；输入单一MD&A即可生成文本隐含的股价图。评估采用留出集对比（vs. 直接LLM提示、通用文生图模型、无领域适配的同架构）及经济有效性检验。

Result: 留出集上生成图表与真实图表高度相似，并优于LLM直接提示、通用文生图模型和无领域适配基线；文本隐含图与真实图的差异（discrepancy）与后续10-K窗口期投资者反应更弱、以及更差的市场与经营表现系统性相关。

Conclusion: 领域适配的多模态生成AI可为财务文本创造一种新的视觉表征，作为研究企业沟通的额外模态，拓展了财务文本分析从数值化走向生成式视觉化的路径。

Abstract: Financial narratives contain rich information about firms' performance and business conditions. Conventional text analysis often summarizes this information into numerical representations, which may not fully preserve the dynamic structure described in the narrative. In this study, we develop FinVision, a domain-specific multimodal generative model that translates financial narratives into visual representations of contemporaneous market dynamics. Using 87,940 paired observations of Management Discussion and Analysis (MD&A) disclosures and stock price charts, FinVision learns the average relationship between a firm's narrative account of a fiscal year and its stock-price dynamics over the same period. Given an MD&A alone, the trained model generates a text-implied stock chart, extending financial text analysis beyond numerical representations toward text-to-visual generation. We validate FinVision in two ways. First, held-out tests show that the model generates stock-chart representations that closely resemble observed charts and outperform direct LLM prompting, general-purpose text-to-image models, and the same architecture without financial-domain adaptation. Second, we examine the economic validity of the generated representations by comparing text-implied and observed stock charts. The resulting discrepancy is systematically associated with weaker investor responses around subsequent 10-K filings and with worse subsequent market and operating outcomes. Overall, our findings show how domain-adapted multimodal generative AI can create a new visual representation of financial text and provide an additional modality for studying business communication.

</details>


### [139] [Multimodal AI in Healthcare: Review of Vision-Language Foundation Models for Real-World Medical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19958)
*Taha Razzaq,Murtaza Taj,Asim Iqbal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于医学视觉语言模型（Medical VLM）的综述，提出了一套系统性分类法，梳理从对比对齐、生成式多模态大模型到密集像素定位、稀疏混合专家（MoE）及推理激励（RL）架构的演进脉络，并批判性剖析"医学瓶颈"：数据稀缺、生成式诊断的幻觉风险、3D体积处理的计算负担以及缺乏标准化临床评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的零样本泛化能力及多模态数据（图像-文本配对）的利用，可降低医疗领域对大量人工标注的依赖，并有望在数据受限场景下支撑诊断、治疗规划与患者照护。然而医学VLM领域术语与方法纷杂、缺乏统一框架，且存在数据、幻觉、算力与评估等系统性障碍，亟需综述性梳理。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，构建医学VLM的权威分类体系（taxonomy），按技术演进阶段组织：早期对比对齐（Contrastive Alignment）→ 生成式多模态大模型（Generative MLLMs）→ 前沿方向（密集像素定位 Dense Pixel Grounding、稀疏混合专家 Sparse MoE、推理激励 RL 架构）；并对"医学瓶颈"进行批判性分析。

Result: 给出医学VLM领域从对比对齐到推理激励架构的完整演进图谱与分类框架，并识别出四类持续性挑战：数据稀缺、生成式诊断中的幻觉风险、3D体积数据处理的计算开销、以及缺乏标准化且具临床依据的评估指标。

Conclusion: 医学VLM已在图像解读、报告生成、问答等任务展现潜力，但"医学瓶颈"尚未解决；未来需在数据效率、幻觉抑制、3D高效计算与临床可信评估体系方面取得突破，才能实现真正的临床落地。

Abstract: The emergence of foundation models has marked a transformative shift in AI, enabling robust generalization across diverse downstream tasks through putative zero-shot learning. Large Language Models and Vision Language Models have demonstrated strong capabilities in tasks such as image interpretation, report generation, and question answering by effectively learning from multimodal data-images paired with associated text often with minimal supervision. In the healthcare domain, this ability to align visual and textual information reduces the reliance on extensive manual annotations, as models can leverage existing clinical reports and imaging data to learn meaningful representations. This integration holds promise for improving diagnostic support, treatment planning, and overall patient care, even in data constrained settings. In this review, we provide a definitive taxonomy of the medical VLM landscape, tracing the evolution from early Contrastive Alignment and Generative MLLMs to the cutting edge frontiers of Dense Pixel Grounding, Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), and Reasoning Incentivized (RL) architectures. We critically examine the "medical bottleneck" identifying the persistent challenges of data scarcity, the "hallucination" risks in generative diagnostics, the computational strain of 3D volumetric processing, and the lack of standardized, clinically grounded evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [140] [CreaTrace: Video Creative Plagiarism Detection and Evidence Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19976)
*Chang Liu,Sheng Li,Zhenxing Qian,Xinpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出新任务视频创意抄袭检测（VCPD）及迭代式创意证据溯源框架CreaTrace，实现创意相似性判定与证据片段定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频拷贝检测方法聚焦视觉内容相似度，难以捕捉与具体视觉实现纠缠的抽象创意；而视频创意抄袭在不改变视觉外观的情况下盗用创意，构成版权保护新挑战。

Method: 设计多模态语义事件分割策略，将相似长视频切分为连贯叙事事件以构建事件级数据集并检索相似片段；采用粗粒度创意匹配，基于叙事语义与镜头语言筛选候选事件对；提出创意解耦，从具体视觉实现中分离抽象创意模式；引入创意交换对比学习，通过跨实现的特征交换增强创意一致性。

Result: 在多种创意抄袭场景下实验表明，CreaTrace可准确检测创意相似性并实现时间定位，同时输出可解释的证据报告，具有有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CreaTrace通过事件分割、粗匹配、创意解耦与交换对比学习的迭代流程，突破视觉相似度局限，为视频创意抄袭检测提供了可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Video creative plagiarism refers to the unauthorized reproduction of creative ideas across videos despite substantial differences in visual appearance, posing a new challenge to video copyright protection. However, existing video copy detection methods primarily focus on visual content similarity, struggling to capture abstract creative that are entangled with specific visual realizations. To address this issue, we introduce a novel task, named Video Creative Plagiarism Detection (VCPD), and propose an iterative creative evidence tracing framework, termed CreaTrace, to verify creative similarity and accurately localize the corresponding evidence segments. Specifically, we design a Multimodal Semantic Event Segmentation strategy to divide similar long videos into coherent narrative events, facilitating event-level dataset construction and retrieving similar segments. Based on these events, CreaTrace employs Coarse Creative Matching to identify candidate event pairs through narrative semantics and camera language. Then CreaTrace presents Creative Decoupling to disentangle abstract creative patterns from specific visual realizations. Meanwhile, Creative Swap Contrastive Learning is devised to enhance creative consistency through cross-realization feature swapping. Benefiting from the above designs, our CreaTrace enables accurate creative similarity detection and temporal localization, while providing interpretable evidence report. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of CreaTrace across diverse creative plagiarism scenarios.

</details>


### [141] [Morphology-Decoupled Segmentation for Multiple Tunnel Defects Under Complex Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19981)
*Mohan Ren,Hang Li,Yifei Liu,Shaowei Wang,James O'Kane,Xuanzhi Wang,Shiyu Zhao,Yumeng Zhang,Songtao Ji,Jurij Karlovsek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出形态解耦分割（MDS）框架，按缺陷形态（线状裂缝 vs 面状渗漏/脱落/破损）路由特征并分别解码，在隧道衬砌多缺陷分割中取得81.17% mIoU，隐藏测试67.55% mIoU并获第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隧道衬砌自动检测需同时分割形态差异极大的缺陷：裂缝细长、对连通性敏感；渗漏、衬砌脱落、管片破损则空间延展且依赖上下文。现有方法或用统一解码器处理所有类别，或将边界、拓扑、方向机制作为孤立增强，难以兼顾不同缺陷的形态特性。

Method: 以缺陷形态作为特征路由、解码与交互的组织原则：1）监督式路由模块将多尺度编码器特征划分为互补的线状流与面状流；2）线状流采用可变形条带注意力（Deformable Strip Attention），以图像条件化方向假设保持裂缝连续性，不预设水平/垂直方向；3）面状流结合金字塔池化与特征金字塔融合，捕获弥散边界与区域上下文；4）通过门控跨形态注意力耦合两流表示，在交换互补信息的同时保留形态专属特征；5）采用分辨率自适应切块与概率融合流程，在变分辨率影像中保留细小缺陷；6）采用选择性动态INT8量化压缩模型。

Result: 内部验证集达81.17% mIoU；相比主干匹配的SegDINO，前景图像级识别提升，主要体现在面状缺陷。消融实验验证路由监督与跨流交互的互补贡献，同时揭示面状类别性能与裂缝专精之间的权衡。组织方隐藏测试达67.55% mIoU，团队排名第二。选择性动态INT8量化使模型存储缩小2.83倍且精度损失可忽略。

Conclusion: 形态驱动的解耦式特征路由与解码能有效适配隧道衬砌多类缺陷的异构几何特性，配合跨形态交互与分辨率自适应推理可在竞赛级隐藏测试中取得领先表现，并具备良好的部署效率。

Abstract: Automated tunnel-lining inspection must segment different defect geometries: cracks are thin, elongated, and connectivity-sensitive, whereas leakage, lining falling off, and segment damage are spatially extensive and context-dependent. Existing approaches generally apply a common decoder across defect classes or introduce boundary, topology, and directional mechanisms as isolated enhancements. This paper introduces Morphology-Decoupled Segmentation (MDS), in which defect morphology becomes the organising principle for feature routing, decoding, and interaction. A supervised routing module partitions multiscale encoder features into complementary linear and areal streams. The linear stream employs Deformable Strip Attention with image-conditioned direction hypotheses to preserve crack continuity without assuming fixed horizontal or vertical orientations, while the areal stream combines Pyramid Pooling with feature-pyramid fusion to capture diffuse boundaries and regional context. The representations are coupled through gated cross-morphology attention, allowing complementary information exchange while retaining morphology-specific features. A resolution-adaptive tiling and probability-blending pipeline preserves fine defects across variable-resolution imagery. MDS achieves 81.17% mIoU on the internal validation set and improves foreground image-level recognition over a backbone-matched SegDINO model, primarily for areal defects. Controlled ablations demonstrate the complementary contributions of routing supervision and cross-stream interaction while revealing a trade-off between areal-class performance and crack specialisation. The submitted model achieved 67.55% mIoU on an organiser-controlled hidden test, and the team placed second. Selective dynamic INT8 quantisation reduced model storage by 2.83 times with negligible accuracy loss.

</details>


### [142] [KneeFS-Net: A Lightweight Deep Learning Model for Knee Osteoarthritis Severity Assessment with Cross-Domain External Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19988)
*Shafqat Alam,Galib Anjum Talukder Mahi,Yeasir Rabbani Tanvir,Md. Ibrahim,Md. Shahed Hossain,Shashank Ghimire,Mahmud Hasan Mostofa Kamal,ASM Shahidul Hossain,Taufiq Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级有序分级网络KneeFS-Net（0.8M参数、0.33 GFLOPs），融合空间、傅里叶与状态空间特征及关键点引导注意力，在OAI上QWK 0.863，并验证跨人群泛化与少标注自监督适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于X光片的Kellgren-Lawrence（KL）膝骨关节炎分级依赖专业判读能力，而此类专家资源往往稀缺，需要准确且高效的自动化分级方法，并考察其在新人群中的可迁移性。

Method: 构建轻量级有序分级器KneeFS-Net，融合空间特征、傅里叶特征与状态空间特征，并引入关键点引导注意力；在OAI数据集上训练与测试，在孟加拉国单中心医院队列和多中心远程放射队列上做零样本跨人群评估；进一步用6,092张无标注X光片做自监督预训练后联合微调。

Result: OAI测试集：QWK 0.863、MAE 0.325、分级误差≤1级准确率97.2%，与参数量多9–35倍的模型相当。零样本跨人群：医院队列QWK 0.803、MAE 0.339、≤1级准确率98.2%；多中心远程放射影像下降至QWK 0.656、MAE 0.522。自监督预训练+联合微调交叉验证：QWK 0.823、MAE 0.329、≤1级准确率99.7%。

Conclusion: KneeFS-Net以极低计算代价实现了准确的KL有序分级，并在有限标注条件下展现出向新人群适配的潜力；但多中心、成像条件差异较大的数据仍是性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Knee osteoarthritis (OA) grading from radiographs using the Kellgren-Lawrence (KL) scale requires expertise that may be scarce. We present KneeFS-Net, a lightweight ordinal grader (0.8 million parameters; 0.33 GFLOPs) combining spatial, Fourier and state-space features with landmark-guided attention. On the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI) test set, KneeFS-Net achieved a quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) of 0.863, mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.325 and 97.2% within-one-grade accuracy, comparable to models with 9-35-fold more parameters. We evaluated cross-population generalization on two Bangladeshi cohorts, a single-center hospital cohort and a multi-center teleradiology cohort. Without further training or adaptation, the OAI-trained model achieved QWK 0.803, MAE 0.339 and 98.2% within-one-grade accuracy on its hospital cohort, declining to QWK 0.656 and MAE 0.522 on multi-center teleradiology images. Self-supervised pretraining on 6,092 unlabeled radiographs followed by joint fine-tuning achieved QWK 0.823, MAE 0.329 and 99.7% within-one-grade accuracy in cross-validation. These findings demonstrate accurate, efficient KL grading and potential adaptation to new populations with limited annotation.

</details>


### [143] [What Matters for Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20000)
*Yipeng Sun,Jiajun Wang,Siyuan Mei,Yan Xia,Siming Bayer,Vincent Christlein,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文检验零配对跨模态对齐的可迁移性：仅COCO成功，其余四种模态失败；揭示对齐需"先定位共变子空间再定向映射"，据此提出少量配对下的LightAlign方法，在3D CT上超越端到端模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Platonic Representation Hypothesis认为不同模态训练的模型趋于共享表示，已有工作在自然图像上实现冻结图文编码器的零配对对齐。但该方法是否可推广到其他模态尚不清楚，且医疗影像被认为是最难对齐的场景，需要厘清失败原因并给出可行的替代方案。

Method: 在四种模态（自然图像、环境音频、胸片、3D CT）、五个数据集上开展零配对对齐实验并分析失败原因，将对齐分解为"定位共变子空间"与"在该子空间内定向映射"两步，并用随机矩阵理论预测所需配对数量。提出LightAlign：利用少量配对在两个冻结编码器间做岭回归，同时完成子空间定位与映射定向，再用仅基于报告文本训练的分类器从映射后的图像预测所见。

Result: 零配对对齐仅在COCO成功，其余四个数据集失败；医疗影像的共享子空间所载方差比例最小。LightAlign在3D CT上优于未使用finding级标签、以图文配对端到端训练的模型：仅200对即超过CT-CLIP（CT-RATE AUROC 0.754 vs. 0.733）；仅100个CT-RATE对即在跨医院OOD数据集RAD-ChestCT上超越；在CheXpert上以约5%的MIMIC-CXR配对量达到0.862平均AUROC。

Conclusion: 零配对对齐的可行性取决于共享子空间的方差占比；当该比例偏低时，必须借助少量配对。LightAlign以极低配对成本实现跨模态与跨机构的稳健表现，说明"少量配对+冻结编码器+岭回归"是医疗影像等难对齐模态的高效范式。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis posits that models trained on different modalities converge toward a shared representation. Related work aligns frozen image and text encoders on natural images without paired data. We test zero-pair alignment on five datasets across four modalities: natural images, environmental audio, chest radiographs, and 3D computed tomography (CT). It succeeds only on COCO and fails on the other four. We analyze the failures and find that alignment must first locate the subspace where the modalities co-vary and then orient the mapping within it. Consistent with previous studies, medical imaging is the hardest case: its shared subspace carries the smallest fraction of the variance. When this fraction is small, alignment needs image-text pairs, and random-matrix theory predicts how many pairs are needed. These findings motivate LightAlign: a few pairs let a ridge regression between two frozen encoders locate and orient the mapping simultaneously, and a classifier fitted only on report text then predicts findings from the mapped images. On 3D CT, LightAlign surpasses models trained end to end on image-report pairs without finding-level labels: with only 200 pairs, LightAlign outperforms CT-CLIP on CT-RATE (0.754 vs. 0.733 AUROC), and with only 100 CT-RATE pairs, it does so on RAD-ChestCT, an out-of-distribution dataset from a different hospital. On CheXpert, it reaches 0.862 mean AUROC with only about 5% as many pairs as models trained on MIMIC-CXR.

</details>


### [144] [Glance, then Select: Evidence-Tempered Visual Token Selection for Extremely Compressed Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20006)
*Yuxuan Wang,Guangxiang Zhao,Yuxuan Tian,Yahui Han,Xinyu Tan,Feiyu Wang,Zongwei Lv,Xiangzheng Zhang,Lin Sun,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Glance-ETD两阶段视觉token缩减框架，在仅保留5%视觉token的情况下保持91.6%的全token性能，并显著加速首token时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型将高分辨率图像编码为数千视觉token，主导prefill开销。现有视觉token缩减方法在极致预算下仍有两大问题：视觉编码器仍需处理全部图像patch；保留的少量token常遗漏承载答案的小区域。

Method: 以轻量问题条件打分器引导的两阶段框架。第一阶段"glance encoder"先低分辨率浏览全图，仅对问题相关区域按原生分辨率重编码；第二阶段Evidence-Tempered Diversity（ETD）将多样性与打分器预测的证据结合，选出覆盖场景且保留答案的最终token。采用单次rollout自蒸馏训练该MLLM。

Result: 在14个基准上，仅用5%视觉token保留Qwen3.5-4B全token性能的91.6%；比最强先前方法LT-OPD高17.3分，比同等训练配方的DivPrune高6.0分；4K图像首token时间加速3.1倍；可泛化至另外三个MLLM及高分辨率文档基准，文本密集任务提升最大。

Conclusion: Glance-ETD通过问题引导的粗看+精看重编码与证据增强的多样性选择，在极端token预算下同时保证覆盖性与答案保留，兼顾效率与性能，并具良好泛化性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) encode a high-resolution image into thousands of visual tokens, which dominate the cost of the prefill stage. Visual token reduction removes most of these tokens, but two problems remain at extreme budgets. The vision encoder still processes every patch of the image, and the few surviving tokens often miss the small regions that hold the answer. In this paper, we propose Glance-ETD, a two-stage token reduction framework guided by a lightweight question-conditioned scorer. In the first stage, a glance encoder views the image at low resolution and re-encodes only the question-relevant regions at native resolution. In the second stage, Evidence-Tempered Diversity (ETD) selects the final tokens by combining diversity with the evidence predicted by the scorer, so that the kept tokens cover the scene and retain the answer. A single-rollout self-distillation recipe trains the MLLM with the glance encoder. With only 5% of the visual tokens, Glance-ETD retains 91.6% of the full-token performance of Qwen3.5-4B across fourteen benchmarks and outperforms the strongest prior method, LT-OPD, by 17.3 points and the same training recipe with DivPrune by 6.0. It also speeds up the time to first token on 4K images by 3.1x and generalizes to three other MLLMs and to high-resolution document benchmarks, with the largest gains on text-rich tasks.

</details>


### [145] [ReactBench-AD: Do Driving VLAs and World-Action Models React to Imminent Hazards in Their Path?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20011)
*Nikos Theodoridis,Reenu Mohandas,Sushil Sharma,Ciarán Hogan,Ganesh Sistu,Anthony Scanlan,Tim Brophy,Ciarán Eising*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ReactBench-AD 基准，用像素级反事实配对场景检验驾驶模型对路径上突然出现物体（穿行/站立行人、静止车辆）的反应能力。评测四个 VLA 与两个 WAM，发现它们反应微弱甚至几乎无反应；但对 ReCogDrive 做短时微调即可显著改善硬刹车行为，并泛化到未见物体与真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 与 WAM 被宣称对长尾场景具备强泛化能力，但现有自动驾驶基准以常规（nominal）场景为主，无法验证这一承诺；尤其缺乏对安全性至关重要的"对路径内出现物体的反应"这一长尾能力的检验手段。

Method: 构建 ReactBench-AD：基于真实 NAVSIM 与 PhysicalAI-Autonomous-Vehicles 场景，将穿行行人、站立行人或静止车辆在所有相机视角与历史帧中一致地合成插入，使每个编辑场景与原始场景构成像素级精确反事实对，从而可隔离插入物体对预测轨迹的因果效应。评测四个 VLA 与两个 WAM；并对 ReCogDrive 在编辑场景上进行短时微调，测试其学习硬刹车行为的效果与泛化性。

Result: 六个模型（4 VLA + 2 WAM）对插入的障碍物反应微弱，常常几乎不反应。经短时微调后，ReCogDrive 的反应显著改善，所学行为能泛化到未见过的物体类别和完全真实的场景，同时对常规驾驶性能的代价很小。

Conclusion: 现有 VLA/WAM 的开箱即用长尾能力存疑，但其可作为习得此类能力的强基础，仅需少量训练；凸显了展示长尾行为的真实或合成数据的价值。代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) and World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged in autonomous driving with the promise of strong generalization to long-tail scenarios. Although these models achieve state-of-the-art results on autonomous driving benchmarks, most existing benchmarks are dominated by nominal scenarios and therefore cannot test this promise. To test one such long-tail capability that is critical for safety, we introduce ReactBench-AD, a benchmark that measures how driving models react to objects that appear in the ego vehicle's path. Built on real NAVSIM and PhysicalAI-Autonomous-Vehicles scenes, ReactBench-AD composites a crossing pedestrian, a standing pedestrian, or a stationary car consistently across all camera views and history frames. Each edited scene thus forms an exact, pixel-level counterfactual pair with its original, which allows us to isolate the causal effect of the inserted object on the predicted trajectory. Evaluating four VLAs and two WAMs, we find that they react weakly, often barely at all. Finally, we test how easily one of the evaluated VLAs, ReCogDrive, can be taught to brake hard for imminent hazards: a short fine-tuning stage on edited scenes substantially improves its reactions, with the learned behavior generalizing to unseen objects and fully real scenes, at minimal cost to nominal driving. These results call into question the out-of-the-box long-tail capabilities of these models, but suggest that they can be a strong foundation for acquiring them with little additional training, highlighting the value of real or synthetic data that demonstrates the long-tail behaviors nominal datasets rarely contain. Code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [146] [FastGRN: Few-Step Generative Refinement Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20021)
*Haoyang Hu,Masataka Seo,Liang Li,Yen-wei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出少步生成精炼网络（FastGRN），通过在自身样本分布上做离散token分布匹配训练，并将精炼过程拆为过采样、非均匀采样与精炼置信度保持三部分，实现少步高质量生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成精炼网络（GRN）虽高保真，但需数十步马尔可夫迭代，推理成本高；直接蒸馏为少步模型存在各步信息量与修复负荷不均的问题。

Method: ①利用GRN自身的均匀状态损坏对离散token做分布匹配，使离散生成器可通过反向传播学习；②以rectified flow方式将噪声与样本配对；③构建三部分少步GRN：过采样（给学生更多步并精炼其判别器）、非均匀采样（仅训练最后一步并赋予其大部分信息）、精炼置信度保持（保留学生已确定的token）。

Result: 在ImageNet 256上，3步FastGRN的FID优于原GRN；在2B二值token文生图GRN上，4步学生模型GenEval接近GRN-T2I 2B。

Conclusion: 少步GRN可显著降低采样步数而保持甚至提升生成质量，表明离散精炼步之间信息不均衡的建模是关键，方法可扩展至大规模文生图模型。

Abstract: Generative Refinement Networks (GRN) generate an image by repeatedly re-predicting and refining every token of a discrete latent. They reach high fidelity, but need dozens of Markov steps. We therefore train few-step GRNs on the distribution of their own samples. We use its own uniform-state corruption to perform distribution matching on discrete tokens, which lets a discrete generator learn by backpropagation, and we pair noise with samples in the manner of rectified flow. We find that the steps of a few-step GRN do not carry the same amount of information or the same repair load. We therefore exploit its discrete refinement during training and build few-step GRNs in three parts. Oversampling samples the student with more steps than it was trained for and refines its judge on these samples. Non-uniform sampling trains only the last step and hands it most of the information at sampling time. Refinement confidence keeping holds on to the tokens the student is already sure of. We train a fast class-conditional generator on ImageNet 256 and, with the same method, a 2B text-to-image model. On ImageNet 256, the 3-step FastGRN beats the FID of GRN. On a 2B binary-token text-to-image GRN, a 4-step student also nearly matches the GenEval of GRN-T2I 2B.

</details>


### [147] [acuSimNet: Multi-View Self-Occlusion-Awared Visibility Learning for Cranio-Cervical Acupuncture Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20025)
*Jiatao Ma,Qilei Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出acuSimNet——一种高效的分层多任务学习架构，用于多视角、自遮挡感知的穴位可见性预测，验证集准确率达99.97%，并显著加快收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穴位由抽象原则而非明确解剖标志定义，定位困难；现有方法多局限于单视角或依赖相对其他标志的间接位置计算，忽略人体解剖差异。同时，穴位可见性（是否被人体自身、毛发、衣物或其他物体遮挡）评估在实际应用中关注不足。穴位定位本身无需三维重建，但推断其与解剖表面的遮挡关系却需要，带来计算开销并限制实时推理。

Method: 提出acuSimNet分层多任务学习架构，实现多视角、自遮挡感知的穴位可见性预测；针对174个颈颅区域穴位的高维分类、可见穴位负收敛问题及任务间调度优化进行了专门设计。

Result: 验证集准确率达99.97%；训练效率大幅提升：现有方法需3000个epoch达到99%验证准确率，而优化框架仅39个epoch达90%、86个epoch达99%。

Conclusion: 该架构克服了现有方法的局限，可支撑穴位检测与可视化的实际应用，推动中医自动化发展。

Abstract: The localization of acupuncture points (acupoints) in Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) presents unique challenges since they are defined by abstract principles rather than distinct anatomical landmarks. Existing approaches are typically constrained to single view or rely on indirect calculations of relative positions with respect to other landmarks, thereby overlooking human anatomical variations. Furthermore, acupoint visibility assessment, which determins whether points are occluded by human itself, hair, clothing, or other objects, has received limited attention in practical applications. Acupoint localization itself does not require 3D reconstruction, but inferring their occlusion relationships with anatomical surfaces does, which adds computational cost and limits real-time inference. In this work, we introduce acuSimNet, an efficient hierarchical multi-task learning architecture for multi-view, self-occlusion-aware visibility prediction of acupoints, achieving 99.97% accuracy on the validation set. Our approach also addresses the challenges of high-dimensional classification (174 acupoints in cervicocranial region), negative convergence issues for visible acupoints, and inter-task scheduling optimization, resulting in substantially accelerated convergence. We improved the training efficiency from the exisiting methods of 3000 epochs to achieve 99% validation accuracy, to our optimized framework achieving 90% accuracy in 39 epochs and 99% accuracy in 86 epochs. This architecture overcomes the limitations of existing methods, could enable practical applications in acupoints detection and visualization, advancing the automation of TCM.

</details>


### [148] [MARLIN: Multiscale And Directional Sparse Representation Learning for Imbalanced SAR Ship Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20065)
*Benyamin Hosseiny,Kamirul Kamirul,Odysseas Pappas,Alin Achim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARLIN：面向SAR舰船类型分类的类频感知框架，采用方向稀疏卷积主干与分层多尺度特征提取，并设计类频加权目标函数以应对类别不平衡与细粒度识别难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR海事监测中舰船分类受限于标注数据稀缺、类别不平衡、类内差异大、类间细粒度相似，以及目标尺度、朝向和散射结构的变化。

Method: 对每幅图像构建两种互补表示并由并行编码分支处理；每个分支以方向稀疏卷积stem捕获细长且依赖朝向的舰船散射模式，后接定制化分层多尺度特征提取模块；提出类频感知目标函数，将margin分类损失与类频加权原型紧致项、类对频率加权margin分离项相结合。

Result: 在三个互补数据集上的大量实验表明，MARLIN在轻量、参数高效的条件下同时提升总体精度与类别均衡指标。

Conclusion: 该框架可有效应对有限且不平衡训练条件下的SAR舰船类型分类，具有鲁棒性与实用性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in SAR maritime monitoring, ship classification remains challenging due to limited labeled data, class imbalance, large intra-class variability, fine-grained inter-class similarity, and variations in target scale, orientation, and backscattering structure. To address these challenges, this paper proposes MARLIN, a class-frequency-aware framework with directional sparse and hierarchical multi-scale feature extraction for ship type classification in SAR images. The proposed architecture first constructs two complementary representations of each input image, where each views are processed by parallel encoder branches. Each branch begins with a directional sparse convolutional stem designed to capture elongated and orientation-dependent ship scattering patterns, followed by customized hierarchical multiscale feature extraction blocks. To improve learning under imbalanced class distributions, we introduce a class-frequency aware objective. The proposed objective combines margin-based classification with class-frequency-weighted prototype compactness and class-pair frequency weighted margin separation. This formulation shapes the embedding space by encouraging samples from the same class to form compact clusters while enforcing stronger separation for class pairs involving underrepresented ship categories. Extensive experiments on three complementary datasets demonstrate that in addition to being lightweight and parameter efficient, MARLIN improves both overall accuracy and class-balanced metrics, confirming its effectiveness for robust SAR ship-type classification under limited and imbalanced training conditions. The codes are available at github.com/benyaminhosseiny/marlin.

</details>


### [149] [SGIP: Learning Scene-Grounded Interaction Priors for Scene-Aware Human Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20076)
*Donghyeon Soon,Jiil Yoon,Hyeondong Kim,Daehee Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGIP框架，显式预测未来运动在场景中的落点（物体与可行走区域），并用该"场景落地交互先验"条件化轨迹与全身姿态预测；先验通过意图蒸馏获得——训练时用LLM+几何特征的教师模型打分，测试时仅需学生模型从观测运动与3D场景复制分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体运动预测（HMP）中，杂乱场景下未来运动的"目的地"本质模糊：相同观测运动可对应不同交互目标或导航模式。现有方法或隐式处理该歧义，或依赖视线（gaze）等实际难获取的辅助信号。

Method: 1）显式预测未来运动在场景中的落点（跨物体与开放导航区域），作为条件输入用于轨迹与全身姿态预测；2）意图蒸馏：训练阶段由教师模型（LLM接收人物意图文本描述 + 几何运动-场景特征）对候选场景区域打分，学生模型仅从观测运动与3D场景回归这些分数；3）推理阶段不需要意图、LLM与视线。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，于推理时既无视线也无意图的真实部署设定下，SGIP在轨迹精度与姿态精度上均优于此前场景感知基线。

Conclusion: 显式建模场景落地交互先验并借助意图蒸馏将其内化到推理可用输入中，是缓解HMP目的地歧义的有效途径。

Abstract: Human motion prediction (HMP) aims to forecast future movement from an observed motion sequence, and recent methods have incorporated 3D scene geometry and object semantics to improve long-term plausibility. However, the destination of future motion remains ambiguous in cluttered scenes: the same observed motion can lead to different interaction targets or navigation modes. Existing methods either handle this ambiguity implicitly or rely on auxiliary signals like gaze that are hard to obtain in practice. To address this, we propose SGIP, which explicitly predicts where in the scene future motion will be grounded across both objects and open navigation regions, and uses it to condition future trajectory and full-body pose prediction. This scene-grounded interaction prior is learned via intent distillation: at training time only, a teacher model scores candidate scene regions by combining an LLM prompted with a textual description of the person's intent with geometric motion-scene features. A student model learns to reproduce these scores from only the observed motion and 3D scene, making intent and LLM unnecessary at test time. Across multiple benchmark datasets, SGIP outperforms prior scene-aware baselines on both trajectory and pose accuracy in a realistic deployment setting where neither gaze nor intent is available at inference, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework. The project page is available at https://sgip2026.github.io.

</details>


### [150] [See the Lives of Stars in a Handful of Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20089)
*Shuhong Liu,Gengjia Chang,Quanfeng Xu,Ziteng Cui,Qianyu Zhou,Xiaoyu Dong,Lin Gu,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Cosmos4D，用"引力 4D 高斯泼溅"与"亮度分层动力学"从多视角时序图像重建星系四维演化，并构建 8 个星系、4 套宇宙学模拟的 4D 数据集，在光度质量与物理准确性上优于动态高斯基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系演化跨越数亿年，观测无法直接"观看"其运动，宇宙学模拟仅以多视角图像序列呈现，因而产生"能否从图像中恢复四维星系"的问题。现有动态场景方法在此失效：数百万恒星每颗仅占几像素、暗弱星族混入弥散光、自由形变虽能拟合图像却不遵循引力。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(1) 引力 4D 高斯泼溅——每颗恒星表示为自发光高斯，其运动经由引力势与所有恒星共享的低维力场积分得到；(2) 亮度分层动力学——亮星赋予个体轨道并在多图像尺度上监督，暗弱星族由光流估计的速度场驱动。

Result: 构建了来自 4 套宇宙学模拟、共 8 个星系的 4D 数据集；在该数据集上，Cosmos4D 在光度质量与物理准确性两方面均优于动态高斯基线，并以 96 帧呈现星系约 8 亿年的生命历程。

Conclusion: 将物理先验（引力势与共享力场）与分层动力学引入动态高斯表示，可在难以直接观测的时间尺度上实现兼顾真实感与物理一致性的四维星系重建，为从图像反演宇宙学演化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Galaxies evolve over hundreds of millions of years, and no telescope has ever watched one move. Cosmological simulations make this evolution visible as a sequence of multi-view images, inviting the question of whether a galaxy can be recovered in four dimensions from its images. This setting defeats existing dynamic scene methods, since millions of stars span only a few pixels each, faint populations blend into diffuse light, and free deformations reproduce the images without obeying gravity. We present Cosmos4D, which rests on two components. Gravitational 4D Gaussian Splatting represents each star as a self-luminous Gaussian whose motion is integrated through a gravitational potential and a low-dimensional force field shared by all stars. Luminosity-stratified dynamics give bright stars individual orbits supervised across image scales and carry faint populations with a velocity field estimated from optical flow. We further build a 4D dataset of eight galaxies from four cosmological simulations, on which Cosmos4D outperforms dynamic Gaussian baselines in both photometric quality and physical accuracy. With these advancements, we glimpse eight hundred million years of a galaxy's life in ninety-six frames.

</details>


### [151] [Latent World Modeling for Point-Cloud Video Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20090)
*Zhuoyue Zhang,Jian Liu,Jihua Zhu,Ajmal Saeed Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PointWM，一种点云视频潜在世界模型，通过仅用历史上下文预测未来帧的特征与几何，消除掩码建模中的信息泄漏，将"补全"任务转变为"预测"任务，在HOI4D动作分割上显著提升F1@50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点云视频掩码自监督范式（MAE式重建或潜在特征预测）存在信息泄漏：点云token锚定在采样点上，在目标自身位置查询会直接暴露其几何移动位置；随机时空掩码使目标两侧帧可见，使任务退化为插值补全而非预测。剥离目标位置后，掩码潜在预测器仅略优于复制上一帧。

Method: PointWM为潜在世界模型：仅以严格更早的上下文为输入，预测未来帧的特征与几何；每个目标只用其最后观测到的位置与时间戳进行查询；并引入无需标注学习的潜在动作（latent action），用于刻画过去无法确定的那部分未来。

Result: 在HOI4D动作分割任务上，采用P4Transformer骨干，PointWM相较从头训练在离线设置下F1@50提升24.9，在严格因果推理下提升29.1。

Conclusion: 结果表明潜在世界模型可作为点云视频无泄漏预训练范式，具有良好前景；代码已开源。

Abstract: Masked modeling is the dominant self-supervised paradigm for point-cloud videos. However, it leaks the information it is meant to predict, whether it reconstructs masked points as in MAE-style methods or predicts their latent features. Because point-cloud tokens are anchored at points sampled from each frame, querying a target at its own position reveals where its geometry has moved, in reconstruction and latent prediction alike. For latent predictors, random spatio-temporal masks introduce a further shortcut, as visible frames on both sides of each target allow interpolation rather than forecasting. The pretext task thus reduces to completion. Once the target positions are withheld, a masked latent predictor performs only slightly better than copying the last frame. To remove this leakage, we propose PointWM, a latent world model that turns completion into forecasting by predicting the features and geometry of future frames from strictly earlier context. Each target is queried only with its last-seen position and timestamp, while a latent action learned without annotations is meant to capture the part of the future that the past leaves undetermined. On HOI4D action segmentation with a P4Transformer backbone, PointWM improves F1@50 over training from scratch by 24.9 offline and 29.1 under strictly causal inference. These results show the promise of latent world models as a leakage-free pretraining paradigm for point-cloud videos.Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/InitalZ/PointWM.

</details>


### [152] [Geo2Gcode: From 3D Geometry to CNC Programs through Structured Machining Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20092)
*Sangmin Hong,Dohee Cho,Mohsen Yavartanoo,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Geo2Gcode 提出一种从三维网格直接生成标准 G-code 的结构化方法：以“加工基元”（索引化加工方向 + 逐视角闭合重建 + 刀半径/层高/清料模式参数）为接口，通过符号距离查询确定性地编译为分层刀路，并用体素毛坯仿真器训练的轻量选择器自动填参，避免逐件人工 CAM 调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 3D 形状转为 CNC 程序目前高度依赖人工：每个零件都需由工艺员手动选择装夹方案、刀具、步降与刀路模式，效率低且难以泛化。作者希望实现“由几何直接生成粗加工程序”，免除逐件 CAM 调优。

Method: 1) 定义结构化加工基元：每个基元耦合索引化加工方向、目标在该视角下的闭合重建，以及显式的刀半径、层高与清料（clearing）模式参数；2) 基元经符号距离场（SDF）查询确定性编译为分层刀路（保真度主要来自该编译器）；3) 使用体素毛坯仿真器训练轻量选择器，自动预测基元参数，替代逐件手动设置。

Result: 在 ABC 数据集仿真中，Geo2Gcode 的粗加工保真度与专家调优的商业 CAM 配置相当，刀路长度略长，并明显优于默认 CAM 配置；参数选择器以约最精细固定设置四分之一的刀路长度达到接近其精度的效果；生成的程序可在商业 G-code 仿真器中运行，且有一个零件在实际 CNC 铣床上完成加工验证。

Conclusion: 结果表明，粗加工程序可完全由几何直接生成，无需逐件 CAM 调参；结构化基元 + 确定性编译 + 学习式参数选择的组合是可行且有效的方法路线。

Abstract: Turning a 3D shape into a Computer Numerical Control (CNC) program is still largely manual. For every part, a machinist chooses setups, tools, step-downs, and toolpath patterns. We present Geo2Gcode, which converts a target mesh directly into standard G-code through structured machining primitives. Each primitive couples an indexed machining direction, a closed per-view reconstruction of the target, and explicit tool-radius, layer-height, and clearing-pattern parameters, and compiles deterministically into layerwise toolpaths via signed-distance queries. Most of the fidelity comes from this compiler. A lightweight selector trained with a voxel stock simulator fills in primitive parameters without per-part manual settings, approaching the finest fixed setting at about a quarter of its toolpath length. In simulation on ABC parts, Geo2Gcode reaches roughing fidelity comparable to an expert-tuned commercial CAM setup with a moderately longer toolpath, and clearly improves on a default CAM configuration. Generated programs run in a commercial G-code simulator, and one part is machined on a physical CNC mill. These results demonstrate that roughing programs can be generated directly from geometry without per-part CAM tuning.

</details>


### [153] [Trading Parameters for Accuracy in MambaVision for Chest Radiograph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20103)
*Qaiser Farooq,Tehseen Zia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将 MambaVision 第四阶段的密集投影替换为轻量单扫描状态空间块加多尺度空洞深度可分离卷积，Tiny 规模省 35.7% 参数、提速 19.3%，但 AUROC 稳定下降 0.003–0.006；蒸馏可缩小但不消除差距，该替换仅在 Tiny/Small 规模是合理权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MambaVision 作为混合状态空间-注意力骨干，参数高度集中于最宽的第四阶段；作者希望量化该阶段对多标签胸片分类的实际贡献，并检验能否以更轻的结构替代以换取体积与速度，同时给出可量化的判别性能代价，供筛查工具开发者决策。

Method: 把第四阶段替换为改编自 Spatial-Mamba 的轻量单扫描状态空间块，并在扫描输出上叠加多尺度空洞深度可分离卷积；保留前三个阶段。在 Tiny/Small/Base 三个规模上，基于 NIH ChestX-ray14（完整 ChestMNIST 与高分辨率子集）和 Indiana Open-i 两个来源队列构造九种数据集-规模组合进行评估；辅以未替换教师的知识蒸馏，并采用 bootstrap 置信区间与等价性判据分析。

Result: Tiny 规模参数量下降 35.7%、GPU 吞吐提升 19.3%，节省主要来自该阶段的密集投影而非去除二次注意力。无蒸馏时 ChestMNIST 平均 AUROC 较基线低 0.004（95% bootstrap CI 0.002–0.006），九种组合均低 0.003–0.006。教师蒸馏将差距缩至 0.001–0.002 并在 ChestMNIST 上满足等价性，但同配方蒸馏基线本身提升 0.001–0.003，对称训练下仍残留 0.002–0.005 差距。Base 规模下未替换的 Small 模型同时更小且更准。

Conclusion: 第四阶段替换仅在 Tiny 与 Small 规模上是有利的权衡；Base 规模不推荐。该工作提供了参数量、吞吐与诊断判别力损失之间的定量对照，便于胸片筛查工具开发者按需取舍。

Abstract: MambaVision, a hybrid state-space and attention backbone, places most of its parameters in its widest, fourth stage. We quantified what that stage contributes to multi-label chest radiograph classification. The fourth stage was replaced with lighter single-scan state-space blocks adapted from Spatial-Mamba, with multi-scale dilated depthwise convolutions applied to the scan output; the first three stages were retained. Tiny, Small, and Base models were evaluated across nine dataset-scale combinations drawn from two source cohorts: the National Institutes of Health ChestX-ray14 archive (full ChestMNIST and a high-resolution subset) and Indiana University Open-i. At the Tiny scale, replacement reduced parameter count by 35.7% and increased graphics processing unit throughput by 19.3%; the savings came from the stage's dense projections, not from removing quadratic attention. Without distillation, the mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve on ChestMNIST was 0.004 lower than the baseline (95% bootstrap confidence interval, 0.002 to 0.006), and 0.003 to 0.006 lower across all nine combinations. Knowledge distillation from an unreplaced teacher narrowed the gap to the undistilled baseline to 0.001-0.002 and met the equivalence criterion on ChestMNIST, but distilling the baseline with the same recipe improved it by 0.001-0.003, so a gap of 0.002-0.005 persisted under symmetric training. At the Base scale, the unreplaced Small model was both smaller and more accurate. Fourth-stage replacement is a useful trade-off only for Tiny and Small models. These measurements let developers of chest radiograph screening tools weigh model size and speed against a quantified loss of diagnostic discrimination.

</details>


### [154] [Aerial Person Detection under Edge Constraints: Held-Out Evaluation and Edge-Ground Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20127)
*Raghu Venkat,Tricha Anjali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在无人机搜救的“像素极少、算力受限、带宽窄”约束下，以预注册为主、探索性为辅地评估人体检测：分块留出使召回从32.7%降至12.3%；46K参数对比度门控检测器达10.4%（全域5.7%）；YOLO11n达54.5%但算力约11倍且跨地点阈值失效；用842K参数地面模型重打分YOLO前16裁剪可将召回从44.9%提升至53.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索无人机场景中目标仅占几个像素、机载计算资源极小、无线链路带宽很窄，需要在这些严苛限制下研究人体检测的可行性与评测可靠性；同时强调评估规范的严谨性（预先声明主实验、后续分析标注为探索性）。

Method: 采用记录分块留出的交叉验证（fold）设计；提出46,000参数、由对比度线索门控的轻量检测器，仅在疑似区域运行；与YOLO11n在相同折上对比；在4K实地航拍（117 m）上评估；对YOLO11n的16个高分裁剪（每帧154 KB）用842,000参数的地面模型重打分，阈值在验证地点冻结；并做跨地点误报率标定实验与15个测试地点的事后配对分析。

Result: 分块留出使固定误报目标下召回从32.7%降至12.3%；轻量门控检测器召回10.4% vs 全域5.7%；YOLO11n在相近误报率下召回54.5%，算力约11倍，4K/117 m画面中行人在其前16检测中占比最高92%（本文板卡74%）；YOLO11n单独使用时在异地误报超目标1.7–3.4倍，最佳高空配置每帧耗4–24次标准前向；地面模型重打分使召回44.9%→53.7%，事后配对增益+8.8百分点[+2.4,+12.5]，合并误报0.86→0.64每帧但差异不显著；两检测器对土色调静止人员覆盖率都很低。

Conclusion: 像素级小目标检测在机载约束下差距显著，域/地点变化导致阈值与误报严重失配；轻量特征+对比度门控可低算力运行，而“粗检+地面重打分”能以有限带宽换取明显召回提升；静止、低对比度人员仍是共同短板，需在后续独立验证。

Abstract: A search drone sees a missing person as a few pixels, carries a small computer, and shares a narrow radio link. We study person detection under those limits; the primary evaluations were declared before they ran, and later analyses are marked exploratory. In an exploratory comparison, holding out recording blocks inferred from thermal frame identifiers cut recall at a fixed false-alarm target from 32.7% to 12.3%. With nothing tuned on test blocks, a 46,000-parameter detector gated by a contrast cue finds 10.4% of people against 5.7% for the same network applied everywhere. A standard detector, YOLO11n, trained under the same folds finds 54.5% at a similar false-alarm rate for about eleven times the arithmetic, and on our 4K field footage at 117 m has a walking person among its top 16 detections in up to 92% of frames against 74% for our board. Run alone, YOLO11n's thresholds set at one location overshot their false-alarm target 1.7 to 3.4 times at new locations, and its best configuration at altitude cost 4 to 24 standard passes per frame. Re-scoring its 16 best crops (154 KB per frame) with an 842,000-parameter ground model, thresholds frozen on a validation location, raised recall from 44.9% to 53.7% against the same 16 crops with YOLO's own scores; in a post hoc paired analysis over 15 test locations the gain was +8.8 points [+2.4, +12.5]. Pooled false detections fell from 0.86 to 0.64 per frame, but that paired change was not distinguishable from zero. Coverage of a still person in earth tones was very low for both detectors in the clips we flew.

</details>


### [155] [GeoReVis: Learning Selective Revision and Evidence Consolidation for Remote Sensing Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20183)
*Ke An,Liang Chen,Yupei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出GeoReVis，统一执行反馈引导的修订、候选选择和跨掩码证据整合，以解决语言引导遥感分割中的修订退化与证据碎片化问题，并构建多轮轨迹数据管道及ReVis-Eval评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导遥感分割中，复杂空间关系导致指代歧义与定位错误。现有掩码检查与修订方法存在"修订退化"：修正缺失区域时可能破坏原本正确的区域；且即使全局更优的掩码也可能丢失较差掩码中的正确区域，即"证据碎片化"——正确目标证据分散在多个次优掩码中。

Method: GeoReVis包含三部分：(1) 由执行反馈引导的细粒度修订学习，从真实能改进分割的动作中学习；(2) 候选选择，通过实际候选间的质量比较选择更优掩码以降低退化风险；(3) 跨掩码证据整合，保留各轮所有候选，将其空间分歧划分为可组合区域，并以完整指令与上下文为条件联合重构。另构建数据管道生成细粒度多轮分割轨迹，从模型自身输出派生出修订动作、候选选择与区域组合的监督信号，超越整掩码IoU监督；并提出ReVis-Eval基准协议评估多轮修订与跨轮证据整合精度。

Result: 在ReVis-Eval及5个遥感推理与指代分割基准上的实验证明了GeoReVis的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将修订、候选选择与跨掩码区域级证据整合统一，并配合细粒度轨迹监督与新评测协议，可有效缓解修订退化与证据碎片化，提升遥感指代分割性能。

Abstract: Recent methods try to inspect and revise masks to mitigate referential ambiguity and localization errors caused by complex spatial relations in language-guided remote sensing segmentation. However, these solutions often lead to Revision Degradation: revising missing regions may introduce errors into previously correct ones. Even a globally better mask can omit correct regions found in inferior masks. We term this Evidence Fragmentation: correct target evidence is scattered across imperfect masks. All masks generated by the model at this time are suboptimal. We propose GeoReVis, which unifies revision guided by execution feedback, Candidate Selection, and Cross-Mask Evidence Consolidation. It learns fine-grained revision from real actions that improve segmentation when executed and learns to select a better mask through quality comparisons between actual candidates, reducing the risk of degradation during revision. The selected whole mask can still discard useful regions. GeoReVis therefore retains all candidates across rounds, partitions their spatial disagreements into composable regions, and jointly reconstructs regions conditioned on the full instruction and context. This makes complementary and fragmentary evidence reusable regardless of its source candidate's overall quality, yielding improved segmentation. We further build a data pipeline to generate fine-grained multi-round segmentation trajectories and derive supervision for revision actions, candidate selection, and region composition from the model's own outputs, extending training beyond whole-mask IoU supervision. We also propose ReVis-Eval, a benchmark protocol evaluating multi-round revision and cross-round evidence consolidation accuracy. Experiments on ReVis-Eval and 5 benchmarks for remote sensing reasoning and referring segmentation demonstrate the effectiveness of GeoReVis.

</details>


### [156] [GlyphAtlas: Disentangling Stroke Detail from Layout for Scene Text Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20192)
*Luozheng Qin,Changhai Zhou,Yiyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GlyphAtlas场景文本合成框架，以独立单元格构成的字形图谱提供细粒度笔画参考，解耦参考分辨率与目标区域尺寸，实现小而密集、结构复杂文本的笔画精确合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过预渲染目标文本生成glyph图像来同时指定布局与字形结构，但这种表示将每个字形的分辨率绑定到其目标区域大小，导致小尺寸、密集排列或结构复杂的文本无法获得细粒度笔画引导。

Method: 保留glyph图像作为空间锚点，另引入专用字形图谱：每个字符或词在独立单元格中渲染并编码，解耦参考分辨率与目标区域尺寸；设计atlas-anchored attention，使每个文本区域仅从对应图谱单元格检索笔画细节，以粗到细方式精化glyph结构；采用mask-weighted flow matching目标与timestep-weighted文本感知损失优化，并对图谱单元格做随机分辨率增强以支持推理时可调图谱预算。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在小而密集及结构复杂文本上实现笔画精确合成，可零样本迁移到任务特定训练中未见过的语言，并在包含大量文本区域的场景中取得领先性能。

Conclusion: 通过"glyph图像粗空间锚点+字形图谱细笔画参考"的粗到细条件机制，GlyphAtlas在保持显式布局对应关系的同时显著提升笔画渲染精度与跨语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Scene text synthesis requires stroke-accurate characters rendered in a specified layout, as well as visual coherence with the surrounding scene. To achieve this, existing methods commonly pre-render the target text at its intended locations to form a glyph image that jointly specifies layout and glyph structure. However, this representation ties the resolution of each glyph to its target region, limiting fine-grained stroke guidance for small, densely packed, or structurally complex text. To tackle this, we propose GlyphAtlas, a scene text synthesis framework that retains the glyph image as a spatial anchor and supplies fine-grained stroke references through a dedicated glyph atlas. The atlas renders and encodes each character or word in a separate cell, decoupling reference resolution from target-region size. We further introduce atlas-anchored attention, which lets each text region retrieve stroke detail only from its corresponding atlas cells to refine the coarse glyph structure provided by the glyph image. This coarse-to-fine conditioning simplifies stroke rendering while preserving explicit layout correspondence. We optimize GlyphAtlas with a mask-weighted flow matching objective and a timestep-weighted text perceptual loss for writing accuracy and background consistency. Random resolution augmentation of atlas cells supports adjustable atlas budgets at inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GlyphAtlas achieves stroke-accurate synthesis of small, dense, and structurally complex text, zero-shot transfer to languages unseen during task-specific training, and leading performance in scenes with many text regions. project page: https://fr0zencrane.github.io/project-glyphatlas/

</details>


### [157] [DoF-Query Transformer for Adaptive Fusion of Egocentric Vision and Forearm HD-sEMG in Continuous Hand Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20196)
*Jiahang Liu,Wenbo Zhang,Junsong Wang,Zhenzhi Ying,Siqi Hou,Naohiko Sugita,Liming Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种手指无束缚的多模态框架，融合自我中心RGB图像与128通道前臂HD-sEMG，连续回归20个手部关节角，R²=0.88、MAE=2.80°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续、准确的手部运动学估计对自然人机交互、具身智能数据集的动作监督采集及灵巧机器人学习具有价值，但单一传感模态在变化工况下难以可靠估计，因此需要多模态互补融合。

Method: 框架由ViT视觉编码器、Transformer EMG编码器及自由度查询式Transformer融合解码器组成；解码器为每个自由度分配独立可学习query，实现按自由度与上下文自适应的视觉-EMG融合；在10名受试者的同步多模态数据上评估。

Result: 融合模型平均R²=0.88、MAE=2.80°，优于纯视觉（0.82/3.21°）与纯EMG（0.68/5.13°），亦优于代表性单模态基线与替代融合设计；视觉歧义关节与视角下融合增益更大，合成视觉遮挡下鲁棒性优于纯视觉。

Conclusion: 自我中心视觉与前臂HD-sEMG具有互补性，可精细捕捉手部运动学，适用于人类示范采集与灵巧机器人学习。

Abstract: Continuous and accurate estimation of human hand kinematics is important not only for intuitive human-machine interaction, but also for capturing fine-grained human manipulation demonstrations that can provide action supervision for embodied-intelligence datasets and dexterous robot learning. However, individual sensing modalities may be insufficient for reliable estimation under varying operating conditions. We present a finger-unencumbered multimodal framework for continuous regression of 20 hand joint angles from egocentric RGB images and 128-channel forearm high-density surface electromyography (HD-sEMG). The framework combines a Vision Transformer-based visual encoder, a Transformer-based EMG encoder, and a degree of freedom (DoF) query Transformer fusion decoder. To account for the varying informativeness of visual and EMG cues across joint DoFs and input conditions, the decoder assigns a separate learnable query to each DoF, enabling DoF-specific and context-adaptive fusion of the two modalities. The framework was evaluated on synchronized multimodal data from 10 participants. Averaged across participants, the proposed fusion model achieved a mean coefficient of determination ($R^2$) of $0.88$ and a mean absolute error (MAE) of $2.80^\circ$, compared with $0.82$ and $3.21^\circ$ for the vision-only model and $0.68$ and $5.13^\circ$ for the EMG-only model. It also outperformed the evaluated representative unimodal baselines and alternative fusion designs. DoF-wise analyses showed larger fusion gains for visually ambiguous joints and viewing conditions, while synthetic visual occlusion demonstrated greater robustness than vision-only estimation. These results highlight the complementary value of egocentric vision and forearm HD-sEMG for capturing fine-grained human hand kinematics, with potential applications in human demonstration acquisition and dexterous robot learning.

</details>


### [158] [Radar Panoptic Transformer: Semantic-Guided Mask Queries for Dynamic Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20203)
*Mayank Mayank,Bharanidhar Duraisamy,Rohit Mohan,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端的Radar Panoptic Transformer（RPT），面向稀疏非均匀车载雷达点云的统一全景分割，在RadarScenes上取得71.6% mIoU与82.2% PQ，并实现31 Hz实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载雷达具备远距离感知与直接多普勒测速能力，但其点云稀疏、非均匀采样，动态目标检测点极少且易被占主导的静态背景淹没，几何线索不足以分离实例，传统全景分割方法难以适用。

Method: 端到端点云网络：主干通过分组向量注意力聚合位姿对齐的时序上下文，并基于距离、方位角与RCS学习逐点邻域半径（雷达自适应特征提取）；点级语义预测用于在前景候选中初始化查询，并通过查询—点类别一致性引导掩码交叉注意力，无需几何聚类即输出实例掩码；另引入RCS一致性损失，对齐预测与标注实例的实例级统计量。

Result: 在RadarScenes上达到SOTA：mIoU 71.6%、PQ 82.2%；在MAN TruckScenes（最远230 m、全周向远距离雷达场景）上优于所评估基线；RadarScenes上端到端推理达31 Hz，超过传感器帧率。

Conclusion: 雷达自适应的特征提取与语义引导的掩码预测可有效应对稀疏雷达点云的全景分割，方法兼顾精度与实时性，并可推广至远距离全周向雷达平台。

Abstract: Automotive radar provides long-range sensing and direct Doppler velocity measurements, but its sparse and non-uniformly sampled point clouds pose significant challenges for panoptic segmentation. Dynamic objects often yield only a few detections amid a dominant static background, providing limiting the geometric cues for separating object instances. We propose \emph{Radar Panoptic Transformer (RPT)}, an end-to-end point-based network that incorporates radar-adaptive feature extraction with semantic-guided mask prediction. Its backbone aggregates pose-aligned temporal context through grouped vector attention and learns per-point neighborhood radii conditioned on range, azimuth, and radar cross section (RCS). Point-wise semantic predictions initialize queries at likely foreground detections and guide masked cross-attention through query-point class agreement, producing instance masks without geometric clustering. An auxiliary RCS-consistency loss matches instance-level statistics between predicted and annotated instances. RPT achieves state-of-the-art performance on RadarScenes with $71.6\%$ mIoU and $82.2\%$ PQ. We further evaluate RPT on MAN TruckScenes, where it outperforms the evaluated baselines in a long-range, full-surround radar setting with sensing ranges up to $230\,\mathrm{m}$. RPT achieves real-time end-to-end inference at $31$\,Hz on RadarScenes, exceeding the sensor frame rate.

</details>


### [159] [AstroPIE-53: Towards Understanding Failure Modes of Human Pose Estimation in Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20207)
*Stephanie Käs,Marten Flaig,Lukas Woyke,Osmanalp Ömeroğlu,Felix Krueckel,Onur Alp Çulha,Bastian Leibe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AstroPIE-53航天员视频试点数据集（53段带标注视频，仅发布视频ID、重建脚本与稠密标注以规避版权），首次系统评测零样本条件下人体检测与姿态估计模型的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天员可靠监测对任务安全、态势感知与自主辅助至关重要，但太空环境下视觉模型的鲁棒性因数据稀缺而基本未被量化。

Method: 从公开任务影像中采集53段航天员视频，提供边界框、挑战类别与人工标注的姿态失效标签；在真实零样本部署条件下基准测试SOTA人体检测与姿态估计模型，并进行首次系统性失效分析。

Result: 模型性能显著下降，主要诱因包括遮挡、异常身体朝向、低对比度与微重力运动模式。

Conclusion: 现有地面视觉模型难以直接迁移至太空场景，需针对遮挡、姿态朝向、成像对比度与微重力运动等特性进行专门的数据构建与模型改进。

Abstract: Reliable monitoring of astronauts is essential for safety, situational awareness, and autonomous assistance in space missions. While modern computer vision enables accurate human detection and pose estimation on Earth, its robustness in space environments remains largely unquantified due to limited data availability. We present AstroPIE-53, a pilot dataset of 53 annotated astronaut videos collected from publicly available mission footage. To respect copyright restrictions, we release video identifiers, reconstruction scripts, and dense annotations rather than raw videos. The dataset includes bounding boxes, challenge classes, and human-annotated pose failure tags. We benchmark state-of-the-art person detection and pose estimation models under realistic zero-shot deployment conditions and perform the first systematic failure analysis for astronaut monitoring. Our results reveal substantial performance degradation caused by occlusions, unusual body orientations, low contrast, and microgravity motion patterns.

</details>


### [160] [Ocular Recognition: Interoperability, Multi-Resolution, and Spectrum Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20219)
*Fabian Stockhardt,Juan E. Tapia,Christoph Busch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究半受控/移动场景下的眼周与虹膜识别，比较三种基于AdaFace的分辨率专用架构（固定分辨率专家、共享头的独立骨干IBSH、分辨率专用前端+共享身份主干）在40x40、80x80、160x160输入下的表现，并考察路由专用训练子集与全池训练两种数据分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼周/虹膜识别多在受控采集条件下开发，难以用于半受控与移动场景；核心障碍是缺乏覆盖真实采集条件的代表性数据（如边检场景下从低分辨率RGB眼周到高分辨率NIR虹膜的异构证据）。由此引出三个问题：分辨率专用处理是否提升识别性能、如何比较不同处理路径产生的模板、如何在各分辨率专家之间分配训练数据。

Method: 构建三种基于AdaFace的架构家族：(1) 固定分辨率专家；(2) 独立骨干+共享头（IBSH）；(3) 分辨率专用前端（40x40、80x80、160x160）后接共享身份主干与嵌入投影。对每种架构分别采用路由专用训练子集和全池训练（所有专家使用完整选定训练集）两种策略进行评估，并考察跨分辨率模板互操作性与公共路由重提取。

Result: 实验揭示同分辨率专用化、跨分辨率互操作性与公共路由重提取之间的权衡。路由专用训练在各路由上产生差异明显的性能画像；全池训练使各路由性能更趋均匀，且主要惠及160x160路由。说明增加训练数据并不均等地提升所有分辨率路由。

Conclusion: 分辨率专用架构设计需在专用化收益与跨路由互操作性之间权衡；训练数据扩展的收益具有路由依赖性，对高分辨率路由更显著。为异构采集条件下的多分辨率识别系统设计与数据分配提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Ocular recognition is typically developed under controlled acquisition conditions, limiting its use in semi-controlled and on-the-move scenarios. A key obstacle is the lack of representative data spanning real-world capture conditions. At a border checkpoint, for example, images may range from low-resolution visible-light periocular crops (RGB) to high-resolution near-infrared iris images (NIR). Using this heterogeneous evidence raises three questions: whether resolution-specific processing improves recognition, how to compare templates from different processing routes, and how to allocate training data across resolution specialists.
  We investigate resolution-specific processing and parameter sharing using three AdaFace-based architecture families: fixed-resolution specialists; Independent Backbones with Shared Heads (IBSH); and models with resolution-specific front-ends for 40x40, 80x80, and 160x160 inputs, followed by a shared identity trunk and embedding projection. We evaluate each architecture with route-specific training subsets and with a full-pool strategy, in which every specialist uses the complete selected training set.
  Results reveal a trade-off among same-resolution specialization, cross-resolution interoperability, and common-route re-extraction. Route-specific training produces distinct performance profiles across routes, whereas full-pool training makes route performance more uniform and primarily benefits the 160x160 route. Thus, adding training data does not improve all resolution routes equally.

</details>


### [161] [EvoMem3D: Adaptive-Depth Temporal Representation Memory for Multi-Camera 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20241)
*Moshe Kimhi,Idan Kligvasser,Yotam Intrator,Regev Cohen,Ehud Rivlin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoMem3D：面向多相机3D检测的自适应深度时序框架，通过缓存全深度外观锚点+策略选择的部分特征，实现约2倍加速且精度基本无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多相机3D检测依赖大型ViT图像编码器，重复进行全深度编码成为推理主要开销；而相邻驾驶帧高度冗余，存在可复用的时序信息。

Method: 将最近一次全深度检测器接口外观表示缓存为只读锚点，与当前帧策略选择的部分特征结合；轻量预编码控制器在图像处理前选择语义编码器退出深度（稳定帧浅层、视觉变化时深层）；解耦时序外观与几何对齐——当前帧校准定义每个时刻的解码器几何，运动条件位置适配器补偿锚点到当前帧的自车运动。

Result: nuScenes上达61.50 NDS/52.76 mAP，端到端延迟由364.4ms降至168.7ms（x2.16）；搭配StreamPETR达61.36 NDS/52.55 mAP、x1.92加速。

Conclusion: 消融实验分离了当前证据、时序复用、记忆校正与自适应路由的贡献；审计表明策略能学习区分语义驾驶状态。

Abstract: Modern multi-camera 3D detectors increasingly rely on large Vision Transformer (ViT) image encoders, making repeated full-depth encoding a dominant inference cost even though adjacent driving frames are highly redundant. We present EvoMem3D, an adaptive-depth temporal framework that caches the most recent full-depth detector-interface appearance representation as a read-only anchor and combines it with policy-selected partial features from the current frame. A lightweight pre-encoding controller chooses among semantic encoder exits before image processing, allocating shallow computation to stable frames and deeper computation when the visual state changes. To reuse appearance without carrying stale geometry, we decouple temporal appearance from geometric alignment: current-frame calibration defines the decoder geometry at every timestamp, while a motion-conditioned positional adapter accounts for anchor-to-current ego motion. On nuScenes, EvoMem3D achieves 61.50 NDS and 52.76 mAP while reducing end-to-end latency from 364.4 to 168.7 ms (x2.16) relative to standard dense DETR3D. With StreamPETR, it reaches 61.36 NDS and 52.55 mAP at a x1.92 speedup. Controlled studies isolate the effects of current evidence, temporal reuse, memory correction, and adaptive routing, while our audit confirms that the policy learns to distinguish semantic driving regimes.

</details>


### [162] [Self-supervised learning for zebrafish embryo phenotype evaluation: Systematic comparison of algorithms, label-efficient classification, and transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20269)
*Thomas Lautenschlager,Gaëlle Hayot,Quentin Brassart,Angelo Jovin Yamachui Sitcheu,Linus Schilpp,Tijana Geroski,Tetyana Goldanova,Stephan Allgeier,Thibaut Soubrié,Milica Nikolic,Thomas Dickmeis,Ralf Mikut,Nils Friederich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在公开EmbryoNet数据集上基准测试多种自监督学习（SSL）算法用于斑马鱼胚胎表型图像，发现基于SwAV训练的ResNet50表征配合线性分类器达86.4%准确率，可媲美全监督模型（87.1%）；标签稀缺时（5%标签）性能大幅领先从头监督训练，并在跨场景小样本三分类任务上无需重训即优于EmbryoNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斑马鱼胚胎是图像表型分析的重要模式生物，但训练ML模型通常需要大量标注数据，且成像条件差异阻碍模型跨实验室复用。SSL可从无标注图像学习可迁移表征以缓解这两个瓶颈，然而算法选择等领域特定问题仍缺乏探索。

Method: 在公开EmbryoNet数据集上对主流SSL算法进行基准测试，评估标签高效学习能力，并测试向未见场景的迁移；采用SwAV训练的ResNet50表征，分别接线性与非线性分类器；在一小规模、不同成像条件、此前未见的斑马鱼毒性数据集上做三分类（正常、死亡、错误裁剪）零重训迁移测试。

Result: SwAV表征+线性分类器达86.4%，非线性分类器达87.1%，与全监督EmbryoNet（87.1%）相当；仅用5%标签时，SwAV达70.2%，显著优于从头监督训练的45.7%；跨场景三分类任务上无需重训即达81.3%，高于EmbryoNet的75.9%。

Conclusion: 用于斑马鱼胚胎的SSL模型主要带来标签效率提升与对新场景的迁移能力，而非提升有充足标注时的监督性能。

Abstract: Zebrafish embryos are a prominent model organism for image-based phenotyping and especially suited for large-scale imaging studies, for which machine learning (ML) models can automate evaluation. However, training ML models usually requires large labeled datasets, and differences in imaging conditions often impede reuse across laboratories. Self-supervised learning (SSL) addresses both constraints by learning transferable representations from unlabeled images. Yet central aspects like the choice of algorithm remain underexplored for domain-specific applications. To close this gap, we benchmark popular SSL algorithms on the public EmbryoNet dataset, explore label-efficient learning, and test transfer to an unseen setting. Using representations from our strongest model, a ResNet50 network trained with SwAV, a simple linear classifier reaches 86.4% accuracy and a non-linear classifier 87.1%, rivaling the fully supervised EmbryoNet model (87.1%). The SwAV model decisively outperforms supervised training from scratch when labels are scarce (70.2% vs. 45.7% accuracy with 5% of labels). On a three-class problem (normal, dead, falsely cropped) with a small, previously unseen zebrafish toxicity dataset, recorded in a different setting, our model reaches 81.3% accuracy without retraining, while EmbryoNet achieves 75.9%. Overall, self-supervised models for zebrafish embryos seem to mostly facilitate label-efficiency and transfer to new settings rather than improving supervised results.

</details>


### [163] [Visual Marking as a Native Reasoning Capability for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20278)
*Jiazhen Liu,Yanghao Wang,Chaolei Tan,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SelfMark让多模态大模型在其原生视觉特征空间中直接构建并使用视觉标记，通过locate-mark-read流程建立标记与图像的空间对应，单一模型联合训练四类任务并在六个基准上超越各任务专用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉推理需维护难以用文本表达的中间空间状态；现有方法依赖外部绘图工具或图像生成器，或在潜在token中编码这些状态但缺乏与输入图像的显式空间对应。

Method: 将视觉token网格视为画布，实现locate-mark-read过程：模型定位相关位置、构建任务相关的标记特征、读取结果视觉特征以引导推理；在空间规划、密集计数、高分辨率VQA和线条追踪上联合训练单一模型。

Result: 在六个基准上优于针对单项任务的最先进MLLM方法，且优势在更强骨干模型上依然保持。

Conclusion: SelfMark通过将空间落地的标记直接整合进MLLM视觉特征空间，为视觉推理提供统一方案。

Abstract: Visual reasoning often requires maintaining intermediate spatial states that are difficult to express in text. Existing approaches either rely on external drawing tools or image generators, or encode these states in latent tokens without explicit spatial correspondence to the input image. We introduce SelfMark, which enables MLLMs to construct and use visual marks directly in their native visual feature space. By treating the visual token grid as a canvas, SelfMark establishes explicit spatial correspondence between marks and images. This is achieved through a locate-mark-read procedure, where the model identifies relevant locations, constructs task-dependent mark features, and reads the resulting visual features to guide its reasoning. We jointly train a single model on spatial planning, dense counting, high-resolution VQA, and line tracing. Across six benchmarks, SelfMark outperforms state-of-the-art MLLM-based methods specialized for individual tasks. These improvements also persist with a stronger backbone, demonstrating the effectiveness of visual marking across different model capabilities. Overall, SelfMark provides a unified approach to visual reasoning by integrating spatially grounded marks directly into the visual feature space of MLLMs.

</details>


### [164] [CiGS: Conduction in Gaussian Splats for Interior Temperature Field from Thermal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20283)
*Liu Zhenyuan,Bharath Seshadri,Bailey Miller,George Kopanas,Ionnais Gkioulekas,Bernd Bickel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需网格、可微的多视角可见光+热图像内部温度场反演方法，基于拉普拉斯边值问题与点云上离散的直接边界积分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在热图像仅覆盖部分表面、且几何拓扑复杂时反演固体内部温度分布，且通常依赖表面/体积网格划分。

Method: 将稳态热传导建模为拉普拉斯边值问题；在点云上离散直接边界积分方程；用可见光谱图像的高斯重建获得边界几何，热图像提供边界数据；由渲染深度采样配点并剔除深度不连续像素；采用密度相减使内部求值在接近表面处仍保持精度；全流程可微。

Result: 在Dirichlet及Dirichlet-Robin混合边界条件的合成与真实数据（含复杂拓扑场景）上验证：结果与解析解一致，精度优于FEM求解器；并可用于从热图像反推加热管道位姿与深度、保温缺陷与裂纹形状。

Conclusion: 无网格、可微的边界积分框架能准确反演部分观测下的内部温度场，并支持下游几何/位姿反演任务。

Abstract: We present a method for inferring the interior temperature distribution of a solid object from registered multi-view visible-spectrum and thermal images, even when the thermal views cover only part of its surface. We formulate steady-state heat conduction as a Laplace boundary value problem and solve it with a direct boundary integral equation discretized over a point cloud: a Gaussian reconstruction of the visible-spectrum images supplies the boundary geometry, and the thermal images supply the boundary data. We do not build surface or volume meshes at any stage in our method. Instead, we sample collocation points from rendered depth without the pixels at depth discontinuities, and use a density subtraction that keeps the interior evaluation accurate up to the surface. The whole chain is differentiable, and we use it to recover the pose and depth of heating pipes, insulation defects, and the shape of cracks from thermal images. We validate our method through experiments on boundary value problems with Dirichlet-only and mixed Dirichlet and Robin boundaries, using synthetic and real captured data on scenes with challenging topology. These experiments show that our method matches analytical solutions and has better accuracy than FEM solvers.

</details>


### [165] [PoCoAttn: Position-Content Collaborative Sparse Attention for Fast and High-Fidelity Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20307)
*Jianwen Wu,Meiling Wang,Junkang Zhou,Yipeng Shen,Haoran You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练稀疏注意力框架PoCoAttn，将视频扩散Transformer的注意力分解为输入稳定的位置先验与内容相关的残余两部分，离线解决前者、在线解决后者，在四个模型三个任务上取得更优的速度-保真度Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer对数十万时空token做3D全注意力，二次复杂度主导推理延迟。现有稀疏注意力陷入两难：静态方法用预定义掩码，无法捕捉输入相关注意力；动态方法反复重建稀疏掩码，在线重复发现本可离线确定的、跨输入基本稳定的位置结构。

Method: (1) 对3D query-key对做相对偏移分析，揭示视频注意力可分解为携带大部分注意力质量的输入稳定位置先验与小的内容相关残余；(2) 位置-内容协同稀疏注意力：从离线校准表检索位置key，在线选择内容key，两类来源间采用head自适应比例，并对丢弃key做补偿以逼近全注意力；(3) 步自适应预算分配算法，建模早期步骤稀疏注意力误差沿去噪轨迹的放大效应，推导每步最优稀疏预算。

Result: 跨四个视频扩散模型与三个任务的实验表明，PoCoAttn持续获得优于SOTA的速度-保真度Pareto前沿，PSNR较基线提升1.9–5.0 dB，相比dense FlashAttention-3实现最高4.1×注意力加速与2.0×去噪加速。

Conclusion: 通过将注意力显式分离为离线可解的位置先验与在线求解的内容残余，并配合步自适应稀疏预算，PoCoAttn以免训练方式在视频扩散推理中实现了显著加速且保真度更高，验证了"位置结构稳定、内容依赖动态"这一分解的有效性。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers rely on 3D full attention over tens of thousands of spatiotemporal tokens, and its quadratic cost dominates inference latency. Sparse attention reduces this cost, but existing methods face a dilemma: static methods apply predefined masks and cannot capture input-dependent attention, whereas dynamic methods reconstruct the sparse mask repeatedly, paying online to rediscover positional structure that is largely stable across inputs. To this end, we propose PoCoAttn, a training-free sparse attention framework that separates input-stable from input-dependent attention and resolves the former offline and the latter online. PoCoAttn features: (1) A relative-offset analysis of 3D query-key pairs, which shows that video attention decomposes into an input-stable positional prior that carries most of the attention mass and a small content-dependent residual. (2) A position-content collaborative sparse attention mechanism, which retrieves positional keys from offline-calibrated tables, selects content keys online with a head-adaptive ratio between the two sources, and compensates for the discarded keys to approximate full attention. (3) A step-adaptive budget allocation algorithm, which models how early-step sparse attention errors are amplified along the denoising trajectory and derives the optimal sparsity budget per step. Extensive experiments across four video diffusion models and three tasks show that PoCoAttn consistently achieves a better speed-fidelity Pareto frontier than state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, improving PSNR by 1.9 to 5.0 dB over baselines and delivering up to 4.1$\times$ attention and 2.0$\times$ denoising speedups over dense FlashAttention-3 attention.

</details>


### [166] [Unreasonable Confidence from Simulator Misspecification: Directional Robustness on the SPEED+ Sim-to-Real Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20321)
*Nikolay Dolgov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文以刻意简化的单目位姿估计器考察 SPEED+ 合成到真实（syn-to-real）域偏移下的置信度校准与迁移特性，发现模型在真实域失效时仍高度自信、迁移呈不对称性、且光度增强仅在骨干适配后才按其所建模的方向起作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器在轨视觉导航缺乏真实标注图像，只能依赖仿真数据训练；而仿真器存在辐射度误差（缺失镜面高光、硬阴影、传感器饱和），导致合成-真实域偏移成为系统核心风险。作者希望系统刻画该偏移对位姿估计的影响。

Method: 在 SPEED+ 基准上（仿真训练、两类真实硬件在环测试域：sunlamp 与 lightbox），采用 2×2 设计（骨干网络适配 × 光度增强），使用刻意简化的单目位姿估计器，并进行共形校准评估；所有复审后实验均预注册，公开代码、逐图预测与预注册记录。

Result: (1) 校准失效：合成域上共形校准至 90% 的区域，在 sunlamp 上仅覆盖 9–15% 的旋转误差、lightbox 上 21–38%，错误位姿带有与正确位姿相同的置信度。(2) 迁移不对称：扩展数据与特征将合成旋转误差从 1.65 降至 1.03 rad，但 sunlamp 仅改善 0.05 rad（不足其随机种子标准差的两倍）；而适配骨干末阶段可将 63–83% 的合成增益迁移到两个真实域。(3) 增强的方向依赖性：骨干适配后，光度增强栈将 lightbox 由 1.17 降至 0.99 rad（所有种子一致），但对 sunlamp 影响不超过种子波动；骨干冻结时则对 sunlamp 更有帮助。

Conclusion: 合成-真实域偏移的主要危害在于置信度失准而非单纯的精度下降；提升合成性能不一定带来真实域收益，骨干适配是迁移的关键杠杆，而数据增强的收益受限于其所建模的照明方向。研究强调需在真实硬件在环域上评估不确定性与预注册实验规范。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation systems for orbit must be trained almost entirely on synthetic imagery, because real labelled images of a given spacecraft in flight rarely exist. Benchmarks like SPEED+ are built for that situation: the training set comes from a simulator, and the test sets are real images of a satellite mockup under two kinds of lighting. That simulator approximates how light reflects off mylar and aluminium, and like every scientific simulator it is misspecified. Its error is radiometric, since it misses the specular highlights, hard shadows and sensor saturation common in real images. We probe the resulting synthetic-to-real gap, the central risk for such systems, with a deliberately simple monocular pose estimator. In a 2x2 study of backbone adaptation and photometric augmentation, trained on synthetic images and tested on both real hardware-in-the-loop domains, we find three things. (1) The model does not know it is failing: regions conformally calibrated to 90% on synthetic data cover only 9-15% of rotation errors on sunlamp and 21-38% on lightbox, so a wrong pose carries the confidence of a correct one. (2) Transfer is asymmetric: scaling data and features improved synthetic rotation error from 1.65 to 1.03 rad but moved the harshly lit sunlamp domain by only 0.05 rad, under twice its seed s.d., whereas adapting the backbone's last stage carried 63-83% of its extra synthetic gain to both real domains. (3) Once the backbone adapts, augmentation helps only in the direction it models: our photometric stack improved the diffusely lit lightbox domain from 1.17 to 0.99 rad on every seed but moved sunlamp no more than seed-to-seed variation, while with the backbone frozen it helped sunlamp more. Every post-review experiment was pre-registered, and the code, per-image predictions and pre-registration record are at https://github.com/lokindoo/spacecraft-pose-estimation.

</details>


### [167] [BlockAlign: Preference Optimization for Trajectory-Distilled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20351)
*Jingyuan Zhu,Biaolong Chen,Yangyang Qin,Shengyang Shen,Yaohua Wang,Weiming Bai,Miao Lu,Aixi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BlockAlign 提出接口一致的偏好优化框架，在块级融合速度预测器上构造块边界状态并监督跨步归一化转移残差，解决轨迹蒸馏视频生成器的训练-推理不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对齐轨迹蒸馏视频生成器可大幅降低 rollout 成本，无需教师微调或再蒸馏。但常规 flow-matching 偏好目标假设单头局部速度监督，与 PDD、PTD 等轨迹蒸馏生成器的有限区间、块平均转移接口不匹配；若仅监督主初子区间速度子头（DirectAlign），会引入严重训练-推理失配，质量甚至低于未对齐基线。

Method: 构造位于有效块边界的状态，查询融合的块级速度预测器，监督跨度归一化的转移残差；统一支持 DPO、NFT 与 rollout 类目标，并保留原生少步采样器。同时构建统一后训练流水线：离线成对数据、无奖励半在线 continuation、在线 rollout，配合层级偏好选择与目标感知的块调度。

Result: 在 MiniMax-H3 Ref2VA 与自研 RefVid-V4 多参考视频生成上，BlockAlign 在结构合理性、参考一致性与视觉质量上稳定优于未对齐蒸馏模型和 DirectAlign。实现 PDD 5 倍少步 rollout 加速、端到端在线训练提速 3 倍；人工偏好显著提升：RefVid-V4 上 54.0% vs 33.3% 胜负（非平局胜率 61.8%），MiniMax-H3 上 51.3% vs 34.7%（59.7%），对 DirectAlign 最高 82.7% 胜率。

Conclusion: 偏好优化必须与蒸馏生成器的块级接口对齐，BlockAlign 通过接口一致的状态构造与残差监督，可直接对已部署的少步模型做后训练对齐，兼顾质量与效率。

Abstract: Directly aligning trajectory-distilled video generators slashes rollout costs and optimizes the deployed few-step model without teacher fine-tuning or re-distillation. However, conventional flow-matching preference objectives assume single-head local velocity supervision, mismatching the finite-interval, block-average transition interface of trajectory-distilled generators such as Parallel Decoding Distillation (PDD) and Parametric Trajectory Distillation (PTD). Naively supervising only the primary initial-subinterval velocity sub-head rather than the fused block-average velocity at block starts (DirectAlign) induces a severe training--inference mismatch that degrades quality below the unaligned baseline. We introduce BlockAlign, an interface-consistent preference optimization framework that constructs states at valid block boundaries, queries the fused block-level velocity predictor, and supervises span-normalized transition residuals-seamlessly supporting DPO-, NFT-, and rollout-style objectives while preserving the native few-step sampler. We further build a unified post-training pipeline integrating offline pairs, reward-free semi-online continuations, and online rollouts with hierarchical preference selection and objective-aware block scheduling. Experiments on multi-reference video generation across MiniMax-H3 Ref2VA and an in-house model RefVid-V4 show that BlockAlign consistently outperforms unaligned distilled models and DirectAlign in structural plausibility, reference consistency, and visual quality. Notably, our approach unlocks PDD's 5x few-step rollout speedup for 3x faster end-to-end online training, achieving statistically significant human preference gains over PDD baselines (54.0% vs. 33.3% wins/losses, or 61.8% non-tie win rate on RefVid-V4; 51.3% vs. 34.7%, or 59.7% non-tie win rate on MiniMax-H3) and up to an 82.7% win rate against DirectAlign.

</details>


### [168] [Structure-Guided Multi-Granularity Spatiotemporal Transformer for Efficient Monocular 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20361)
*Sheng Xu,Aoduo Li,Rongli He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知的多粒度时空Transformer，将骨架拓扑先验融入空间与时间表示，并通过关键帧注意力降低计算冗余，实现单目3D人体姿态估计的精度与效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多粒度方法偏重空间关节特征，对骨架结构（全局连通性与时间连续性）利用不足；同时对整段帧序列建模计算开销大。

Method: 三级特征提取器在关节、骨骼、肢体层级刻画人体拓扑；门控自适应空间注意力（GASA）将结构约束嵌入空间注意力；多粒度特征增强（MGFE）用层次化池化与融合强化局部细节与全局语义；时间分段融合（TSF）对齐跨粒度时间上下文；层次跨注意力（HCA）促进粒度间时空交互；关键帧注意力（KFA）筛选信息量大的运动片段以降低冗余与算力。

Result: 相较传统Transformer类方法，在单目3D人体姿态估计上取得更高的精度与效率。

Conclusion: 结构感知的多粒度建模对单目3D人体姿态估计有效，可同时兼顾精度与计算效率。

Abstract: Existing multi-granularity methods for monocular 3D human pose estimation emphasize spatial joint features while underusing skeletal structure, including global connectivity and temporal continuity. Moreover, modeling entire frame sequences incurs high computational costs. We propose a Structure-Aware Multi-Granularity Spatiotemporal Transformer that integrates structural priors across spatial and temporal representations for accurate and efficient pose estimation. A three-level feature extractor captures human topology at the joint, bone, and limb levels. Gated Adaptive Spatial Attention (GASA) embeds structural constraints into spatial attention, while Multi-Granularity Feature Enhancement (MGFE) uses hierarchical pooling and fusion to strengthen local details and global semantics. Temporal Segment Fusion (TSF) aligns temporal contexts across granularities to capture both short- and long-range motion patterns. Hierarchical Cross-Attention (HCA) promotes spatiotemporal interaction between granularities, and Keyframe Attention (KFA) selects informative motion segments to reduce redundancy and computational cost. Experiments demonstrate improved accuracy and efficiency over conventional Transformer-based approaches, highlighting the effectiveness of structure-aware multi-granularity modeling for monocular 3D human pose estimation.

</details>


### [169] [VisDrone-ReID: A Unified Multi-Category Object Re-Identification Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20372)
*Asmau Lawal Bilbis,Nathanael L. Baisa,Amudhavel Jayavel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VisDrone-ReID：一个基于 VisDrone2019-MOT 转换而来的无人机视角、十类别统一目标重识别基准（9,516 个身份、150,016 张图像、80 段序列），并采用留一法（LOO）评估协议与四种 CNN/Transformer 基线给出首个系统性能参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Re-ID 基准多为单类别评估，难以支撑跨多类目标的统一系统研发；同时 MOT 标注与 Re-ID 评测需求之间存在结构性不兼容。

Method: 设计原则化的 MOT→Re-ID 转换流程，解决序列内身份冲突、时间冗余及缺少身份级训练/测试划分三类问题；构建十类别（行人、人群、自行车、汽车、厢式货车、卡车、三轮车、遮阳三轮车、公交车、摩托车）UAV 基准，采用 leave-one-out 评估协议，并以 4 个代表性 CNN 与 Transformer 模型开展基线实验，进行逐类别分析。

Result: 所有模型均呈现一致的 Rank-1 与 mAP 间差距，表明任务具有超越最近邻匹配的真实检索难度；逐类别分析显示"行人"类别最具挑战性，视觉-语言预训练在语义区分度高的类别上带来可测增益。

Conclusion: VisDrone-ReID 为研究真实监控场景中尺度变化、视角挑战与类别多样性下的多类别目标重识别提供了统一平台。

Abstract: Object re-identification (Re-ID) benchmarks remain predominantly confined to single-category evaluations, limiting the development of unified systems capable of operating across diverse object types. This paper presents VisDrone-ReID, a unified multi-category Re-ID benchmark derived from the VisDrone2019-MOT dataset through a principled conversion pipeline. The pipeline resolves structural incompatibilities between multi-object tracking annotations and Re-ID evaluation requirements, addressing sequence-scoped identity collision, temporal redundancy, and the absence of a principled identity-level train/test split. The resulting benchmark comprises 9,516 identities and 150,016 images across ten object categories - pedestrian, people, bicycle, car, van, truck, tricycle, awning-tricycle, bus, and motor, sourced from 80 unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) sequences captured across urban environments. A leave-one-out (LOO) evaluation protocol is adopted to provide a comprehensive assessment. Baseline experiments across four representative Re-ID models spanning CNN and transformer architectures establish the first systematic performance reference on this benchmark. Results reveal a consistent Rank-1 to mAP gap across all models, suggesting that VisDrone-ReID presents a genuine retrieval challenge beyond nearest-neighbour matching. Per-category analysis identifies pedestrian as the most challenging category and reveals that vision-language pretraining provides a measurable advantage on semantically distinctive categories. VisDrone-ReID provides a consistent platform for studying multi-category object re-identification under the scale variation, viewpoint challenges, and category diversity characteristic of real-world surveillance scenarios.

</details>


### [170] [Trait Collapse in Text-to-Image Generation: Evaluating and Mitigating Personality Inconsistency in Visual Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20381)
*Yuanbo Tong,Haoyuan Li,Jibiao Jiang,Zirui Wang,Yuchen Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示T2I模型存在"特质崩塌"(trait collapse)现象，提出ViStoryBench-Personality诊断基准与免训练的TBV提示框架，将人格特质分解为可渲染视觉约束，Personality Alignment Score最高提升22.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉叙事中，可信角色不仅需跨场景外观一致，还应以反映人格的方式行动。现有T2I模型虽能较好保持视觉外观，却持续无法维持与人格相关的行为，且CLIP Score、DINO相似度等常规指标对此完全失察，抽象人格描述与场景级情绪标签亦无明显改善。

Method: 构建ViStoryBench-Personality故事级基准：含专家验证的Visual Cues Lexicon（Fleiss' κ=0.757）与六维指标套件（人格对齐、跨场景稳定性、行为一致性），评测7个T2I系统；并提出Trait-Behavior-Visual (TBV) 免训练提示框架，把人格特质分解为可渲染的视觉约束。

Result: 实验确认抽象特质标签与视觉证据之间存在持续语义鸿沟，且视觉身份一致性并不蕴含人格一致性；TBV使Personality Alignment Score最高提升+22.1%，在语言条件能力更强的模型上增益更大。

Conclusion: 人格一致的角色生成需从抽象标签转向可渲染视觉线索；ViStoryBench-Personality提供了系统诊断手段，TBV为免训练、可即插即用的改进方案，但其效果依赖模型的语言条件能力。

Abstract: In visual storytelling, a believable character must not only look consistent across scenes but also \textit{behave} in ways that reflect their personality. Current text-to-image (T2I) models preserve visual appearance effectively, yet consistently fail to maintain personality-relevant behavior---a failure we term \textit{trait collapse}. We show that conventional metrics (CLIP Score, DINO similarity) are blind to this failure, and that abstract personality descriptors and scene-level emotion labels do not produce consistent improvements over neutral prompts across models. To systematically diagnose trait collapse, we introduce \textbf{ViStoryBench-Personality}, a story-level benchmark combining an expert-validated Visual Cues Lexicon (Fleiss' $κ= 0.757$) with a six-dimensional metric suite covering personality alignment, cross-scene stability, and behavioral coherence.
