Evaluating seven T2I systems, we confirm a persistent semantic gap between abstract trait labels and visual evidence, and find that visual identity consistency does not imply personality consistency. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{Trait-Behavior-Visual (TBV)}, a training-free prompting framework that decomposes personality traits into renderable visual constraints. TBV improves Personality Alignment Score by up to $+22.1\%$, with larger gains in models with stronger language-conditioning capacity.

</details>


### [171] [Signs of Wear: Evaluating Intelligent Speed Assistance Under Synthetic Adverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20382)
*Johannes Fabian Hahn,Mao Shan,Julie Stephany Berrio Pere*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ISA-Wear数据集：用多模态图像编辑模型给真实街景限速标志添加雪、锈、植被、褪色、泥污等磨损，在受控条件下评估ISA系统，发现磨损首先破坏检测（检出率仅62%）而非识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ISA（智能速度辅助）基准未充分覆盖乡村道路常见的风化、脏污、被植被遮挡的限速标志，导致对真实场景鲁棒性评估不足；且需要能归因于磨损本身的受控评测方法。

Method: 用文本指令引导的多模态图像编辑模型，在街景限速标志上添加雪、锈、植被、褪色颜料、泥污，分三档严重度，必要时同步调整周边场景并保持视角；构建成对的原始/编辑图像，仅评估原始图像可被正确识别的帧；对21%发生几何形变的编辑用几何检查加人工复核过滤；以LPIPS距离度量严重度。

Result: 在716张磨损帧上：检测-再识别流水线中，检测器对磨损标志仅检出62%（原图100%）；漏检占识别失败的78–87%，零样本检测器呈现相同模式；给定真值框时分类器可识别78%，仅依赖检测器时降至56%。

Conclusion: 磨损对ISA的瓶颈在于检测环节而非识别环节，检测失败是识别失败的主因；现有基于裁剪框的基准高估了系统鲁棒性，需在完整检测-识别流水线上评估，并补充乡村磨损场景的测试数据。

Abstract: Intelligent Speed Assistance (ISA) systems rely on cameras to recognize speed-limit signs, yet existing benchmarks underrepresent the weathered, dirty, and overgrown signs commonly encountered on rural roads. We use generative image editing to introduce different forms of wear into real street-level images of speed-limit signs. The resulting ISA-Wear dataset contains paired original and edited images. Guided by text instructions, a multimodal image-editing model adds snow, rust, vegetation, faded paint, or mud to the speed-limit sign in a street-level frame at three severity levels. Where appropriate, the surrounding scene is adapted as well, and the viewpoint is kept. Every edited frame has an unedited counterpart, and we only evaluate frames whose original is recognized correctly, so any failure can be attributed to the wear. Since the model alters the sign's geometry in 21\% of the edits, all edits are filtered by a geometric check and a manual review. Severity is measured as the LPIPS distance between the original and the edited sign plate. On 716 worn frames, we find that in detect-then-read pipelines, wear breaks detection before it breaks reading. The detector finds only 62\% of the worn signs, even though it finds every one of them in the original frames. Missed detections account for 78--87\% of failed reads, a pattern that a zero-shot detector shares. Given the annotated sign box, as in the crop-based benchmarks, a classifier still reads 78\% of the worn signs, but only 56\% when it has to rely on the detector

</details>


### [172] [DominoBench: Evaluating Multi-Step Physical Reasoning from Still Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20385)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DominoBench基准（854个2D刚体场景图像、427个最小对、四级提示梯），评测10个家族30个模型，发现无辅助条件下无模型能可靠预测静止图像的物理结局：前沿模型在最关键人物上至多72%正确、成对双胞胎全对至多34%，仅在给出决定性计算及其所需量（含图像不可见的速度）后才达82–92%，表明失败环节在定量步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型能流畅描述场景并解答教科书物理题，这让人以为它们也能从一张静止图像推断由斜坡、平台、摆、跷跷板和多米诺构成的场景在球被推动后的结局。作者要检验这一主张，并定位模型究竟在哪一环节失败。

Method: 构建DominoBench：2D刚体场景静止图像，结局由物理模拟器计算；共854项、构成427个最小对，每对仅在某一个量上改变1–3个网格步长即可使"某个人是否存活"翻转；每项配有四级提示梯，末级给出决定结局的精确计算。在来自10个模型家族的30个模型上进行系统评测，考察无辅助、指向决定性步骤、以文本给出精确几何、以及写出决定性计算及所需量（含静止图像无法显示的速度）等不同信息条件下的表现。

Result: 无辅助情况下无模型可靠预测结局；Anthropic、Google、OpenAI的前沿模型在"命运翻转者"上正确率至多72%，一对双胞胎同时答对至多34%。指向决定性步骤帮助有限，以文本给出精确几何也几乎无益；一旦写出决定性计算及其所需量，同一批模型在决定性人物上达到82–92%，而小型开放权重模型仍接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 从静止图像进行物理预测的瓶颈在定量步骤：最强模型在被给定决定性量时能用好它，却不能自行产生该量。对需在物理世界中规划或行动的模型而言，流畅的场景描述并不构成正确预测的证据；此类模型必须无提示地找出并计算决定性量，DominoBench正是检验其能否做到。

Abstract: Can a vision-language model look at a picture of a chain reaction and tell what will happen? Recent multimodal models describe scenes fluently and solve textbook physics problems, which suggests they should also predict how a scene of ramps, ledges, pendulums, seesaws and dominoes plays out once a ball is pushed. We tested this claim on 30 models from 10 families and reached a clear conclusion: unaided, no model predicts the outcome reliably. We built DominoBench, a benchmark of still images of 2D rigid-body scenes whose outcome a physics simulator computes. It holds 854 items in 427 minimal pairs, in which a change of one to three grid steps in one quantity decides whether one person survives, and every item comes with a four-rung hint ladder ending in the exact calculation that decides the outcome. Unaided, frontier models from Anthropic, Google and OpenAI are right about the person whose fate flips in at most 72% of items and answer both twins of a pair correctly in at most 34%. Pointing at the deciding step helps little, and exact geometry given as text barely helps, yet once the deciding calculation and the quantities it needs, including speeds that a still image cannot show, are written out, the same models reach 82-92% on the deciding person, while small open-weight models stay near chance. Physical prediction from a still image therefore fails at the quantitative step: the strongest models use the deciding quantity when they are given it, but do not produce it on their own. For models that plan or act in the physical world, a fluent account of a scene is thus no evidence of a correct prediction: such models must find and compute the deciding quantity unprompted, and DominoBench tests whether they do.

</details>


### [173] [Training-Free Anonymization of Driving Data with Promptable Segmentation and 3D Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20412)
*Max A. Buettner,Mark Azer,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练匿名化流水线FM-Anonymize，结合可提示分割、3D人体网格恢复与生成式替换，在PP4AV与自录数据上人脸/车牌隐私召回率均达最优，且合成替换显著提升全身关键点AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶相机录制数据中的面部与车牌需在标注、共享与训练前匿名化；专用检测器易漏检小车牌及挡风玻璃后的面部。

Method: 免训练流水线：SAM 3定位人、脸、车辆并对车辆裁剪图缩放检索车牌、乘员与车窗；SAM 3D Body由头部朝向判断面部可见性并投影头部区域进行模糊；可选扩散修复依据估计姿态与关键点将合格模糊头部替换为合成人脸。

Result: PP4AV上模糊流水线保护97.8%人脸、95.5%车牌，默认设置下隐私召回最高；自录数据上为97.1%/93.4%，对比UrbanAnonymizer 93.7%/73.0%、EgoBlur 42.7%/40.7%、deface 96.6%人脸。合成替换将全身关键点AP由54.1%提至79.6%（自录）及39.9%提至68.3%（PP4AV）。

Conclusion: 免训练组合式流水线在隐私保护召回上优于专用匿名化工具，生成式替换还能在匿名化同时保持下游姿态估计可用性。

Abstract: Camera recordings for autonomous driving contain faces and license plates that should be anonymized before labeling, sharing, or training. Dedicated detectors can miss small plates and faces behind vehicle glass. We present FM-Anonymize, a training-free anonymization pipeline that combines promptable segmentation, 3D human mesh recovery, and generative replacement. SAM 3 locates people, faces, and vehicles and searches zoomed vehicle crops for plates, occupants, and windows. SAM 3D Body assesses face visibility from head orientation and projects head regions for blurring. Optional diffusion inpainting replaces eligible blurred heads with synthetic faces, guided by estimated pose and keypoints. On PP4AV, the blur pipeline protects 97.8% of faces and 95.5% of plates, the highest privacy recall of all compared methods at their default settings. On our recordings, where the localization thresholds were set, it protects 97.1% of faces and 93.4% of plates, compared with 93.7%/73.0% for UrbanAnonymizer, 42.7%/40.7% for EgoBlur, and 96.6% of faces for deface. Synthetic replacement raises full-body keypoint average precision over blurring from 54.1% to 79.6% on our recordings and from 39.9% to 68.3% on PP4AV. Code and configurations are available on https://github.com/max-a-ai/FM-Anonymize

</details>


### [174] [InterRSGaussian: Interactive Multi-view Instance Segmentation for 3D Remote Sensing Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20425)
*Zipeng Qi,Zhengxia Zou,Zhenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将交互式实例分割从单视图扩展到多视图框架，构建了遥感多视图交互分割数据集，并提出基于3D高斯（InterRSGaussian）的方法，实现跨视图一致的实例分割并可投影到3D点云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式实例分割研究主要针对单视图图像，难以全面理解复杂的三维遥感场景，因此需要将交互式实例分割从单视图扩展到多视图/3D框架。

Method: 1）构建遥感多视图交互式实例分割数据集，包含5个场景，每个场景约100个视角；2）提出InterRSGaussian——基于3D高斯表示的实例分割方法，用户可在任意视角对目标点击，无需人工标注即可生成跨所有输入视图一致的实例分割结果，并可投影至3D点云实现3D分割；3）引入先验蒸馏网络进行初始化，以提升训练效率和分割性能。

Result: InterRSGaussian支持任意视角的点选交互，输出跨视图一致的实例分割结果，并可投影到3D点云获得3D分割；先验蒸馏初始化提升了训练效率与分割表现。

Conclusion: 该研究将传统二维遥感场景感知扩展为三维多视图交互框架，能够在复杂环境中以用户交互方式更全面地理解目标对象。

Abstract: Remote sensing image segmentation plays a critical role in various applications. Interactive instance segmentation, a specialized task within image segmentation, aims to generate precise segmentation results for target objects based on user input prompts. However, previous studies on interactive instance segmentation primarily focus on single-view images, limiting their ability to comprehensively understand complex 3D remote sensing scenes. In this study, we extend interactive instance segmentation from a single-view to a multi-view framework, which can also be easily converted to a 3D segmentation format, thus achieving more accurate and comprehensive 3D remote sensing scene perception across multiple views. First, we construct a multi-view interactive instance segmentation dataset for remote sensing, comprising five distinct scenes, each captured from about one hundred different views. Second, we propose InterRSGaussian, a novel instance segmentation method based on 3D Gaussians. InterRSGaussian enables users to provide point clicks on target objects from any arbitrary view and generates view-consistent instance segmentation results across all input views without requiring manual annotations. The resulting multi-view segmentation can further be projected onto 3D point clouds for 3D segmentation. To improve training efficiency and segmentation performance, we introduce a prior distillation network for initialization. Overall, this study extends traditional 2D remote sensing scene perception into a 3D multi-view framework, enabling a more comprehensive understanding of target objects in complex environments with user interactivity.

</details>


### [175] [UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge 2026: Site-Calibrated Synthetic Data and Multi-Detector Fusion for Roadside 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20431)
*Arnav Kumar,Brijesh Soni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是IEEE BigData Cup 2026 UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR挑战赛（V2X-Real赛道）的技术报告：仅用CARLA合成路侧LiDAR数据训练车辆/行人/卡车3D检测器，并生成难以与真实扫描区分的合成点云。方案含标定至固定传感器站位的CARLA管线（精确建模含无回波光束的距离图像）、VoxelNeXt/PV-RCNN/SECOND三模型组合推理、逐扫描对地平面校准与多高度推理、BEV聚类融合及类别特异后处理，最终综合得分0.8947。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧LiDAR真实标注稀缺，需验证仅靠合成数据即可完成Sim2Real 3D检测与真实感合成数据生成；同时真实与合成间存在传感器模型、地面高度和域差异。

Method: 1) 基于CARLA构建并依据无标注真实开发扫描标定管线，对齐固定传感器站点，精确建模距离图像（含no-return光束）；2) 三个仅在合成数据上训练的3D检测器（VoxelNeXt、PV-RCNN、SECOND）组成组合推理；3) 每个扫描对齐合成地平面，在多个地面高度上推理；4) BEV聚类融合候选框，再用类别特异后处理精修朝向与框尺寸并过滤低置信检测。

Result: 最终提交综合得分0.8947；消融显示逐扫描地面校准使真实扫描mAP提升一倍以上，跨高度与跨模型框融合进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 精细的合成传感器建模与免标注标定、辅以多模型多高度推理融合和类别特异后处理，可有效弥合路侧LiDAR的Sim2Real鸿沟；地面高度对齐是收益最大的环节。

Abstract: This report summarizes the work submitted by team arnav007 (submission ID 1054222) for the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge (V2X-Real track) at the IEEE BigData Cup 2026. The primary objective is to train 3D detectors for vehicles, pedestrians, and trucks using only synthetic roadside LiDAR data, and to generate synthetic scans that are realistic enough to be indistinguishable from real ones. A CARLA based pipeline is developed and calibrated to the fixed sensor site, accurately modeling the range image, including no-return beams, based on unlabelled real development scans. A combined inference detector, using multiple 3D detectors (VoxelNeXt, PV-RCNN, and SECOND) trained exclusively on synthetic data, is also proposed. Each scan is aligned to the synthetic ground plane and processed through the detectors at multiple ground heights. The resulting bounding boxes are fused using bird's-eye view (BEV) clustering, followed by class-specific post-processing to refine heading, box size, and filter low-confidence detections. The final submission achieved a combined score of 0.8947. The ablation study shows that per-scan ground leveling more than doubled the real-scan mAP, while fusing boxes across different heights and models further improved performance.

</details>


### [176] [ReScene: Incremental Scene Chronology from Sparse Revisits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20433)
*Wei Qin Chuah,Zeji Hui,Amirali Khodadadian Gostar,Alireza Bab-Hadiashar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReScene将每次访问存储为不可变的紧凑增量更新，按序应用即可精确恢复任一早前状态，在多状态基准上以更少存储实现优于顺序3DGS检查点的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景随时间在多次访问间发生物体位置、存在性与形状变化，需保留每个历史状态，但每次重访仅有少量图像。为每次访问保存完整模型会重复存储未变场景，而更新单一共享模型又会破坏早期状态。因此需要一个既能精确回溯、又存储高效的增量表示。

Method: 1) 将每次访问记录为一个不可变紧凑更新，按顺序应用即可精确重建任一早前访问，无需保留历史图像；2) 通过高斯合成权重，把前序重建与新图像间的差异归因到各个高斯，从而抑制过时高斯、精化持久高斯；3) 新可见表面由学习的多视图深度初始化；4) 提出Multi-State基准，涵盖非刚性形变与部分材质移除，含带真值几何的合成场景。

Result: 相比顺序3DGS检查点，在未压缩历史存储少2.5–4.8倍的情况下取得更高渲染质量；在相近PSNR下，相比顺序ChronoGS将前景深度误差降低3.2倍，表明精确渲染并不必然意味着精确几何。

Conclusion: ReScene以不可变增量更新实现可精确回溯的场景时间序列重建，兼顾存储效率与几何精度，并揭示了渲染质量与几何质量可能脱节，提供了配套基准与代码。

Abstract: Scene chronology aims to reconstruct a scene across visits separated by hours or days, during which objects may change in position, presence or shape. Such reconstruction must retain every earlier state, yet each revisit often provides only a few images. Storing a complete model for each visit duplicates the unchanged scene, whereas updating a single shared model alters earlier states. We present ReScene, which stores each visit as an immutable compact update, so that any earlier visit can be recovered exactly by applying the stored updates in order, without retaining past images. To localise each update, ReScene attributes the disagreement between the preceding reconstruction and the new images to individual Gaussians through their compositing weights. This attribution guides the suppression of outdated Gaussians and the refinement of persistent ones, while newly visible surfaces are initialised from learned multi-view depth. We also introduce Multi-State, a benchmark of non-rigid deformation and partial material removal, including synthetic scenes with ground-truth geometry. ReScene achieves quality higher than sequential 3DGS checkpointing with 2.5--4.8$\times$ less uncompressed history storage. At comparable PSNR, it also reduces foreground depth error by 3.2$\times$ relative to sequential ChronoGS, showing that accurate rendering does not necessarily imply accurate geometry. Code and benchmark are available at https://waychin-weiqin.github.io/ReScene/.

</details>


### [177] [LEDGE: Separating Critic and Editor Failures in Text-to-Image Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20446)
*Vyom Agarwal,Mokshda Gangrade,Samet Ayhan,Giacinto Paolo Saggese*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEDGE框架将T2I自纠正解耦为"诊断"与"编辑"两个瓶颈：oracle指令实验表明编辑器能力是主瓶颈（644张失败图中435张无法修复）；用oracle标签监督critic可将精确诊断率从33.3%提至70.8%，在SD1.5上把GenEval从42.2%升至59.4%，并可跨模型、跨编辑器、跨基准迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I自纠正（VLM critic诊断+图像编辑器执行）多以端到端指标评估，无法区分失败源于"诊断错误"还是"编辑器无法执行有效指令"，导致改进方向不明。

Method: 提出LEDGE：从GenEval编译oracle编辑指令，固定生成图像与编辑器，从而隔离编辑器的纠正能力与critic的诊断准确率；另用来自不相交训练提示的oracle标签对critic进行监督训练。

Result: SD3.5 Large上oracle指令使GenEval由72.0%升至81.2%（基线VLM critic为74.1%），但644张失败图中仍有435张未被修复，暴露显著editor gap；oracle监督使精确诊断率由33.3%升至70.8%；同一编辑器下，SD1.5由42.2%升至59.4%（基线critic 50.9%），SD3.5 Large由72.0%升至76.0%，且仅在SD1.5上训练；改进可迁移至未见物体类别、第二个编辑器及独立的T2I-CompBench判分器与提示；小规模人工评估确认了排序但增益小于基于检测器的评估。

Conclusion: 自纠正存在"知道该改什么"与"能够改好"两重上限：当前编辑器能力是主要瓶颈，而critic可通过oracle监督显著提升并具良好泛化性；自动检测器评估可能高估人类感知层面的实际收益。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) self-correction combines a vision-language model (VLM) critic that diagnoses errors and proposes instructions with an image editor that executes them. End-to-end evaluation obscures whether failures arise from incorrect diagnoses or an editor unable to execute valid instructions. We introduce LEDGE (Learning Error Diagnosis in Generation and Editing), a framework that separates these bottlenecks. LEDGE compiles oracle edit instructions from GenEval while holding the generated images and the editor fixed, isolating the editor's correction capability from the critic's diagnostic accuracy. On Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large, oracle instructions raise GenEval from 72.0% to 81.2%, versus 74.1% for the baseline VLM critic. Yet 435 of 644 failed images remain unrepaired, exposing a substantial editor gap. We also use oracle labels from disjoint training prompts to supervise the critic. Oracle supervision raises exact diagnosis from 33.3% (the same VLM with our structured output, untrained) to 70.8%. With the same editor, the resulting critic improves GenEval from 42.2% (uncorrected) to 59.4% on Stable Diffusion 1.5, against 50.9% for the baseline critic, and from 72.0% to 76.0% on Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large, despite training only on Stable Diffusion 1.5. Improvements transfer to unseen object classes, a second editor, and independent T2I-CompBench judges and prompts. A small human evaluation confirms the ranking in estimated net corrections but suggests a smaller gain than detector-based evaluation. LEDGE thus distinguishes two limits of self-correction: knowing what to fix and being able to fix it.

</details>


### [178] [VISTA: A Framework for Vision-Informed Semantic Traffic Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20458)
*Murat Arda Onsu,Poonam Lohan,Burak Kantarci,Aisha Syed,Matthew Andrews,Sean Kennedy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA：路侧用V-JEPA+注意力池化将交通视频压缩为未来场景嵌入并经V2X传输，车载TinyLlama+视觉适配器结合自然语言查询进行可解释推理，实现高精度碰撞预警并大幅降低通信负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X传输原始交通视频带宽开销大；在资源受限车辆上直接部署大型多模态模型不现实。需在通信效率与车载算力约束间取得平衡。

Method: 路侧监控单元采用视频联合嵌入预测架构（V-JEPA）加注意力池化模块，将视频片段转为紧凑的未来场景嵌入；嵌入经V2X链路传给车载系统，由带自定义视觉适配器的TinyLlama小型语言模型结合自然语言交通查询进行解释与推理。

Result: 数字孪生城市交通环境中，未来碰撞预测准确率97.77%、F1 89%，通信负载较原始RGB视频传输降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 验证了基于嵌入的多模态智能在低时延、通信高效、安全导向的IoT交通系统中可行；车辆无需接收原始视频即可获得可解释的交通态势感知与碰撞风险识别。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) is transforming intelligent transportation systems by connecting roadside cameras, edge computing units, vehicles, and traffic infrastructure into a shared sensing and decision-making ecosystem. However, transmitting raw traffic video over vehicle-to-everything (V2X) links is bandwidth-intensive, while deploying large multimodal models directly on resource-constrained vehicles is often impractical. To address these limitations, this article proposes VISTA (Vision-Informed Semantic Traffic Awareness), a multimodal vision-language edge-intelligence framework that combines predictive video representation learning with lightweight language-based reasoning to provide communication-efficient traffic situational awareness. In the proposed architecture, roadside surveillance units employ the Video Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (V-JEPA) and an attentive-pooler module to convert traffic video clips into compact future-scene embeddings. These embeddings are transmitted through V2X links to vehicle-mounted systems, where a TinyLlama small language model, enhanced with a custom visual adapter, interprets the embeddings alongside natural-language (NL) traffic queries. VISTA enables vehicles to obtain interpretable traffic information, reason about upcoming scene dynamics, and identify potential collision risks without receiving raw video streams. Experimental results in a digital-twin urban traffic environment show that VISTA achieves 97.77% accuracy and an 89% F1 score for future collision prediction while reducing the communication payload by several orders of magnitude compared with raw RGB video transmission. These results demonstrate the feasibility of embedding-based multimodal intelligence for low-latency, communication-efficient, and safety-oriented IoT-enabled transportation systems.

</details>


### [179] [LiveView: Real-Time Camera-Controlled Re-filming of Streaming Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20461)
*Youcan Xu,Xinjie Huang,Jiaxin Shi,Hengye Lyu,Zhen Wang,Jun Xiao,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出教师-学生蒸馏框架，将高保真双向重拍摄教师模型蒸馏为少步因果自回归学生模型，实现低延迟、长时稳定的在线相机可控视频重拍摄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频重拍摄方法多为离线全序列处理，依赖双向注意力或昂贵的基于深度的几何预处理，无法满足低延迟流式场景的需求；同时在线流式生成还需解决跨视角同步与内容一致性、长时程稳定性两大挑战。

Method: 1) 教师-学生框架：将高保真双向重拍摄教师蒸馏为少步因果自回归学生；2) Cross-Frame Injection：将每个到来的源帧与其同步的目标潜变量交错注入，建立直接的跨视角对应关系；3) Shared Relative RoPE：将时间索引约束在预训练范围内以稳定长时程生成；4) Cycle Streaming Tuning：在流式训练中让学生模型接触扩展的循环轨迹。

Result: 实现了低延迟的在线视频重拍摄，具备精确的相机控制，并在长时间流式推演中保持持续的时空一致性。

Conclusion: 通过蒸馏少步因果自回归模型并结合跨帧注入与位置编码、训练策略设计，可有效替代离线双向方案，实现在线实时相机可控视频重拍摄。

Abstract: Real-time camera-controlled video re-filming aims to synthesize target views from a streaming source video under online user-specified camera trajectories. This task poses two key challenges: maintaining synchronization and content consistency across views, and achieving low-latency rendering with long-horizon stability. Existing approaches are predominantly designed for offline full-sequence processing and rely on bidirectional attention or costly depth-based geometric preprocessing, making them unsuitable for low-latency streaming scenarios. To address these limitations, we develop a teacher-to-student framework that distills a high-fidelity bidirectional re-filming teacher into a few-step causal autoregressive student. We introduce Cross-Frame Injection to interleave each incoming source frame with its synchronized target latent, thereby establishing direct cross-view correspondence and improving content consistency. To ensure stable long-horizon generation, we further propose Shared Relative RoPE, which bounds temporal indices within the pretrained range, and Cycle Streaming Tuning, which exposes the student to extended cyclic trajectories during streaming training. These designs enable low-latency online video re-filming with precise camera control and sustained spatiotemporal consistency over long streaming rollouts.

</details>


### [180] [Improving X-Ray Baggage Instance Segmentation with Shared Point-Based Mask Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20476)
*Abdulaziz Alqahtani,Qasim Umer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究在 Cascade Mask R-CNN 中引入权重共享的基于点的掩码精修模块，提升 X 射线行包图像中违禁品的实例分割质量，MLP 变体取得最高平均掩码 AP 72.47（较基线 +1.97）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X 射线行包图像中目标重叠投影与复杂轮廓导致违禁品难以精确勾画边界，现有检测器输出的低分辨率掩码（28×28）难以刻画不确定区域，且缺乏遮挡物监督。

Method: 以 ResNet-101 为骨干、结合注意力特征金字塔的 Cascade Mask R-CNN 为基础，构建单一共享的点式掩码精修模块，跨级联阶段与精修层级复用；逐步更新不确定掩码位置，将掩码分辨率由 28×28 提升至 112×112；并对比 Transformer 与 MLP 两种变体以考察所选点间交互的作用。在官方 PIXray 划分、三个训练种子下评估十种配置。

Result: Transformer 与 MLP 精修器平均掩码 AP 分别为 71.79 与 72.47，未精修检测器为 70.50，增益 1.29 与 1.97 AP；MLP 配置最高；两种变体均改善常见检出目标的边界吻合度，但检测诊断指标未呈现相应正向增益。

Conclusion: 共享的点式精修可在该级联架构中利用既有目标掩码标注提升分割质量，无需额外遮挡物监督；其泛化性仍需在独立 X 射线数据集上验证。

Abstract: Overlapping projections and complex object contours make prohibited items difficult to delineate in X-ray baggage images. This study investigates shared point-based mask refinement to improve instance segmentation within Cascade Mask R-CNN. The framework combines a ResNet-101 backbone with an attention-based feature pyramid and progressively updates uncertain mask locations, increasing mask resolution from $28 \times 28$ to $112 \times 112$. A single refinement module is shared across cascade stages and refinement levels, with transformer and multilayer perceptron (MLP) variants used to examine the role of interactions among selected points. Ten configurations were evaluated on the official PIXray split across three training seeds using the original target-instance annotations for evaluation. The transformer and MLP refiners achieved mean mask average precision (AP) of 71.79 and 72.47, respectively, compared with 70.50 for the unrefined detector, yielding gains of 1.29 and 1.97 AP points. The MLP achieved the highest mean mask AP among the evaluated configurations. Both variants also improved boundary agreement on commonly detected objects, supporting improved mask delineation, while detection diagnostics did not establish a corresponding positive gain. These findings demonstrate that shared point-based refinement can improve segmentation quality in the tested cascade architecture using existing target-mask annotations, without additional occluder supervision. Evaluation on independent X-ray datasets is needed to determine how well these improvements generalize.

</details>


### [181] [Beyond Seeing: Fine-Grained Preference Validation and Severity-Aware Alignment for Mitigating Visual Hallucinations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20478)
*Abdulaziz Alqahtani,Alwaleed Alharthi,Muzammil Behzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究实现并对齐了一套细粒度幻觉偏好优化流水线，实验表明对齐后生成式幻觉指标显著下降，但视觉覆盖度降低、POPE对抗F1未提升，说明收益具有基准依赖性而非严重度感知对齐的普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型常生成图像不支持的对象、属性或关系（细粒度幻觉），需要检验基于偏好对齐的缓解方案是否真实有效，并厘清判定、修复与训练目标各自的贡献边界。

Method: 构建已实现的流水线：规范化公开细粒度幻觉监督数据、用视觉语言评判器校验chosen-rejected偏好对、修复被拒的chosen回复，并以标准或严重度感知DPO训练LLaVA适配器。使用16,143条规范化反馈记录和8,386对偏好数据，对比直接训练、零样本与两样本判定的严重度边际变体。

Result: 零样本变体将Object HalBench CHAIRs由53.00降至36.00、CHAIRi由15.72降至10.41；对齐模型AMBER-Hal由35.90降至25.10–26.90，但视觉覆盖度同步下降；基模型POPE对抗F1最高（84.18 vs 对齐后83.87–83.91）。

Conclusion: 对齐可带来基准相关的生成式幻觉降低，但不构成严重度感知对齐的普遍优越性；作者区分了流水线组件与实验实际使用的偏好路径，并指出无法将增益单独归因于判定、修复或训练目标。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can produce fluent responses containing objects, attributes, or relations unsupported by the image. We study an implemented alignment pipeline that normalizes released fine-grained hallucination supervision, validates chosen-rejected preference pairs with vision-language judges, repairs rejected chosen responses, and trains LLaVA adapters with standard or severity-aware direct preference optimization. The study uses 16,143 normalized feedback records and 8,386 released preference pairs, comparing direct training with zero-shot and two-shot judged severity-margin variants. The zero-shot variant reduces Object HalBench CHAIRs from 53.00 to 36.00 and CHAIRi from 15.72 to 10.41. Across aligned models, AMBER-Hal decreases from 35.90 to 25.10-26.90, accompanied by reduced visual coverage. In contrast, the base model retains the highest valid POPE adversarial F1, 84.18 versus 83.87-83.91 after alignment. These results support benchmark-dependent reductions in generative hallucination rather than uniform superiority of severity-aware alignment. We distinguish implemented pipeline components from the preference paths used in the reported experiments and discuss the limits of attributing gains to judging, repair, or the training objective individually.

</details>


### [182] [Pointillist Maize: 3D Reconstruction of Field-Grown Maize Using RGB UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20502)
*Dewi Endah Kharismawati,Toni Kazic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 使用消费级无人机RGB视频结合SfM/MVS重建田间玉米器官，考察采集轨迹与抽帧策略对重建完整性与点云离散度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维重建可将田间表型测量细化到单器官水平，但玉米细薄、柔性、相互遮挡的器官以及植株运动使重建困难；需评估低成本的消费级无人机影像能否恢复可辨识的田间玉米结构，并厘清采集策略的影响。

Method: 用消费级无人机RGB视频，经COLMAP（SfM+MVS）重建田间生长的玉米。对比同一视频的两组各295帧：单轨道5帧/秒 vs. 三高度轨道（"裙摆"式）2帧/秒；去除地面后比较点数、点集中度、空洞与孤立点比例。在密植苗行中比较单轨道1帧/秒、增加蛇形航线、以及3帧/秒抽帧下的可见空洞比例；另于2024年另一田块测试"卷曲"轨迹。

Result: 重建可辨识出叶片、茎、雄穗分枝、果穗、花丝与气生根。等帧数对比中去地面后分别得520,601与375,318个点：单轨道点更集中于植株结构、可见空洞更少；裙摆式散点更少、孤立点比例略低。密植行中可见空洞占比从单轨道1帧/秒的17.57%降至增加蛇形航线后的5.07%，原轨道3帧/秒时为2.68%；增加观测也提高了与植株运动相关的点离散度。卷曲轨迹可区分单株且无明显重复结构，但仍有大量空洞（主要在土表）。

Conclusion: 消费级无人机影像可恢复可辨识的玉米器官，但采集策略在重建完整性与点云离散度之间存在权衡；定量表型应用前仍需独立的几何精度验证。

Abstract: Three-dimensional reconstruction can extend field phenotyping to individual maize organs, but thin, flexible, occluding structures and plant movement complicate reconstruction. We evaluated whether consumer-grade UAV RGB video processed with Structure from Motion and multi-view stereo could recover recognizable field-grown maize structures, and examined the effects of acquisition trajectories and frame sampling. COLMAP reconstructions preserved recognizable leaves, stems, tassel branches, ears, silks, and brace roots. For an equal-frame comparison, we reconstructed one plant from two sets of 295 frames from the same video: a single orbit sampled at 5 frames/second and three orbits at different altitudes (ribbon skirt) sampled at 2 frames/second. After ground removal, the reconstructions contained 520,601 and 375,318 points, respectively. The single orbit yielded greater point concentration within plant structures and fewer visible gaps, while the ribbon skirt yielded fewer scattered points and slightly lower proportions of isolated points. In densely planted nursery rows, visible voids occupied 17.57% of the rendered scene with one orbit at 1 frame/second, decreasing to 5.07% with additional serpentine passes and 2.68% when the original orbit was sampled at 3 frames/second. Additional observations also increased point dispersion associated with plant movement. A curlicue trajectory tested in a different field in 2024 produced distinguishable individual plants without visible duplicated structures, although substantial voids remained, mainly on the soil surface. These results demonstrate the feasibility of recovering recognizable maize organs from consumer UAV imagery and reveal trade-offs between acquisition strategies, reconstruction completeness, and point dispersion. Independent geometric validation is still needed before quantitative phenotyping.

</details>


### [183] [How Reliable Is Visual Token Selection? Rethinking Pruning in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20505)
*Chaehee Song,Seokil Ham,KiHyun Seol,Jihyun Lee,Yubin Choi,Changick Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了多模态大模型中视觉token剪枝的选择信号可靠性，揭示局部不稳定与全局不一致两大问题，并提出免训练框架CoRePruner。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝依赖注意力或多样性等选择信号，但这些信号的可靠性从未被系统检验。作者通过语义保持扰动下的分析发现：注意力重要性在扰动下非均匀偏移，仅凭幅值无法区分稳定证据与不稳定显著（局部不稳定）；注意力与多样性方法选出的子集差异显著却性能相当，说明有用证据并不局限于唯一子集（全局不一致）。

Method: 提出CoRePruner，一个免训练框架，将指令感知多样性（instruction-aware diversity）与可靠性感知注意力（reliability-aware attention）相结合来构建可靠的token集合。

Result: 在图像基准、多种MLLM架构及不同token预算下，CoRePruner均提升性能，尤其在激进剪枝下效果显著，并在视频基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 研究确立了"可靠性"应作为视觉token选择的核心考量，并为可靠token集构建提供了实证依据与可行方案。

Abstract: Visual token pruning improves the efficiency of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) by removing redundant visual tokens, yet the reliability of existing selection signals has not been systematically examined. We analyze token selection under semantics-preserving perturbations and across representative pruning criteria, revealing two limitations. First, attention-based importance shifts non-uniformly under perturbations, so magnitude alone cannot distinguish stable evidence from unstable saliency (local instability). Second, attention- and diversity-based methods select substantially different subsets despite comparable performance, showing that useful evidence is not confined to a unique subset (global inconsistency). These findings motivate reliable token-set construction. We instantiate this perspective in CoRePruner, a training-free framework combining instruction-aware diversity with reliability-aware attention. Across image benchmarks, multiple MLLM architectures, and token budgets, CoRePruner improves performance, especially under aggressive pruning, and remains competitive on video benchmarks. Our results establish reliability as a central consideration in visual token selection.

</details>


### [184] [VoxSAOR: Semantic-Aware Occupancy Robustification via Aleatoric Uncertainty Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20507)
*Mengfei Duan,Yuheng Zhang,Guoqiang Zhao,Yuanfan Zheng,Kailun Yang,Zhiyong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VoxSAOR框架，用特征空间代理建模体素认知不确定性，通过局部语义一致性先验、跨视角语义一致性过滤及类条件对角高斯分布刻画稳定-模糊特征转移，并用渐进扰动式多预测头集成提升三维语义占据预测的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉三维占据预测受遮挡、深度歧义和投影误差影响，体素特征可靠性在空间上高度不均；现有方法直接将体素表示映射为语义预测，未显式建模这种观测导致特征变化，因而缺乏对不可靠区域的鲁棒处理。

Method: 1) 计算局部语义一致性（LSC）得到体素级结构稳定性先验；2) 利用跨视角语义共识过滤不可靠语义对应；3) 在每个语义类别内，将空间匹配的稳定体素与模糊体素构成“稳定→模糊”特征转移，用类条件对角高斯分布建模特征位移与离散度作为代理；4) 提出认知状态集成学习AREL，按结构可靠性调制扰动强度，在递增尺度上对特征施加扰动，训练独立参数化的多个预测头；5) 推理时关闭代理建模与采样，各头共享同一观测特征并对输出概率取平均，得到确定性共识预测。

Result: 在多个数据集与多种占据骨干网络上实验表明，VoxSAOR有效提升语义占据预测精度、长尾类别识别能力及预测校准性能。

Conclusion: 通过特征空间代理显式刻画观测诱导的不确定性，并配合免采样的确定性集成推理，VoxSAOR在几乎不增加推理开销的前提下增强了三维占据预测的可靠性；代码将开源。

Abstract: Vision-based 3D occupancy prediction provides a dense semantic representation of scenes for autonomous driving and embodied perception, yet remains vulnerable to occlusion, depth ambiguity, and projection errors that induce spatially varying reliability in voxel features. Existing methods typically map voxel representations to semantic predictions without explicitly modeling such observation-induced feature variations. To address this limitation, we propose VoxSAOR, a semantic-aware occupancy robustification framework that models aleatoric uncertainty through feature-space proxies. It first quantifies Local Semantic Consistency (LSC) to derive a voxel-wise structural stability prior and uses cross-view semantic consensus to filter unreliable semantic correspondences. Within each semantic category, spatially matched stable and ambiguous voxels form stable-to-ambiguous feature transitions, which are modeled by a class-conditional diagonal Gaussian distribution, capturing both feature displacement and dispersion. Guided by these proxy distributions, Aleatoric Regime Ensemble Learning (AREL) trains independently parameterized prediction heads using feature perturbations at progressively increasing scales, with perturbation strength modulated by structural reliability. At inference, proxy modeling and sampling are disabled, and all heads process the same observed features; their output probabilities are then averaged to produce a deterministic consensus prediction without requiring sampling. Experiments across multiple datasets and occupancy backbones demonstrate the effectiveness of VoxSAOR in improving semantic occupancy prediction, long-tail recognition, and predictive calibration. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/MengfeiD/VoxSAOR.

</details>


### [185] [What the Averages Hide: Monocular Metric Depth Models Still Fail in Everyday Scenes, Mostly on Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20518)
*Hualie Jiang,Yujie Luo,Ziyang Song,Ximeng Li,Zhiqiang Lou,Rui Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出逐图像评估框架（failure rate、结构与尺度分离指标、跨模型共享失败分析），在11个架构18项评估、7个基准4406张图像上表明日常场景的单目度量深度仍未解决且主要困难在尺度：最佳评估失败率仍达7.7%，78.8%的失败图像结构完好、尺度校正后98.3%不再失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本单目度量深度评估依赖在数据集上平均度量误差与尺度对齐后误差，平均值无法揭示：多少图像度量深度仍严重错误、深度被高估还是低估、不同模型是否在同一批图像上失败。因此需要基于逐图像结果的细粒度评估。

Method: 构建逐图像评估体系：(1) 失败率（metric δ1 < 0.5）；(2) 结构用尺度对齐后指标衡量，尺度用对齐系数的偏差、离散度与绝对误差衡量；(3) 跨模型比较以区分共享失败与模型特化失败。应用于11个近期架构、含/不含真值内参共18项模型评估，覆盖MoGe-2所用的7个日常场景基准（4,406张图像）。

Result: 日常场景的度量深度估计尚未解决，剩余困难主要来自尺度：18项评估中最佳者失败率仍为7.7%（数据集等权）；在所有失败样本中78.8%结构完好，尺度校正后98.3%不再失败。至少被6个架构同时失败的图像占数据7.3%，集中于HAMMER与DIODE；仅由其他族架构选出的这类图像仍占前6模型34%–40%的误差，而同等数量随机图像仅占14%–18%。增加物体可降低多数模型的尺度误差；真值内参与多数族中更大模型可降低但无法消除失败。

Conclusion: 建议报告指标时分列失败率、结构与尺度，并按失败模型数量分层给出结果。将公开代码、带共享失败标签的逐图像结果，以及基于TUM RGB-D构建的两个诊断集。

Abstract: Zero-shot monocular metric depth models are evaluated by averaging metric and scale-aligned scores across datasets. Averages cannot show how many images still have severely wrong metric depth, whether depth is under- or overestimated, or whether different models fail on the same images. We propose an evaluation built on per-image results: the failure rate (metric $δ_1$ < 0.5); scale-aligned metrics for structure, and the bias, dispersion and absolute error of the alignment scale for scale; and a cross-model comparison that separates shared from model-specific failures. We apply it to 11 recent architectures with and without ground-truth intrinsics (18 model evaluations) on the 7 everyday-scene benchmarks (4,406 images) used by MoGe-2. Metric depth estimation is not solved in everyday scenes, and the remaining difficulty is mainly scale: the best of the 18 evaluations still has a failure rate of 7.7% (datasets weighted equally); pooled over all failures, 78.8% have intact structure, and after scale correction 98.3% no longer fail. Images failed by at least 6 architectures make up 7.3% of the data, concentrated on HAMMER and DIODE; even when selected only by architectures of other families, they hold 34% to 40% of the error of the top 6 models, against 14% to 18% for as many random images from the same datasets. Adding objects reduces the scale error of most models; ground-truth intrinsics and, in most families, larger models reduce the failures but do not remove them. We recommend reporting failure rates, structure and scale separately, and results stratified by how many models fail. We will release the code, the per-image results with shared-failure labels, and two diagnostic sets built from TUM RGB-D.

</details>


### [186] [AFRNet: Adaptive Frequency Redistribution Network for Image Deraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20522)
*Dezhi Zhang,Shangrong Yang,Husheng Guo,Zhijie Shen,Wenjian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应频率重分配网络AFRNet，通过跨子带调制与子带补偿解决频域去雨中各子带独立处理导致的信息不互通问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频率感知去雨方法在频域去除雨纹，但雨纹与背景纹理常共存于若干相关子带中；以往模型对各子带独立增强，缺乏跨子带信息交互，导致单一子带内雨与干净内容无法分离，出现雨纹残留或细节过平滑。

Method: AFRNet包含两个核心模块：(1) 自适应频率重组合模块AFRM，在小波子带间进行跨带调制，实现退化频率分量的信息交互与自适应重组；(2) 子带补偿块SCB，在小波域通过子带间注意力，利用编码器子带特征补偿解码器子带的退化，缓解重建中的细节损失。

Result: 大量实验表明AFRNet在多个去雨基准上取得有竞争力的性能，且结果表现一致稳定。

Conclusion: 跨子带信息交互与编码器-解码器子带补偿是提升频域去雨效果的有效途径，AFRNet为频域去雨提供了可推广的解决范式。

Abstract: Image deraining aims to recover rain-free images from rain-degraded observations. Recent frequency-aware deraining methods have demonstrated promising performance by removing rain artifacts in the spectral domain. However, since rain artifacts and background textures often appear in several correlated subbands, prior frequency-domain deraining models still enhance frequency subbands separately. Such band-wise processing limits information exchange across subbands. Without context from other subbands, rain and clean content that appear together in one subband cannot be separated. This leads to either residual rain streaks or over-smoothing of fine details. To address this limitation, we propose AFRNet, an Adaptive Frequency Redistribution Network for image deraining. First, we introduce an Adaptive Frequency Recombination Module (AFRM) that performs cross-band modulation among wavelet subbands, enabling information interaction and adaptive recombination of degraded frequency components. We then develop a Subband Compensation Block (SCB) that compensates for decoder subband degradation by leveraging encoder subband features through inter-subband attention in the wavelet domain, alleviating detail loss during reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AFRNet achieves competitive performance and consistent results across diverse deraining benchmarks.

</details>


### [187] [SmoothShift: Disrupting Visual Adversarial Attacks on LVLMs at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20537)
*Xiaoqing Chen,Jiayi Ouyang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示视觉对抗扰动通过逐层放大的表征差异影响LVLM，且其有效性依赖细粒度扰动模式与模型patch计算的耦合；据此提出免训练防御SmoothShift（局部平滑+空间变换）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉对抗扰动仅带来细微输入变化却能严重损害大型视觉语言模型（LVLM），其机理尚不清晰，缺乏针对性、轻量的防御手段。

Method: 先分析对抗扰动的优化过程与在模型中的传播：发现强攻击依赖更精细的迭代优化，细微扰动诱导的表征差异经视觉编码器逐层放大并延续至语言模型；同时，破坏patch内输入模式与模型计算的对齐可降低干净-对抗表征差异。基于此提出SmoothShift：结合局部平滑（削弱细粒度扰动模式）与空间变换（打乱其与编码器patch网格的对齐），无需模型更新、辅助网络或额外推理。

Result: 在3个LVLM系列、4个数据集上，以及8个分类数据集上鲁棒微调的CLIP模型上，跨8种攻击方法与多种扰动预算评估显示：鲁棒性提升且基本保持干净性能；在采用混合梯度BPDA与EOT的防御感知攻击下仍有增益。

Conclusion: 对抗有效性取决于细粒度扰动模式及其与模型计算的耦合；SmoothShift以极低开销有效提升LVLM与CLIP的对抗鲁棒性，兼顾干净性能与实用性。

Abstract: Visual adversarial perturbations can substantially impair large vision-language models (LVLMs) despite introducing only subtle input changes. To understand how these small changes cause substantial errors, we investigate their optimization and propagation through the model, revealing two key findings. First, stronger attacks rely on finer iterative optimization, while subtle input perturbations induce representation discrepancies that are amplified layer by layer through the vision encoder and persist in the language model. Second, disrupting the alignment between within-patch input patterns and the model's computation reduces clean-adversarial representation discrepancies and substantially weakens attacks. Together, these findings suggest that adversarial effectiveness depends on both fine-grained perturbation patterns and their coupling with the model's computation. Motivated by these findings, we propose SmoothShift, a lightweight, training-free defense that combines local smoothing and spatial transformations. Smoothing weakens fine-grained perturbation patterns, while spatial transformations disrupt their alignment with the encoder's patch grid. SmoothShift requires no model updates, auxiliary networks, or additional model inference. Experiments cover three LVLM families on four datasets and robustly fine-tuned Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) models on eight classification datasets. Evaluations across eight attack methods and multiple perturbation budgets demonstrate improved robustness while largely preserving clean performance. SmoothShift also provides robustness gains under defense-aware attacks using mixed-gradient BPDA with EOT.

</details>


### [188] [Learning a Task-Adaptive Low-Dimensional Semantic Space for Improved Visual Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20553)
*Nikalal Kaldera,Wanqing Li,Chris Bunn,Philip O. Ogunbona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaSS提出一种任务自适应的低维语义空间学习方法，通过对预训练文本嵌入进行参数化几何变换增强类间可分性，并用轻量MLP对齐冻结视觉特征，在动作识别、视频与图像分类上平均提升超3个点、最高8.5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义视觉分类依赖预训练文本编码器诱导的语义空间，但该空间维度高、与视觉特征几何弱对齐、且对下游分类判别性不足。

Method: 对预训练文本嵌入施加参数化几何变换，构造低维任务自适应语义空间：通过距离重塑扩大类间间隔，并以原型保持的语义约束提升类内紧致度；随后用轻量MLP将冻结视觉编码器特征对齐到TaSS，无需微调骨干；并给出保持语义一致性、增强类间分离与控制类内方差的理论分析。

Result: 在骨架人体动作识别、视频分类与图像分类基准上稳定优于现有语义对齐方法，平均提升超过3个百分点，困难数据集上最高提升8.5个点。

Conclusion: 在保持语义关系结构的前提下构造判别性几何的任务自适应语义空间，是一种高效且不需微调骨干的语义对齐方案。

Abstract: Semantic visual classification commonly aligns visual embeddings with semantic spaces induced by pretrained text encoders. However, such spaces are typically high-dimensional, weakly aligned with visual feature geometry, and insufficiently discriminative for downstream classification tasks. We propose TaSS, a framework for learning a task-adaptive low-dimensional semantic space that preserves semantic structure while explicitly optimizing class separability. TaSS is constructed through a parameterized geometry transformation of pretrained text embeddings that enlarges inter-class margins via distance reshaping and improves intra-class compactness through prototype-preserving semantic constraints. The resulting semantic space maintains meaningful semantic relationships while producing a discriminative geometry tailored for classification. Frozen visual encoder features are subsequently aligned to TaSS using a lightweight MLP, enabling improved discriminative learning without fine-tuning pretrained backbones. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that TaSS preserves semantic consistency while enforcing enhanced inter-class separation and controlled intra-class variance. Extensive experiments on skeleton-based human action recognition, video classification, and image classification benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing semantic alignment approaches, with gains exceeding 3 percentage points on average and up to 8.5 points on challenging datasets.

</details>


### [189] [PerturbJEPA: Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning of Cellular Perturbation Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20575)
*Jiangshuan Pang,Youzhe He,Yiting Deng,Wangyang Tang,Huinan Xu,Qi Lu,Shiping Liu,Zhenkun Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PerturbJEPA，将处理组与对照组均值基因表达的差异编码为效应向量，并把潜在质心位移与之一致对齐、正则化跨群体效应向量变化，以避免"干预对比坍缩"，在Tahoe-100M上实现91.46%的联合药物-剂量Recall@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别产生细胞响应的药物与剂量，需要在保留每个细胞背景内处理效应的同时不丢失背景间差异。联合嵌入预测器可能在保持编码器区分背景的同时，抹除同一背景内处理组与对照组之间的差异，作者将这一失效模式称为"干预对比坍缩"（interventional contrast collapse）。

Method: PerturbJEPA以处理组与对照组平均基因表达之差构造效应向量；将两组潜在质心的位移与该效应向量对齐，并对效应向量施加正则化以保留跨群体对的变化；理论上证明当效应向量变异超过均方根对齐误差时，批内位移不可能全部重合。模型采用分离的药物与剂量排序目标训练，从公共对照群体预测候选响应并与观测响应匹配，无需细胞配对或表达重建。

Result: 在留出的已知干预与细胞背景组合上评估3,592个Tahoe-100M查询，每个查询在固定背景特异目录中设有700余个药物-剂量候选。PerturbJEPA取得91.46%宏平均联合药物-剂量Recall@1，比STATE高3.40个百分点（95%药物簇自助区间1.72–5.07）。受控诊断表明，即使表征整体保持分散，处理-对照差异仍可能收缩；在固定药物排序目标下，向对齐项加入效应向量正则化可将联合Recall@1从18.57%提升至87.47%。

Conclusion: 干预对比坍缩是联合嵌入预测的关键风险，仅靠表征整体分散性无法诊断；显式建模并正则化效应向量对保持处理效应至关重要，能显著提升药物-剂量联合识别性能。

Abstract: Identifying the drug and dose that produced a cellular response requires preserving treatment effects within each cellular background. A joint-embedding predictor can match its targets while ignoring the intervention if its encoder preserves differences across backgrounds but erases differences between treated and control populations within each one. We call this failure interventional contrast collapse. PerturbJEPA encodes the difference in mean gene expression between treated and control populations as an effect vector. It aligns the displacement between their latent centroids with this vector and regularizes the vectors to preserve variation across population pairs. We show that the measured displacements cannot all coincide within a batch when effect-vector variation exceeds the root-mean-square alignment error. Trained with separate drug and dose ranking objectives, the model predicts candidate responses from a common control population and matches them to the observed response without cell pairing or expression reconstruction. We evaluate 3,592 Tahoe-100M queries on held-out combinations of known interventions and cellular backgrounds, each with over 700 drug-dose candidates in a fixed, background-specific catalog. PerturbJEPA achieves 91.46% macro joint drug-dose Recall@1, exceeding STATE by 3.40 percentage points (95% drug-cluster bootstrap interval of 1.72 to 5.07 points). Controlled diagnostics show that treatment-control differences can shrink even when representations remain globally dispersed. With the drug ranking objective fixed, adding effect-vector regularization to alignment raises joint Recall@1 from 18.57% to 87.47%.

</details>


### [190] [SCoRe: Support-Calibrated Role Fusion for Training-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20582)
*Abhishek Kathpal,Adarsh Jagan Sathyamoorthy,Utsav Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCoRe是一个免训练框架，融合两个视觉基础模型分割器（一者定位准、一者轮廓好），用于跨域少样本分割，平均前景mIoU达72.738%，较RSRM提升5.210个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于CD-FSS的视觉基础模型具备互补能力：一个模型能正确定位目标，另一个模型能给出更好的轮廓，但单一模型难以兼顾定位与轮廓精度。

Method: 提出支持-校准角色融合（SCoRe）：1）为主概念识别一个主导FSS任务的分割器；2）在主导分割器预测边界处利用近像素带与远像素带进行确定性边界校正；3）由另一分割器执行区域校正，保守地剪除成分、填补空洞并桥接短间隙。

Result: 在10个数据集的3,314个匹配五样本情节上评估RSRM与FSS-SAM3；SCoRe平均前景mIoU为72.738%，较RSRM提升5.210个百分点（配对95% CI [4.618, 5.972]），单数据集最大增益达8.461个百分点，并在7/10数据集上取得最佳点估计。

Conclusion: 双VFM互补角色融合可有效提升CD-FSS精度，且无需训练，具有稳定且显著的性能增益。

Abstract: We present SCoRe (Support-Calibrated Role fusion), a framework to utilize two complementary vision foundation model-based (VFM) segmenters for training-free cross-domain few-shot segmentation (CD-FSS). Our approach is based on the observation that current VFMs for CD-FSS possess capabilities where one model locates the correct object, while another model computes the better contour. Our approach uses: 1. a method to identify a VFM segmenter to lead Few-Shot Segmentation (FSS) task for a given concept, 2. a novel deterministic boundary correction method using bands of near- and far-pixels at the lead segmenter's predicted boundary of an object, and 3. a region correction mechanism for the other segmenter to conservatively prune components, fill holes, and bridge short gaps on the lead segmenter's predicted masks to improve accuracy. We evaluate SCoRe using RSRM and FSS-SAM3 across 3,314 matched five-shot episodes from 10 datasets. SCoRe achieves 72.738% foreground mIoU averaged across datasets, a gain of 5.210 percentage points over RSRM (paired 95% CI [4.618, 5.972]), with per-dataset gains up to 8.461 points over the strongest standalone method and the best point estimate on 7 of 10 datasets.

</details>


### [191] [From Joint Risk to Decision Invariance: Decision-Preserving Regularization for Exemplar-Free Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20619)
*Qi Zhu,Ziang Gan,Wanting Zhang,Libao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出决策保持正则化（DPR）：通过对旧类两两logit margin变化施加惩罚（等价于中心化相对logit惩罚），并结合谱白化与基于闭式仿射映射的旧类高斯统计传输来校准全局分类器，在无样本持续学习中兼顾准确率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无样本持续学习无法直接访问历史数据来优化联合风险。作者观察到：在分类任务中，保持模型对已学类别的决策不变有助于保留旧知识，由此引出对旧类logit margin施加正则的思路；同时，全局分类器需在表示漂移下仍对所有已见类保持校准。

Method: 1) 决策保持正则化：惩罚所有旧类成对logit margin的变化，代数上化简为中心化相对logit惩罚；2) 谱白化：消除分类器的谱尺度偏差；3) 统计传输与校准：用闭式仿射映射将配对当前任务特征映射以传输旧类高斯均值与协方差到更新后的特征空间，再用这些分布与当前任务特征校准全局分类器。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet、ImageNet-Subset与ImageNet-1K的冷启动协议下，与SOTA方法相比，DPR在多数设置中取得更高准确率，且保持较高效率。

Conclusion: 保持旧类决策、消除谱尺度偏差并对漂移后的旧类统计进行闭式传输与校准，是无样本持续学习中兼顾性能与效率的有效途径。

Abstract: Exemplar-free continual learning seeks to minimize joint risk over all seen tasks, yet historical data are unavailable for direct optimization. We observe that, for classification, preserving the model's decisions on previously learned classes helps retain old knowledge. This motivates Decision-Preserving Regularization (DPR). Penalizing changes in all pairwise old-class logit margins reduces algebraically to a centered relative-logit penalty. We further apply spectral whitening to remove the classifier's spectral scale bias. Because the global classifier supplies the decision constraints for the next stage, it must remain calibrated across all seen classes despite the drift of stored old-class statistics during representation updates. We therefore fit a closed-form affine map from paired current-task features to transport old-class Gaussian means and covariances into the updated feature space, then calibrate the global classifier using these distributions and current-task features. Across CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet, ImageNet-Subset, and ImageNet-1K under cold-start protocols, comparisons with state-of-the-art methods show that DPR achieves higher accuracy in most settings while remaining highly efficient. Code is available at https://github.com/bnufw/dpr.

</details>


### [192] [Example-Based Spatial Guidance for Training-Free Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20623)
*Younghwan Kil,Giung Nam,Joonhyeong Park,Jinwoo Shin,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的EBSG方法，用可编辑样本包提供细粒度空间引导以精准擦除违规概念，并引入基于VLM的细粒度评估协议，在多个安全基准上达到SOTA且可迁移至SD3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I扩散模型安全防护与评估在执行和评价上均较为"单体化"，仅停留在宽泛不安全概念的粗粒度分类，忽视违规的具体构成因素，导致学习信号弱，模型坍塌（model collapse）可绕过安全检查。

Method: EBSG为免训练方法：利用用户可编辑的示范包（exemplar packs，文本-图像对），将宽泛概念分解为具体子概念，提供显式、局部化的空间引导信号，实现精细概念操控而非整图粗粒度远离；同时提出基于VLM的评估协议进行细粒度评估，替代易被模型坍塌绕过的二值评估器。

Result: 在多样安全数据集与概念类别上取得SOTA擦除效果：覆盖4个裸露基准、7个I2P有害概念切片、4个MJA隐喻类别，并支持多概念移除及迁移到现代骨干网络SD3。

Conclusion: 细粒度、局部化的空间引导与细粒度VLM评估能更稳健地实现概念擦除与安全控制，且具备多概念与跨骨干网络的通用性。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models often synthesize policy-violating content, necessitating robust safeguards and rigorous evaluation frameworks. However, current approaches remain largely monolithic in both execution and evaluation, limited to coarse-grained categorization within broad unsafe concepts. Such approaches fail to account for the specific constituent factors of a violation, resulting in weak learning signals and allowing model collapse to bypass safety checks. To overcome these limitations, we propose Example-Based Spatial Guidance (EBSG), a training-free method that utilizes user-editable exemplar packs to provide granular spatial guidance for precise concept steering. Unlike existing methods that coarsely steer the entire image away from a broad concept, EBSG decomposes these categories into specific sub-concepts via exemplar text-image pairs, providing explicit, localized steering signals for more robust safety control. Furthermore, we introduce a vision language model (VLM)-based evaluation protocol that provides a fine-grained assessment, avoiding the pitfall of conventional binary evaluators that permit model collapse to bypass safety checks. Empirically, EBSG achieves state-of-the-art erasure performance across diverse safety datasets and concept categories, spanning four nudity benchmarks, seven I2P harmful-concept slices, and four MJA metaphor categories, while supporting multi-concept removal and transferring to a modern backbone, SD3.

</details>


### [193] [ASCG: Adaptive Spatial Classifier Guidance for Surgical Concept Suppression in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20625)
*Younghwan Kil,Joonhyeong Park,Giung Nam,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应空间分类器引导(ASCG)，按需触发、局部抑制有害概念，并引入基于VLM的细粒度评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I扩散模型安全机制多为全局、无差别干预，导致语义漂移与图像质量下降；且NudeNet等传统指标无法评估语义完整性。

Method: ASCG：以概率监控判定有害性、按需激活引导；利用Grad-CAM实现局部化抑制以移除有害概念；并构建VLM-based评估框架，细粒度分析安全性与上下文保持。

Result: ASCG在安全与保真之间取得平衡，语义保真优于常规方法；与现有框架具备即插即用协同性，达到state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 按需、局部化的引导是兼顾安全性与生成质量的有效路径，VLM评估可弥补传统指标在语义完整性上的缺陷。

Abstract: While text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models achieve remarkable quality, they risk producing harmful content. Existing safety mechanisms often rely on indiscriminate, global interventions that induce unintended semantic drift and degrade image quality. To address this, we propose Adaptive Spatial Classifier Guidance (ASCG), a necessity-driven framework that employs probabilistic monitoring to activate guidance selectively upon harm detection. By leveraging Grad-CAM for localized suppression, ASCG effectively removes harmful concepts while preserving superior semantic fidelity compared to conventional methods. Furthermore, we overcome the limitations of traditional metrics like NudeNet---which fails to assess semantic integrity---by introducing a VLM-based evaluation framework to provide a fine-grained, robust analysis of both safety and context preservation. Our results demonstrate that ASCG not only balances safety and fidelity but also exhibits remarkable plug-and-play synergy with existing frameworks to establish state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [194] [PixelBench: Evaluating Language Models for Pixel-Art Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20628)
*Mutian Hong,Jianxuan Yao,Mingkun Yue,Changkai Li,Hower Yang,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PixelBench，系统评测语言模型在像素画生成与编辑任务上的能力；通过代码调用绘图工具的智能体环境创作作品，结合人类评估与多模态大模型评判，发现通用语言模型（GPT-6 Astra）表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素画在游戏与数字内容中广泛使用，需在有限分辨率与调色板约束下满足可读性、空间连贯性与色彩和谐；语言模型在视觉创作上潜力渐显，但其像素画创作能力缺乏系统性评测。

Method: 构建PixelBench基准，形式化文本到图像生成与指令引导编辑两类任务；设计语言模型智能体通过代码调用绘图工具的创作环境；采用人工评估并以多模态大模型作为评判者，验证自动评分与人类偏好的一致性；在多样主题与画布尺寸上比较不同模型与配置。

Result: GPT-6 Astra在平均评判分数与平均人类排名上均取得最高，表明通用语言模型在像素画创作上具有潜力；自动化评判与人类偏好呈现一致性。

Conclusion: PixelBench为像素画创作提供了系统评测框架，验证了LLM智能体结合工具调用进行像素画创作的可行性；公开任务、全部作品、人类排名及评判协议以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Pixel art is widely used in games and digital content creation, requiring readable forms, coherent spatial relationships, and harmonious colors under limited resolution and palette constraints. Language models show growing promise in visual creation, yet their pixel art creation capabilities lack systematic evaluation. We introduce PixelBench, formalizing text-to-image generation and instruction-guided editing for pixel art and providing a benchmark for these tasks. We further present a creation environment in which language model agents invoke drawing tools through code to generate and edit pixel art. We assess the quality of generated artwork through human evaluation and employ a multimodal large language model as a judge, validating the agreement between its automated assessments and human preferences. We compare language models and configurations across diverse subjects and canvas sizes. In our experiments, GPT-6 Astra achieves both the highest average judge score and the best average human rank, highlighting the potential of general-purpose language models for pixel art creation. We release the tasks, all artworks, the human rankings, and the judge protocol.

</details>


### [195] [Event-Guided Stereo Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20629)
*Hanyu Xuan,Youtao Yao,Cheng Zhao,Kun Li,Xin Du,Fei Wang,Zhiliang Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次探索事件相机引导的立体视频超分辨率（SVSR），提出融合事件驱动纹理增强与跨视点纹理交互的框架，提升快速运动与复杂光照下的重建质量与立体一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVSR方法依赖帧间时序与双目空间互补线索，但在快速运动和复杂光照下难以恢复高频纹理细节，导致重建质量与立体一致性受损；事件相机具备高时间分辨率与宽动态范围，可提供运动与纹理线索，但其在SVSR中的潜力尚未被挖掘。

Method: 提出事件引导SVSR框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 事件引导纹理增强（EGTE）模块，结合双向时序特征传播与事件引导的纹理细化，恢复复杂运动区域的高频细节；(2) 跨视点纹理交互（CVTI）模块，建模跨视点空间对应关系并聚合互补纹理信息。整体融合事件驱动的运动/纹理信息、帧间时序依赖与跨视点空间关系。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建质量和立体一致性上一致优于现有超分辨率方法。

Conclusion: 事件相机引导为SVSR提供了有效的新范式，通过事件-时序-跨视点三方面信息协同，可有效恢复受运动影响的细节并保持立体一致性。

Abstract: Stereo Video Super-Resolution (SVSR) aims to reconstruct high-resolution stereo videos by exploiting temporal cues across consecutive frames and complementary spatial cues between stereo views. However, existing methods struggle to recover high-frequency texture details under rapid motion and complex illumination, compromising reconstruction quality and stereo consistency. Event cameras provide valuable motion and texture cues with high temporal resolution and wide dynamic range, yet their potential for SVSR remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we present the first investigation of event-guided SVSR and propose a novel framework. Specifically, we develop an Event-Guided Texture Enhancement (EGTE) module that combines bidirectional temporal feature propagation with event-guided texture refinement to restore high-frequency details in complex motion regions. Subsequently, we introduce a Cross-View Texture Interaction (CVTI) module to model cross-view spatial correspondences and aggregate cross-view complementary texture information. With this design, our method effectively integrates event-driven motion and texture information, inter-frame temporal dependencies, and cross-view spatial relationships to recover motion-affected details and maintain stereo consistency. Extensive experiments on both real-world and synthetic datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing super-resolution methods in reconstruction quality and stereo consistency. Project Page: https://wzlwlm13.github.io/EvSVSR

</details>


### [196] [Instructions Before Actions: Prospective and Retrospective Language Supervision for Driving Vision-Language-Action Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20631)
*Angel Martinez-Sanchez,Ross Greer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出ADVLAT-Engine同步语音指令、道路视频与车辆轨迹，区分"前瞻性指令"与"回溯性标注"，并据此揭示驾驶VLA模型的轨迹预测能力不等于指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶VLA模型能接受导航指令并预测轨迹，但轨迹误差降低并不能证明模型真正遵循指令；同时驾驶-语言监督数据的采集方式（动作前下达的前瞻性指令 vs 动作后书写的回溯性标注）长期未被系统区分与评估。

Method: 构建ADVLAT-Engine，用于对齐口语指令、道路场景视频与车辆轨迹；在48条回放路线上对比三款导航App与乘客指令的语言差异（以Google Maps与乘客共同播报的87个操作为样本）；对四个开源驾驶VLA在导航App前瞻性驾驶数据微调前后进行评估；在真实路口用反事实指令测试指令响应性；另收集四人回溯性标注（定量对比分析待完成）。

Result: 乘客指令比导航App更依赖场景上下文；微调使乘客驾驶轨迹误差降低23-59%，但指令效应不一致；真实路口的反事实指令显示更强响应性：微调后的Alpamayo 2 Super在15 m与5 m处准确率分别提升31和18个百分点，Qwen-Drive在离散命令下比在自然语言句子下更可靠地执行替代操作。

Conclusion: 研究将"机动预测"与"指令遵循"明确区分，并建立了比较前瞻性与回溯性监督的框架；回溯性标注的内容、时序与下游学习效果的受控对比分析尚待完成。

Abstract: Driving vision-language-action (VLA) models can accept navigation instructions, yet predicting a driver's trajectory does not establish that a model follows the instruction. We study how driving-language supervision is collected and evaluated, distinguishing prospective instructions issued before an action from retrospective labels written after observing it. We develop ADVLAT-Engine to synchronize spoken instructions, road-scene video, and vehicle trajectories, and compare three navigation apps on 48 replayed routes. On 87 maneuvers announced by both Google Maps and a passenger, the passenger uses more scene-dependent language. We evaluate four open driving VLAs before and after fine-tuning on prospective navigation-app drives. Fine-tuning reduces passenger-drive trajectory error by 23-59%, but instruction effects remain inconsistent. At real intersections, counterfactual instructions reveal stronger responsiveness: fine-tuned Alpamayo~2 Super gains 31 and 18 accuracy points at 15 and 5~m, while Qwen-Drive follows alternative maneuvers more reliably with a discrete command than with a sentence. We additionally collected retrospective annotations from four people for the driving recordings and describe controlled comparisons of annotation content, timing, and downstream learning; those analyses are pending. Our findings separate maneuver prediction from instruction-following and establish a framework for comparing prospective and retrospective supervision.

</details>


### [197] [Counterfactual Feature Steering for Adjacency-Preserving Identity Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20637)
*FNU Shivam,Layth Sliman,Ramana Reddy,Donald Adjeroh,Binod Bhattarai,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出反事实特征引导(CFS)，在稀疏自编码器潜空间做有界推理时编辑，实现文本到图像扩散模型的身份遗忘并保持邻近身份；提出AWUS指标衡量擦除与附带损伤的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份遗忘需移除模型生成特定个体的能力，但现有方法在擦除目标身份时易损害相邻相似身份（附带损伤），缺少同时刻画擦除与保持的评估指标。

Method: 一次性训练SAE并附着于文本编码器；通过对比遗忘与保留分布导出目标方向；按每个token与该方向的当前对齐度施加成比例的逐token投影，得到反事实表示；提出AWUS衡量擦除-损伤权衡，并用ArcFace扩展为面部身份相似度。

Result: 在Celeb-100衍生的4,000条提示、100个名人身份上，CFS在目标擦除与相邻/通用概念保持间取得最佳权衡，且在不同干预强度下稳定，优于其他引导式方法；ArcFace独立评估与10,000条COCO描述研究进一步验证身份与通用保持；SD1.4上定量裸体抑制基准与定性艺术风格示例显示跨概念迁移。

Conclusion: 有界推理时的反事实特征引导可在SAE潜空间有效实现身份遗忘并保持相邻身份，兼具稳定性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Identity unlearning in text-to-image diffusion models is increasingly important for privacy, safety, and ethical deployment, as it aims to remove the model's ability to generate specific individuals. In this work, we study identity unlearning with \emph{adjacency preservation}, where the goal is to remove a target individual's identity while preserving related non-target identities. We propose Counterfactual Feature Steering (CFS), a method requiring one-time SAE training that performs bounded inference-time concept editing in the latent space of a sparse autoencoder attached to the text encoder. CFS derives a target direction by contrasting forget and retain distributions, then applies a per-token projection proportional to each token's current alignment with this direction, yielding counterfactual representations that suppress the target while limiting collateral damage. To evaluate adjacency preservation, we introduce the Adjacency-Weighted Unlearning Score (AWUS), which measures the tradeoff between target erasure and collateral damage, and extend it to face-specific identity similarity using ArcFace. Across 4,000 prompts derived from Celeb-100, spanning 100 celebrity identities, CFS achieves the strongest evaluated tradeoff between identity erasure of target identities and preservation of adjacent and generic concepts, while remaining stable across intervention strengths where other steering-based methods degrade. Independent ArcFace evaluation and a 10,000-caption COCO study further examine identity and general preservation. Quantitative nudity-suppression benchmarks and qualitative artistic-style examples provide evidence of transfer beyond identities on SD~1.4.

</details>


### [198] [Defect-Aware Masked Autoencoders for Unsupervised Clustering of Steel Surface Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20645)
*Shrey Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Defect-aware MAE，一种无标签掩码自编码器，用于钢铁表面缺陷聚类；在 NEU-DET 保留集上达到 98.6% 匈牙利匹配准确率、NMI 0.968、ARI 0.967、轮廓系数 0.838，且在同编码器与训练计划下优于 SimCLR 对比基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢铁表面缺陷的可靠识别对自动化质检至关重要，但监督方法依赖人工标注，成本高昂。因此需要能够直接从无标注表面图像中学习有效缺陷表征的方法。

Method: Defect-aware MAE：每张图像切分为 196 个 patch，随机遮蔽 147 个、仅 49 个输入 ViT 编码器；采用基于纹理能量的掩码策略，优先遮蔽结构信息丰富的 patch；浅层解码器重建 HOG 描述子以捕获局部纹理与边缘方向；使用强增强与中间层读出构成最终图像特征。预训练不使用类别标签、边界框、检测分支或对比损失。

Result: 在 NEU-DET 保留划分上，UMAP 降维后经 Ward 聚类，取得 98.6% 匈牙利匹配准确率、0.968 NMI、0.967 ARI、0.838 轮廓系数；在相同编码器与训练计划下，于 5 个随机种子上在准确率、NMI、ARI 与轮廓系数上均优于 SimCLR 对比基线。

Conclusion: 无标签的纹理能量引导掩码 + HOG 重建的自监督预训练可为钢铁缺陷聚类提供判别性强、结构良好的特征表征，并能超越对比学习基线，表明无需标注即可实现高效缺陷表征学习。

Abstract: Reliable recognition of steel surface defects is essential for automated quality inspection, but supervised methods require manually annotated images that are costly to produce. This creates a need for methods that can learn useful defect representations directly from unlabelled surface images. This work presents Defect aware MAE, a label free masked autoencoder for clustering steel surface defects. Each image is divided into 196 patches, with 147 masked and 49 processed by a Vision Transformer encoder. Texture energy based masking preferentially hides structurally informative patches, while a shallow decoder reconstructs HOG descriptors that capture local texture and edge orientation. Strong augmentation and an intermediate layer readout are used to form the final image features. Pretraining uses no class labels, bounding boxes, detection branch, or contrastive loss. On the held out NEU DET split, UMAP followed by Ward clustering achieves 98.6% Hungarian matched accuracy, 0.968 NMI, 0.967 ARI, and 0.838 silhouette score. Under an identical encoder and training schedule, it exceeds a SimCLR contrastive baseline in accuracy, NMI, ARI and silhouette scores across 5 seeds.

</details>


### [199] [Competent Autonomous Driving Through Fine Tuning Vision Language Action Models with Symbolic Traffic Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20648)
*Barkin Dagda,Vinal Asodia,Iman Sharifi,Peng Wei,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出规则引导的VLA框架，将《英国公路法规》条款形式化为可执行一阶逻辑规则，通过符号规则反馈的强化学习优化驾驶策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型可统一感知、推理与控制，但仅提升动作预测精度无法保证行为符合交通法规，需将显式规则约束引入策略优化。

Method: 先用专家演示做监督微调，再用强化学习精调，奖励项鼓励遵守编码规则并惩罚偏离专家动作；用PyReason将《英国公路法规》中限速适应、跟车距离、路权优先等条款形式化为一阶逻辑规则；冻结的VLM提取场景谓词，结合车辆状态评估候选控制动作的预期后果；在CARLA数据上离线对比三种基于组的RL方法。

Result: 相比监督基线，动作预测准确率与规则符合度均提升；最佳变体将总体规则通过率从92.9%提升至97.6%，评估样本中未观察到违反路权优先规则的情况。

Conclusion: 符号规则反馈可有效提升VLA策略与显式交通法规标准的一致性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models offer a unified approach to perception, reasoning, and control for autonomous driving, but accurate action prediction alone does not establish compliance with traffic regulations. This paper presents a rule-guided VLA framework that incorporates symbolic traffic-rule feedback into policy optimisation. The framework combines supervised fine-tuning on expert demonstrations with reinforcement learning (RL) refinement using a reward that encourages encoded-rule compliance while penalising deviations from expert actions. Selected provisions of the United Kingdom Highway Code are formalised as executable first-order logic rules using PyReason, covering speed adaptation, following distance, and road-user priority. A frozen vision-language model extracts scene predicates, which are combined with vehicle-state information to assess the projected consequences of candidate control actions. Offline evaluation on held-out data collected in the CARLA simulator compares three group-based RL methods. The results show improvements in both action prediction accuracy and encoded-rule compliance over the supervised baseline. The best-performing variant increases the overall rule pass rate from 92.9% to 97.6%, with zero observed violations of the encoded road-user-priority check in the evaluated samples. These findings support symbolic rule feedback as a means of improving VLA policy alignment with explicit traffic-rule criteria.

</details>


### [200] [RAMP: Rethinking Multimodal Evaluation through Answer Readout Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20653)
*Jianye Zeng,Qiao Yikang,Duan Huang,Ziying Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出RAMP框架，系统比较不同答案读出（readout）协议对多模态大模型评测结果的影响，发现读出方式可使准确率变动达20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评测通常将模型输出转换为离散答案，但转换协议本身决定了哪些分数成为预测，可能显著影响报告结果，此前缺乏系统性考察。

Method: 提出RAMP（Readout Aware Multimodal Protocol）框架，在固定模型检查点与配对视觉问题条件下，比较直接yes/no token logits、表面形式分数、带类型或反向的A/B接口等多种读出方式，并进一步分析提示措辞、候选形式、选项位置、跨任务效应与校准；另做独立离线重放验证可复现性。

Result: 在500道POPE对抗性问题上，typed与bare读出使SmolVLM-256M准确率变化−20.2个百分点、TinyLLaVA-1.1B变化−16.4、Qwen2.5-VL-3B变化+1.8；配对转移显示即使净差异小，预测仍大量改变：ChartQA中104道二分类题有79道正确性改变，而净差异仅+1.0点；离线重放以保存的原始分数复现16,140个首token决策且完全一致。

Conclusion: 答案读出是多模态评测中可测量的组成部分，RAMP为模型比较提供了可复现的分析协议。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are often evaluated by converting model outputs into discrete answers. The conversion protocol determines which scores become predictions and can strongly influence the reported results. We introduce RAMP (Readout Aware Multimodal Protocol), a systematic framework that compares direct yes/no token logits, surface-form scores, and typed or reversed A/B interfaces under fixed model checkpoints and paired visual questions. On 500 POPE adversarial questions, typed versus bare readout changes accuracy by $-20.2$ percentage points for SmolVLM-256M, $-16.4$ for TinyLLaVA-1.1B, and $+1.8$ for Qwen2.5-VL-3B. Paired transitions reveal extensive prediction changes even when the net accuracy difference is small: 79 of 104 binary ChartQA items change correctness for SmolVLM-500M, despite a $+1.0$-point net difference. We further analyze prompt wording, candidate forms, option position, cross-task effects, and calibration. An independent offline replay reproduces 16,140 first-token decisions from saved raw scores with exact agreement. Our findings establish answer readout as a measurable component of multimodal evaluation and provide a reproducible protocol for analyzing model comparisons.

</details>


### [201] [Room for Supervision: Training-Free Dataset Distillation for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20680)
*Ziang Gan,Qi Zhu,Libao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRASP 提出一种无需模型训练/推理的目标检测数据集蒸馏方法，通过联合搜索图像选择与空间布局，将密集的原生标注打包进有限像素，在 MS COCO 与 PASCAL VOC 上以 0.25%–2% 压缩率取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏多集中于单标签图像分类；目标检测需要对多个目标的类别与边界框进行精确标注，并要求视觉内容与标注一致。已有检测蒸馏流程依赖已训练模型，且可能改变目标与背景间的空间关系，因此需要一个不依赖网络、能保留原始监督信息与空间布局的蒸馏方案。

Method: GRASP 不使用网络训练或推理，直接在像素层面打包密集原生监督。它把图像选择与空间布局作为联合优化问题，用凸分配子问题求解，并对目标分辨率不足、图像过度压缩、以及源上下文无法填充的填充区域施加惩罚。组合阶段对每张选中图像裁剪出覆盖全部标注对象的区域，并在不缩小目标尺度的前提下向周边上下文扩展，再将缩放后的区域拼合为蒸馏图像，从而保留区域内空间布局及全部原始类别标签与边界框。

Result: 在 MS COCO 与 PASCAL VOC 上以 0.25%–2% 压缩率评估；构建 1% MS COCO 数据集耗时不到两分钟；在硬标签的常规训练协议下，以 CNN 与 ViT 骨干均达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: GRASP 表明无需模型参与即可高效完成检测数据集蒸馏，在极小数据预算下同时保持标注完整性与空间结构，为多目标密集监督的蒸馏提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) reduces storage and training costs by compressing large datasets into small collections. Most existing methods focus on image classification, where each image typically has a single category label. In contrast, object detection requires accurate category and bounding box annotations for multiple objects, together with visual content consistent with those annotations. Existing detection distillation pipelines rely on learned models and can alter the spatial relationships between objects and their backgrounds. We introduce GRASP, which packs dense native supervision into limited pixels without network training or inference. GRASP jointly searches over image selections and spatial layouts using convex allocation subproblems that penalize insufficient object resolution, excessive image compression, and padding that source context cannot fill. During composition, each selected image is cropped around all annotated objects and expanded into surrounding context without reducing object scale. The resized regions are then combined into distilled images, preserving the spatial layout within each region and retaining all original class labels and bounding box annotations. We evaluate GRASP on MS COCO and PASCAL VOC with compression ratios ranging from 0.25\% to 2\%. GRASP constructs the 1\% MS COCO dataset in under two minutes. Under a common training protocol using hard labels, GRASP achieves the state of the art with both CNN and ViT backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/tade1s/GRASP

</details>


### [202] [STARS: Stage-Adaptive Responsibility Separation for Shadow Removal in Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20698)
*Gaoliang Song,Chengjie Li,Pengju Liu,Chenchen Chang,Jinhao You,Hongzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出级联式阴影去除框架 STARS，将阴影去除解耦为"阴影区域复原"与"非阴影区域恒等"两个任务并分阶段处理，配合特征选择与融合适配器 FSFA 引入大模型先验，在 UAV-SC+、ISTD+、WSRD+ 2026 三个数据集上验证优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码先验虽为修复网络提供显式位置信息，但会使非阴影区域受到阴影先验调制，产生系统性亮度偏移，且该现象与掩码是否准确无关；遥感图像中非阴影区域占比更大，其微小偏移在全图指标上的代价高于阴影区域同等收益。

Method: 采用任务解耦视角，将阴影去除分解为阴影区域复原与非阴影区域恒等两项任务，在级联架构中顺序处理，按阶段划分区域职责；无需复杂的并行双分支结构，同时保留掩码优势。另设计特征选择与融合适配器（FSFA），将大模型先验知识引入遥感任务。

Result: 在 UAV-SC+、ISTD+ 与 WSRD+ 2026 三个基准数据集上进行了大量实验，结果证明 STARS 具有优越性。

Conclusion: 区域职责解耦可化解掩码先验的内在矛盾，为存在区域异质性的图像复原提供了新方向。

Abstract: Shadows are a ubiquitous radiative attenuation phenomenon in remote sensing images and can severely affect high-resolution remote sensing interpretation tasks. In shadow removal, although introducing a mask prior provides explicit positional information for the restoration network, it also causes non-shadow regions to be modulated by the shadow prior, producing a systematic brightness shift; notably, this phenomenon is independent of whether the mask is accurate. In remote sensing images, non-shadow regions usually occupy a larger proportion, so a small shift over these regions incurs a much higher cost on whole-image metrics than an equal gain in shadow regions. Adopting a task-decoupling perspective, we therefore decompose shadow removal into two tasks, shadow-region restoration and non-shadow-region identity, within a cascaded architecture, and handle them sequentially. Based on this insight, we propose STARS, a cascaded shadow removal framework that separates regional responsibilities across stages. The method retains the advantages of the shadow mask while resolving the degradation of non-shadow regions, without requiring a complex parallel dual-branch structure. Meanwhile, to introduce large-model prior knowledge into remote sensing tasks, we further design a Feature Selection and Fusion Adapter (FSFA). We conduct extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets, UAV-SC+, ISTD+ and WSRD+ 2026, demonstrating the superiority of STARS. The framework shows that decoupling regional responsibilities can resolve the intrinsic contradiction of mask priors, providing a new direction for image restoration with region heterogeneity.

</details>


### [203] [Efficient Multi-View Generation with Iterative Global-Local Proxy Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20703)
*Wei Tan,Kunming Luo,Hongyu Yan,Yu Zhang,Zihao Zhang,Xiatian Zhu,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GLPA（全局-局部代理注意力），在视图内保留全分辨率交互、跨视图用紧凑全局代理通信，并配合迭代代理精化，在FLUX与Stable Diffusion骨干上以更高效率生成多视图一致RGB与法线图，提升重建与NVS质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单图生成多视图RGB与法线图可为3D重建提供外观与几何互补线索，但跨视图一致性依赖视图间信息交换；稠密跨视图注意力的计算开销随视图数与空间分辨率增长而急剧上升。

Method: GLPA：视图内保持全分辨率注意力以保留细粒度信息，跨视图通过紧凑的全局代理进行信息交换以降低通信成本；引入迭代代理精化，在单个去噪步内由更新后的特征重建代理，并复用同一套注意力参数进行后续交互。在FLUX与Stable Diffusion两种结构不同的扩散骨干上验证。

Result: FLUX上单步去噪注意力计算相对稠密注意力加速2.65×，重建IoU由0.6849提升至0.7019，NVS LPIPS由0.086降至0.076；Stable Diffusion上注意力加速1.80×，五项质量指标全部提升。

Conclusion: 以代理式跨视图通信替代稠密跨视图注意力，可在显著降低计算成本的同时提升多视图一致性与3D重建质量，且方法对异构扩散骨干具有良好通用性。

Abstract: Generating consistent multi-view RGB images and normal maps from a single input image provides complementary appearance and geometric cues for 3D reconstruction. However, maintaining cross-view consistency requires information exchange across viewpoints, and dense cross-view attention becomes computationally expensive as the number of views and spatial resolution increase. In this paper, we propose Global-Local Proxy Attention (GLPA) to reduce cross-view communication costs while preserving fine-grained within-view processing. Specifically, GLPA retains full-resolution interactions within each view and uses compact global proxies for cross-view communication. To further leverage the exchanged information, GLPA incorporates iterative proxy refinement, which rebuilds the proxies from updated features and reuses the same attention parameters for subsequent interactions within a single denoising step. We evaluate GLPA on two structurally different diffusion backbones, FLUX and Stable Diffusion. Experiments on FLUX show that GLPA achieves a $2.65\times$ speedup in attention computation per denoising step over dense attention, while improving reconstruction IoU from 0.6849 to 0.7019 and reducing NVS LPIPS from 0.086 to 0.076. Results on Stable Diffusion further demonstrate the effectiveness of GLPA, with a $1.80\times$ attention speedup and improvements across all five quality metrics.

</details>


### [204] [QASK-Edit: Adaptive Sparse Attention Across Queries and Key Sources for Efficient Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20704)
*Hengrui Liu,Wenbo Xu,Ziqi Sheng,Wei lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练稀疏注意力框架QASK-Edit，按query与key来源独立分配计算，实现视频编辑DiT最高1.46×端到端加速且保持质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT视频编辑因条件输入与生成视频构成的长token序列导致推理延迟高；现有免训练稀疏注意力采用固定保留率或全局单一阈值，忽略了不同key来源（源视频、参考图像、音频、文本、生成视频）及同一来源内不同query的注意力需求差异。

Method: 将视频编辑视为条件生成，提出QASK-Edit：对每个query和每个key来源独立分配计算。对每个query，同时考虑每个block的注意力质量与近似风险，并对候选路由阈值按各key来源的风险预算进行评估，从而在精确计算与近似之间动态选择、逐query调整稀疏度。全流程免训练。

Result: 在三个视频编辑模型上，相较稠密注意力流水线取得最高1.46×端到端加速，同时保持编辑质量与未编辑区域保真度；可直接泛化至Reference-to-Video生成，获得最高1.36×加速并保持生成质量。

Conclusion: 细粒度的query自适应、按key来源分治的稀疏注意力是降低DiT视频编辑推理成本的有效免训练方案，且具备跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have achieved strong performance in generation based video editing, but suffer from high inference latency due to the long token sequences formed by conditioning inputs and generated videos. Although training-free sparse attention reduces this cost, existing methods typically apply a fixed retention ratio or a single threshold shared across all tokens. In this work, we view video editing as conditional generation and identify a key insight: attention demand varies significantly both across different key sources (e.g., source videos, reference images, audio, text, and generated videos) and across queries within each source. Motivated by this, we propose QASK-Edit (Query-Adaptive Sparsity over Key Sources for Video Editing), a training-free sparse attention framework that allocates computation independently for each query and key source. Specifically, for each query, QASK-Edit considers both the attention mass and approximation risk of each block. It then evaluates candidate routing thresholds against each key source's risk budget, choosing exact computation or approximations to dynamically adjust sparsity for each query. Extensive experiments on three video editing models demonstrate that QASK-Edit achieves up to $1.46\times$ end-to-end speedup over dense attention pipeline while preserving both editing quality and unedited region fidelity. Furthermore, QASK-Edit generalizes directly to Reference-to-Video generation, delivering up to $1.36\times$ speedup while maintaining generation quality.

</details>


### [205] [CLIFT: Contrastive LiDAR Instance Features for 3D Pedestrian Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20737)
*German Devchich,Timur Akhtyamov,Denis Fatykhoph,Gonzalo Ferrer*

Main category: cs.CV
