TL;DR: 提出CLIFT方法，通过在3D LiDAR检测器的中间体素特征上引入实例级对比学习目标，将检测器本身改造为跟踪器；在JRDB 3D跟踪榜单上以仅LiDAR输入取得HOTA 38.58与IDF1 37.99，均列第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需持续感知周围人员以规划运动，这依赖3D多目标跟踪。LiDAR模态的主流范式为"检测器+独立运动模型/重识别网络"的两阶段串联结构，模块冗余且非端到端。

Method: 在检测器骨干的体素特征上增加实例级对比目标，从检测框内体素聚合读出嵌入；跨帧以余弦相似度与匈牙利算法匹配，不使用运动模型；骨干网络与该目标联合微调，学习身份可判别特征。

Result: 该方法仅依赖检测器已有体素特征，与骨干无关。JRDB 3D跟踪榜单上HOTA 38.58、IDF1 37.99排名第一，分别比次优已发表方法高13.04与9.30；MOTA与OSPA排名第二。检测精度基本不受影响。

Conclusion: 跟踪能力可内生于检测器的特征表示，无需额外运动模型或ReID模块即可实现强性能，为轻量、骨干无关的LiDAR 3D跟踪提供新范式。

Abstract: A social robot has to keep track of the people around it over time to plan its motion, a task handled by 3D multi-object tracking. For the LiDAR modality, trackers usually sit on top of a detector and link its detections across frames with a separate motion model or re-identification network. We instead show how a 3D LiDAR detector itself can be turned into a tracker by adding an instance-level contrastive objective on its intermediate voxel features. Each embedding is read from the voxels inside a detected box, and boxes are matched across frames by cosine similarity and the Hungarian algorithm, without a motion model. The detector backbone is fine-tuned together with this objective, which learns identity-aware features while leaving detection accuracy essen- tially intact. Because the method uses only the voxel features that Li- DAR detectors already produce, it is backbone-agnostic. On the JRDB 3D tracking leaderboard our LiDAR-only tracker ranks first on HOTA (38.58) and IDF1 (37.99), 13.04 and 9.30 points above the next pub- lished method, and second on MOTA and OSPA. Code is available at https://hermak56.github.io/CLIFT/.

</details>


### [206] [A Seismic Foundation Model for Dense Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20745)
*Zhixiang Guo,Xinming Wu,Yimin Dou,Hui Gao,Siyao Zhao,He Huang,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出70亿参数地震基础模型SeisDFE，在超500个地震体的534万张2D图像上预训练，冻结编码器即可支持四类地震解释任务，稀缺监督下增益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习地震解释受制于高质量标注稀缺，且现有模型多为单一任务分别训练，缺乏可跨任务复用的密集特征表示。

Method: 构建7B参数地震基础模型SeisDFE，数据为源自500余个地震体的5.34百万张2D图像；采用三阶段预训练策略：判别式自监督学习、Gram特征锚定、多分辨率适配，以提升特征判别力并保持空间连贯性；使用时冻结编码器，配合任务专用解码器。

Result: 在地震相分类、盐丘检测、断层检测、相对地质时间估计四项任务上均优于现有地震基础模型；除断层检测外均优于自然图像预训练模型；监督稀缺时增益最大——仅用F3数据集两个标注剖面，全数据体mIoU较基线提升12–19个百分点；并有效迁移至合成到野外数据及稀疏3D地质体解释。

Conclusion: 大规模地震预训练可将地质信息组织为可直接跨解释任务复用的密集表示，冻结编码器加轻量解码器即为有效范式。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based seismic interpretation is constrained by scarce high-quality annotations, and most existing models are trained separately for individual tasks. We present SeisDFE, a 7B-parameter seismic foundation model for dense feature extraction, pretrained on 5.34 million 2D images derived from more than 500 seismic volumes. A three-stage pretraining strategy, combining discriminative self-supervised learning, Gram feature anchoring, and multi-resolution adaptation, improves feature discriminability while preserving spatial coherence. With its encoder frozen, SeisDFE supports seismic facies classification, salt detection, fault detection, and relative geologic time estimation. It outperforms existing seismic foundation models on all four tasks and a natural-image-pretrained model on all but fault detection, with the largest gains under scarce supervision: using only two annotated sections of the F3 dataset, it improves whole-volume mIoU by 12-19 percentage points over the baselines. The learned features remain effective with task-specific decoders and extend to synthetic-to-field transfer and sparse 3D geobody interpretation. These results show that large-scale seismic pretraining organizes geological information into dense representations that are directly reusable across interpretation tasks.

</details>


### [207] [Deep Learning for Satellite-Based Mountain Hazard Screening: A Retrospective Failure Analysis on the 2021 Chamoli Rock-Ice Avalanche](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20769)
*Snigdhajyoti Rakshit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用Sentinel-2十波段与地形信息构建深度学习流水线，对山区潜在灾害源进行分割、变化检测，并结合 Monte Carlo 采样与 D8 最陡下降流路计算势能、识别下游暴露建筑物并排序风险；以2021年2月7日印度喜马拉雅Chamoli岩冰雪崩事件为回溯验证，宏观F1为0.648，优于简单光谱规则的0.519。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 山区岩冰雪崩等灾害源识别困难，需在事件发生前给出潜在灾害源定位与风险排序，而单纯光谱规则方法精度不足，因此需要一套可自动化的遥感+地形+深度学习流水线。

Method: 以Sentinel-2的10个波段叠加地形信息作为输入，采用U-Net对卫星影像进行地表分割，检测变化以识别候选灾害带；随后用Monte Carlo采样估计沿D8最陡下降流路的势能，识别下游暴露结构物，并按风险得分对区域排序。

Result: 宏观F1达0.648，高于简单光谱规则的0.519；但岩石与土壤类别F1偏低，仅约0.3量级；冬季光学变化检测中仅未能识别一处东北朝向坡面，该坡面在事件前有73%处于阴影。参数针对该单一场景设定、非独立确定，属回溯性测试。

Conclusion: 该回溯测试成功定位了一次已发生失效的位置，主要贡献在于揭示并定位流水线中的泄漏（pipeline leak）环节；方法在宏观指标上优于光谱规则，但在特定地物类别与阴影条件下仍存在明显局限。

Abstract: The approach utilizes 10 bands from Sentinel-2 imagery, along with terrain information, in a deep learning pipeline to classify potential hazard sources in any given mountainous region. The performance of our model has been evaluated against the data collected during the rock-ice avalanche event in the Chamoli region in the Indian Himalayas on 7th February 2021. U-Net performs segmentation of the detected land surface in the satellite image, detects changes in the identify candidate hazard zones, and then the following steps are performed to calculate the potential energy using the Monte Carlo sampling for steepest descent (D8) flow paths, finding the exposed structures in the downstream locations, and ranking the zones depending on the risk score. In comparison to the simple spectral rule, our approach achieved the higher macro F1 score of 0.648 (as compared to 0.519). But the F1 score in case of rock and soil was low, being in the range of 0.3s. In case of optical change detection in winter, it's only that northeast-facing slope which couldn't be detected, as it was under 73% of shade prior to the incident. The parameters have been set for this particular scene alone and not independently; hence, this is a retrospective test, and this test locates a failure which has occurred earlier. The primary contribution of this paper is locating the pipeline leak.

</details>


### [208] [ThermoSight: From Appearance to Internal Thermofluid Fields via Physics-Guided Neural Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20786)
*Yuhao Fang,Chenglin Lu,Hefei Ling,Chun Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThermoSight：一个物理引导的神经框架，从有限标定火焰图像中联合重建连续三维温度场与烟尘体积分数场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数神经重建方法只复现场景外观，而从视觉观测推断物理状态对机器理解物理世界至关重要。火焰中温度与烟尘共同决定可见辐射，且烟尘自吸收改变到达相机的光；在有限视角下，两场的耦合变化会在RGB图像中相互抵消，导致温度与烟尘难以解耦。重建热流体的内部温度与烟尘场对能效提升、热暴露评估与火蔓延分析具重要价值。

Method: 提出ThermoSight，无需场级真值监督即可优化。核心是可微分光谱模型，通过热辐射、自吸收与标定相机响应将物理场映射到实测RGB；利用温度相关的发射光谱与波长相关衰减约束跨RGB通道的共享场，降低温度-烟尘歧义。并辅以色度一致性、观测导出的空间约束与烟尘正则项。

Result: 合成消融实验中，相比三通道近似，波长分辨衰减将高温高烟尘区域的温度RMSE从234 K降至112 K，而RGB PSNR几乎不变（65.23 vs 65.24 dB）。实测数据得到连贯的物理场，并获得有竞争力的RGB重建。

Conclusion: 物理引导的神经场可恢复超出视觉一致外观之外的可解释内部物理量，为从图像进行科学级物理场反演提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Most neural reconstruction methods focus on reproducing scene appearance, yet inferring physical states from visual observations is essential for machine understanding of the physical world. Reconstructing the internal temperature and soot fields of radiating thermofluids such as flames is challenging yet essential for improving energy efficiency and assessing heat exposure and fire spread. In flames, temperature and soot concentration jointly govern visible emission, and soot self-absorption alters the light that reaches each camera. With limited views, changes in the two fields can offset one another in the recorded RGB images, making temperature and soot difficult to disentangle. ThermoSight, a physics-guided neural framework for scientific imaging, jointly reconstructs continuous three-dimensional temperature and soot volume fraction fields from limited calibrated flame images without field-level ground truth during optimization. A differentiable spectral model links these fields to measured RGB through thermal emission, self-absorption, and calibrated camera responses. Temperature-dependent emission spectra and wavelength-dependent attenuation constrain shared fields across RGB channels, reducing temperature-soot ambiguity. Chromaticity consistency, observation-derived spatial constraints, and soot regularization complement intensity fitting. In a synthetic ablation, wavelength-resolved attenuation reduces temperature RMSE in the hot, sooty region from 234 to 112 K relative to a three-channel approximation, with nearly identical RGB PSNR (65.23 vs. 65.24 dB). Measured data yield coherent fields and competitive RGB reconstructions. These results demonstrate that physics-guided neural fields recover interpretable internal physical quantities beyond visually consistent appearance.

</details>


### [209] [PhaseCLIP: Phase-Aware Frequency-Domain Adaptation of Vision-Language Models for Few-Shot Remote-Sensing Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20787)
*Mehmet Sergen Catal,Abdurrahman Gumus,Mehmet Zübeyir Ünlü*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhaseCLIP，在冻结CLIP视觉编码器中插入相位可用的频谱模块与特征门控模块，仅训练1.30%参数；凭借幅相共享参数布局实现两阶段无额外参数的迁移，在五个遥感数据集上取得84.28%平均精度并显著优于八个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感场景分类常面临标注样本极少、标签跨传感器/区域迁移性差的问题。现有基于VLM（如CLIP）的提示学习与特征适配器方法完全在空间域构建表示，而遥感场景以周期性纹理和规则几何为主，傅里叶频谱能以幅度（纹理与对比度统计）和相位（空间结构）紧凑刻画。已有频域适配要么只用幅度，要么不分离幅相，导致结构信息未被利用。

Method: 在冻结的CLIP视觉编码器中引入具备相位处理能力的频谱模块和特征门控模块，仅更新1.30%参数。幅度与相位两条通路参数布局完全一致，故在相位上学习的一套权重可精确迁移到幅度，形成无额外参数的两阶段方案。模块作用于图像块与Transformer token的频谱，文本编码器保持不变。

Result: 在EuroSAT、PatternNet、UC Merced、LSRS、RSSCN7五个公开遥感数据集上，与八个基线在统一协议下对比，PhaseCLIP取得84.28%平均精度，在30个"数据集×shot"组合中的23个上表现最佳；相位分量整体贡献1.93个百分点，在最难数据集上贡献4.36个百分点。

Conclusion: 相位信息的显式建模是遥感场景少样本分类的有效增益来源，且无需额外参数即可同时利用幅度与相位。由于模块仅作用于频谱且不修改文本编码器，方法可扩展到其他遥感传感器与任务，以及类别证据体现在空间结构中的其他领域。

Abstract: Remote-sensing (RS) image scene classification aims to assign a semantic category to an overhead image, and in practice must often do so from only a handful of labelled examples, since annotation requires expert interpretation and the resulting labels transfer poorly across sensors and regions. Recent progress has come from adapting vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP, through learned prompts or feature adapters. Although effective, these methods construct their representation entirely in the spatial domain. RS scenes are dominated by periodic texture and regular geometry, which the Fourier spectrum describes compactly through an amplitude component, carrying texture and contrast statistics, and a phase component, carrying spatial structure. Existing frequency-domain adaptation of VLMs either exploits amplitude alone or does not separate amplitude from phase, leaving the structural component unused. In this article, we propose PhaseCLIP, which inserts phase-capable spectral modules and a feature-gating module into a frozen CLIP visual encoder and trains only 1.30% of its parameters. Because the amplitude and phase pathways share an identical parameter layout, a single weight set is learned on phase and transferred exactly to amplitude, giving a two-stage scheme that adds no parameters. Experiments on five public RS datasets, EuroSAT, PatternNet, UC Merced, LSRS and RSSCN7, against eight baselines under a matched protocol, show that PhaseCLIP attains 84.28% mean accuracy and is the best method in 23 of 30 dataset-by-shot cells, with phase contributing 1.93 points overall and 4.36 points on the most difficult dataset. Because the modules act on the spectrum of image patches and transformer tokens and leave the text encoder untouched, the approach can be extended to other RS sensors and tasks, and to other domains in which class evidence lies in spatial structure.

</details>


### [210] [VA-Assembly: View-Aligned Score Guidance for Training-Free 3D Part Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20788)
*Yeon-Jae Kim,Sangwon Kang,Byung-Kwon Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VA-Assembly：无需训练的3D部件装配框架，利用视角对齐的扩散引导，从单张参考图像优化各部件（平移、旋转、缩放）的变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到3D基础模型输出整体式表示，缺乏显式部件结构；部件级生成与学习式装配方法依赖部件级监督、类别先验或成对的部件布局训练数据。论文关注一个实际但少被探索的设定：独立准备或生成的部件之间没有预定义关系、共享坐标系或一致尺度，需从单张图像装配为与参考一致的物体。

Method: 给定参考图像与固定几何的3D部件，VA-Assembly以冻结的视角条件扩散模型为基础，将其条件化为由参考图像导出的整体式3D表示在同一视角下的渲染结果，从而产生与部件配置几何对齐的分数梯度，作为变换级监督，直接优化每个部件的平移、旋转与缩放；该方法无需标注部件布局、预定义部件分类、类别特定装配训练或基于包围盒的布局初始化。

Result: 与几何配准及组合式布局基线（按其各自支持的变换分量）对比，部件级变换估计更优；消融实验表明，视角对齐的条件化（而非扩散引导本身）是可靠变换梯度的关键；仅更新部件变换保持了显式的部件可分离性，并支持部件级编辑、替换与重配置。

Conclusion: 将预训练视觉—结构先验重新用作变换级监督，可实现免训练、免标注的3D部件装配，并在初始错位较大时提供多视角结构线索，适用于可编辑的部件化3D内容构建。

Abstract: Image-to-3D foundation models have improved 3D reconstruction, but typically produce monolithic representations without explicit part-level structure. Part-aware generation and learning-based assembly methods often require part-level supervision, category-specific priors, or paired part-layout training data. We instead consider a practical but underexplored setting in which independently prepared or generated 3D parts have no predefined inter-part relationships, shared coordinate frames, or consistent scales, and must be assembled into a reference-consistent object from a single image. We present VA-Assembly, a training-free framework for 3D part assembly through part-level transformation estimation using view-aligned diffusion guidance. Given a reference image and fixed 3D part geometries, VA-Assembly optimizes each part's translation, rotation, and scale. We condition a frozen view-conditioned diffusion model on same-view renderings of a reference-derived monolithic 3D representation, producing score gradients geometrically aligned with the part configuration. This guidance provides multi-view structural cues under large initial misalignment. By repurposing pretrained visual-structural priors as transformation-level supervision, VA-Assembly requires no annotated part layouts, predefined part taxonomies, category-specific assembly training, or bounding-box-based layout initialization. Experiments compare VA-Assembly with geometric registration and compositional layout baselines using each baseline's supported transformation components, showing improved part-wise transformation estimation. Ablation studies show that view-aligned conditioning, rather than diffusion guidance alone, is critical for reliable transformation gradients. Updating only part transformations preserves explicit component separability and supports component-level editing, replacement, and reconfiguration.

</details>


### [211] [TRR: A Thermal Interaction Engine for Robot Simulation and RGBT Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20792)
*Biwen Yang,Zhe Cao,Jin Zhang,Haoyang Sun,Zonglu Hu,Jintao Zhao,Zhehao Zhang,Kaizheng Chen,Chengyi Liu,Chengyu Wei,Siyuan Wu,Ruiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRR：为机器人接触任务附加深度分辨热状态并模拟接触/搬运/分离热交换的RGB-热渲染器，产出含2,224对静态-动态轨迹（共4,448条）的六任务基准，验证热历史对接触结果与迁移学习的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动作会改变后续交互的热条件（释放后残留热、随物体携带的热、表层下储存的热），即便当前温度相近也会影响后续接触结果；训练轨迹需刻画早期动作与后期观测、任务结果之间的这种因果联系，而现有仿真/数据缺乏此类热历史建模。

Method: 提出TRR（Thermal Robot Renderer）：为两个接触对象附加空间化、深度分辨的热状态，通过接触、搬运与分离更新局部热交换，并在统一执行时钟下同步生成RGB-热（RGBT）观测与事件级任务检查；通过受控干预实验分离残留热、空间输运与内部储热的作用；由同一状态生成配对六任务基准（静态-动态对），并提供RGBT观测适配器（MuJoCo、Isaac Sim）、10种机器人配置与6个相机视角的工程清单及场景导出工作流。

Result: 受控干预验证残留热、空间输运与内部储热对后续接触结果的决定性影响；基准含2,224对静态-动态样本、4,448条轨迹，配双视角同步观测、状态与动作；在学习语料上，可见热历史将安全接触点选择成功率由75.0%提升至91.7%；在共享反馈执行的新加热流程下，动态历史训练使仿真迁移完成率提升50个百分点。

Conclusion: 热历史（残留、输运、储热）应作为机器人学习数据的关键状态变量显式建模；TRR提供可复现的渲染、基准与工作流，基准、工作流及第6节平台资源将作为计划公开释放的首批内容。

Abstract: A robot's actions change the thermal conditions of its next interaction: heat retained after release, carried with objects or stored beneath the surface can change a later contact's outcome despite similar current temperatures. Training trajectories must capture how these effects connect earlier actions to later observations and task outcomes. We present TRR, a \emph{Thermal Robot Renderer} that attaches spatial and depth-resolved thermal state to both contact partners, updates local heat exchange through contact, transport and separation, and generates synchronized RGB--thermal (RGBT) observations and event-local task checks on one execution clock. Controlled interventions show why retained heat, spatial transport and internal storage matter for subsequent contact outcomes. From the same state, TRR produces a paired six-task benchmark of 2,224 static--dynamic pairs, that is, 4,448 trajectories with synchronized dual-view observations, states and actions. Visible thermal history raises safe contact-site selection from 75.0\% to 91.7\% on a dedicated learning corpus, and training on dynamic histories raises simulated transfer completion by 50 percentage points under a new heating procedure with shared feedback execution. We demonstrate RGBT observation adapters on MuJoCo and Isaac Sim, report an engineering inventory of ten prepared robot configurations and six demonstrated camera viewpoints, and describe a developer workflow that carries an existing robot scene to exported RGBT records. The benchmark, the workflow and the platform resources described in Section 6 form the first items of a planned public release. Demonstrations and resource updates are available on the project page.

</details>


### [212] [Correspondence-Free Camera Pose Estimation by Matching Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20797)
*Subhransu S. Bhattacharjee,Dylan Campbell,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无学习、免对应的盲PnP方法MomentPnP：用14个四阶以下置换不变图像矩辨识相机位姿，再以EM精化位姿与对应权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲透视n点问题需在2D-3D点集对应关系未知时估计标定相机位姿，现有方法多依赖学习且对离群点与稠密点集鲁棒性不足。

Method: 理论证明：对完整无噪观测，14个最高四阶的置换不变图像矩可对几乎任意≥20点的3D点集唯一确定固定相机位姿。据此设计MomentPnP：在观测2D点与投影3D点之间匹配这些矩以无对应地初始化位姿，再用期望最大化（EM）联合精化位姿与对应权重。

Result: 在三个基准测试集上，无论是否含离群点，中位旋转误差较最优学习基线降低5–170倍；在离群点与内点数量相当（1:1）时仍能恢复准确位姿；在稠密ETH3D扫描上，点集增大时位姿精度提升，即便单点对应更难分辨。

Conclusion: 矩不变量的辨识性为免对应位姿估计提供了理论与实用途径，MomentPnP在精度与鲁棒性上显著优于学习型基线，并可受益于点集稠密化。

Abstract: The blind perspective-$n$-point (PnP) problem is to estimate the pose of a calibrated camera from 2D and 3D point sets with unknown correspondences. We show that, for complete, noiseless observations, fourteen permutation-invariant image moments up to order four identify a fixed camera pose for almost every 3D point set containing at least twenty points. Our learning-free method, MomentPnP, matches these moments between the observed 2D points and the projected 3D points to initialize a camera pose without correspondences and refines the pose and correspondence weights using expectation--maximization. Across the test sets of three benchmarks, our method reduces median rotation error by $5$--$170\times$ relative to the best learned baseline, both with and without outliers. We recover accurate poses even with as many outliers as inliers. On dense ETH3D scans, pose accuracy improves as the point sets grow, even as individual correspondences become harder to resolve.

</details>


### [213] [An Efficient Training Paradigm for Image Restoration via Improved Fréchet Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20805)
*Shanyuan Jie,Wu Shi,Xinqi Lin,Kainan Yan,Zian Wang,Yingxue Pang,Yan Wang,Shijie Zhao,Chao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FDIR 提出一种无需扩散蒸馏或对抗训练的"回归式监督"框架，用 Fréchet 距离在冻结表征空间匹配特征分布，仅优化 LoRA 参数即可训练单步生成式图像复原模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式图像复原感知质量好但依赖迭代采样、推理慢；现有单步方法依赖扩散蒸馏或对抗训练，优化复杂。作者希望简化训练流程。

Method: FDIR 通过可直接计算的回归式目标进行单遍生成式复原训练：①采用 Fréchet 距离在冻结表征空间中匹配复原图像与真实图像的特征分布，提供显式的分布级监督；②引入局部 patch 特征以捕捉细粒度复原差异；③提出实例自适应统计量，将每个 SR-HQ 对的特征矩与同步的历史统计量结合，使分布匹配兼顾输入特定对应关系与分布级上下文；④仅优化复原骨干网络的 LoRA 参数。

Result: 实验表明 FDIR 具有较强感知复原质量和高效训练特性。

Conclusion: FDIR 为生成式图像复原提供了一种更简洁的训练范式，避免了扩散蒸馏与对抗训练的复杂性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image restoration achieves strong perceptual quality but typically relies on iterative sampling, leading to slow inference. Existing single-step methods often use diffusion distillation or adversarial training, complicating optimization. To simplify training, we propose FDIR, a framework for training single-pass generative restoration models through regression-style supervised optimization using directly computable objectives. To realize this paradigm, FDIR employs the Fréchet distance to match the feature distributions of restored and real images in frozen representation spaces, providing explicit distribution-level supervision. To better accommodate image restoration, we incorporate local patch features to capture fine-grained restoration discrepancies. Moreover, we introduce instance-adaptive statistics by combining the feature moments of each SR-HQ pair with synchronized historical statistics, allowing distribution matching to incorporate input-specific correspondence while retaining distribution-level context. FDIR optimizes only the LoRA parameters of the restoration backbone. Experiments demonstrate strong perceptual restoration quality and efficient training, suggesting a simpler paradigm for generative image restoration.

</details>


### [214] [Interpretable deep concepts of organoid morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20840)
*Lion Jasper Gleiter,Tingying Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文基于DINOv3与ViTAMIn-O两个基础模型训练稀疏自编码器（SAE），学习与类器官形态相关的可解释概念，并通过空间标注、数据集特异性与任务相关性分析，验证概念过滤可提升下游分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像分类可判断类器官的药物响应、活性或分化成败，但学习到的表征具有黑箱性质，无法揭示分类器依赖的形态线索。

Method: 以通用模型DINOv3与类器官专用模型ViTAMIn-O为基础训练SAE，获得可解释概念；为概念标注空间标签（类器官、类器官壁、背景），在五个任务上计算其数据集特异性与任务相关性；并做定性分析。

Result: DINOv3概念的空间标签可在域外数据集上产生粗略的类器官分割；在DINOv3-SAE中按空间标签过滤概念，使五个任务中的三个下游AUROC提升；定性分析还发现某一任务中由预处理引入的混杂因素。

Conclusion: 可解释概念有助于提升分类器可靠性，并可为类器官表征模型的后续开发提供指导；训练好的SAE将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Image-based classification of organoid morphologies can indicate drug response, viability, or successful differentiation. In recent work, learned representations outperform handcrafted features as the basis for downstream classifiers. However, their black-box nature hides the morphological cues a classifier relies on. Based on the foundation models DINOv3 (general-purpose) and ViTAMIn-O (organoid-specific), we train sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to learn interpretable concepts related to distinct organoid morphologies. We annotate concepts with spatial labels (organoid, organoid wall, background) and compute their dataset specificity and task relevance across five tasks. The spatial labels of DINOv3 concepts yield coarse organoid segmentations on out-of-domain datasets. Filtering concepts by spatial label in the DINOv3-SAE improves downstream AUROC for three of the five tasks. Qualitative analysis of the most relevant concepts further reveals a confounding factor introduced by our preprocessing in one task. Through such insights, interpretable concepts could help improve the reliability of classifiers and guide the future development of organoid representation models. The trained SAEs will be made available upon publication.

</details>


### [215] [Vision-Language Model-Based Phase-OTDR Event Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20841)
*Ahmet Bestami Kose,Mehmet Sergen Catal,Muhammet Cagri Yeke,Kivilcim Yuksel,Abdurrahman Gumus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索用视觉-语言模型CLIP配合少样本Tip-Adapter-F，实现Phase-OTDR振动事件的低标注高效分类，128-shot达96.81%±0.42%准确率，16-shot达90.17%±0.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Phase-OTDR事件分类长期依赖需大量标注数据的全监督模型，标注成本高、泛化受限，作者希望检验预训练视觉-语言模型能否以少样本方式实现可靠的分布式振动事件识别。

Method: 将原始信号转为STFT dB幅度谱图并做颜色映射与尺寸缩放生成RGB拼图，输入CLIP图像编码器（ViT-L-14，OpenAI权重）；少样本适配仅优化Tip-Adapter-F的缓存键，编码器冻结。采用防泄漏协议：每类512张训练侧支持/验证池、每类120张固定留出测试集、选择时不访问测试集、20次独立支持采样试验；并进行提示词与骨干网络的消融分析。

Result: 所选配置（ViT-L-14 + 128 shots/类）在留出测试集上达96.81±0.42%准确率与96.79±0.42%宏平均F1；16-shot（六类共96张标注支持图）即达90.17±0.81%。Tip-Adapter-F显著优于零样本CLIP与免训练Tip-Adapter；性能在不同ViT编码器间稳定，且对提示词措辞不敏感。

Conclusion: CLIP视觉特征含有可迁移的结构信息，仅需轻量支持缓存即可构建标签高效的Phase-OTDR事件分类器；代码与图像数据集已在GitHub公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Phase-sensitive optical time-domain reflectometry (Phase-OTDR) enables distributed vibration sensing, but reliable event classification has traditionally relied on fully supervised models that require extensive annotated data. This study examines the adaptability of a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for Phase-OTDR event recognition through few-shot learning. Raw samples are converted into Short-Time Fourier Transform (STFT) dB-magnitude spectrogram mosaics, color-mapped and resized to RGB images, and processed by a Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) image encoder; few-shot adaptation optimizes only the Tip-Adapter-F cache keys, leaving both encoders unchanged. Under a leakage-controlled protocol (train-side support/validation pool of 512 images per class, fixed held-out test of 120 per class, no test-set access during selection, 20 distinct support-sampling trials), the selected configuration (the ViT-L-14 Vision Transformer encoder with OpenAI weights, 128 shots per class) reaches 96.81 +/- 0.42% accuracy and a macro-averaged F1 score of 96.79 +/- 0.42% on the held-out test set; a label-efficient 16-shot setting, that is, 96 labeled support images at adaptation time across the six classes, already attains 90.17 +/- 0.81%. Tip-Adapter-F clearly outperforms both zero-shot CLIP and the standard training-free Tip-Adapter, and prompt and backbone analyses show that performance is stable across Vision Transformer encoders and insensitive to prompt wording at the selected operating point. Overall, CLIP visual features carry a transferable structure that a lightweight support cache turns into a label-efficient Phase-OTDR event classifier. The code and the corresponding image dataset are made publicly available on GitHub to support further research: https://github.com/miralab-ai/vlm-phase-otdr.

</details>


### [216] [When Is the Expensive Method Worth It? A Content-Driven Analysis of Classical Bayer Demosaicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20854)
*Taranum Wasu,Harsh Kashyap,HS Pannu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过受控仿真-重建流程评估去马赛克经典方法的性价比，发现边缘感知方法的增益随图像内容大幅波动（-6.6至+6.8 dB，31%图像上为零或负），且可用平均饱和度预测（r=-0.86），并构建深度2决策树为图像推荐最佳廉价方法，留一验证下优于基线20个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典去马赛克方法从简单平均到方向性边缘感知插值，后者计算成本显著更高。作者提出一个实用问题：对给定图像，昂贵的边缘感知方法是否值得其代价？逐图质量排名无法回答该问题，因此需要量化增益的波动性及其可预测性。

Method: 在80张基准图像（Kodak、McMaster、BSD100、Urban100四个公开数据集）上进行受控的仿真-重建流程，比较六种经典方法；图像内容描述子在未见任何结果前即固定。计算边缘感知方法（Menon 2007）相对最佳廉价线性方法的PSNR增益，分析其与图像描述子的相关性，并构建可解释的深度2决策树在图像描述子上推荐最佳廉价方法，采用留一图像交叉验证。全程不使用深度学习。

Result: Menon 2007相对最佳廉价线性方法的增益范围为-6.6至+6.8 dB，在31%的图像上为零或负，即廉价方法可持平或胜出。增益与图像平均饱和度相关，r=-0.86（p<10^{-6}）。所构建的深度2决策树在留一图像验证下比"总是选择最常见方法"的基线高出20.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 昂贵的方向性去马赛克方法并非普适更优，其价值强烈依赖图像内容且可由简单描述子预测；基于可解释决策树的内容自适应方法选择是可行且透明的替代方案。

Abstract: Demosaicing reconstructs a full-colour image from a single-sensor colour-filter-array (CFA) mosaic, where only one colour is measured per pixel. Classical methods make different assumptions about images, from simple averaging to directional edge-aware interpolation, and the directional methods cost markedly more compute. We ask a practical question that a per-image quality ranking does not answer: for a given image, is the expensive directional method worth its cost? Using a controlled simulate-reconstruct pipeline on 80 benchmark images from four public datasets (Kodak, McMaster, BSD100, Urban100), six classical methods, and image-content descriptors fixed before any results were seen, we show that the quality gain of the edge-aware method (Menon 2007) over the best cheap linear method is not constant. It ranges from $-6.6$ to $+6.8$ dB, and on 31% of images it is zero or negative, meaning a cheap method matches or beats the expensive one. The gain is also predictable from content: it correlates with mean image saturation at $r=-0.86$ ($p<10^{-6}$). We then build an interpretable depth-2 decision tree that recommends the best cheap method from image descriptors, beating an always-pick-the-most-common baseline by 20.0 percentage points under leave-one-image-out validation. The analysis uses no deep learning, and every step can be inspected.

</details>


### [217] [LiDSeg: Linear DehazeFormer Guidance for Efficient Semantic Segmentation in Adverse Weather Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20864)
*Yobsan Bayisa Leta,Christos Kyrkou,Maria K. Michael,Theocharis Theocharides*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiDSeg 通过训练期引入重建引导编码器、推理期丢弃重建解码器的设计，在低参数开销下显著提升恶劣天气语义分割鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气下的鲁棒语义分割对安全关键的自动驾驶系统至关重要。现有"先复原再分割"流程引入延迟且偏重像素级复原而非语义判别性；域适应方法则面临表征瓶颈与计算复杂度问题。

Method: 提出 LiDSeg 框架，利用重建信息引导分割。训练阶段，轻量引导编码器与分割分支联合优化，使用分割目标与辅助重建目标；推理阶段丢弃重建解码器，保留引导编码器提取天气鲁棒特征，并通过轻量门控融合模块将特征融入分割分支。

Result: Foggy Cityscapes 上清晰到浓雾的 mIoU 退化仅 2.5–2.8 个百分点，较基线降幅缩小五倍以上；ACDC 官方测试服务器上 LiDSeg-PIDNet 达 75.07% 全条件池化 mIoU，与监督 HRNet-W48 的 75.0% 相当但模型显著更小；零样本泛化至 Foggy Driving 与 Foggy Zurich；仅增加 1.9–3.1M 参数，总模型规模 5.2–39.5M，推理无重建开销，并在 Jetson AGX Orin 上实时运行。

Conclusion: LiDSeg 以极小的参数增量与零推理开销实现了恶劣天气分割的强鲁棒性与良好泛化，兼顾清晰天气精度与实时部署可行性。

Abstract: Robust semantic segmentation in adverse weather is essential for safety-critical autonomous systems. Existing restore-then-segment pipelines introduce latency and prioritize pixel-level restoration over semantic discriminability, while domain adaptation methods often face representation bottlenecks and computational complexity. We introduce LiDSeg, a framework that uses reconstruction-informed guidance to improve segmentation under adverse weather. During training, a lightweight guidance encoder is jointly optimized with the segmentation branch using segmentation and auxiliary reconstruction objectives. At inference, the reconstruction decoder is discarded, while the guidance encoder is retained to extract weather-robust features. A lightweight gated fusion module integrates these features into the segmentation branch, preserving clear-weather accuracy while improving adverse-weather robustness. On Foggy Cityscapes, LiDSeg limits the clear-to-dense-fog mIoU degradation to 2.5-2.8 percentage points, reducing the baseline performance drop by more than fivefold. On the official ACDC test server, LiDSeg-PIDNet achieves 75.07% pooled all-condition mIoU, comparable to supervised HRNet-W48 at 75.0%, while remaining substantially smaller. The gains generalize zero-shot to Foggy Driving and Foggy Zurich without training on real-fog images. LiDSeg adds only 1.9-3.1M parameters across several models, with total model sizes of 5.2-39.5M parameters and no inference-time reconstruction overhead. It delivers real-time inference on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Code: https://github.com/yobsanb/LiDSeg

</details>


### [218] [MVSTF: Multi-View Spatio-Temporal Fusion for LiDAR-Based Moving Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20872)
*Jiaoju Zhou,Chao Ye,Xuebo Yang,Weiyang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端多视角时空融合网络MVSTF，利用序列点云生成多视角图像与特征差异，并行提取时空与维度特征，实现高精度运动目标分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人在动态环境中识别物体运动属性面临挑战，现有方法受限于边界模糊、遮挡、视角约束及训练复杂度高等问题。

Method: 1) 用序列点云生成多视角图像与特征差异；2) 设计含双下采样块DDB、空间注意力块SAB、多核卷积块MKCNN、双特征融合块DFFB及点融合模块PF的多分支端到端网络，从特征差异提取运动特征、从多视角图像提取几何与语义特征并并行融合；3) 引入辅助语义头提升鲁棒性与可解释性。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI与KITTI-Road基准上验证，在SemanticKITTI MOS验证集与KITTI-Road验证集取得SOTA，在隐藏的SemanticKITTI-MOS测试集上取得有竞争力结果。

Conclusion: MVSTF通过多视角时空融合与辅助语义监督，有效缓解边界模糊、遮挡与视角约束问题，实现高精度运动目标分割，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots confront significant chal lenges in recognizing the motion properties of objects in dy namic environments. However, existing methods are hindered by limitations such as boundary ambiguity, occlusion, viewpoint constraints, and training complexity. In this paper, we propose an innovative end-to-end Multi-View Spatio-Temporal Fusion network, named MVSTF, which utilizes sequential point clouds to generate multi-view images and feature differences, and designs a multi-branch network to extract spatio-temporal and dimen sional features in parallel, and realizes high-precision moving object segmentation. First, sequential point clouds are utilized to generate multi-view images and feature differences. Then, an end-to-end multi-branch network including Dual Down-sampling Blocks (DDB), Spatial Attention Blocks (SAB), Multi-Kernel Convolutional Neural Network blocks (MKCNN), Dual Feature Fusion Blocks (DFFB), and the Point Fusion (PF) module, is designed to extract motion features from feature differences and geometric and semantic features from multi-view images. Finally, an auxiliary semantic head is introduced to further improve the algorithm's robustness and the model's interpretability. This work is validated on the SemanticKITTI and KITTI-Road bench marks, achieving state-of-the-art results on the SemanticKITTI MOS validation set and the KITTI-Road validation set, and competitive results on the hidden SemanticKITTI-MOS test set. The code is available at https://github.com/ZhouJJ111/MVSTF.

</details>


### [219] [Multi-Rate Flow Matching for Point Cloud Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20879)
*Jingkai Shang,Edgar Torres,Duy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多速率流匹配（MRFM），在推理时为每个局部patch自适应分配积分预算，在冻结速度场的前提下实现patch级时间分辨率分配，提升点云上采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配点云上采样器采用固定的时间离散化和全局统一的求解器预算，但形状不同局部的重建需求差异很大，统一的时间分辨率无法匹配局部需求。

Method: MRFM将离散积分预算松弛为连续变量，利用可微分软推演（soft rollouts）配合由输入导出的几何保真度目标进行在线优化，再将结果投影回离散预算用于最终求解器推演；速度场保持冻结，仅做测试时优化。

Result: 在PU1K和PUGAN上取得较强的重建质量；匹配预算的置换实验与多因素分析表明，收益来自patch特定的分配方式，而非单纯的步数分布。

Conclusion: patch级自适应积分预算有效提升流匹配点云上采样性能；该方法以精度为导向，有意使用额外的测试时优化来获得更合适的局部时间分辨率。

Abstract: Flow-matching point-cloud upsamplers refine sparse inputs into dense point clouds by integrating a learned velocity field with a fixed temporal discretization. However, local reconstruction requirements vary substantially across a shape, while a shared solver budget applies the same temporal resolution everywhere. We propose Multi-Rate Flow Matching (MRFM), a patch-adaptive framework that selects an integration budget for each local patch at inference time while keeping the learned velocity field frozen. MRFM relaxes the discrete budget into a continuous variable, optimizes it online through differentiable soft rollouts using an input-derived geometric fidelity objective, and projects the result back to a discrete budget for the final solver rollout. Experiments on PU1K and PUGAN show strong reconstruction quality, while matched-budget permutation and multi-factor analyses demonstrate that the benefit depends on patch-specific allocation rather than on the step distribution alone. MRFM is accuracy-oriented and intentionally uses additional test-time optimization to obtain more suitable local temporal resolutions.

</details>


### [220] [ProSE: Probing Reconstruction Error for Semantic-guided Expert Skipping in MoE-MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20885)
*Jiameng Li,Minye Wu,Matthew B. Blaschko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProSE是一个免训练、语义引导的专家跳过框架，通过逐层探测重构误差并构建查询感知语义掩码，渐进校准各MoE层的模态专属跳过阈值，在保持语义一致性的同时实现自适应专家分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE-MLLM虽通过稀疏专家激活实现可扩展推理，但已激活专家间仍存在大量计算冗余。现有专家跳过方法多以目标跳过率或最终输出差异来校准阈值，忽视了跳过误差在层间的累积以及各token语义重要性的差异。

Method: 提出ProSE框架：1）构建查询感知的语义掩码以识别显著视觉token；2）逐层探测重构误差以促进与默认激活的表示一致性；3）在给定预算下最小化掩码重构误差，渐进校准每层模态专属的跳过阈值，从而在有限激活预算下实现自适应专家分配。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在9个基准上相比现有专家跳过方法取得一致的平均提升，在激进跳过设置下尤为明显；在KimiVL-A3B-Inst上以83%的专家跳过率保持超过96%的性能，并将预填充加速1.7倍。

Conclusion: 以语义引导的层间重构误差探针替代传统基于跳过率或输出差异的阈值校准，可有效缓解误差累积并兼顾token语义重要性，在激进跳过下仍维持性能，为MoE-MLLM免训练推理加速提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE-MLLMs) offer scalable inference through sparse expert activation, yet substantial computational redundancy remains among the activated experts. Existing expert skipping methods typically calibrate thresholds based on target skipping ratios or final-output divergence, overlooking the accumulation of skipping-induced error across layers and the varying semantic importance of individual tokens. To address that, we propose ProSE, a training-free, semantic-guided expert skipping framework that probes layer-wise reconstruction error to promote representation consistency compared with default activation. Specifically, we construct query-aware semantic masks to identify salient visual tokens and progressively calibrate modality-specific skipping thresholds for each MoE layer. By minimizing masked reconstruction error under a given budget, ProSE maintains semantic consistency after skipping and allows adaptive expert allocation under limited activation budgets. Extensive experiments on 9 benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements on average over existing expert skipping methods, particularly under aggressive skipping settings. ProSE maintains over 96\% performance on KimiVL-A3B-Inst with 83\% expert skipping and accelerates prefilling by $1.7\times$.

</details>


### [221] [Certified, Not Explained: Exact Token Erasure in Gated Vision State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20897)
*Sadia Parveen,Tehseen Zia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文检验了视觉Mamba模型中"选择性步长Delta作为可解释性依据"的假设，证明可解释性实际来自掩码加权的读出结构而非门控本身，并提出"保证—检验流程—预注册审计"的可解释性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Mamba模型常被解释为其选择性步长Delta：未被扫描接纳的token不能影响预测。作者要在真实任务上检验这一直觉是否成立，并澄清"门控"到底解释了什么。

Method: 基于单个门控、双向选择性状态空间块构建肺结节恶性度概念瓶颈模型（LIDC-IDRI数据集，15折交叉验证）。理论上分析固定门控模式下的不变性条件；提出保证（掩蔽块中因果卷积输入+读出时关闭token置零权重→概念与任务logits严格不变）及检验流程；进行逐路径knockout验证、与无门控Mamba瓶颈对比、后验排序器比较、置换标签对照、重训练下token信息量分析、Oracle Impurity Score分解，并做预注册审计。

Result: 条件成立时最大logit变化恰为0，不成立时为4.1–6.7；门控模型pooled OOF AUROC 0.8425±0.0085，与无门控（0.8428）持平；后验排序器排序优于门控；置换标签模型的gate图与真实模型间差异不比两个真实种子间更大；门控token信息量高于随机token、在偏移裁剪上高于中心token，但不高于hidden attention所选token；拆分Oracle Impurity Score揭示聚合分数遗漏的差异；分区读出略微降低泄漏，最终模型达到真实概念相关性所隐含的水平。

Conclusion: 可解释性保证属于掩码模型而非门控，门控不应被当作解释；门控应理解为掩码监督产生的"经验证的支撑集"（certified support set），门控本身不充分也不必要。

Abstract: Vision models built on Mamba are often explained through their selective step size Delta, on the assumption that a token the scan does not admit cannot influence the prediction. We test that in a concept bottleneck model for lung-nodule malignancy built on a single gated, bidirectional selective state-space block. The answer becomes an interpretability framework: a guarantee, a procedure that checks it, and a pre-registered audit of what it explains. For a fixed gate pattern, masking the input of the causal convolution of the block and giving closed tokens zero weight at readout makes the concept and task logits exactly invariant to the closed tokens. Gating Delta is neither necessary nor sufficient. On LIDC-IDRI, a path-by-path knockout over 15 cross-validated models agrees with this in every run: the largest logit change is exactly 0 whenever the conditions hold and between 4.1 and 6.7 when they do not. The gated model reaches a pooled out-of-fold AUROC of 0.8425 +/- 0.0085, level with an ungated Mamba bottleneck (0.8428). The guarantee belongs to the masked model, not to the gate as an explanation. Post-hoc rankers, which inherit the guarantee on this model, order tokens better than the gate. Gate maps from a model trained on permuted labels are as close to the real maps as two real-label seeds are to each other. Under retraining, the tokens of the gate carry more task information than random tokens and, on shifted crops, more than central ones, but no more than the tokens chosen by hidden attention. We therefore read the gate as a certified support set produced by mask supervision. Splitting the Oracle Impurity Score into own-concept and cross-concept parts reveals differences the aggregate score misses. Partitioning the readout reduced leakage slightly under an earlier recipe, and the final model sits at, rather than below, the level implied by correlations among the true concepts.

</details>


### [222] [SciWorldBench: Do Video World Models Really Understand Scientific Experiments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20899)
*Xue Yang,Haonan Cai,Xinyu Hang,Zhimeng Huang,Xuanyi Liu,Chuanmin Jia,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SciWorldBench基准，用"仅改变单一条件的配对科学实验"检验视频生成模型是否真正理解科学原理，发现十个TI2V模型普遍对配对条件生成相同（错误）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成评测多对单个样本孤立打分，难以识别只拟合表面视觉先验而非掌握科学原理的模型，缺乏能暴露这一缺陷的评测手段。

Method: 构建由配对科学实验组成的基准：每对仅改变一个条件、其余设置固定，对应两个互斥的正确结果；提示词只给条件、不给结果。用独立VLM评测视频，且评测时不提供条件文本与正确结果标签；仅当两段视频各自呈现其科学正确结果时整对判对。

Result: 在十个TI2V模型上发现，条件要求不同结果时，同一配对中的两段视频仍频繁呈现相同结果，即模型对条件的敏感性失效。

Conclusion: 当前视频世界模型难以在生成中可靠应用科学原理，多停留在视觉先验层面；基准数据集将公开、评测代码将开源。

Abstract: While the ability of video generation models to accurately depict scientific phenomena has received growing attention, determining whether these models genuinely understand the underlying principles remains a critical challenge. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate video samples in isolation, making it difficult to expose models that simply fit superficial visual priors. In scientific experiments, a small change in one condition produces a markedly different outcome even when the rest of the setup remains fixed. Capitalizing on this sensitivity, we introduce SciWorldBench, a benchmark comprising paired scientific experiments. Each pair varies a single condition in an otherwise fixed setup, yielding two mutually exclusive expected outcomes. Each generation prompt specifies the condition but omits the expected outcome. We evaluate ten text-and-image-to-video (TI2V) models using an independent vision-language model (VLM) that assesses each video without access to the condition text or the label identifying the correct outcome. A pair is scored as correct only when both videos depict their respective scientifically correct outcomes. Across all ten models, we find that the two videos in an experimental pair frequently depict the same outcome even though the conditions require different results. This recurring failure suggests that current video world models struggle to apply the underlying scientific principles reliably during generation. We will publicly release the benchmark dataset and open-source the evaluation code.

</details>


### [223] [Can Image Generators Hold a Scene Together When Space Is Broken Apart?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20906)
*Jhen-Ke Lin,Chung-Chun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出GJBench基准，将"属于同一整体"与"空间相邻"解耦，用"散乱拼图"任务检验图像生成模型是否真正理解场景整体性。结果显示所有开放与闭源模型整体绘制良好，但无一能在散乱拼图条件下保持场景连续性，该问题仍为开放难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成模型与评测基准中，"属于同一整体"与"彼此相邻"总是同时出现，因此无法判断模型是真正把握了场景整体，还是仅让各局部与相邻局部相连。需要将二者解耦以进行严格检验。

Method: 提出GJBench：要求模型将场景绘制为散乱拼图——图像被切分为互相咬合的拼块，再被分离并旋转，使场景的空间合理性仅体现在各拼块共同指向同一整体。任务可提供参考图、目标布局、二者兼有或均无。分别评测三项要求：是否分解为指定数量的独立拼块、拼块是否咬合、拼块能否重建参考场景。

Result: 所有被评测的开放与闭源模型均能良好地整体绘制此类场景，但均无法将其保持为散乱拼图。三项要求将失败划分为不同模式：模型能绘制独立拼块但以不同方式数错数量；除非提供轮廓，拼块很少咬合；其内容也无法重建参考图。目标布局有助于拼块咬合，参考图对拼块数量与咬合均无改善。

Conclusion: 在画布不再显示的空间范围内保持场景连续性，对图像生成器仍是未解决的问题，揭示了当前模型整体场景理解能力的根本局限。

Abstract: Image generators are good at drawing scenes in which things that belong together appear next to each other. In almost every image they generate, and in almost every benchmark used to evaluate these images, "belonging to the same whole" and "adjacent to each other" go together. Therefore, we cannot know whether the model truly grasps the scene as a whole, or simply makes each part connect with its adjacent parts. We propose GJBench to disentangle the two. It requires the model to draw a scene as a scattered jigsaw puzzle: an image is cut into interlocking pieces, then moved apart and rotated. Thus, the spatial plausibility of the scene exists only in how these pieces collectively point to a whole. The model may have a reference image, a target layout, both, or neither. We separately evaluate three requirements: whether the scene is broken down into a specified number of separate pieces, whether the pieces interlock, and whether the pieces can reconstruct the reference scene. While every open and closed model we evaluated could draw these scenes well as a whole, none could keep them together as scattered puzzle pieces, and the three requirements categorized failures into different patterns. The models draw separate pieces but miscount them in different ways, and unless their outlines are provided, the pieces rarely interlock, and their content does not reconstruct the reference image. We also found that the target layout helps the puzzle pieces fit together, while the reference image improves neither their count nor their fit. These findings show that keeping a scene continuous across space that the canvas no longer shows remains an open problem for image generators.

</details>


### [224] [Palmprint Decomposer: Interpretable and Editable Factorization via Synthetic Learning and Instance-Wise Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20913)
*Licheng Yan,Yuqi Wang,Jingtao Wang,Bob Zhang,Andrew Beng Jin Teoh,Lu Leng,Shuyi Li,Ziyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Palmprint Decomposer，一个可解释的掌纹表示框架，将掌纹图像分解为独立可编辑的因子（颜色图、光照图表征基础外观，结构图与全局纹理强度参数表征纹理调制），并通过乘法组合模型重建图像；借助合成数据监督、非对称解码与分解约束抑制纹理泄漏，并以实例级精化提升真实图像重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掌纹识别与合成方法或聚焦判别性纹理提取，或生成多样化外观的图像，但普遍缺乏显式的图像级表示来将掌纹纹理与基础外观分离，因而无法进行独立分析或可控编辑。

Method: 提出通过独立可编辑因子表示掌纹的框架，采用乘法组合模型重建图像：颜色图与光照图刻画基础外观，结构图与全局纹理强度参数描述纹理调制。为缓解纹理泄漏到基础外观的歧义，仅在带直接因子监督的合成数据上训练分解器，使用非对称解码路径与分解约束抑制纹理泄漏；并提出Instance-Wise Refinement，在推理时直接对每个输入优化预测因子，不依赖已训练网络。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明重建保真度高，提取的结构图在验证任务上性能总体与原图相当；与现有方法相比，在保持更具判别性掌纹纹理信息的同时实现了更好的因子分离。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了显式、可解释且可独立编辑的掌纹图像级因子化表示，验证了纹理与基础外观有效分离的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Palmprint recognition and synthesis methods focus on extracting discriminative textures or generating images with varied textures and appearances. However, they do not generally provide an explicit image-level representation that separates palmprint texture from its underlying base appearance for independent analysis or controlled editing. We introduce Palmprint Decomposer, an interpretable framework that represents a palmprint through independently editable factors and reconstructs the image through a multiplicative composition model. A color map and a light map characterize the base appearance, while a structure map and a global texture strength parameter describe texture modulation. However, accurate reconstruction alone does not guarantee separation of palmprint texture from base appearance, as fine palmprint textures may leak into the estimated base appearance. To mitigate this ambiguity, we train the decomposer exclusively on synthetic data with direct factor supervision, using asymmetric decoding paths and decomposition constraints to suppress texture leakage. Instance-Wise Refinement further improves real-image reconstruction by directly optimizing the predicted factors for each input without relying on the trained network. Experiments across seven datasets demonstrate high reconstruction fidelity, with the extracted structure maps generally achieving verification performance comparable to the original images. Compared with existing methods, our approach achieves better factor separation while preserving more discriminative palmprint texture information.

</details>


### [225] [EviBridge: Encoding Predictive Visual Evidence for Proactive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20916)
*Zhichao Huang,Changheng Cai,Song Ren,Zhichen Zhao,Hongsen Liu,Chaoyang Zheng,Wenxi Wu,Yichen Long,Yuan Fang,Luya Wang,Gufeng Liu,Yuchen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviBridge 将冻结的 V-JEPA 2 预测性视觉表征通过可训练证据接口注入视觉语言交互模型，用于工具交接中的联合状态与工具预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具交接交互需要"先准备、后响应"：模型须等待交互线索出现，在累积证据中判断应响应还是取消。现有任务适配的视觉语言基线缺乏前瞻性视觉证据，难以在流式长时程中区分近期/历史线索并把握响应时机。

Method: 提出 EviBridge 框架：用可训练证据接口把冻结 V-JEPA 2 编码器的特征与原生视觉输入一同映射进语言解码器，保留时序上下文，且只使用各决策时刻可得的观测。两个实例：EviBridge-E 区分近期与历史证据并编码每次观测的时间与空间位置；EviBridge-I 保留池化 V-JEPA 2 特征与交互轮次的时间顺序并生成专用交互 token。在流式视频基准 FuGIBench 上评测准备、响应、取消三类行为。

Result: EviBridge-E 与 EviBridge-I 最佳变体 Overall 得分分别为 0.6448 与 0.5892，对应任务适配原生基线为 0.2178 与 0.5327；增益覆盖目标交接、长等待与被取消请求三类场景。

Conclusion: 预测性视觉证据对交互决策具有明确价值；以轻量适配接口复用冻结自监督视频编码器，可在不明显改动主干的前提下显著提升流式工具交接的时机判断与取消能力。

Abstract: Tool-handover interaction calls for preparation before a response is warranted. The model waits until interaction cues appear, then prepares while assessing whether the accumulating evidence supports a response or calls for cancellation. We introduce EviBridge, a framework that integrates predictive visual representations into a vision-language interaction model for joint state and tool prediction. A trainable evidence interface maps features from a frozen V-JEPA 2 encoder into the language decoder alongside native visual inputs, preserving their temporal context and using only observations available at each decision time. The framework has two instantiations. EviBridge-E distinguishes recent from historical evidence and encodes the timing and spatial location of each observation. EviBridge-I preserves the temporal order of pooled V-JEPA 2 features and interaction turns and generates dedicated interaction tokens. We evaluate on FuGIBench, a streaming-video benchmark for preparation, response, and cancellation in tool-handover assistance. The best-performing variants of EviBridge-E and EviBridge-I achieve Overall scores of 0.6448 and 0.5892, compared with 0.2178 and 0.5327 for their respective task-adapted native baselines. These gains span target handovers, long waits, and cancelled requests, demonstrating the value of predictive visual evidence for interaction decisions.

</details>


### [226] [ReAccess: Look Once, Stay Focused with Efficient Test Time Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20936)
*Tianhao Chen,Yilong Fan,Yuheng Wu,Xiaobin Hu,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReAccess，一个轻量框架，通过紧凑的联想记忆（associative memory）保存并复用与问题相关的视觉证据，单次视觉预填充构建，支持渐进式证据检索以引导注意力查询和隐表示，从而在无需重复编码图像或显式推理轨迹的情况下实现有效视觉定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在细粒度视觉理解上仍存不足，往往无法有效利用已编码于视觉表示中的关键证据；现有方法通过重复视觉处理或显式推理来弥补，带来显著计算开销。

Method: 提出 ReAccess 轻量框架：在单次视觉预填充（visual prefill）过程中构建紧凑的联想记忆，用于保存与问题相关的视觉证据；推理时进行渐进式证据检索，以引导注意力查询（attention queries）与隐表示（hidden representations），实现视觉grounding。该方法避免重复图像编码与显式推理轨迹。

Result: 大量实验表明，在通用视觉理解与细粒度感知两方面均取得一致提升，同时推理速度显著快于现有推理密集型方法。

Conclusion: ReAccess 为高效多模态推理提供了一条有前景的路径：通过记忆复用的证据检索替代昂贵的重复视觉处理与显式推理，在性能与效率之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Despite recent advances, vision-language models (VLMs) still struggle with fine-grained visual understanding, often failing to effectively utilize critical evidence already encoded in their visual representations. Existing approaches address this limitation through repeated visual processing or explicit reasoning, introducing substantial computational overhead. In this paper, we propose ReAccess, a lightweight framework that preserves and reuses question-relevant visual evidence through compact associative memory. Constructed during a single visual prefill, the memory supports progressive evidence retrieval that steers both attention queries and hidden representations, enabling effective visual grounding without repeated image encoding or explicit reasoning traces. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in both general visual understanding and fine-grained perception, while achieving substantially faster inference than existing reasoning-intensive approaches, demonstrating a promising path to efficient multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [227] [vividRGB: Various visual cue detection in RGB photographs with forced focused attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20942)
*Masahiro Ryo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、编码器无关的"强制聚焦注意力"(FFA)及其Python实现vividRGB：对放大重叠局部裁剪应用冻结编码器、对齐反射取平均并在源坐标重建特征场，在五个生态域上RGB边缘边界F1显著优于直接DINOv3编码与SAM3掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然照片包含密集、重叠结构，整图视觉编码器的特征图中此类结构表现微弱，导致密集视觉线索难以被保留。

Method: FFA为推理阶段流程：将冻结编码器（DINOv3 ViT-B/16）应用于放大且重叠的局部裁剪，对逆对齐的反射结果取平均，再在源图像坐标中重建特征场；实验使用50张高分辨率照片，覆盖草地植被、真菌网络、机载森林、珊瑚礁与浮游植物显微五个域，以RGB边缘边界F1为指标与直接编码及SAM3自动掩码对比。

Result: FFA在5%或10%聚焦比例下各域平均边界F1为0.244–0.596，直接DINOv3为0.028–0.076，SAM3为0.020–0.090；定性特征图显示更精细的外观结构；更窄聚焦比例通常提升边界保持，但提取时间与GPU能耗急剧上升且收益递减。

Conclusion: 结果仅涉及RGB边缘保持，而非已验证物种、性状或生物分割；FFA可能适用于重视密集视觉线索的小规模高分辨率生态影像集；发布alpha版vividRGB 0.1.0及带版本的研究资源以支持可复现的线索检查。

Abstract: Natural photographs contain dense, overlapping structures that can be weakly expressed in feature maps from whole-image vision encoders. We introduce forced focused attention (FFA) and its Python implementation, vividRGB. FFA is an encoder-agnostic inference-time procedure that applies a frozen encoder to enlarged, overlapping local crops, averages inverse-aligned reflections, and reconstructs a feature field in source coordinates. We evaluate 50 high-resolution photographs from grassland vegetation, fungal networks, airborne forests, coral reefs, and phytoplankton microscopy, using DINOv3 ViT-B/16. Across the five domains, FFA at 5% or 10% focus produces higher mean RGB-edge boundary F1 than direct DINOv3 encoding and automatic SAM3 masks. Domain means range from 0.244 to 0.596 for FFA, versus 0.028-0.076 for direct DINOv3 and 0.020-0.090 for SAM3. Qualitative feature maps also show finer appearance structure. These results concern RGB-edge preservation rather than verified species, traits, or biological segmentation. Narrower focus generally improves boundary preservation, while extraction time and GPU energy use increase sharply and gains diminish at finer settings. FFA may be useful for relatively small, high-resolution ecological image collections where dense visual cues matter. The alpha release vividRGB 0.1.0 and versioned research resources support reproducible cue inspection.

</details>


### [228] [FAVR: Flow-Aware Visual Residual Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20971)
*Qixuan Wu,Xiaole Yang,Yuguang Yang,Canyu Chen,Linlin Yang,Haixia Pan,Guodong Guo,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAVR：用线性TTT维持紧凑时序视觉状态，并沿流式动作生成检索动作条件视觉特征，通过盒约束优化实现流感知通道门控，轻量提升冻结VLA策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频表征蕴含丰富时序信息，但难以有效注入现有VLA策略；同时需保持轻量、不破坏冻结主干。

Method: 线性Test-Time Training维护紧凑时序视觉状态；利用冻结动作头仿射结构刻画注入视觉特征对动作速度的影响；将流感知通道选择建模为盒约束优化，采用FISTA求解联合门控，并经对角化与重缩放给出闭式近似。

Result: 仅1.74–2.19M可训练参数、5000步适配即在多基准提升预训练VLA；RoboTwin 2.0上LingBot-VLA由87.12%升至90.04%（干净）、86.88%升至89.38%（随机化）；五任务消融中流感知门控使平均成功率由72.0%提升至82.0%。

Conclusion: 沿动作生成各流步自适应视觉条件优于跨步共享门控，FAVR以极小开销实现有效视觉残差对齐。

Abstract: Pretrained video representations provide rich temporal information for robotic manipulation, yet effectively integrating them into existing vision-language-action (VLA) policies remains challenging. We present Flow-Aware Visual Residual Alignment (FAVR), a lightweight framework for flow-aware visual conditioning of frozen VLA policies. FAVR maintains a compact temporal visual state through linear Test-Time Training (TTT) and retrieves action-conditioned visual features throughout flow-based action generation. By exploiting the affine structure of the frozen action head, we explicitly characterize how injected visual features influence predicted action velocities and formulate flow-aware channel selection as a box-constrained optimization problem. We develop a FISTA-based solver for joint gate optimization and derive an efficient closed-form approximation through diagonalization and rescaling. With only 1.74-2.19M trainable parameters and 5,000 adaptation steps, FAVR improves pretrained VLA policies across benchmarks. On RoboTwin 2.0, FAVR improves LingBot-VLA from 87.12% to 90.04% under clean conditions and from 86.88% to 89.38% under randomized conditions. A controlled ablation on five RoboTwin tasks further shows that flow-aware gating increases average success from 72.0% with a gate shared across flow steps to 82.0%, demonstrating the benefit of adapting visual conditioning throughout action generation.

</details>


### [229] [MedJev: A System One Vision-Language Decision Model for Fast and Effective Medical Image Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20992)
*Fan Zhang,Hao Wu,Jinyang Wu,Hongyu Qu,Zheng Lian,Dongfu Yin,Qingsong Wen,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedJev 是一个"系统一"式医学视觉语言决策模型，将图像、问题、候选答案经单次多模态前向直接映射为决策，去除自回归生成，实现更快更准的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像理解 VLM 依赖自回归生成推理与答案 token，导致推理成本随响应长度增长，且需从自由文本中解析答案，带来可避免的延迟与决策错误。

Method: 移除上层语言层与生成式输出通路，保留完整视觉通路与全部视觉 token；在缩短的主干上使用决策头，通过预训练 query–key 匹配为各候选打分，并用轻量 MLP 残差精化分数，将预训练匹配结构与任务特定非线性判别相结合。

Result: 基于 Qwen3.5-4B 的 MedJev-4B 在四个医学图像分类与 VQA 基准上均同时提升准确率与速度，绝对准确率提升 7.93%，端到端加速 6.04 倍；与 20 个前沿模型比较，在 4B–9B 通用及医学 VLM 中达到 SOTA 性能与推理速度。

Conclusion: 直接多模态决策是通往快速、有效医学图像理解的有效路径。

Abstract: Medical image understanding with vision-language models (VLMs) typically relies on autoregressively generating reasoning and answer tokens. These unnecessary tokens make the inference cost scale with the response length and force the answer to be parsed from free-form text, incurring avoidable latency and decision errors. To this end, we introduce \textsc{MedJev}, a \emph{system one} vision-language decision model that maps medical images, questions, and candidate answers directly to decisions in a single multimodal forward pass, without any autoregressive generation process. For fast inference, \textsc{MedJev} removes the upper language layers and the generative output pathway, while keeping the full vision pathway and all visual tokens. On this shortened backbone, a decision head scores each candidate by pretrained query--key matching and refines the scores with a lightweight multilayer perceptron (MLP) residual, coupling the pretrained matching structure with task-specific nonlinear discrimination. Built on Qwen3.5-4B, \textsc{MedJev}-4B improves both accuracy and speed over the original model on each of four medical image classification and visual question answering benchmarks, with an absolute accuracy gain of \textbf{7.93\%} and a \textbf{6.04$\times$} end-to-end speedup. Further comparison with 20 cutting-edge models shows that \textsc{MedJev}-4B achieves state-of-the-art performance and inference speed among 4B--9B general and medical VLMs, indicating that direct multimodal decision making is an effective route to fast and effective medical image understanding.

</details>


### [230] [Digits in Notation Are Not Numbers: A Scoring Pitfall in PDF-to-Markdown Conversion of Japanese Technical Documents (Preliminary Evaluation)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20993)
*Mitsumasa Wada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估MinerU、纯文本OCR基线(NDLOCR-Lite)及确定性后处理流水线在48页日文文档上的转换质量，发现预设的数字-单位F1与双引擎分歧检查均因评分器统计转换器记法噪声而失真；去除记法后两者F1持平(0.575 vs 0.594)，重分类检查仅标记13%数字错误。OCR基线正文CER更低，流水线仅提升多余数字召回但降低标题一致性、公式修复从未触发。依预设标准，本研究仍为技术报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDF转Markdown已成为检索与语言模型的常规预处理，但对其输出中数字的评分容易出错（记法噪声、共享漏检等），需在日文文档上系统检验转换器与OCR基线及后处理流水线的真实表现。

Method: 在48个公开日文页面（3个文档，文本层与栅格化两种版本）上对比MinerU、纯文本OCR基线NDLOCR-Lite及一个确定性后处理流水线（锚点插入、脚注重排、固定上下文形近字符修正、双独立OCR伙伴门控的公式修复、标题重建）；采用预设的数字-单位F1、双引擎分歧检查与事后去除记法的复核，并辅以正文CER、多余数字召回、标题级一致性及人工判定的精度门限(0.80)。

Result: 预设数字-单位F1偏向OCR基线(0.575 vs 0.397)，分歧检查似可标记转换器约54.6%的数字错误，但二者均为伪象：评分器统计了转换器记法（图片链接路径、HTML属性）中的数字。去除记法后F1持平(0.575 vs 0.594)，检查仅标记13%错误，因多数错误系两引擎共享的漏检。OCR基线正文CER更低(栅格化0.012 vs 0.053)；流水线提升页级多余数字召回(+0.145，文档级区间触及0)，主要靠恢复页码，但标题级一致性由0.133降至0.061，虚假公式未变，公式修复从未触发。预设精度门限达标(0.929 vs 0.80)。

Conclusion: 后处理假设在其改进项上未能成立；建议在去除记法后再对数字评分，并将关键数字与源文档核对。按预设标准，本研究仍定位为技术报告而非完整实证结论。

Abstract: Converting PDFs to Markdown is now routine preprocessing for search and language models, yet scoring the numbers in the output is easy to get wrong. We evaluate MinerU, a plain-text OCR baseline (NDLOCR-Lite), and a deterministic post-processing pipeline (anchor insertion, footnote reordering, fixes of look-alike characters in fixed contexts, gated formula repair using two independent OCR partners, and heading reconstruction) on 48 public Japanese pages (three documents), text-layer and rasterized. Our pre-specified number-and-unit F1 favored the OCR baseline (0.575 vs 0.397), and a disagreement check between the two engines seemed to flag about half of the converter's number errors (54.6%). Both were artifacts. The scorer counted digits in the converter's notation (image-link paths, HTML attributes), which plain text lacks. With the notation removed (a post-hoc check), the F1 scores tie (0.575 vs 0.594), and the check flags only 13% of number errors, because most errors are misses that both engines share. What remains: the OCR baseline has a lower body character error rate (0.012 vs 0.053 rasterized). The pipeline raises extra-number recall at page level (+0.145; the document-level interval touches 0), mostly by restoring page numbers, but lowers heading-level agreement (0.133 to 0.061) and leaves spurious formulas unchanged; its formula repair never fires. A pre-specified precision gate for its flags (0.80, human-judged) is met at 0.929, yet the post-processing hypothesis fails on its improvement items. We recommend scoring numbers after removing notation and checking critical figures against the source. By the pre-specified criteria, the study stays a technical report.

</details>


### [231] [Sparse Attention for Visual Generation: Toward Algorithm-System Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21029)
*Majun Zhang,Yuan Guo,Decheng Wu,Jiayang Xu,Changhao Pan,Fan Zhuo,Xiaoda Yang,Chenyuhao Wen,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是关于视觉生成中稀疏注意力的综述，提出"4+1+3"分析框架，将算法设计与系统实现联系起来，并总结评测指标与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像与视频生成向高分辨率、长时长发展使视觉token序列急剧增长，稠密自注意力的二次复杂度成为主要瓶颈；稀疏注意力可降低计算成本，但端到端收益取决于稀疏支撑集构建的代价以及工作负载向硬件的映射效率，亟需算法与系统协同设计的系统性梳理。

Method: 提出4+1+3分析框架：4个算法维度考察静态与动态方法如何利用来源信息及其证据来决定保留哪些query-key交互及保留粒度；1个维度考察复用（减少重复计算）与校正（补偿被省略贡献）的作用；3个系统维度（数据组织、执行、数据流）将算法选择对应到硬件实现。

Result: 在该框架下系统梳理了静态/动态稀疏方法的决策依据与粒度、复用与校正机制，并将其与硬件实现维度关联；同时汇总了常用评测指标。

Conclusion: 为稀疏注意力算法与系统的联合设计提供参考，并讨论开放挑战与未来研究方向。

Abstract: Rapid advances in image and video generation have enabled high-fidelity synthesis at higher resolutions and longer durations, making the quadratic cost of dense self-attention a major bottleneck as visual token sequences grow. Sparse attention seeks to preserve generation quality at lower computational cost by retaining informative query-key interactions and omitting less consequential ones, with end-to-end gains depending on the cost of constructing sparse support and the efficiency with which the system maps the resulting workload to hardware. We survey sparse attention for visual generation through a 4+1+3 analytical framework that links algorithmic design to system implementation. The framework examines how static and dynamic methods use source information and the evidence derived from it to decide which interactions to retain and at what granularity, while accounting for the roles of reuse in reducing repeated computation and correction in compensating for omitted contributions. We relate these algorithmic choices to their implementation on hardware through the framework's three system dimensions of data organization, execution, and dataflow. We summarize common evaluation metrics and discuss open challenges and research directions, providing researchers and practitioners with a reference for the joint design of sparse attention algorithms and systems.

</details>


### [232] [POKE: Probe Offline, Keep Efficiently for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21033)
*Jaehyuk Lee,Yeajin Lee,Suhyun Bae,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 POKE：离线在全图上计算动作敏感度并蒸馏到<0.3M参数的学生网络，实现推理时无需反传的视觉token剪枝，避免"评分图锁定"偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型推理成本高，视觉token剪枝可降本，但基于注意力的评分并不直接反映token对预测动作的影响；而在线计算动作敏感度代价高：全图评分需每步额外前向，剪枝图评分则导致"评分图锁定"——被删token失去后续层的直接通路而得分持续偏低，难以回归。实验显示剪枝图评分在重放观测上会重选59%的掩码token（随机期望仅6%），注意力历史也有类似偏差。

Method: 将动作敏感度定义为"token被替换为其相机均值token时动作的一阶变化幅度"，用单次反向传播为所有token估计；POKE(Probe Offline, Keep Efficiently)离线在全图上计算该敏感度，蒸馏到一个<0.3M参数的学生网络；推理时学生网络仅凭当前观测打分，无需反传或历史掩码，再以分数引导的多样性选择保留token。

Result: 在LIBERO上从512个视觉token中保留32个：OpenVLA-OFT成功率95.2%（1.85×加速），π0.5成功率90.8%（1.26×加速），优于基于注意力、多样性及蒸馏的剪枝方法；在LIBERO-Plus上优势更明显。

Conclusion: 以离线全图动作敏感度为教师、轻量学生在线打分，可规避剪枝图评分偏差，在显著剪枝率下保持高成功率并带来实际加速，是VLA推理加速的有效方案。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the inference cost of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, but attention-based scores do not directly measure a token's effect on the predicted action. We define action sensitivity as the magnitude of the first-order action change when a token is replaced by its camera's mean token and estimate it for all tokens with a single backward pass. Computing it online is costly: scoring on the full (unpruned) graph needs an extra forward pass at every control step. Scoring on the pruned forward pass is cheaper but causes scoring-graph lock-in: removed tokens lose their direct paths through later layers and tend to receive lower scores, making those locations less likely to return. On replayed observations, even with a random conditioning mask, pruned-graph scoring re-selects 59% of the mask's tokens, versus 6% expected by chance; attention histories show a similar bias. POKE (Probe Offline, Keep Efficiently) computes full-graph action sensitivities offline and distills them into a student with under 0.3M parameters. At inference, the student scores the current observation without backpropagation or previous masks, and score-guided diversity selection retains the tokens. Keeping 32 of 512 visual tokens on LIBERO, POKE achieves 95.2% success on OpenVLA-OFT and 90.8% on $π_{0.5}$ at $1.85\times$ and $1.26\times$ speedups, outperforming attention-, diversity-, and distillation-based pruners, with larger margins on LIBERO-Plus.

</details>


### [233] [ScoutCache: Efficient Few-Step Autoregressive Video Diffusion via Conditional Readout and Selective Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21038)
*Hao Tan,Jinghui Qin,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScoutCache 是一个免训练框架，联合优化去噪、历史 KV 读取与缓存构建，在 Self-Forcing 与 LongLive 上分别实现最高 2.28× 与 1.86× 加速且生成质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步自回归视频扩散虽能高效生成长视频，但推理阶段重复去噪与 KV 缓存操作仍带来巨大计算开销；现有加速方法只覆盖部分开销，历史 KV 读取与跨视频块缓存构建依然昂贵。

Method: 基于两点观察——浅层特征变化可用于估计干净潜变量更新、历史 KV 依赖随层与注意力头变化但跨视频块较稳定——提出三个组件：条件去噪读出（CDR）由浅层特征变化与已知噪声变化估计中间去噪更新；输出风险引导的历史 KV 读取（ORHR）按注意力输出扰动分配读取预算；内容与覆盖引导的稀疏缓存构建（CCSC）选择有信息量的空间 token 并保持空间覆盖以降低构建成本。

Result: 在 Self-Forcing 与 LongLive 上使用 VBench 与 MovieGen 评测，分别取得最高 2.28× 与 1.86× 加速，同时在未修改预训练模型参数的前提下持续提升生成质量。

Conclusion: 免训练的统一加速框架可同时优化去噪计算、历史 KV 读取与缓存构建，在不改动模型权重下兼顾效率与画质，适用于长视频自回归扩散推理。

Abstract: Few-step autoregressive video diffusion enables efficient long video generation, but inference still incurs substantial computational costs from repeated denoising and KV cache operations. Existing acceleration methods reduce parts of this overhead, while historical KV reading and cache construction remain costly across video chunks. To address these challenges, we propose ScoutCache, a training-free framework that jointly optimizes denoising, historical KV reading, and cache construction. We observe that shallow feature changes provide useful information for estimating clean latent updates, while historical KV dependence varies across Transformer layers and attention heads but remains relatively stable across video chunks. Based on these observations, we develop Conditional Denoising Readout (CDR) to estimate intermediate denoising updates from shallow feature changes and known noise variations. We further introduce Output Risk Guided Historical KV Reading (ORHR) to allocate historical reading budgets based on attention output perturbations, and Content and Coverage Guided Sparse Cache Construction (CCSC) to reduce cache construction costs by selecting informative spatial tokens while maintaining spatial coverage. Extensive experiments on Self-Forcing and LongLive using VBench and MovieGen demonstrate that ScoutCache achieves up to 2.28$\times$ and 1.86$\times$ speedups, respectively, while consistently improving generation quality without modifying pretrained model parameters.

</details>


### [234] [Body of Evidence: Triangulating 3D Human Pose with Geometry-Conditioned Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21044)
*Erik Brorsson,Kilian Freitag,Josef Bengtson,Kristofer Bengtsson,Lars Hammarstrand,Knut Åkesson,Fredrik Kahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Body of Evidence (BoE)多视角3D人体姿态估计器，通过每相机关节深度预测、几何条件深度预测与可学习深度感知三角化(LDAT)，实现单相机也能重建，稀疏相机下显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相机设置下遮挡仍会使某些关节仅有单一2D观测，该观测只确定一条视线而深度未定，导致三角化失效。单目深度估计又存在固有的深度歧义。

Method: BoE在预测2D关节位置的同时预测每相机关节深度；提出几何条件深度预测，将图像特征与已重建3D人体几何作为上下文深度线索融合以缓解单目歧义；提出可学习深度感知三角化(LDAT)，在可微代数三角化中引入预测深度约束，使单视角也能重建，多视角时保留几何三角化优势；重建结果迭代反馈指导下一轮深度预测，实现深度与3D几何互相精化。在CMU Panoptic上验证。

Result: 在CMU Panoptic上达到域内SOTA，在各相机子集与未见相机布局下平均性能更优；稀疏相机覆盖下尤其有效，三相机设置下较此前SOTA提升10.6 AP25。

Conclusion: 将深度预测与几何三角化在迭代框架中耦合，可有效应对遮挡与稀疏视角，为多视角3D人体姿态估计提供稳健方案。

Abstract: Even in multi-camera setups, occlusions can leave individual body joints with only a single usable 2D observation. Such an observation defines a viewing ray but leaves the joint's depth undetermined. We introduce Body of Evidence (BoE), a multi-view 3D human pose estimator that predicts per-camera joint depths alongside 2D joint locations. To reduce monocular depth ambiguity, we propose geometry-conditioned depth prediction, which combines image features with previously reconstructed 3D body geometry as contextual depth cues. We further introduce learnable depth-aware triangulation (LDAT), which augments differentiable algebraic triangulation with predicted depth constraints, enabling reconstruction even from a single contributing camera while preserving the benefits of geometric triangulation when multiple views are available. The reconstructed 3D poses subsequently guide depth prediction in the next iteration, allowing depth estimates and 3D geometry to progressively refine each other. Experiments on CMU Panoptic demonstrate state-of-the-art in-domain performance and superior average performance across evaluated camera subsets and unseen camera arrangements. BoE is particularly effective under sparse camera coverage, outperforming the previous state of the art by 10.6 AP25 points in the challenging three-camera setting.

</details>


### [235] [Dataset Distillation for Handwritten Text Recognition: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21054)
*Félix Fuentes-Hurtado,Aric Ayllón,Juan C. Alfaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个面向行级手写文本识别（HTR）的数据集蒸馏研究：以字符n-gram聚类转录定义文本伪类，将GM/DM/MTT迁移到CTC训练的CRNN，结果显示蒸馏集均不优于真实初始行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据集蒸馏（DD）用小型合成集替代大规模训练集以降低计算成本，但此前仅用于分类；HTR使用未对齐序列标签、输出空间近乎无限，缺乏对应研究。

Method: 以字符n-gram对转录聚类构造文本伪类；将梯度匹配（GM）、分布匹配（DM）、轨迹匹配（MTT）适配到用CTC训练的CRNN；合成图像仅优化真实起始行（随机选取或文本medoid）的像素，转录保持不变。

Result: 在IAM、George Washington、READ 2016、LAM四个数据集及1–10%三个预算下，没有任何蒸馏集优于其真实初始行；随机行优于medoid；额外优化只会降低识别性能；DM目标下降但无收益，GM与MTT目标几乎不变。

Conclusion: 分类式DD方法难以直接迁移到HTR，据此提出评估HTR数据压缩的准则。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) replaces large training datasets with smaller synthetic ones to reduce computational costs. While traditionally used for classification, we present, to our knowledge, the first DD study for line-level handwritten text recognition (HTR). Unlike classification tasks, which use discrete labels, HTR employs unaligned sequences from a virtually infinite output space as labels. To adapt classification-based DD methods to this scenario, we define text pseudo-classes by clustering transcriptions based on character n-grams. We adapt gradient matching (GM), distribution matching (DM) and trajectory matching (MTT) to a Convolutional Recurrent Neural Network (CRNN) trained with Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC). In this process, synthetic line images are generated by optimising only the pixels of real starting lines (either randomly chosen or selected as text medoids), while keeping their original transcriptions fixed. Across four datasets (IAM, George Washington, READ\,2016, LAM) and three budgets (1--10\,\% of the training lines), no distilled set outperforms its real initialisation lines. Random lines prove better than medoids, and further optimisation only degrades recognition. Methodologically, DM lowers its objective without benefit, while GM and MTT barely move theirs. We turn these findings into guidelines for evaluating data compression in HTR.

</details>


### [236] [MetricHand: Geometry-Aware Flow Matching for 4D Hand Reconstruction in Metric World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21060)
*Yibo Bai,Likui Zhang,Jinglei Zhang,Zhengbin Pang,Xingshen Song,Xiaoqing Yin,Jinsheng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何感知的流匹配框架MetricHand，实现度量世界空间中的4D手部重建，显著降低相机空间与世界空间MPJPE、腕部定位及加速度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称手部运动可为灵巧操作与视觉-语言-动作预训练提供物理运动监督，但需在度量世界空间中获得准确且时间连贯的4D手部重建。现有方法将弱透视下的逐帧估计“提升”到世界坐标，忽略近距离下与关节相关的深度变化，导致平移、朝向与关节化误差相互耦合，且在遮挡下恶化。

Method: 提出MetricHand，一个几何感知的流匹配框架。其从手-物体交互区域编码深度先验为关节关联表示，保留局部空间结构与度量尺度；与视觉特征融合后引导粗粒度度量估计，并作为时间生成每一步的条件。模型联合恢复世界空间平移、全局朝向、MANO关节化与形状，借助结构先验与时间上下文支持部分遮挡下的估计。

Result: 相对HaWoR，相机空间MPJPE降低49.3%与52.9%，绝对世界空间MPJPE降低28.7%与21.7%，腕部定位误差降低48.5%与37.9%；世界空间加速度误差降低44.2%与64.3%，在更准确定位的同时提升时间保真度。

Conclusion: 通过将深度几何先验与时间生成相融合的流匹配框架，可在度量世界空间实现更准确、时间更连贯的4D手部重建，为自我中心手部运动的物理监督提供可靠基础。

Abstract: Egocentric hand motion provides physical motion supervision for dexterous manipulation and vision-language-action pretraining. Reliable use of this supervision requires accurate and temporally coherent 4D hand reconstruction in metric world space. Existing methods typically lift framewise estimates produced under weak perspective into world coordinates. By ignoring joint-dependent depth variation at close range, this approximation couples errors in translation, orientation, and articulation that persist after world lifting and worsen under occlusion. We present MetricHand, a geometry-aware flow matching framework for 4D hand reconstruction in metric world space. MetricHand encodes depth priors from hand-object interaction regions into joint-associated representations that preserve local spatial structure and metric scale. Fused with visual features, this geometry guides coarse metric estimation and conditions every step of temporal generation. The model jointly recovers world-space translation, global orientation, MANO articulation, and shape, while structural priors and temporal context support estimation under partial occlusion. MetricHand reduces camera-space MPJPE by 49.3% and 52.9%, absolute world-space MPJPE by 28.7% and 21.7%, and wrist localization error by 48.5% and 37.9% relative to HaWoR, respectively. World-space acceleration error decreases by 44.2% and 64.3%, demonstrating improved temporal fidelity alongside more accurate absolute localization.

</details>


### [237] [What Makes a Good Move? Learning to Act from Experience in Visual Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21065)
*Cehao Yang,Ziyi He,Jing Yi,Jian Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightAct把交互片段凝练为经验条目并整合成游戏技能以指导探索，随后在每个决策根节点用短rollout比较候选动作、将分数增益归一化为相对动作奖励，用少量决策根数据训练视觉策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习视觉策略需要把交互经验转化为对单步决策的指导：轨迹层面的结果无法反映单个动作质量，而无指导的探索又易重复已熟悉的局面，因此需要既能扩大状态覆盖又能提供细粒度动作监督的框架。

Method: 将交互片段总结为经验条目并consolidate为游戏专属技能，引导根节点采集以提升状态覆盖；在每个根节点下，候选动作在共享冻结策略下做短rollout，把环境分数增益在多次延续上取平均并归一化为相对动作奖励，作为可复用的RL监督；策略训练仅使用1024个决策根（来自单一源游戏）及其动作奖励。

Result: 在覆盖34款游戏、170个GameWorld任务上，2B/4B/9B规模分别提升总体进度23.6、29.2、13.0个百分点，最强模型与已报道的专有模型结果相当；消融显示技能引导带来更广的根状态覆盖，且在三种策略优化器上均有一致增益；案例表明rollout能区分即时增益相同的动作。

Conclusion: 将技能引导探索与基于rollout的相对动作奖励相结合，可用极少决策根数据高效实现视觉策略内化，并具备跨规模与优化器的稳健性。

Abstract: Learning visual policies requires turning interaction experience into guidance for individual decisions. Trajectory outcomes obscure action quality, while unguided exploration can revisit familiar situations. We present \textsc{LightAct}, an experience-learning framework that connects skill-guided exploration with policy internalization. Interaction segments are summarized into experience entries and consolidated into a game-specific skill that guides root collection toward broader state coverage. At each root, candidate actions are compared through short rollouts under a shared frozen policy. Environment-reported score gains are averaged across continuations and normalized into relative action rewards, producing reusable supervision for reinforcement learning. Policy training uses a compact dataset of just 1,024 decision roots collected in a single source game and paired with their action rewards. Across 170 GameWorld tasks spanning 34 games, \textsc{LightAct} improves overall progress by 23.6, 29.2, and 13.0 percentage points at the 2B, 4B, and 9B scales, respectively, with our strongest model competitive with reported proprietary-model results. Ablations show broader root-state coverage with skill guidance and consistent gains across three policy optimizers. Case studies show how rollouts distinguish actions with identical immediate gains.

</details>


### [238] [MORE: Merge Once, Route Early for Training-Free Multi-Subject Composition from Large LoRA Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21077)
*Tian Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MORE 是一种面向 FLUX 的免训练两阶段 LoRA 组合流水线：先将整个 LoRA 池"一次合并"为单一固定秩适配器（SSR-Merge，含 ridge、秩与校准设计，并精确分解为共享部分与各主体部分），再在去噪前 30% 步数中"早期路由"（按布局区域门控、衰减软注意力偏置、循环交换引导），从而在 K=10/20 时分别以 94.2%/78.3% 保留双主体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共平台为同一 T2I 骨干提供大量个性化 LoRA，但在单张图像中组合两个 LoRA 极不稳定：FLUX 下同时加载 K=10/20 个主体 LoRA 时增量相互干扰，即使只加载目标两个 LoRA，双主体同时出现的比例也低于 4%，表现为主体消失、融合或拼贴。作者希望在不训练的前提下解决多 LoRA 组合的主体保持与布局分离问题。

Method: 两阶段免训练框架：(1) Merge once——用 SSR-Merge 的子空间回归路由器把整个 LoRA 池折叠为一个固定秩单一适配器，通过 ridge 正则、秩选择与校准保证可靠性，并将其精确分解为共享部分与逐主体部分；(2) Route early——在去噪前 30% 步内将各主体部分门控到其布局区域，使用衰减的软注意力偏置把每个区域与其主体 token 绑定，并采用循环交换引导（以主体区域置换后的预测作外推）抑制区域同质化。

Result: 在留出种子上，MORE 在 K=10 与 K=20 时双主体保留率达 94.2%/78.3%，显著优于 FreeFuse（50.8%/35.8%）与 LoRAShop（25.0%/24.2%）；ImageReward 达 1.32（最佳基线 0.07），且所有 K 个主体共存于同一适配器；当布局自动推断时，双主体保留率仍达 70.0%/55.0%。

Conclusion: 将多 LoRA 组合问题转化为"一次合并 + 早期路由"，可在单一适配器中容纳大规模 LoRA 池并保持稳定多主体生成；早期去噪阶段的区域路由与交换引导是克服增量干扰与布局坍塌的关键，方法免训练且对自动布局推断具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Public hubs offer many personalized LoRAs per text-to-image backbone, yet composing two of them in one image remains fragile. In FLUX, keeping a pool of $K=10$ or $20$ subject LoRAs loaded makes their deltas interfere, and even loading only the two requested LoRAs keeps both subjects in fewer than 4% of images: one subject vanishes, the two fuse, or the image splits into a collage. We present MORE (Merge Once, Route Early), a training-free two-stage pipeline for FLUX. Merge once: the whole pool is folded into a single fixed-rank adapter by the subspace-regression router of SSR-Merge, made reliable by ridge, rank and calibration choices, and factored exactly into a shared part and per-subject parts. Route early: during the first 30% of the denoising steps each subject's part is gated to its layout region; a decaying soft attention bias ties every region to its own subject tokens, and a cyclic swap guidance extrapolates away from the prediction with the subjects' regions permuted, which counteracts region homogenization. On held-out seeds, MORE keeps both subjects in 94.2% ($K=10$) and 78.3% ($K=20$) of images, against 50.8% / 35.8% for FreeFuse and 25.0% / 24.2% for LoRAShop, and reaches an ImageReward of 1.32 (best baseline 0.07), while all $K$ subjects stay in one adapter. With layouts inferred automatically it still keeps both subjects in 70.0% / 55.0% of images.

</details>


### [239] [State-Message Interaction Adapter for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21085)
*Hang Xing,Yan Xia,Yongjiang Liu,Siqi Zhang,Xueming Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出State-Message Interaction Adapter (SMIA)，在冻结DINOv3的每头注意力聚合后，以状态（pre-attention特征）为条件调制消息更新，仅0.51M可训练参数，实现跨域小样本分割六数据集均值mIoU 77.40%(5-shot)/80.99%(10-shot)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像预训练编码器迁移至医学/工业等跨域小样本分割时，标注支持掩码极少。冻结DINOv3的中间逐头注意力消息已隐含目标-背景区分性，但基线所用的末层相似度与预测并不稳定地反映这一点，故需在消息层面进行适配而非仅调整最终表示。

Method: SMIA插在逐头聚合之后：以每个patch的pre-attention特征为"状态"、每头聚合输出为"消息"，条件化地学习消息对接收token表示的更新，使相同上下文消息因接收位置不同而产生不同甚至相反的更新；预训练注意力与FFN参数全部冻结。针对支持掩码覆盖的状态-消息组合有限问题，采用共享低秩双线性映射，将学习到的交互约束在低维状态、消息与输出子空间；更新置于各适配块冻结FFN之前，由冻结FFN与更深层进一步精炼后线性读出。推理为单次前向、无需支持集。
