Result: 仅0.51M可训练参数，20个医学与工业任务、六数据集均值mIoU：5-shot 77.40%、10-shot 80.99%，较最强先前方法分别提升+3.61与+3.93个百分点。在同一DINOv3骨干的三基准10-shot受控比较中，优于参数量匹配的AdaptFormer、LoRA与ParaX，且精确率-召回率平衡更佳。

Conclusion: 逐头消息层面的条件化交互适配是跨域小样本分割的高效途径：极低参数量（0.51M）与免支持集推理即可显著超越既有PEFT类方法与强基线，说明利用冻结骨干内在的注意力区分性比末层特征微调更具价值。

Abstract: Cross-domain few-shot segmentation adapts natural-image pretrained encoders to medical or industrial domains from a few pixel-annotated support masks. Within frozen DINOv3, intermediate per-head attention messages already carry target-background distinctions that baseline final-layer similarities and predictions do not consistently reflect. We propose the State-Message Interaction Adapter (SMIA) to learn how per-head messages should update each receiving token representation while keeping pretrained attention and FFN parameters frozen. Inserted right after per-head aggregation, SMIA conditions the transformation of each head's aggregated output (message) on each patch's pre-attention feature (state), allowing similar contextual messages to produce distinct or opposing updates depending on the receiving position. Because scarce support masks cover only limited state-message combinations, a shared low-rank bilinear map confines the learned interactions to low-dimensional state, message, and output subspaces. Added before each adapted block's frozen FFN, these updates yield adapted features that are refined by frozen FFNs and deeper blocks for linear readout. With only 0.51M trainable parameters and single-pass, support-free inference, SMIA achieves six-dataset mean mIoUs of 77.40% at 5 shots and 80.99% at 10 shots across 20 medical and industrial tasks, surpassing the strongest prior methods by +3.61 and +3.93 percentage points. In controlled 10-shot comparisons across three benchmarks on the same DINOv3 backbone, SMIA also outperforms parameter-matched AdaptFormer, LoRA, and ParaX with a better precision-recall balance.

</details>


### [240] [MedicalDPO: Multi-Objective Direct Preference Optimization for CT to PET Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21098)
*Huiting Zhang,Yingxin Cai,Siyuan Wang,Xi Yang,Yuyao Yan,Rui Zhang,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MedicalDPO，将布朗桥扩散模型同一CT条件下多候选PET的"条件内质量层级"自动转化为偏好监督，用于CT-to-PET合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT-to-PET合成可用于辅助分析、表征预训练与缺失模态补全。布朗桥扩散模型在相同CT条件下可生成质量不同的多个PET候选，形成条件内质量层级，且候选质量在空间、结构、强度三维度上互补；但仅用DPO微调可能损害绝对合成保真度。

Method: 1) 由配对CT-PET训练数据自动构建偏好对：依三个保真度维度对多候选排序，并用严格的跨指标一致性验证，仅保留质量差异一致的preferred-rejected对；2) 采用相对去噪误差的偏好优化（relative denoising-error preference optimization），与原始布朗桥目标联合训练，以锚定预训练的CT-to-PET映射；3) 设计条件均衡的多对轮换策略，使偏好监督在各CT条件间均衡分布。

Result: 在BBDM与CPDM上，LPIPS、MAE、SSIM、PSNR四项指标均取得提升；分析表明各指标间具有互补性且偏好对分离清晰；消融实验显示原始训练目标可避免DPO-only微调带来的显著性能退化。

Conclusion: MedicalDPO通过自动构建的跨指标一致性偏好监督并与原扩散目标联合优化，在不牺牲绝对保真度的前提下提升了CT-to-PET合成质量，且具备良好的模型通用性。

Abstract: CT-to-PET synthesis estimates functional PET images from anatomical CT scans for auxiliary analysis, representation pretraining, and missing-modality completion. Brownian Bridge diffusion models can produce multiple PET candidates of different quality under the same CT condition, exposing an intra-condition quality hierarchy. Candidate quality spans complementary spatial, structural, and intensity dimensions, while DPO-only fine-tuning may compromise absolute synthesis fidelity. We propose MedicalDPO which converts this hierarchy into preference supervision automatically constructed from paired CT-PET training data. Multiple candidates are ranked across the three fidelity dimensions, and strict cross-metric verification retains preferred-rejected pairs with consistent quality separation. Relative denoising-error preference optimization is jointly trained with the original Brownian Bridge objective to anchor the pretrained CT-to-PET mapping, while condition-balanced multi-pair rotation distributes preference supervision across CT conditions. Experiments on BBDM and CPDM show improvements across LPIPS, MAE, SSIM, and PSNR. Further analyses demonstrate metric complementarity and clear pair separation, while ablations show that the original training objective prevents the substantial degradation caused by DPO-only fine-tuning.

</details>


### [241] [Beyond Family Labels: A Taxonomic Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Prior for Avian 3D Shape Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21102)
*Yunchan Jeon,SooGon Kim,Seungwook Kim,CheolWon Lee,Jongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出将分类学Ornstein-Uhlenbeck先验施加于AVES参数化鸟类网格模型的形状码上，以连续、生成式的方式利用物种树结构，改善从单张图像恢复鸟类3D姿态与形状时对未见物种的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鸟类约1万物种，仅几十种有3D标注训练数据，测试集常含训练未见物种，跨支系泛化是核心难题。现有SOTA AniMer+仅用家族级对比损失，每对样本只传递二值"同科/不同科"指示，丢弃了物种树的连续结构。

Method: 在AVES参数化鸟类网格模型的形状码上引入分类学Ornstein-Uhlenbeck先验：一个多元高斯分布，其协方差随林奈阶元距离指数衰减，选择强度与噪声尺度均可学习。该先验将家族对比损失推广为连续生成式形式，显式刻画跨科与科内的梯度相似性，把连续比较系统发育学作为生物学先验注入长尾场景。

Result: 在未见物种基准CowBird的两项指标上以及CtrlAVES3D上均优于AniMer+，在已见物种的CUB上保持可比。置换消融将分类学结构从通用高斯平滑中分离出来；阶元粒度消融表明，跨目层面的区分（即超越"同科与否"二值指示的结构）是未见物种性能提升的主要来源。

Conclusion: 将物种树的分层连续结构与OU过程结合作为形状码先验，能有效提升鸟类3D重建的跨物种泛化，且增益可归因于细粒度分类学结构而非单纯正则化。

Abstract: Recovering 3D pose and shape of birds from single images is bottlenecked by the long tail of avian diversity: out of ~10,000 species, only a few dozen have 3D-labeled training data, and in-the-wild test sets routinely contain species never seen at training. Generalizing across clades is therefore a central problem. The current state of the art, AniMer+, mitigates it with a family-level contrastive loss that conveys only a binary same-family indicator per pair and discards the continuous structure of the species tree. We propose a taxonomic Ornstein-Uhlenbeck prior on the shape code of the AVES parametric bird mesh model: a multivariate Gaussian whose covariance decays exponentially with Linnaean rank distance, with a learnable selection strength and noise scale. The prior generalizes family contrastive into a continuous, generative form, exposing the graded cross- and within-family similarity that a binary indicator cannot, and brings continuous comparative phylogenetics in as a biology-grounded inductive bias for the long tail. Our method improves over AniMer+ on both metrics of the unseen-species CowBird benchmark and on CtrlAVES3D, while remaining comparable on seen-species CUB. A permutation ablation isolates taxonomic structure from generic Gaussian smoothing, and a rank-granularity ablation identifies cross-order distinctions (the structure beyond a family-vs-not indicator) as the dominant source of the unseen-species gain.

</details>


### [242] [GRAZE: Text-Free Grasp Synthesis for Unseen Objects from Human Gaze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21104)
*Jeongho Lee,Boeun Kim,Hyung Jin Chang,Jungchan Cho,Sang-Il Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRAZE 用“注视（gaze）”替代物体/部件名称作为条件，生成手-物抓取动作，在未见物体上显著优于文本条件基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件虽提供语义控制，但难以高效指定“交互发生的位置”：命名抓取部件需要物体专有的词，命名接近方向又缺少提示词中可用的参考系。

Method: 提出 GRAZE：Geometry-Aware Gaze Encoding（GeoGaze）将注视射线映射为物体几何上的线索，再用交叉注意力以这些线索条件化扩散 Transformer；并构建 HOT3D-Grasp 抓取基准（基于 HOT3D，含物体不相交划分）。

Result: 未见物体上抓取目标部件成功率 49.3% vs 最强文本基线 43.6%，抓取成功率 66.3% vs 46.6%；零微调迁移到 GazeHOI 达 45.3% vs 33.8%。注视绕物体旋转时接近方向随之改变 59.1%（重新训练时钟词文本模型 20.5%）。同骨干下文本更擅长命名部件，注视补充接近方向；两者同时给定时 GRAZE 保持提示词的部件准确率并遵循注视。

Conclusion: 注视是比语言更直接的空间条件信号，能有效传达接近方向；文本与注视互补，联合条件化可兼顾部件选择与方向控制。

Abstract: Text-conditioned hand-object interaction (HOI) generation provides intuitive semantic control, but language is an indirect way to specify where an interaction should occur on an object. Naming the part to grasp requires object-specific words, and naming the approach side requires a reference frame that a prompt lacks. We propose GRAZE (GRAsp from gaZE), which generates hand-object grasp motion from recorded gaze instead of object and part names. Its Geometry-Aware Gaze Encoding (GeoGaze) maps gaze rays to cues on object geometry, and cross-attention conditions a diffusion Transformer on these cues. We also introduce HOT3D-Grasp, a grasp-centered benchmark with object-disjoint folds built on HOT3D recordings. On unseen objects, GRAZE grasps the target part more often than the strongest text-conditioned baseline (49.3% vs. 43.6%), achieves the highest grasp success (66.3% vs. 46.6%), and transfers without fine-tuning to GazeHOI (45.3% vs. 33.8%). Gaze conveys the approach side: when gaze is rotated around the object, the approach turns with it in 59.1% of cases, against 20.5% for a text model retrained with clock words. On the same backbone, a text prompt names parts better, but gaze adds the approach side. Given both, GRAZE keeps the prompt's part accuracy and follows gaze.

</details>


### [243] [Source-Free Domain Adaptation for Multi-Label Image Classification: A Framework, Baselines, and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21107)
*Salih Numan Büyükbaş,Erchan Aptoula,Mamatha Thota,Petra Bosilj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出多标签源无域自适应（SFDA）问题并建立基准，发现将单标签方法直接扩展不可靠，进而提出MPACT方法，在全部五项任务上均优于未适配的源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFDA文献几乎都假设类别互斥，但许多实际部署场景为多标签分类，缺少相应基准与方法；同时单标签方法所依赖的最近质心伪标签、softmax置信度、信息最大化多样性项和置信度加权原型等均隐含互斥假设。

Method: 构建首个多标签SFDA基准：6个数据集（遥感、自然、clipart风格、驾驶图像）上的5项任务，将4种单标签SFDA方法扩展为基线，并与1种已发表多标签方法在3个随机种子下对比；提出MPACT（多标签原型对齐+对比教学），在均值教师框架下重新设计目标函数、原型构建与对齐项以适配重叠标签集；并引入原型一致性诊断，区分特征空间对齐与分类器自强化一致。

Result: 扩展的单标签基线中有一半未能超过未适配源模型，且在5项任务中的2项上无一基线取得提升；仅将softmax换成逐类sigmoid是有效但不充分的扩展。MPACT在所有报告指标上取得最佳平均值，是唯一在全部五项任务上均优于源模型的方法；置信度伪标签过滤得到负结果。

Conclusion: 多标签SFDA不能简单由单标签方法迁移而来，需针对重叠标签集重构目标、原型与对齐机制；MPACT验证了该思路的有效性，并为后续研究提供了基准与诊断工具。

Abstract: Source-free domain adaptation (SFDA) adapts a model trained on a labelled source domain to an unlabelled target domain without access to the source data. Almost the entire SFDA literature assumes mutually exclusive classes, yet many deployed settings are multi-label. This paper formulates SFDA for multi-label classification and establishes the first benchmark for it: five adaptation tasks over six datasets spanning remote sensing, natural, clipart-style and driving imagery, with four single-label SFDA methods extended to provide the first baselines and a published multi-label method run alongside them, over three seeds. The benchmark shows that extending a single-label method is unreliable rather than merely suboptimal: half of the extended baselines fail to improve on the unadapted source model at all, and on two of the five tasks none of them does. The failures are not incidental. Nearest-centroid pseudo-labelling, softmax-based confidence, the diversity term of information maximization and confidence-weighted prototype construction each rely on mutual exclusivity, and each fails differently without it, so replacing the softmax with per-class sigmoids is a valid but insufficient extension. Multi-label Prototype Alignment with Contrastive Teaching (MPACT) is proposed in response, a mean-teacher framework whose objective, prototype construction and alignment term are each reconsidered for overlapping label sets. It attains the best average on every reported metric and is the only method that improves on the unadapted source model on all five tasks. The analysis further introduces a prototype-agreement diagnostic separating feature-space alignment from self-reinforcing agreement with the classifier, and reports a negative result for confidence-based pseudo-label filtering.

</details>


### [244] [VAE Rhythm: Temporal Decoding Traces for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21114)
*Ke Sun,Shuwei Li,Haiyang Mei,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出生成视频具有"VAE Rhythm"周期性痕迹，可用无需训练的相位扫描检测，并以此构建检测器CaDet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见伪影会随生成器迭代被消除，因此不可靠；需要寻找生成流水线自身留下的、难以去除的内在痕迹。

Method: 利用3D因果VAE按时间分组解码导致的帧保真度周期性波动（VAE Rhythm），设计中无需训练的相位扫描：用现成VAE以不同起始偏移重建片段并度量重建误差的周期性；将该节奏用作零样本分数、现有检测器插件，以及配对真实视频与VAE重建的数据引擎来训练CaDet。

Result: 在31个生成器的39个变体（含Sora、Kling 3.0、Seedance 2.0）中发现节奏痕迹；DF26上零样本节奏分数优于全部15个重训练基线，作为插件使19个检测器AUROC提升6.7%–23.7%；CaDet在AIGVDBench、DF26及三个2026闭源模型上取得最佳AUROC，无生成视频训练的变体在AIGVDBench仍超90%。

Conclusion: VAE Rhythm是一种普遍、鲁棒的生成视频指纹，相位扫描提供免训练检测能力，CaDet进一步将其转化为强泛化的检测框架。

Abstract: Visible artifacts are an unreliable cue for detecting generated videos, because newer generators learn to remove them. We instead detect a trace left by the generation pipeline itself. Most video generators decode latents with a 3D causal VAE that groups consecutive frames in time, so the fidelity of generated frames rises and falls periodically with their position in a group. We call this periodic trace VAE Rhythm. Real videos are captured frame by frame and do not have it. To reveal it without training, we propose a phase scan. The scan reconstructs a clip with an off-the-shelf VAE from successive start offsets and measures the periodicity of the reconstruction error across these offsets. We find VAE Rhythm in most of the 39 variants of 31 generators we test, including the closed-source Sora, Kling 3.0 and Seedance 2.0. We use the rhythm as a zero-shot score, as a plug-in for existing detectors and as a data engine that pairs real videos with their VAE reconstructions to train our detector CaDet. On DF26, the training-free rhythm score outperforms all 15 retrained baselines, and as a plug-in it improves all 19 tested detectors by 6.7% to 23.7% AUROC. CaDet achieves the best AUROC on AIGVDBench, DF26 and three 2026 closed-source models, and its variant trained without any generated video still exceeds 90% on AIGVDBench.

</details>


### [245] [IGRecon4D: Dynamic-Object-Aware Feed-Forward Reconstruction of Driving Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21126)
*Yifei Tong,Ziyang Huang,Yizhao Han,Xiaoxiao Long,Hao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出前馈4D驾驶场景重建框架IGRecon4D，通过训练期实例先验蒸馏与LiDAR锚定高斯定位，解决动态参与者语义泄漏到静态表示与跨视角高斯几何漂移两类失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈4D驾驶场景重建存在两类互补失效：一是交通参与者语义泄漏进静态表示（动态-静态泄漏），二是高斯位置在不同视角间发生几何漂移，导致重建质量受限。

Method: (1) 实例级语义分支提供潜在动态参与者先验，经Instance-Dynamic Consistency Constraint蒸馏到动态掩码学习中，实现易泄漏区域的高召回定位；(2) 稀疏LiDAR测量引导稠密深度估计，反投影深度以锚定高斯位置，提升多视角几何一致性。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset上主基准全图重建性能提升；在nuScenes零样本跨数据集评测中，交通参与者区域重建效果更强。

Conclusion: 实例先验蒸馏与LiDAR锚定定位可协同缓解语义泄漏与几何漂移，提升前馈4D驾驶场景重建的精度与跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 4D driving-scene reconstruction enables efficient modeling of dynamic autonomous-driving scenes, but current methods suffer from two complementary failure modes: semantic leakage of traffic participants into the static representation and geometric drift of Gaussian locations across viewpoints. We propose \textbf{IGRecon4D}, a feed-forward dynamic scene reconstruction framework that addresses these failures with training-time instance-prior distillation and LiDAR-anchored Gaussian positioning. Specifically, an instance-level semantic branch provides a potentially dynamic participant prior, which is distilled into dynamic-mask learning through the Instance-Dynamic Consistency Constraint to encourage high-recall localization of traffic-participant regions prone to dynamic-static leakage. In parallel, sparse LiDAR measurements guide dense depth estimation, and the resulting depths are back-projected to anchor Gaussian positions for improved multi-view geometric consistency. Experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and zero-shot evaluation on nuScenes demonstrate improved full-image reconstruction on the main benchmark and stronger traffic-participant-region reconstruction under cross-dataset evaluation.

</details>


### [246] [How to Explain your Unexplainable Video Anomaly Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21131)
*Giacomom D'Amicantonio,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ETU框架：在图结构（agent-action-outcome）特征空间做反事实干预，审计冻结的弱监督视频异常检测模型，验证检测器依赖"场景语义"与"outcome链"而非随机扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督VAD模型从高维特征给出异常分数，但其预测缺乏解释。显著图只显示"看哪里"而非"依赖什么"，VLM生成的描述由异常分数从未读取的分支产生，存在faithfulness gap。

Method: ETU为分类器无关框架：将视频特征映射为离散依赖图 A_r→A_a→A_o（agent/action/outcome），用图条件扩散模型在掩码选定节点后重新生成特征，测量冻结检测器分数的变化；同一套干预不加改动地施加于所有检测器。生成器仅以重建输入特征为目标训练，故无法学到被测量的对比。实验逐一排除替代解释（重建代价、同规模随机删除）。

Result: 在CLIP编码的UCF-Crime特征上：替换场景语义对多数检测器造成最大分数下降；移除outcome链（Δ_rel）对全部六个被测检测器的异常分数改变远大于移除等量随机节点，尽管随机删除对特征的扰动程度相当。该定向效应源自图嵌入的divergence目标：去掉它，两种干预不可区分；若生成器也加divergence目标，分数变化则跟随重建位移而非被移除的语义。

Conclusion: ETU提供了忠实（faithful）、分类器无关的VAD审计手段，表明检测器依赖图语义结构而非重建伪影，并强调divergence目标在图嵌入中的关键作用。

Abstract: Weakly-supervised Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) models assign anomaly scores from high-dimensional features, and their predictions remain unexplained. Saliency maps show where a model looks but not what it relies on, and the descriptions produced by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are generated by branches that the anomaly score never reads. To address this faithfulness gap, we propose \textbf{Explain The Unexplainable (ETU)}, a classifier-agnostic framework that audits frozen VAD models through counterfactual interventions in their feature space. ETU maps video features to a discrete dependency graph of agents, actions and outcomes ($A_r \to A_a \to A_o$), regenerates the features by graph-conditioned diffusion with selected nodes of the graph masked, and measures the change in the score of a frozen detector; one set of interventions is applied unchanged to every detector. The generator is trained only to reconstruct the input features, so it cannot learn the contrast it is asked to measure, and we validate the audit with experiments that test every alternative explanation of its results, from the cost of regeneration to random deletions of matched size. On UCF-Crime features obtained from a CLIP encoder, we show that substituting the scene semantics results in the largest drop for most detectors, and that removing the outcome chain of the graph ($Δ_{rel}$) changes the anomaly score of all six audited detectors far more than removing the same number of random nodes, even though the random deletion perturbs the features about as much. We show that this targeted behavior comes from the divergence objective of the graph embedding. Without it, the two interventions become indistinguishable, and with a divergence objective in the generator as well, the score change of the detector follows the displacement of the reconstruction rather than the semantics that were removed.

</details>


### [247] [Region-VAD: Video Anomaly Localization and Detection via Region Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21132)
*Giacomo D'Amicantonio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Region-VAD 先选区域再检测异常：用冻结基础模型 token 上的显式矩形做注意力池化，仅训练池化与时间头，在 UCF-Crime 与 MSAD 上取得最优异常定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督视频异常检测把整帧池化为单一向量再排序，异常与其背景混杂，导致检测器可仅凭场景上下文获得高基准分数，却无法说明异常发生的具体位置（定位依赖事后显著性图）。

Method: 在冻结基础模型 token 网格上显式选取矩形：该矩形由相邻片段在编码器四个特征投影中变化最大的 token 区域确定，通过网格上矩形穷举搜索得到，不参与学习；仅对矩形内 token 做注意力池化，再接标准时间头，且训练只用视频级标签（无需框、掩码或帧级标注），所有可训练组件均不读取区域外 token。

Result: 在五个数据集上，单一定位器配置跨数据集与骨干网络共享；UCF-Crime 与 MSAD 上达到最优异常定位性能，检测性能具竞争力。

Conclusion: 以“先定位区域、再由区域判定异常”替代全帧池化，可在弱监督设定下同时获得强定位能力与有竞争力的检测性能，并验证定位与检测可解耦。

Abstract: Anomalies are local events, yet weakly supervised video anomaly detection scores them globally. Most methods pool an entire frame into a single vector, rank frames by that vector, and derive a location afterwards as a saliency map of the score. Full-frame pooling mixes the anomaly with its background, so a detector can reach competitive benchmark scores from scene context without being able to show where in the scene the anomaly occurs. We introduce Region-VAD, which first selects a region and then detects the anomaly from that region alone. Region-VAD selects an explicit rectangle over the tokens of a frozen foundation model and scores anomalousness only from the tokens inside the box, through attention pooling followed by a standard temporal head. The rectangle is not learned, it is the box in which the frozen tokens change most between consecutive segments, in all four feature projections of the encoder, and it is found by exhaustive search over the rectangles of the token grid. Only the pooling and the temporal head are trained, from video-level labels without boxes, masks or frame-level annotations, and no trained component reads a token outside the selected region. Experiments on five datasets, with one localizer configuration shared by all datasets and backbones, show that Region-VAD achieves state-of-the-art anomaly localization on UCF-Crime and MSAD at competitive detection performance.

</details>


### [248] [Model-based neural network for global floodplains analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21146)
*Pierre Audisio,Barbara Belletti,Nelly Pustelnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出用于多光谱遥感超分辨的配对数据集DLhasa-L2S（Landsat→Sentinel-2），并比较前向-后向即插即用与展开式FB网络同SwinIR、RDN；展开网络在真实RGB分量上PSNR/SSIM最优且推理快，NDWI水体分割无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期洪泛平原监测需要高时空分辨率多光谱影像，Landsat与Sentinel-2分辨率不匹配，且缺乏面向河流分析的多光谱超分辨配对数据集与兼顾精度与效率的方法。

Method: 构建DLhasa-L2S配对数据集（拉萨河，利用两任务同期观测）；提出两种基于模型的神经网络：前向-后向(FB) plug-and-play方案与展开(unfolded) FB方案；与SwinIR、RDN等学习型基线在合成数据、真实场景及全量真实配对数据统计检验上对比。

Result: 展开式网络在真实RGB分量上取得最高PSNR与SSIM，推理速度快，精度-计算成本权衡最佳；在基于NDWI的水体分割上与最佳方法无统计显著差异。

Conclusion: DLhasa-L2S为河流多光谱超分辨提供首个配对基准，展开式FB网络是高效且精度具竞争力的方案。

Abstract: We address the super-resolution of multispectral remote sensing imagery for long-term floodplain monitoring, enhancing Landsat acquisitions towards Sentinel-2 resolution. Exploiting the period during which both missions observed the same areas, we build DLhasa-L2S , to our knowledge the first paired dataset dedicated to multispectral super-resolution for river analysis. It covers the Lhasa River (Tibet), whose strong anthropogenic changes and diverse hydromorphological profiles make it an ideal study area. We developed two model-based neural networks: a forward-backward (FB) plug-and-play scheme and an unfolded FB scheme. We compared them against learned neural networks baselines (SwinIR, RDN) on synthetic data, on real scenes, and through a statistical test over the whole real paired Landsat-Sentinel dataset. The unfolded network achieves the highest PSNR and SSIM on the real RGB components with fast inference, offering the best trade-off between accuracy and computational cost. For NDWI-based water segmentation, its performance shows no statistically significant difference from that of the best methods.

</details>


### [249] [DRaGAN: Dynamic Graph Attention Network for Multi-Radar Point Cloud Encoding in Automotive Radar Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21162)
*Christof Leitgeb,Thomas Puchleitner,Max Peter Ronecker,Daniel Watzenig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DRaGAN：面向多雷达点云的动态图注意力网络，结合传感器相关Fourier编码、门控远近程枢纽节点与注意力引导动态重连，提升雷达点云编码效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达对雾、雨、雪具有穿透性且分辨率不断提升，是鲁棒感知的关键模态；但原始雷达数据处理开销大，现有工作多依赖类似LiDAR的稀疏点云，且LiDAR导向的编码器难以适配雷达点的稀疏性与传感器相关各向异性。

Method: 提出DRaGAN动态图注意力网络，包含：1）角度与距离的传感器相关Fourier编码以刻画固定孔径雷达特性；2）沿各传感器视线的门控近程/远程枢纽节点实现高效局部-全局消息传递；3）注意力引导动态重连，剪枝弱边并仅在两跳邻域内添加有界数量的潜在连接，从而减少深层处理的边数。

Result: 在TruckScenes上，相较所评估的RadarGNN基线，测试NDS提升10.10点，FLOPs降低63.7%；代价为延迟从40 ms升至53.16 ms，可训练参数从1.2M增至1.7M。

Conclusion: DRaGAN通过传感器相关编码与动态图重连，在显著提升雷达点云检测性能的同时大幅削减计算量，但牺牲了一定推理时延与参数量；代码与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Radar represents a key modality for weather-robust perception. Unlike optical sensors such as camera and LiDAR, radar can penetrate fog, rain and snow due to its longer wavelength, and recent hardware advances have steadily improved its spatial resolution, thus making it increasingly relevant for autonomous systems. While processing raw radar data requires substantial memory computation, most existing work has focused on preprocessed point-cloud data similar to LiDAR. However, the sparse radar points and sensor-relative anisotropy make LiDAR-oriented encoders suboptimal. To address this problem, we present DRaGAN, a dynamic graph attention network for multi-radar point clouds. DRaGAN introduces a sensor-relative Fourier encoding of angle and range to capture fixed-aperture radar characteristics, gated short- and long-range hub nodes along each sensor's line of sight for efficient local-global message passing, and attention-guided dynamic rewiring that prunes weak edges and adds a bounded number of latent connections restricted to two-hop neighborhoods. This reduces the number of processed edges in deeper layers while preserving representation quality. On TruckScenes, DRaGAN improves test NDS by 10.10 points over the evaluated RadarGNN baseline and reduces the reported model FLOP count by 63.7%. This trade-off includes an increase in model latency from 40 to 53.16 ms and in trainable parameters from 1.2M to 1.7M on the evaluated implementations. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/chr-is-tof/DRaGAN.

</details>


### [250] [Beyond Token Stacking: Multi-scale Visual Modulation and Adaptive Fused-feature Calibration for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21168)
*Ye Xiao,Shesheng Song,Yi Ren,Shasha Li,Bin Ji,Xiaodong Liu,Jun Ma,Jie Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BTS框架：用多尺度视觉调制(MVM)在不增加token长度的前提下于输入阶段注入多尺度视觉信息，并用自适应融合特征校准(AFC)在FFN中逐通道仿射校准以解耦视觉注入与分布对齐，缓解冗余token与激活分布漂移问题，取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM两条范式各有缺陷：拼接视觉与文本token会引入大量冗余视觉token、计算开销高；将视觉知识注入FFN虽缩短序列长度，却使图像脱离输入阶段，可能妨碍图像相关知识激活；且朴素注入视觉知识会因跨模态语义差异导致激活分布漂移。

Method: 提出Beyond Token Stacking (BTS)视觉增强框架，包含两个组件：1) Multi-scale Visual Modulation (MVM)，在输入阶段通过调制注入多尺度视觉信息，不增加token序列长度；2) Adaptive Fused-feature Calibration (AFC)，在FFN层执行逐通道仿射校准，将视觉注入与分布对齐解耦，在保留语言先验的同时提升多模态融合稳定性。

Result: 实验表明该方法达到state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: BTS通过输入阶段的多尺度视觉调制与FFN内的自适应特征校准，兼顾计算效率、图像知识激活与分布稳定性，为MLLM视觉增强提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have advanced rapidly and follow two main paradigms: one involves concatenating visual and textual tokens for input into Large Language Model (LLM), while the other injects visual knowledge into Feed-Forward Networks (FFNs) of the LLM. However, the former introduces a large number of redundant visual tokens, substantially increasing computational overhead. The latter reduces sequence length but removes images from the input stage entirely, which may hinder the activation of image-related knowledge. Moreover, naively injecting visual knowledge into the FFNs could induce activation distribution shifts due to cross-modal semantic discrepancies. To address these challenges, we develop Beyond Token Stacking (BTS), a novel visual enhancement framework for MLLMs. Specifically, we propose a Multi-scale Visual Modulation (MVM) mechanism that injects multi-scale visual information at the input stage via modulation without increasing the token sequence length. Furthermore, we develop an Adaptive Fused-feature Calibration (AFC) module that performs per-channel affine calibration to decouple visual injection from distribution alignment in the FFN layers, improving multimodal fusion stability while preserving language priors. Experiments show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [251] [RAGE: Residual-Adaptive Gating for Flow-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21177)
*Ruofeng Mei,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAGE将无需求逆的流式图像编辑中的源保真控制建模为随机最优控制问题，导出"残差门控原理"：按残差幅度指数衰减的增益将隐变量拉回源，抑制微小漂移而放行语义编辑，单步仅需一次轻量门控插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于微分速度场的 inversion-free 编辑方法未显式约束哪些区域应被保留，导致非编辑区域出现非预期漂移。

Method: 将源保真控制形式化为沿编辑轨迹对编辑残差施加选择性保真项的随机最优控制问题；求解并近似后得到 Residual Gating Principle——每个隐分量的回拉增益随局部残差幅度指数衰减。RAGE 实现为每个采样步一次的轻量门控插值，无需掩码估计、注意力图、重训练或架构改动，可叠加于 FlowEdit 类编辑器。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 上，RAGE 在 17 种方法中取得最佳综合排名；在 Stable Diffusion 3 与 FLUX 骨干上显著提升背景保持能力，同时保持有竞争力的编辑质量。

Conclusion: 显式的选择性保真控制可有效缓解无需求逆流式编辑的背景漂移，且以极低实现成本获得优异综合性能。

Abstract: Recent inversion-free, flow-based image editing methods evolve the source image toward the target via a differential velocity field. We observe that this velocity field does not explicitly account for which regions should be preserved, leading to unintended drift in non-editing regions. To address this, we formulate source fidelity control as a stochastic optimal control problem in which a selective fidelity term on the editing residual is enforced along the editing trajectory. Solving this problem and approximating its solution yields the Residual Gating Principle: each latent component is pulled back toward the source with a gain that decays exponentially with the local residual magnitude, so that small deviations are suppressed while large, semantically meaningful edits pass through unimpeded. The proposed method, RAGE, requires only one lightweight gated interpolation per sampling step, involves no mask estimation, attention maps, retraining, or architecture-specific modifications, and is applied on top of FlowEdit-based editors. On PIE-Bench, RAGE achieves the best overall rank among 17 methods and substantially improves background preservation on both Stable Diffusion 3 and FLUX backbones while maintaining competitive editing quality.

</details>


### [252] [A Study on Performance Enhancement of a Webcam-Based Real-Time Eye-Tracking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21233)
*Minuk Jung,Kyeonghun Kim,Sooyeon Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于普通RGB摄像头的实时视线追踪系统，结合ETH-XGaze视线估计、九点校准与多项式岭回归，并用RTMPose提取的头姿特征做条件性残差校正，单被试实验中屏幕坐标平均误差204.24像素，显著低于两个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于普通网络摄像头的视线追踪成本低、易部署，但受头部位置变化影响较大，导致屏幕坐标估计误差偏高，需要在用户特定校准基础上进行姿态相关误差补偿。

Method: 系统流水线为：ETH-XGaze模型进行基础视线估计；九点校准配合多项式岭回归实现用户特定映射；再用RTMPose提取头部与姿态特征，生成条件性残差校正项，是否启用取决于检测特征可靠性与校准数据特性。

Result: 单被试12个屏幕目标实验中，平均屏幕坐标误差204.24像素，比xgaze-js低35.83%，比gaze_tracker低57.69%；12个目标均获得有效估计。

Conclusion: 初步结果验证了"用户特定校准+条件性姿态残差校正"用于摄像头视线追踪的可行性，但仅一名被试，需扩展至更多被试与更广泛姿态范围以验证泛化性。

Abstract: This study proposes a real-time gaze-tracking system that uses a standard RGB webcam to estimate screen coordinates. The system combines ETH-XGaze-based gaze estimation with nine-point calibration and polynomial ridge regression. To reduce errors caused by changes in head position, it also applies a conditional residual correction derived from head and pose features extracted with RTMPose. Whether this correction is used depends on the reliability of the detected features and the characteristics of the calibration data. In a preliminary experiment, one participant completed trials at 12 screen targets. The resulting mean screen-coordinate error was 204.24 pixels, which was 35.83% lower than that of xgaze-js and 57.69% lower than that of gaze_tracker. The system produced valid gaze estimates for all 12 targets. These preliminary findings demonstrate the feasibility of combining user-specific calibration with conditional pose-based correction for webcam gaze tracking. Because the evaluation involved only one participant, further experiments with more participants and a wider range of postures are required.

</details>


### [253] [QuantileGS: Quantile-Based Conformal Bounds on RGB Rendering Errors for Novel View Synthesis with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21253)
*Haiyang Xu,Ziye Zhang,Bin Pan,Xia Xu,Zhenwei Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出后处理框架 QuantileGS，将学习式渲染误差分位数与尺度归一化保形校准结合，为 3D Gaussian Splatting 的新视角合成提供具有有限样本边际覆盖保证的逐像素 RGB 误差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 新视角合成的可靠性取决于渲染颜色与真实颜色的差异，但真实图像不可得，无法直接度量；现有学习式误差上界可能低估误差、高估可靠性，因此需要校准到规定覆盖水平。

Method: 三阶段后处理框架：(1) 渲染误差计算，在冻结的 3DGS 模型上度量观测图像与预测之间的 RGB 平均绝对误差；(2) 有序高斯分位场，为每个高斯学习两个标量属性以预测误差下/上分位数；(3) 尺度归一化保形校准，在留出图像上校准上界预测，并利用分位间隙缩放修正量。

Result: 名义覆盖率 90% 下，LLFF 平均经验测试覆盖率由 69.6% 提升至 89.6%，Mip-NeRF 360 由 80.1% 提升至 92.1%；LF 上校准前后分别为 96.3% 与 96.4%，保持保守。

Conclusion: 在给定假设下所得上界具备有限样本边际覆盖保证，且空间自适应；该上界可用于主动视角选择、高斯剪枝与 RGB-D SLAM 等下游决策。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification for novel view synthesis with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) assesses rendering reliability at unseen viewpoints. This reliability depends on the discrepancy between rendered and true colors, which cannot be measured without ground-truth images. We therefore quantify uncertainty in this unknown discrepancy through upper bounds on per-pixel RGB mean absolute error. However, learned bounds may underestimate these errors and overstate rendering reliability, motivating calibration to a prescribed coverage level. In this paper, we propose QuantileGS, a post-hoc framework that combines learned rendering-error quantiles with scale-normalized conformal calibration to produce spatially adaptive error bounds. The framework comprises three phases. Phase 1, Rendering-Error Computation, measures RGB mean absolute error between observed images and predictions from a frozen 3DGS model. Phase 2, Ordered Gaussian Quantile Fields, learns two scalar attributes per Gaussian to predict lower and upper rendering-error quantiles. Phase 3, Scale-Normalized Conformal Calibration, calibrates the upper prediction on held-out images, using the quantile gap to scale the correction. Under the stated assumptions, the resulting bounds provide finite-sample marginal coverage. At a nominal coverage level of 90%, calibration raises mean empirical test coverage from 69.6% to 89.6% on LLFF and from 80.1% to 92.1% on Mip-NeRF 360. On LF, coverage remains conservative at 96.3% before and 96.4% after calibration. Applications to active view selection, Gaussian pruning, and RGB-D simultaneous localization and mapping illustrate how these bounds inform downstream decisions.

</details>


### [254] [PLECTA: Overlap-aware instance segmentation of dense strand networks from binary masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21283)
*Oday Allan,Igor Ostanin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLECTA是一种确定性的纤维分割方法，仅用二值骨架图像即可在交叉处完成片段配准，并辅以灰度图估计交叠上下关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳纳米管薄膜、胶原、纸张等材料由相互交叠的纤维构成，其几何排布决定有效性质；从显微图像中测量几何需在交叉与信号断裂处追踪单根纤维，而现有做法易将不同纤维误并或把一根纤维拆成碎片。

Method: 将每个交叉点处的片段延续判定建模为精确最大权部分匹配问题，对未配对片段施加显式惩罚，且不限制进入交叉点的片段数量；输出允许交叉像素归属多条纤维；另设独立阶段利用对应灰度显微图估计每个交叉处的上下层关系。

Result: 在合成场景的每个测试密度下，PLECTA的纤维身份解析精度均优于三个已公开的对比方法；在独立发布的生成器以其自身参数运行时，排名保持一致；并在碳纳米管薄膜SEM图像上完成从原始图像到带交叉顺序的单纤维实例的全流程演示。

Conclusion: PLECTA以确定性、无训练、无人工标注输入的方式实现稳健的纤维追踪与交叠顺序推断，可直接用于材料显微结构的定量几何表征。

Abstract: Many materials and tissues, including carbon-nanotube sheets, collagen and paper, comprise overlapping strands whose arrangement influences their effective properties. Measuring this geometry from micrographs requires individual strands to be traced through crossings and signal gaps, which merge distinct strands or split one strand into disconnected fragments. We present PLECTA (from Latin plecta, a braid), a deterministic method that segments individual strands using only a binary image whose foreground pixels mark strand centrelines. At each junction, PLECTA selects which strand segments continue into one another while allowing segments to terminate. It formulates this decision as an exact maximum-weight partial-matching problem, with an explicit penalty for unpaired segments and no restriction on the number of segments entering a junction. The output permits crossing pixels to belong to multiple strands. On synthetic scenes, PLECTA resolved strand identities more accurately than three released alternatives at every density tested. The same ranking held on an independently published generator run at its own settings. A separate stage uses the corresponding greyscale micrograph to estimate which strand lies on top at each crossing. The complete workflow is demonstrated on scanning electron micrographs of carbon nanotube sheets, from raw images to individual strand instances with estimated crossing order, without manual annotations for training or as pipeline inputs.

</details>


### [255] [FM-Recon: Exploring Foundation Model Features for Accelerated Cardiac Cine MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21290)
*Donghang Lyu,Marius Staring,Ruochen Gao,Jochen Keupp,Hildo J. Lamb,Mariya Doneva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出FM-Recon框架，将冻结的预训练基础模型图像编码器提取的特征精炼后注入展开式重建网络，用于加速心脏电影MRI重建，并系统评估了不同加速设置下基础模型特征的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在多种医学影像任务中展现出强特征提取能力，但其在MRI重建（尤其是展开式重建网络及伪影污染输入条件下）的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 以冻结的基础模型图像编码器为特征来源，设计特征精炼与融合模块，将提取特征整合进展开式（unrolled）重建网络，形成FM-Recon框架；在CMRxRecon2023数据集上开展多组重建设置的对比实验。

Result: 基础模型能够从受伪影污染的输入中提取有信息的表示，并提升重建性能，在中等加速倍数下效果尤为明显。

Conclusion: 基础模型特征对加速MRI重建具有应用潜力，并为其在展开式重建网络中的有效集成提供了实践性见解。

Abstract: Foundation models have demonstrated strong feature extraction capabilities across various medical imaging tasks, yet their potential for MRI reconstruction remains largely unexplored, particularly within unrolled reconstruction networks and under artifact-corrupted inputs. In this work, we explore whether and how features from pretrained foundation models can benefit accelerated cardiac cine MRI reconstruction. To this end, we propose FM-Recon, a framework that refines and integrates extracted features from frozen image encoder of foundation models into an unrolled reconstruction network. Through extensive experiments on the CMRxRecon2023 dataset, we systematically evaluate foundation model features across different reconstruction settings. Our results show that foundation models can extract informative representations from artifact-corrupted inputs and improve reconstruction performance, particularly at moderate acceleration factors. These findings demonstrate the potential of foundation model features for accelerated MRI reconstruction and provide insights into their effective integration into unrolled reconstruction networks.

</details>


### [256] [AnachroBench: Evaluating Historical Consistency in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21292)
*Keunhyeung Park,Byeonggeuk Lim,Kahyeon Kim,Sunhee Jo,Hayeong Ryu,Hoejoon Kwon,Jinhee Jang,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnachroBench，一个受控判别式基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在指定时代与国家下历史场景的一致性判断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM基准主要评估日常通用场景的视觉理解，对"与特定时代和国家的历史一致性"几乎未加探索，缺乏针对性评测手段。

Method: 构建含8个国家、覆盖19世纪末至20世纪初的1,600对图像，每对由原始历史图像与无缝插入现代物体的时代错乱版本组成；从三方面评测：插入物体感知（能否检测到现代物体）、时代-国家一致性判断、以及仅在正确感知插入物体条件下的条件一致性判断，并以"感知到现代物体却仍判为历史一致"定义错误一致性判断。

Result: 实验显示模型在插入物体感知与时代-国家一致性判断之间存在明显差距，即能识别现代物体却仍给出错误的一致性结论。

Conclusion: AnachroBench揭示了LVLM历史一致性推理的薄弱环节，为该类行为的定向评测提供了可控、细粒度的基准。

Abstract: Existing Large Vision-Language Model benchmarks primarily evaluate visual understanding in general everyday scenarios, leaving historical consistency with a specified era and country largely underexplored. To address this gap, we propose AnachroBench, a controlled discriminative benchmark for evaluating historical consistency in visual scenes. It contains 1,600 image pairs from eight countries spanning the late 19th to the early 20th century, each pairing an original historical image with an anachronistic version containing a seamlessly inserted modern object. AnachroBench evaluates three aspects: Inserted Object Perception, which tests whether models detect the inserted modern object; Era-Country Consistency, which assesses whether the scene is consistent with the specified era and country; and Conditional Era-Country Consistency, which measures consistency judgments only when the inserted object is correctly perceived. A false consistency judgment occurs when a model perceives the modern object but still judges the scene as historically consistent. Overall, AnachroBench reveals a gap between inserted object perception and era-country consistency judgments, providing a targeted evaluation of this behavior.

</details>


### [257] [Déjà-Events: Multi-view 3D Geometry with Event Cameras via Sparsity-Aware Adaptive Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21302)
*Arda Cinar Demirtas,Pablo Varela Bernal,Marc Pollefeys,Sunghwan Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出纯事件相机的前馈三维重建模型 Déjà-Events，通过从 RGB 教师模型蒸馏、无需深度/位姿真值，单次前向预测深度、位姿与点图，并在真实数据集零样本取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在快速或弱光场景下不模糊、不过曝，适合三维感知；但带密集三维真值的事件数据集规模与多样性远小于图像数据集，导致直接训练前馈三维重建模型受限。因此需要摆脱对稀缺三维标注的依赖。

Method: 构建仅用事件的多视图前馈模型，单次前向输出深度、相机位姿与点图。核心为蒸馏：在成对的 RGB 与事件录制上，让事件学生复现冻结的 RGB 教师（Déjà View，重复应用同一共享 transformer 块精炼 token）的最终 token，无需深度或位姿真值，仅训练约 1% 参数。针对事件在边缘与运动纹理处稠密、无纹理区近乎缺失的特性，引入稀疏感知自适应门控，将共享块的门控适配到事件，并按每 patch 的事件密度调整，使增加精炼步数时精度不退化。训练仅用模拟数据。

Result: 在 EventScape 上及零样本迁移到真实 MVSEC、DSEC 上，点图误差在所评估的事件方法中最低；DSEC 上 AbsRel 为 0.15，约为最强基线（0.30）的一半，参数量约为其十分之一；在 EventScape 与 DSEC 上深度误差最低，EventScape 上轨迹误差最低。

Conclusion: 跨模态蒸馏结合稀疏感知门控，可在无三维真值、仅模拟数据训练、极小可训练参数下，使事件模型在真实场景零样本达到领先的点图、深度与轨迹精度，为事件三维感知提供了绕开标注瓶颈的有效路径。

Abstract: Event cameras keep capturing structure where conventional cameras blur or saturate, which makes them attractive for 3D perception in fast or poorly lit scenes. Event datasets with dense 3D ground truth, however, are far smaller and less diverse than the image collections behind feed-forward 3D reconstruction models. We present Déjà-Events, an event-only model that predicts depth, camera pose and pointmaps from multiple views in a single forward pass. Rather than relying on scarce 3D labels, we distil it from Déjà View, a feed-forward RGB model that refines its tokens by repeatedly applying one shared transformer block. On paired RGB and event recordings, the event student learns to reproduce the frozen teacher's final tokens, which requires no depth or pose ground truth and trains only about 1% of its parameters. Because events are dense at edges and moving texture but nearly absent in textureless regions, we further introduce sparsity-aware adaptive gating, which adapts the shared block's gates to events and adjusts them per patch by event density, so that accuracy holds as refinement steps are added. Trained only on simulated data, Déjà-Events achieves the lowest pointmap error among the event-based methods we evaluate, on EventScape and zero-shot on the real MVSEC and DSEC datasets. On DSEC it halves the error of the strongest baseline (0.15 vs. 0.30 AbsRel) with about a tenth of its parameters. It also has the lowest depth error on EventScape and DSEC and the lowest trajectory error on EventScape.

</details>


### [258] [Fix, Don't Retake: Physics-Simulated Training for Correcting Patient Positioning Errors in Dental Panoramic Radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21304)
*Sema Helali,Lina Abu Nada,Faleh Tamimi,Rafat Damseh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探索用人工智能对已拍摄的全景X光片中的患者体位误差进行事后校正，并系统比较五类模型的校正效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者体位不当会使全景X光片产生形变失真，进而导致重复拍摄，增加患者辐射暴露与检查成本。

Method: 基于物理的仿真器从锥形束CT（CBCT）体数据生成配对的正位与错位全景图像，并可控地引入单一及组合体位误差。比较五种方法：U-Net、pix2pix、条件扩散模型、误差条件化模型，以及由误差专用专家模型组成的混合模型。

Result: 在三个测试集上以图像相似度与几何指标评估，所有方法均提升了平均图像相似度并降低了几何误差；U-Net与两种误差感知方法（误差条件化模型、专家混合模型）优于pix2pix与条件扩散模型。

Conclusion: 对已有全景片进行AI姿态校正具有可行性，误差感知设计能带来更优的校正性能；但结论依赖仿真数据，尚需在真实临床错位图像上验证。

Abstract: Patient-positioning errors can distort panoramic radiographs and lead to repeat examinations. This study investigated artificial intelligence for correcting these errors in existing images. A physics-based simulator generated paired correctly positioned and mispositioned panoramics from cone-beam computed tomography volumes, with controlled individual and combined positioning errors. Five approaches were compared: U-Net, pix2pix, conditional diffusion, an error-conditioned model, and a mixture of error-specific specialists. Evaluation used image similarity and geometric metrics across three test sets. All approaches improved mean image similarity and reduced geometric errors, with U-Net and the two error-aware approaches outperforming pix2pix and conditional diffusion.

</details>


### [259] [A2A-UAV: A Multimodal Air-to-Air Dataset for UAV Detection and Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21311)
*Hongtao Liang,Yiyao Wan,Kezhi Zhu,Jiayi Zhang,Zequn Jiang,Ziyu Zhou,Jiahuan Ji,Fuhui Zhou,Qihui Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出A2A-UAV，一个面向无人机检测与识别的多模态空对空数据集，通过无人机搭载平台采集RGB、全景、热成像、射频时频图与毫米波雷达点云，共105,294个时间对齐样本及209,539个人工标注边界框。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空空域中非合作无人机的违规入侵风险上升，需要可靠的检测与识别手段；但现有公开数据集多来自固定地面视角，且仅限成像或射频单一模态，限制了感知覆盖范围与部署灵活性。

Method: 构建空对空采集平台：以无人机为载体，搭载RGB-热成像云台相机、全景相机、射频频谱分析仪和毫米波雷达；在三个室外场地、白天与夜间条件下，对13个商用型号共15架目标无人机进行记录；所有边界框均经人工标注与交叉校验。

Result: 数据集包含105,294个时间对齐的多模态样本（RGB、全景、热成像、射频时频图像及雷达点云），共209,539个边界框标注，目标以小型无人机为主。

Conclusion: A2A-UAV为低空空域中的空对空无人机检测、识别及多模态融合研究提供了有价值的资源。

Abstract: The rapid growth of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) in low-altitude airspace increases the risk of unauthorized intrusions, making reliable detection and identification of non-cooperative UAVs essential for low-altitude airspace safety. However, the existing public UAV datasets are mostly acquired from fixed ground-based viewpoints and are limited to imaging or radio-frequency (RF) observations, which restricts the sensing coverage and deployment flexibility. To address this gap, we present A2A-UAV, a multimodal air-to-air dataset for UAV detection and identification. Specifically, 15 target UAVs of 13 commercial models were recorded at three outdoor sites under daytime and nighttime conditions by using a UAV-mounted platform equipped with an RGB-thermal gimbal camera, a panoramic camera, an RF spectrum analyzer, and a millimeter-wave radar. The dataset contains 105,294 time-aligned samples of RGB, panoramic, thermal, and RF time-frequency images and radar point clouds. Moreover, all 209,539 bounding boxes of the predominantly small UAV targets were manually annotated and cross-checked. Therefore, A2A-UAV provides a valuable resource for air-to-air UAV detection, identification, and multimodal fusion in low-altitude airspace.

</details>


### [260] [MovieRoll: A Systematic Framework for Rolling Out Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21339)
*Yunyang Ge,Weijie Kong,Ryan Xu,Yue Wu,Qi Tian,Jiangfeng Xiong,Bin Lin,Mingzhe Zheng,Shenghai Yuan,Zheyuan Liu,Jianwei Zhang,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MovieRoll 的 base-to-distilled 流式视频生成框架，结合流式微调、长 rollout 蒸馏与长时程推理，在 60s 生成上取得最佳综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成面临三重挑战：需在超出训练时长的区间维持质量、需处理自生成历史、且 KV cache 成本随历史增长。

Method: 1) 用 Window-wise Teacher Forcing (WinTF) 构建多步基座：窗口内双向注意力、对前序历史因果访问，逐级噪声联合去噪；2) HieraKV 分层 KV-cache 压缩：浅层保留完整历史、深层保留 sink–recent 历史；3) CuLT-DMD：固定参数做长自 rollout、平均局部梯度后更新，单次更新仅保留一张计算图，实现少步生成器的高效长 rollout 蒸馏；4) KV 重计算限制上下文以延伸生成长度，AnnealCFG 在接近干净端点时衰减引导以稳定基座；5) FBCA 注意力与 PS-VAE 解码支持高效训练与流式输出。

Result: HieraKV 将缓存内存最多降低 78% 且提升生成质量；HeliosBench 上，MovieRoll-Base 仅在≤15s 视频上训练却在 30s 仍具竞争力；MovieRoll-Distilled 在单事件与多事件评测（最长 60s）中综合得分最高，兼顾持续自然性与主动运动。

Conclusion: 通过基座训练—蒸馏—长时程推理的统一设计，可在控制缓存与计算成本的同时将流式视频生成有效外推到训练时长之外。

Abstract: Streaming video generation must sustain quality beyond the training horizon while handling self-generated history and growing cache costs. To address these challenges, we present MovieRoll, a base-to-distilled framework that connects streaming fine-tuning, long-rollout distillation, and long-horizon inference. We first establish a robust multi-step base through Window-wise Teacher Forcing (WinTF), which jointly denoises chunks at progressive noise levels with bidirectional attention within each window and causal access to preceding history. To retain historical context efficiently, hierarchical KV-cache compression (HieraKV) exploits layer-wise attention patterns, retaining full history in shallow layers and sink--recent history in deeper layers. This reduces cache memory by up to 78% while improving generation quality. Building on this base, cumulative long tuning with distribution matching distillation (CuLT-DMD) holds parameters fixed throughout each long self-rollout and averages local gradients before updating. Each update therefore combines supervision across temporal positions while retaining only one local computation graph, enabling efficient long-rollout training of a few-step generator. To extend generation beyond the training horizon, KV recomputation bounds context for both models, while guidance annealing (AnnealCFG) reduces guidance toward the clean endpoint to stabilize MovieRoll-Base. Complementing these modeling advances, FBCA attention and PS-VAE decoding support efficient training and streaming output. On HeliosBench, MovieRoll-Base remains competitive at 30s after training on videos of up to 15s, and MovieRoll-Distilled achieves the highest total scores among the compared methods across single- and multi-event evaluations up to 60s, combining sustained naturalness with active motion.

</details>


### [261] [Symmetry Detection with Patch Embedding Models by Means of a Scalar Product Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21363)
*Christoph Dalitz,Jan Beeker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Scalar Product Transform (SPT)，将图像patch的向量嵌入转换为patch交叉点处点对称性的得分，无需针对对称检测训练即可超越专用CNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法进行对称检测通常需要专门针对该任务训练；作者希望利用现成的预训练patch嵌入模型，无需特定训练步骤即可完成点反射对称检测。

Method: 受Gradient Product Transform启发，用patch的向量嵌入替代灰度梯度，构建"Scalar Product Transform"，在patch交叉点处计算点反射对称得分；方法可与任意预训练patch嵌入模型配合，实验中使用了DINOv3与Franca。

Result: 测试中SPT优于Gradient Product Transform，甚至超过专为对称检测设计并训练的卷积网络EquiSym；SPT运行时间高于EquiSym，但能直接给出对称区域的边界框。

Conclusion: SPT证明了预训练patch嵌入可有效用于点反射对称检测且性能优越；其主要优势是无需任务特定训练并直接输出对称区域边界框，代价是计算开销较大。

Abstract: We present a transformation of vector embeddings representing image patches into scores for point reflection symmetry at the crossing points of patches. The transformation is heavily inspired by the Gradient Product Transform, but it uses the vector embeddings instead of the grayscale gradients. We therefore call it the "Scalar Product Transform" (SPT). Unlike deep learning models for symmetry detection, the method does not require a specific training step tailored for symmetry detection, but it works with arbitrary pre-trained patch embedding models. We have tested the method with DINOv3 and Franca. In our tests, the method outperformed the Gradient Product Transform and was even better than EquiSym, a convolutional neural network specifically designed and trained for symmetry detection. Although the runtime of the SPT is greater than that of EquiSym, the SPT has the advantage that it directly yields a bounding box for the symmetric region.

</details>


### [262] [Exploring the link between predictive uncertainty and the sim-to-real gap in deep learning models utilizing synthetic data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21365)
*Alexander Moriz,Lukas Krebs,Amon Göppert,Robert H. Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文用集成方法估计合成数据训练的深度学习质检模型的预测不确定性，发现sim-to-real域偏移下预测不确定性上升，但性能下降主要由系统性sim-to-real偏差而非集成分歧所主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器视觉质检依赖深度学习，但工业落地受制于数据稀疏与对模型可靠性的信任不足。合成数据可提供完美标注，却存在sim-to-real gap导致的性能退化风险，因此需要评估不确定性以支撑可靠部署。

Method: 采用集成（ensemble）方法，对在合成数据上训练的深度学习质检模型进行预测不确定性估计，并在真实域数据上考察域偏移下不确定性与性能的变化。

Result: 在sim-to-real域偏移下预测不确定性增大；然而观测到的性能下降主要源于系统性的sim-to-real偏差，而非集成模型间的分歧（ensemble disagreement）。

Conclusion: 研究支持合成数据在工业质检中的有效利用，同时强调在部署深度学习质检方案时进行风险评估的重要性。

Abstract: Today, state-of-the-art methods for quality control (QC) are based on Machine Vision, combining deep learning (DL) models with camera-based inspection. Yet, the integration of such solutions in industrial applications is often impeded by data sparsity and trust in model reliability. While synthetic data offers a promising approach to generate the necessary data with perfect annotations, it poses risks related to performance degradation due to the possible occurrence of the sim-to-real gap. This study conducts experiments using the ensemble method to estimate the predictive uncertainty of DL models trained on synthetic data. The results show that predictive uncertainty increases under the sim-to-real domain shift. However, the observed performance drop is dominated by a systematic sim-to-real bias rather than by ensemble disagreement.These findings contribute to the effective utilization of synthetic data in industrial settings while highlighting the importance of risk assessment for the deployment of DL solutions for QC tasks.

</details>


### [263] [ALIVE: Making Streaming Video Models Interactive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21366)
*Jinzhao Li,Yuan Xu,Jinxuan Wu,Yukun Wang,Yijia Lei,Hanyu Ji,Yinuo Chen,Zining Huang,Chi Zhang,Hang Ye,Yiwu Zhong,Honglei Yan,Panwang Pan,Yizhou Wang,Yin Li,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ALIVE，一个支持主动监控、流式任务协调与反馈寻求交互的交互式流式视频大模型，并配套两阶段训练（对比式SFT + 带行为对齐信用分配的强化学习）与 FSI-Bench 评测基准，在四个公开基准和 FSI-Bench 上取得最高综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频 LLM 虽能持续观察世界，但往往以孤立方式处理用户请求，缺乏主动性与多轮交互能力（何时响应、如何响应、何时保持沉默）。

Method: 构建 ALIVE-SFT-288K 与 ALIVE-RL-3K 两阶段数据；第一阶段用对比式监督微调教会模型何时与如何响应；第二阶段用带 Behavior-Aligned Credit Assignment (BACA) 的强化学习，融合轨迹级与局部优势，优化多轮交互中的决策、回答与沉默行为；同时提出 FSI-Bench 评测反馈寻求及后续任务完成。

Result: ALIVE 在四个公开基准与 FSI-Bench 上均取得对比模型中的最高综合得分，展现出较强的流式理解与交互能力。

Conclusion: 将流式视频理解与主动交互机制结合，并通过“对比式SFT + 行为对齐信用分配的RL”两阶段训练，可有效提升流式视频模型的主动性、交互质量与任务完成度。

Abstract: Streaming video LLMs can continuously observe the world, yet often handle user requests in isolation. We introduce ALIVE, an interactive streaming video model supporting proactive monitoring, streaming task coordination, and feedback-seeking interaction. We construct ALIVE-SFT-288K and ALIVE-RL-3K for two-stage training. Contrastive supervised fine-tuning teaches when and how to respond, while reinforcement learning with Behavior-Aligned Credit Assignment (BACA) combines trajectory-level and local advantages to refine decisions, responses, and silence across multi-turn interactions. We also introduce FSI-Bench to evaluate feedback seeking and subsequent task completion. ALIVE achieves the highest overall scores among the compared models on four public benchmarks and FSI-Bench, demonstrating strong streaming understanding and interactive capabilities.

</details>


### [264] [WeVisForensics: Self-Evolving Agent for Omni AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21373)
*Yipeng Du,Yuxi Li,Ying Zhang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WeVisForensics，一个主调查智能体协调自进化取证检查员子智能体库（视觉与音频线索）的框架，配合双时间尺度策略优化 DTPO 与 ForenCue-30K 数据集，平均检测准确率 95.2%，较最强基线提升 9.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成能力进步要求具备明确取证证据支撑的可靠检测，但现有方法要么依赖单次思维链推理而忽略短促或局部伪影，要么依赖预定义多智能体流程、检查选择灵活性不足；且即便引入自适应调查，仅优化调查策略也无法直接提升固定专家的取证能力。

Method: 主调查智能体协调一个自进化的取证检查员子智能体库，覆盖视觉与音频线索；调查者学习检查何种伪影类型、在哪个时间窗口检查、何时终止，交互为两层智能体提供共享反馈。提出双时间尺度策略优化 DTPO：对调查者做快速强化学习，对检查员做较慢的精细化调整，并以标注伪影区间为引导，使其报告此前忽略的细微伪影。同时构建 ForenCue-30K，含角色特定的取证标注与交互轨迹，用于监督单次检查及其协同，并附带强化学习池。

Result: 在域内、域外及智能体基准上平均检测准确率达 95.2%，比最强已有检测器高 9.0%，且判决以局部化证据为依据。

Conclusion: 分层自适应调查与双时间尺度联合优化可同时提升调查策略与检查员能力，实现高准确率且证据可定位的视频取证检测。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation call for reliable detection grounded in explicit forensic evidence. However, current methods primarily depend on single-pass chain-of-thought reasoning that overlooks brief or localized artifacts, or on predefined multi-agent workflows that offer limited flexibility in selecting relevant inspections. Even with adaptive investigation, optimizing the investigation policy alone does not directly improve the forensic capabilities of fixed experts. We introduce WeVisForensics, an agentic framework in which a main investigator agent coordinates a self-evolving library of forensic inspector sub-agents spanning visual and audio cues. The investigator learns which artifact type to examine, over which window, and when to conclude, and these interactions provide shared feedback for both levels of agents. Our dual-timescale policy optimization strategy, DTPO, closes this loop through fast reinforcement learning of the investigator and slower refinement of the inspectors, guided by annotated artifact intervals, to report subtle artifacts they once dismissed. Furthermore, we curate ForenCue-30K, combining role-specific forensic annotations and interaction trajectories to supervise individual inspections and their coordination, with an accompanying reinforcement-learning pool. Experiments show that WeVisForensics averages 95.2% detection accuracy across in-domain, out-of-domain and agentic benchmarks, 9.0% above the strongest prior detector, while grounding its verdicts in localized evidence.

</details>


### [265] [The Mask Is Not the Object: Volumetric Supervision for 3D Gaussian Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21383)
*Jeonghwan Cho,Minsu Kim,Junyoung Hong,Seon Joo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的监督目标VODA，利用局部深度结构分组逐像素贡献者，直接监督所选深度簇内全部高斯，实现体素级完整的3D高斯分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯分割方法主要在渲染视图上训练与评测，存在训练/评测域错配：alpha混合的特征监督集中于主要贡献高斯，遮挡或低贡献高斯监督微弱，导致渲染掩码看似准确但3D选择不完整。

Method: VODA：即插即用监督目标，借助局部深度结构对逐像素贡献者进行分组，直接监督所选深度簇内的所有高斯，绕过基于贡献加权的梯度传递。另构建Removal-3D合成物体移除基准与Removal Depth Error（RDE）指标，通过对比预测与真值深度变化评估体素完整性。

Result: 在四种基于渲染的基线方法上，VODA在Removal-3D上一致提升体素分割效果，并在LERF与ScanNet上保持或提升性能。分析表明VODA能恢复低贡献高斯，而简单梯度重加权与空间传播无法复现同等增益。

Conclusion: 渲染视图监督不足以保证3D高斯选择的体素完整性；基于深度簇的直接监督是有效且通用的改进方向，并需配套体素级评测基准与指标。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting enables object-level operations on Gaussian primitives, yet existing segmentation methods are largely trained and evaluated through rendered views. This mismatch can produce accurate rendered masks from incomplete 3D selections since alpha-blended feature supervision concentrates learning on dominant contributors while leaving occluded or low-contribution Gaussians weakly supervised. We propose VODA, a plug-and-play supervision objective that exploits local depth structure to group per-pixel contributors and directly supervises all Gaussians in selected depth clusters, bypassing contribution-weighted gradient delivery. To evaluate volumetric completeness, we introduce Removal-3D, a synthetic object-removal benchmark, and the Removal Depth Error (RDE), a metric comparing predicted and ground-truth depth changes. Across four rendering-based baselines, VODA consistently improves volumetric segmentation on Removal-3D while maintaining or improving performance on LERF and ScanNet. Further analysis shows that VODA recovers lower-contribution Gaussians, while simple gradient reweighting and spatial propagation fail to reproduce comparable gains.

</details>


### [266] [VideoNexus: Video Perception Beyond the Reasoning Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21392)
*Yancheng Long,Xuanyu Zheng,Wei Chen,Luoyuan Zhang,Haoyang Xu,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoNexus 是一个无需训练的框架，将细粒度视频感知与证据驱动的规划推理解耦：规划器决定看哪里、解析什么、采样密度，感知子代理在独立上下文中处理原生音视频并返回紧凑、时间定位的证据，用于迭代搜索与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需要在密集视觉序列中定位证据、解析细节并整合答案，给感知与推理带来双重负担。视频代理虽可选择性查看，但把帧回传给规划器仍会累积视觉上下文；而委派感知又会因稀疏采样漏掉关键细节，或通过逐帧描述依然造成上下文膨胀。

Method: 训练无关框架：规划器控制检视位置、需解析内容与采样密度；感知子代理在相互独立的上下文中处理原生音视频，返回紧凑且具时间定位的证据；两者通过迭代式搜索与验证进行紧凑证据交换。

Result: 以 Gemini 3.8 Flash 同时作为规划与感知骨干，在完整 LVBench 上达到 88.6% 准确率，分别比同骨干静态输入与内置代理式处理高 3.6 和 1.1 个百分点，且 API 成本仅为后者的 44%；配对 LVBench 子集消融显示规划器输入更少且准确率更高。以 Kimi K3 为共享骨干时，相比官方 Video-GAIA ReAct 框架提升 8.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 紧凑证据交换能够将细致观察与高效规划推理结合，是长视频代理的可扩展范式；但结论主要基于单一数据集与特定商业骨干，泛化性与成本结构的普适性仍需更多验证。

Abstract: Understanding long videos requires locating evidence, resolving visual details, and integrating observations into an answer. Even when relevant evidence is present, performing these operations within long, dense visual sequences burdens both perception and reasoning. Video agents enable selective inspection, but returning frames to the planner still accumulates visual information in its reasoning context. Delegating perception reduces this burden, yet sparse sampling can miss decisive details, and per-frame descriptions can preserve context growth. We introduce VideoNexus, a training-free framework that separates detailed video perception from evidence-driven planning and reasoning. The planner controls where to inspect, what to resolve, and how densely to sample. Perception sub-agents process native audio-video in independent contexts and return compact, temporally grounded evidence for iterative search and verification. Using Gemini 3.8 Flash for both planning and perception, VideoNexus achieves 88.6% accuracy on full LVBench, exceeding same-backbone static ingestion and built-in agentic processing by 3.6 and 1.1 points, respectively, at 44% of the latter's measured API cost. A paired LVBench subset ablation shows lower planner input and higher accuracy. With Kimi K3 as the shared backbone, VideoNexus improves over the official Video-GAIA ReAct harness by 8.5 points in our evaluation. These results support compact evidence exchange for combining detailed observation with efficient planning and reasoning.

</details>


### [267] [Portrait Fitness as a Pretext: Probing Implicit Demographic Bias in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21403)
*Alexandre Fournier-Montgieux,Akram Elbouanani,Aboubacar Tuo,Adrian Popescu,Hervé Le Borgne,Bertrand Luvison*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BALPORTRAITS数据集（9,000张虚构人物肖像，平衡族裔/性别/年龄）与广告选角框架，审计16个VLM的面部相关偏见；发现所有模型均存在人口学分散性，多数配对组间差异显著，单一场景或单一属性审计不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM中与面部相关的人口学偏见缺乏受控、可避免直接推断受保护属性的评估协议；现有审计多为单场景、单属性，结论不稳健。

Method: 构建平衡虚构肖像数据集BALPORTRAITS；在广告选角框架下由16个VLM对职业、品牌、活动、银行/保险产品给出肖像适配度评分；将条目级评分与场景特定声望分数聚合，跨人口学群体比较排序。

Result: 偏见的方向与幅度随场景、模型族与人口学维度而异，年龄、族裔、性别在任务间贡献不同；所有被评估模型均表现出人口学分散性，多数配对组间差异达到统计显著。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了评估隐性面部关联的受控协议，并证明单场景或单属性审计不足以刻画VLM的面部偏见。

Abstract: We audit face-related bias in VLMs using BALPORTRAITS, a new balanced dataset of 9,000 fictional portraits across ethnicity, gender, and age segments. Sixteen VLMs estimate portrait fitness scores for occupations, brands, activities, and banking or insurance products within an advertising-casting framework designed to avoid direct inference of protected attributes. Item-level estimations are aggregated with scenario-specific prestige scores to compare rankings across demographic groups. While the magnitude and direction of these biases vary across scenarios, model families, and demographic dimensions, with age, ethnicity, and gender contributing differently depending on the task, we find demographic dispersion across all evaluated models, with a majority of pairwise segment differences reaching statistical significance. This study introduces a controlled protocol to assess implicit face-related associations and demonstrates that single-scenario or single-attribute audits are insufficient.

</details>


### [268] [Flexible Multi-Stack Super-Resolution of Clinical Brain MRI Using Hetero-Orientation Variational Encoding: Expert Reader Evaluation in a Nigerian Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21412)
*Kabir Hamzah Muhammad,Lorena Garcia-Foncillas Macias,Soumya Snigdha Kundu,Emily Guilhem,Jon Cleary,Amal Saleh Nour,Yodit Abraham Yaynishet,Chinasa Kalaiwo,Peter Chibuta,Ugumba Kwikima,Udunna Anazodo,Jonathan Shapey,Tom Vercauteren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MS-HVED，一种序列无关的多堆栈脑MRI超分辨率框架，用视场感知高斯乘积融合与物理域随机化，并在尼日利亚队列等真实临床数据上经盲评验证优于监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规临床脑MRI多为快速各向异性2D采集，方向、对齐与覆盖范围不一致导致堆栈仅部分重叠；现有学习式超分辨依赖单一欠约束输入或固定多堆栈理想几何，难以适应真实采集条件。

Method: MS-HVED将可用的轴位、冠状位、矢状位堆栈独立编码为多尺度高斯后验，并用视场感知的Product-of-Gaussians融合抑制无支撑区域贡献；同时采用物理信息域随机化模拟倾斜、部分覆盖与成像伪影等异质采集条件；框架与序列无关。

Result: 在合成基准上重建保真度具竞争力或更优，下游组织分割优于低分辨率输入。30例尼日利亚常规脑MRI外部验证（为44例盲评研究中最大组成部分，7名神经放射专家）：该队列210次配对评估中187次偏好MS-HVED；全部临床队列总体质量5.93 vs 4.49（p<.001），省略轴位或冠状位输入后偏好仍显著（p<.001）。

Conclusion: MS-HVED能灵活融合任意可用堆栈并在真实、代表性不足人群的临床数据上获得专家偏好，具备良好的泛化性与实用价值。

Abstract: High-resolution brain MRI is important for neuroradiological assessment, yet routine clinical protocols often rely on fast, anisotropic 2D acquisitions, particularly in resource-constrained settings. These acquisitions may differ in orientation, alignment and anatomical coverage, resulting in partially overlapping image stacks. Existing learning-based super-resolution methods typically rely on single, under-constrained inputs or fixed multi-stack configurations with idealised acquisition geometry. We introduce MS-HVED, a sequence-agnostic framework for flexible multi-stack brain MRI super-resolution. Available axial, coronal and sagittal stacks are independently encoded into multi-scale Gaussian posteriors and fused using a field-of-view-aware Product-of-Gaussians formulation that limits contributions from unsupported regions. Physics-informed domain randomisation simulates heterogeneous clinical acquisition conditions, including obliqueness, partial coverage and imaging artefacts. MS-HVED achieved competitive or superior reconstruction fidelity across synthetic benchmarks and improved downstream tissue segmentation relative to low-resolution inputs. To assess generalisation beyond the training cohorts, we performed external clinical validation on 30 routine brain MRI examinations from a Nigerian cohort, representing an under-represented population in medical imaging research. These examinations formed the largest component of a blinded reader study involving 44 real cases and seven expert neuroradiologists. In the Nigerian cohort, MS-HVED was preferred over the supervised baseline for overall image quality in 187 of 210 paired assessments. Across all clinical cohorts, MS-HVED achieved higher overall-quality scores with all available inputs (5.93 vs. 4.49, p<.001), with the preference persisting when axial or coronal inputs were omitted (p<.001). Code at github.com/cai4cai/ms-hved

</details>


### [269] [Quality and Similarity Indicators for Video-Based Heart Rate and Respiratory Rate Fusion: A Controlled Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21419)
*Nhi Nguyen,Le Nguyen,Manuel Lage Cañellas,Miguel Bordallo López,Constantino Álvarez Casado*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了11种质量指标与8种方法间相似度度量在7个心率(HR)和10个呼吸率(RR)提取器融合中的有效性,发现质量引导融合对视频HR有效,但对RR无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机式HR/RR估计在不同窗口间可靠性波动,常用做法是用无参考信号质量指标加权融合多种提取方法,但少有研究检验这些指标能否在每窗口中识别最准确的方法,或是否优于更简单的融合策略。

Method: 在四个公开数据集(75名测试受试者)上,评估11种质量指标和8种方法间相似度度量,用于融合7个HR和10个RR提取器。滤波器、指标方向、加权参数及学习式岭回归基线在独立的5人集合上调优后测试。融合结果与等权平均、常数典型率预测器、预先选定与事后最优的单一方法、以及带置换检验零分布的逐窗口oracle进行比较,采用受试者级bootstrap置信区间。

Result: HR方面,质量加权融合相对等权平均降低平均绝对误差10.9% [5.2, 18.4],相对预先选定方法降低15.6% [8.1, 23.1],与事后最优方法在统计上无差异;基于方法间一致性的加权未优于等权平均。RR方面,指标无法识别更准确的估计:锐化质量加权反而增加误差(-2.6% [-4.8, -0.8]),融合估计与参考值不相关(r ≤ 0.22),等权平均不如典型率预测器(-3.1% [-6.1, -0.4]);使用第二个呼吸参考时,表观RR增益实为估计向典型率靠拢所致。

Conclusion: 质量引导融合对视频HR有价值;对RR而言,30秒窗口虽能改善估计,但并未改善融合效果,RR融合融合策略不可靠。

Abstract: Camera-based heart rate (HR) and respiratory rate (RR) estimates vary in reliability from window to window, and a common remedy is to fuse several extraction methods with weights from reference-free signal quality indicators. However, few studies test whether these indicators identify the most accurate method in each window or outperform simpler fusion. We evaluate 11 quality indicators and 8 inter-method similarity measures for fusing 7 HR and 10 RR extractors on four public datasets (75 test subjects). Filters, indicator directions, weighting parameters and a learned ridge baseline are selected on a separate five-subject set before testing. Fusion is compared with equal averaging, a constant typical-rate predictor, the best single method selected in advance and in hindsight, and a per-window oracle with a permutation-based null, using subject-level bootstrap confidence intervals. For HR, quality-weighted fusion reduced mean absolute error by 10.9% [5.2, 18.4] relative to averaging and by 15.6% [8.1, 23.1] relative to the method selected in advance, and was statistically indistinguishable from the best method selected in hindsight. Weighting methods by their agreement did not improve on averaging. For RR, the indicators could not identify the more accurate estimates: sharpened quality weighting increased error (-2.6% [-4.8, -0.8]), fused estimates were uncorrelated with the reference (r <= 0.22), and averaging was less accurate than the typical-rate predictor (-3.1% [-6.1, -0.4]). With a second respiratory reference, apparent RR gains reflected estimates moving toward its typical rate. Quality-guided fusion is worthwhile for video HR; for RR, 30-s windows improved estimates but not fusion.

</details>


### [270] [Power Line Detection in Point Clouds with Point-SAM and RANSAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21438)
*Christoph Dalitz,Oskar Waedt,Steffen Goebbels,Mahdieh Shamsadini Motlagh,Gabriel Gerzabek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文研究机载激光扫描点云中电力线的定位：将电力线与地面、植被等背景分离（分割），并区分单条电力线（个体线检测）。分割比较规则方法与基于迁移学习的 Point-SAM；单线检测推导3D悬链线 RANSAC 的数学公式，并与非参数方法 TriplClust 对比。所有方法在新建并公开的数据集 PowLiDFR 上评测，样本取自法国 LiDAR HD 项目，覆盖多种地形与点云分辨率。结果表明：无监督迁移学习优于纯规则分割；RANSAC 在贴合悬链线形状上优于非参数算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力线在机载激光扫描点云中的自动定位具有实际需求，需同时解决两类子问题：一是从地面与植被等背景中分割出电力线（类别不平衡、背景复杂），二是识别并分离单条电力线（线状目标相互平行、形状为悬链线）。现有方法（规则法、非参数聚类）在精度与形状贴合度上存在不足，且缺乏覆盖多地形、多分辨率的公开评测基准。

Method: 1）分割阶段：对比规则（rule-based）方法与基于迁移学习的 Point-SAM（无监督迁移学习）。2）单线检测阶段：推导将 RANSAC 应用于3D悬链线（catenary）的数学公式，并与非参数方法 TriplClust 对比。3）评测：构建并公开数据集 PowLiDFR，样本取自法国 LiDAR HD 项目，覆盖多样地形布局与点云分辨率。

Result: 在 PowLiDFR 数据集上，无监督迁移学习（Point-SAM）的分割效果优于纯规则方法；在单条电力线检测中，基于3D悬链线模型的 RANSAC 在贴合悬链线形状方面优于非参数算法 TriplClust。

Conclusion: 无监督迁移学习适合替代规则式电力线分割；将悬链线几何先验嵌入 RANSAC 能更准确地刻画单条电力线。公开的 PowLiDFR 数据集为后续研究提供了可复现的多场景基准。

Abstract: This article deals with the problem of detecting the position of power lines in airborne laser scanning point clouds. We address both the problem of segmenting power lines from background like ground and vegetation and of identifying individual lines. For segmentation we compare a rule-based approach with one based on transfer learning (Point-SAM). For individual line detection, we derive the mathematical formulas for RANSAC applied to 3D catenaries and compare it with a non-parametric method (TriplClust). All methods are evaluated on a newly created dataset "PowLiDFR", which we make publicly available. The samples have been taken from the French project LiDAR HD and cover a variety of terrain layouts and point cloud resolutions. On this dataset, an unsupervised transfer learning outperformed a mere rule-based segmentation, and for individual power line detection, RANSAC was better in following the shape of catenaries than the non-parametric algorithm.

</details>


### [271] [[CLS] and [EOS] Tokens are not Enough for CLIP-based Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21440)
*Hao Sun,Tao Hu,Shixiong Kai,Siyuan Xu,Wenqian Zhao,Mingxuan Yuan,Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE：利用CLIP中被忽略的patch级视觉与token级文本局部表征，通过跨模态融合与投影式参数更新实现类增量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的CIL方法仅使用全局表征（视觉[CLS]与文本[EOS]）做跨模态对齐，忽略了CLIP固有的丰富patch级视觉特征和token级文本语义，而这些局部细节对细粒度识别至关重要。

Method: 1) 基于[CLS]/[EOS]全局表征选出top-k关键视觉patch与文本token；2) 设计跨模态融合模块，用局部文本语义精炼视觉表征、用局部视觉特征增强文本语义；3) 因融合模块跨任务共享，直接微调会引发灾难性遗忘，故采用基于投影的更新策略，在适配新任务时保护旧任务的关键参数子空间。

Result: 在多个CIL基准上显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 挖掘CLIP长期被忽视的局部表征并结合跨模态融合与投影式更新，可有效提升类增量学习的性能并缓解遗忘。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) aims to empower models to continuously acquire new knowledge while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. With the advent of vision-language models like CLIP, leveraging their cross-modal alignment capabilities to address CIL challenges has emerged as a dominant paradigm. Existing methods leverage CLIP's global representations (i.e., the visual [CLS] token and the textual [EOS] token) for cross-modal alignment. While achieving effective classification, they overlook the rich patch-level visual features and token-level textual semantics inherent in CLIP. For example, the [CLS] token aggregates local information to capture the general concept of "cat", while the patch-level details retain specific localized features essential for recognition, such as the tail, fur, and face. We propose PACE (PAtch-token Cross-modal Enhancement) to uncover the long-neglected local representations within CLIP. First, based on the global representations like [CLS] token and [EOS] token, we identify the top-k most crucial visual patches and textual tokens. Subsequently, to better utilize the extracted key local features, we design a cross-modal fusion module. This module leverages local textual semantics to refine visual representations, while augmenting textual semantics with local visual features. As the fusion module is shared across all tasks, directly fine-tuning it on new tasks would interfere with the knowledge learned from previous tasks, leading to catastrophic forgetting. To alleviate this, we employ a projection-based update strategy to effectively preserve the critical parameter space of previous tasks during the adaptation to new tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PACE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple CIL benchmarks.

</details>


### [272] [Out of Sight, Into Memory: Compact Visual Latents Retain Local Evidence in Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21457)
*Meng Tang,Siyuan Du,Yuxin Liu,Shuaishuai Cao,Di Wang,Haonan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文验证压缩视觉隐向量（latent memory）能否在超高分辨率遥感问答中保留细粒度局部证据，并区分"证据保留"与"区域选择"两个因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHR遥感场景对MLLM存在张力：全局概览丢失小目标与局部属性，而密集裁剪消耗大量上下文。将裁剪压缩为短潜变量序列可作紧凑记忆，但自然图像上的诊断研究表明模型常忽略潜变量内容，因此尚不清楚此类紧凑记忆能否在降采样UHR场景中保留细粒度目标。

Method: 将高分辨率裁剪编码为latent memory token，接入冻结骨干；在LRS-GRO与XLRS-Bench上以Qwen-VL两代模型评估其预测效用与内容依赖性，通过替换其他图像/同场景其他区域记忆、置零或移除记忆、恢复源裁剪等对照实验隔离证据保留与区域选择。

Result: 64-token记忆对保留裁剪token实现7.6–9.9倍压缩，较仅用降采样全局图准确率提升2.5–4.8个百分点，恢复高分辨率裁剪增益的大部分；替换记忆降低5.8–7.4个百分点且低于完全移除记忆，说明预测依赖所编码的区域证据；恢复源裁剪可缓解置零/移除记忆带来的损失。

Conclusion: 紧凑视觉潜变量在UHR遥感中能保留有用的局部证据，证据可得性是决定其贡献的关键因素。

Abstract: Question answering over ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing scenes poses a tension for multimodal large language models: global overviews obscure small objects and local attributes, whereas detailed crops consume substantial context. Compressing inspected crops into short sequences of continuous visual latents offers a compact memory once crop tokens are discarded, yet diagnostic studies on natural images find that models often ignore latent content, leaving unclear whether compact latents can retain fine-grained targets lost in downsampled UHR remote sensing scenes. To isolate evidence retention from region selection, we encode high-resolution crops into latent memory tokens for frozen backbones and evaluate both their predictive utility and content dependence. Across two generations of Qwen-VL on LRS-GRO, a 64-token memory achieves 7.6- to 9.9-fold compression of retained crop tokens and improves accuracy by 2.5 to 4.8 percentage points over the downsampled global image alone, recovering most of the high-resolution crop gain with consistent behavior on XLRS-Bench. Substituting memory from another image or another region of the same scene reduces accuracy by 5.8 to 7.4 percentage points and yields lower accuracy than removing memory altogether, demonstrating that predictions depend on the encoded regional evidence. Restoring the source crop within the same model reduces the accuracy loss caused by zeroing or removing memory. These findings establish that compact visual latents retain useful local evidence in UHR remote sensing and identify evidence availability as a key factor governing their contribution.

</details>


### [273] [Balanced Twin Weight Factorization for Efficient MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21459)
*Bryan A. Plummer,Shan Lu,Keval Doshi,Noranart Vesdapunt,Wei Wang,Jim Thomas,Kah Kuen Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Balanced Twin Weight Factorization (BTWF)，利用孪生层共享基分解实现推理延迟与学习容量的同步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重分解（WF）虽能减少参数量与FLOPs，但把单个大权重矩阵替换为多个小矩阵会引入额外开销，导致低FLOPs难以转化为低延迟；同时现有共享基WF方法在孪生层尺寸不一致时偏向较大层，表征不均衡。

Method: 在共享基WF设定下，将两层或多层分解为共享基矩阵与层特定混合矩阵；若共享层具有相同输入（孪生层），则共享前向仅执行一次以降低推理时间。引入 Factor Component Balancing (FCB) 保证各共享成员在所选因子中被充分表示，并用 Block Contribution Balancing (BCB) 考虑下游模型效应；此外允许训练期过参数化（因子FLOPs高于原始）以提升性能，而测试期不增加资源。

Result: 在6个模型、9个基准上，BTWF 在可比计算成本下较SOTA提升2-6%（辅以昂贵微调时提升1-3.5%）。

Conclusion: 利用孪生层共享前向的结构优势，BTWF 可同时降低延迟并提升学习容量，配平衡策略与训练期过参数化进一步增益，且无需测试期额外开销。

Abstract: Weight Factorization (WF) methods aim to reduce a model's latency, memory, or both. These typically decompose a pretrained model into two or more factors that approximate the original model weights with fewer parameters and floating point operations (FLOPs). However, translating fewer FLOPs into reduced latency is challenging for WF methods in practice due to overhead introduced by replacing a single large weight matrix with multiple smaller ones. To address this, we propose Balanced Twin Weight Factorization (BTWF), which uses the unique structure of factorized models to simultaneously lower latency and increase learning capacity. Specifically, BTWF operates in the shared basis WF setting, where two or more layers are decomposed into a shared basis matrix and a layer-specific mixing matrix. If these shared layers have the same input (i.e., are twins), then we need only perform this shared forward pass once, reducing inference time. However, twin layers are often of varying sizes, and methods from prior work tend to favor the larger layer. In contrast, our approach boosts performance by combining Factor Component Balancing (FCB), which ensures every shared member is well represented in the selected factors, with Block Contribution Balancing (BCB), which considers downstream model effects. Further, we also show that an overparameterized BTWF, where some layer's factors result in higher FLOPs than the original during training, can boost task performance without additional resources at test time. Experiments on six models over nine benchmarks show BTWF obtains a 2-6% gain over the state-of-the-art without expensive finetuning (1-3.5% gain with it) at comparable computational costs.

</details>


### [274] [Event-Triggered Visual Perception for Real-Time Harvest Outcome Estimation in Mechanical Blueberry Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21472)
*Nicolas Miroslav Husár,Jelle Driessen,Petra Bosilj,Başak Sakçak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种事件触发式机器视觉系统，在采收机上对已采收蓝莓进行成熟度分级（熟、半熟、未熟），仅用现有CPU工业计算机即可在14 s侧间间隔内完成分析，田间试验的成熟比例估计与人工结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械采收蓝莓需在"摘取成熟果"与"避免过早摘除可留待后续采收的未熟果"之间取得平衡；但操作者普遍缺乏实时了解所收果实成熟度构成的信息，难以评估采收选择性。

Method: 构建事件触发式机载机器视觉系统：当机器移动一个装满的果箱时自动触发RGB图像采集，采用三类别检测器（熟、半熟、未熟）进行分析；算法在现有仅CPU的工业计算机上本地运行，既不需要连续图像处理，也不需要专用GPU。以79幅果箱图像（含48,634个可见蓝莓，人工核验参考标注）进行田间评估。

Result: 系统估计总体成熟果比例为90.22%，人工标注为90.97%；单幅图像成熟比例的估计平均绝对误差为0.935个百分点，Pearson相关系数r=0.967。采收机两侧光照条件不同，但成熟度估计相近，尽管直射阳光下绝对计数性能较差；全部田间事件均在每侧14 s的果箱间隔内完成处理。

Conclusion: 该方法可在机器运行过程中实用地量化采收选择性的一个组成部分，为操作者决策提供依据；若与果实回收率和损失测量相结合，可为未来基于反馈的机械采收控制奠定基础。

Abstract: Mechanical blueberry harvesting requires a balance between removing ripe fruit and avoiding premature removal of unripe berries that could remain on the bush for a later harvest. However, operators generally lack real-time information about the maturity composition of the fruit being collected. This study develops an event-triggered machine-vision system that measures the maturity of harvested blueberries directly on the harvester. When a filled crate is moved by the machine, an RGB image is automatically captured and analysed using a three-class detector that identifies ripe, semi-ripe, and unripe berries. The system operates locally on the existing CPU-only industrial computer and therefore requires neither continuous image processing nor dedicated GPU hardware. During field testing, 79 crate images containing 48,634 visible blueberries in manually verified reference annotations were evaluated. The vision system estimated an overall ripe-fruit fraction of 90.22%, compared with 90.97% determined manually. Per-image ripe-fruit estimates showed a mean absolute error of 0.935 percentage points and a Pearson correlation of r=0.967. Maturity estimates remained similar between the two sides of the harvester for which the light conditions varied despite poorer absolute counting performance under direct sunlight. All field events were processed within the available 14 s per-side crate interval.
