The proposed approach provides a practical means of quantifying one component of harvest selectivity while the machine is operating. Such information could support operator decision-making and, when combined with measurements of fruit recovery and losses, contribute to future feedback-based control of mechanical harvesting.

</details>


### [275] [LynnReal-Hand: Unlocking World Interaction with Hand Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21479)
*Shaohao Rui,Chaoyang Zhang,Xiaofeng Mao,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LynnReal-Hand手部控制视频世界模型，统一精细交互建模与高效流式生成，通过因果流映射、区域感知分数蒸馏与Ultra-Fast-VAE实现实时540p生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型依赖文本与相机控制，难以直接操纵物体；手部作为自然交互接口，但连续手部动作到连贯视觉响应的映射面临复杂手-物动力学建模与实时计算的双重挑战。

Method: 构建10万条真实与合成环境交互片段数据集并设计手部感知监督；学习因果流映射实现少步生成；引入区域感知分数蒸馏融合视频与图像先验以优化交互动态与外观；提出紧凑解码器Ultra-Fast-VAE降低计算开销。

Result: HandBench上手部跟随精度与视觉质量优于现有方法：完整序列模型FVD 181.67、MANO Dice 93.02；三步因果模型MANO Dice 92.49；单张H200上540p流式生成达18.03 FPS、741ms延迟、峰值显存34.89GB。

Conclusion: 该工作实现手控视频世界模型的精度与实时性统一，并承诺开源数据集、模型权重及训练推理代码。

Abstract: Interactive video world models enable exploration through text and camera controls, yet direct object interaction requires finer control. Hands provide a natural interface, but translating continuous hand movements into coherent visual responses remains challenging because of complex hand-object dynamics and demanding real-time computation. To address these challenges, we introduce LynnReal-Hand, a hand-controlled video world model that unifies precise interaction modeling with efficient streaming generation. We curate 100K interaction clips spanning diverse real and synthetic environments and develop hand-aware supervision to improve fine-grained hand motions and object interactions. Building on this foundation, we learn a causal flow map for few-step generation and introduce region-aware score distillation that combines complementary video and image priors to refine interaction dynamics and visual appearance. To further reduce inference latency, we propose Ultra-Fast-VAE, a compact video decoder that preserves high reconstruction fidelity with substantially lower computational cost. Extensive evaluations on HandBench demonstrate superior hand-following accuracy and visual quality compared with existing approaches. Our full-sequence model achieves an FVD of 181.67 and a MANO Dice score of 93.02, while the three-step causal model maintains a MANO Dice score of 92.49. For real-time interaction, 540p streaming on a single H200 reaches 18.03 FPS with 741ms latency and an optimized peak GPU memory footprint of only 34.89GB. We will release the datasets, model weights, and the full training and inference code to support research on hand-controlled video world models.

</details>


### [276] [Lost in the Hallway: Benchmarking Persistent Spatial Memory Across Revisits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21485)
*Henry Zheng,Weibo Gong,Yang Yue,Rui Huang,Yang Zhang,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReSpaceBench，一个用于评测具身智能体"持久空间理解"能力的基准，包含 590 个场景、9 类任务、3244 道题，通过受控走场以物理房间进入定义访问，并引入同类型房间干扰与跨会话物体变化；评测 32 个模型与 3 个 agentic 视频理解框架，最强零样本模型仅 50.5% 准确率（人类 90.1%），并配套发布 RS-30K 训练集，微调 Qwen3-VL-8B 可将准确率从 35.5% 提升至 60.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需要在反复进入的环境中可靠运行，因而必须具备持久空间理解：在视角变化下识别重访地点、区分外观相似的房间、并随环境变化更新记忆。然而现有评测往往将跨多次访问的"物理地点同一性"隐式处理，缺乏对重访关联与状态持续性的显式考察，导致对该能力的真实水平缺乏可靠度量。

Method: 构建 ReSpaceBench：涵盖 590 个合成与真实家庭场景、3244 个问题、9 项任务，分为房间关联、持久状态追踪与下游推理三类；通过受控走场以"物理房间进入"界定一次访问，显式植入同类型房间干扰物与跨会话物体变化。评测 32 个模型和 3 个 agentic 视频理解框架，分析 agent 执行轨迹以定位失败模式，并开展熟悉房间识别与正确访问房间辨识的对比分析、标注引导采样分析。另构建无模板/场景/路线/视频重叠的合成训练集 RS-30K 用于微调验证。

Result: 最强零样本模型整体准确率 50.5%（450 题子集 52.2%），人类标注者为 90.1%；所评测的 agentic 框架相对同基座模型直接推理无整体提升。执行轨迹分析显示主要失败模式为证据遗漏与跨访问房间关联错误。分析还揭示"识别熟悉房间"与"定位正确访问房间"之间存在显著差距，标注引导采样表明保留中间访问与转场至关重要。用 RS-30K 微调 Qwen3-VL-8B，整体准确率由 35.5% 升至 60.4%，关联任务增益最大，并泛化至真实场景。

Conclusion: 现有模型与 agentic 框架在持久空间理解上远落后于人类，仅靠迭代式证据检索并不能保证一致的空间记忆；显式建模跨访问的地点同一性、保留中间访问与转场信息是关键。ReSpaceBench 提供了可量化该能力的评测协议，RS-30K 与微调结果证明该能力可通过针对性训练显著改善并迁移至真实环境。

Abstract: Persistent spatial understanding is essential for embodied agents to operate reliably in environments they repeatedly encounter. It requires recognizing revisited places despite viewpoint changes, distinguishing them from similar-looking rooms, and updating memories as environments change. Yet existing evaluations often leave physical place identity across separated visits implicit. We introduce ReSpaceBench, a benchmark comprising 3,244 questions across 590 synthetic and real-home scenes, organized into 9 tasks spanning room association, persistent state tracking, and downstream reasoning. Controlled walkthroughs define visits by physical room entry and deliberately include same-type room distractors and cross-session object changes. Our evaluation of 32 models and 3 agentic video-understanding frameworks reveals substantial limitations: the strongest evaluated zero-shot model achieves 50.5% overall accuracy and 52.2% on a 450-question subset, compared with 90.1% for human annotators. The evaluated agentic frameworks offer no overall improvement over direct inference with the same base model. Analysis of agent execution traces reveals evidence omissions and incorrect cross-visit room associations as major failure modes, suggesting that iterative evidence seeking alone does not ensure consistent spatial memory. Further analyses expose a gap between recognizing familiar rooms and identifying the correct visited room, while annotation-guided sampling highlights the importance of preserving intervening visits and transitions. We also provide RS-30K, a separate training dataset from synthetic sources with no template, scene, route, or video overlap with the benchmark. Fine-tuning Qwen3-VL-8B raises overall accuracy from 35.5% to 60.4%, with the largest gains in association and improvements extending to real-life scenes.

</details>


### [277] [DuoFlow: Towards Synchronized Speech and Motion Generation with Fine-Grained Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21530)
*Jun Dong,Ronghui Li,Zhongyuan Hu,Yunqi Zhao,Lijie Guo,Ruizhang Xu,Di Kang,Linchao Bao,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化基准 MotionSpeech 与双流潜在流 Transformer 模型 DuoFlow，实现可控的语音-动作联合生成，支持细粒度动作层级、身体部位与嗓音风格的显式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音-动作联合生成模型多由文本语义或中间语音表征隐式驱动，缺乏对动作层级、身体部位及嗓音风格的显式控制能力。

Method: 构建 MotionSpeech 基准（含转写文本、嗓音风格描述及两级层次动作条件）；提出 DuoFlow 双流潜在流 Transformer，在共享噪声水平与统一的秒级时间坐标下联合去噪语音与动作，配合模态特定残差连接器注入结构化条件、逐层双向跨模态注意力协调生成、以及融合归一化进度锚点与 CTC 监督的单调音素通路。

Result: DuoFlow 提供细粒度动作与嗓音风格控制的统一接口，可合成高质量、同步且与指令一致的语音-动作序列。

Conclusion: 显式结构化条件与双流协同建模能有效提升联合语音-动作生成的可控性与一致性。

Abstract: An embodied speaker's expressiveness depends not only on what is said, but also on how the voice sounds and how the body moves. However, existing joint speech-motion generation models are largely driven implicitly by transcript semantics or intermediate speech representations, offering little explicit control over motion hierarchy, body parts, or vocal style. To bridge this gap, we construct MotionSpeech, a structured benchmark for controllable joint speech-motion generation that includes transcripts, voice-style descriptions, and motion conditions at two hierarchical levels. Building on this benchmark, we propose DuoFlow, a dual-stream latent flow transformer that jointly denoises speech and motion under a shared noise level and a unified second-based temporal coordinate. Modality-specific residual connectors inject structured conditions into the corresponding streams, layer-wise bidirectional cross-modal attention coordinates their generation, and a monotonic phoneme pathway combines normalized progress anchors with CTC supervision to strengthen speech temporal modeling. DuoFlow provides a unified interface for fine-grained motion and vocal-style control and demonstrates the ability to synthesize high-quality, synchronized, and instruction-consistent speech-motion sequences.

</details>


### [278] [Seen but Unsaid: Locating and Repairing Cross-Image Comparison in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21539)
*Zhaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在Qwen3-VL、InternVL3.5-8B与Gemma-3-12B中追踪视觉匹配失败链路，定位出两类失败点（精确复制可比较但读不出、语义对应存在于视觉token但不进入答案），并发现16–32个注意力头承载比较；仅训练这些头的Q/K投影（0.02%参数）即可在SPair-71k上提升28.3/25.1点并迁移至HPatches与BLINK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在两图中匹配点或物体时常常失败，即便答案可从其自身隐藏状态中解码出来，说明失败并非信息缺失而是信息未被利用。作者希望定位从视觉token到答案字母这一链路上的具体失效环节，并验证该环节是否可被极小代价的干预修复。

Method: 在合成形状匹配、同实例点对应（HPatches）与语义对应（SPair-71k）三类任务上，跨Qwen3-VL（2B–32B）、InternVL3.5-8B、Gemma-3-12B进行逐层追踪。手段包括：答案位置探针（answer-position probe）与校准输出对比、探针方向引导（steering）、保持注意力质量不变的跨图注意力重分配（消融）、仅训练指定头的query/key投影、以及无约束全层适配器的增益路由分析。所有决策规则在运行前冻结，数字由独立脚本复算。

Result: 两类失败点：(1) 准确复制的视觉信息可被比较但未被读出——答案位置探针在各模型族均高出校准输出15–25点（可命名与不可命名形状皆然），而沿探针方向引导并不比随机方向带来更多正确答案；(2) 语义对应存在于视觉token中却从未到达答案。通路层面：重分配16–32个头的跨图注意力（保持其质量）使合成任务准确率下降17–22点、HPatches下降15–32点，随机或注意力匹配头至多7点。训练这些头的Q/K投影使SPair-71k提升28.3点（Qwen3-VL-8B）与25.1点（InternVL3.5-8B），优于层匹配与注意力匹配对照，并可迁移至HPatches与BLINK。无约束全层适配器有21–51%的增益经由同一批头的注意力，随机头至多7%，且主要机制是改变其读取内容（占83–86%）而非权重。

Conclusion: VLM的跨图匹配失败可归因于少数特定注意力头的读出与路由缺陷，而非表征缺失；针对这16–32个头的Q/K投影做极轻量训练（0.02%参数）即可大幅修复语义对应并迁移到其他基准。该发现为定位与低成本修复VLM感知—推理接口失效提供了机制性证据。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often fail to match a point or an object across two images, even when the answer can be decoded from their own hidden states. We trace this failure from the visual tokens to the answer letter, in Qwen3-VL (2B-32B), InternVL3.5-8B, and Gemma-3-12B, on synthetic shape matching, same-instance point correspondence (HPatches), and semantic correspondence (SPair-71k), and find two distinct failure points, a small set of attention heads that carries the comparison, and a repair that touches 0.02% of the parameters. Two failure points: exact copies are compared but not read out. An answer-position probe exceeds the calibrated output by 15 to 25 points in every family, for nameable and unnameable shapes alike, and steering along the probe's direction moves no more answers than random directions. Semantic correspondences are present in the visual tokens but never reach the answer. One pathway: redistributing the cross-image attention of 16-32 heads, with its mass preserved, removes 17-22 points of synthetic and 15-32 points of HPatches accuracy, against at most 7 for random or attention-matched heads. A lever that training finds on its own: training only these heads' query and key projections raises SPair-71k accuracy by 28.3 points (Qwen3-VL-8B) and 25.1 points (InternVL3.5-8B), beats both layer-matched and attention-matched heads, and transfers to HPatches and BLINK. An unconstrained all-layer adapter routes 21-51% of its gain through the attention of the same heads, against at most 7% for random heads, and does so by changing what they read (83-86% of the effect) rather than their weights. Every decision rule was frozen before its run, and every reported number is recomputed by independent verification scripts.

</details>


### [279] [BLIP-Live: Balancing Timeliness & Firing Precision in Proactive Streaming Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21553)
*Kanchana Ranasinghe,Honglu Zhou,Dominick Reilly,Le Xue,Shusheng Yang,Juan Carlos Niebles,Silvio Savarese,Ran Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BLIP-Live框架，以"何时说、说什么"的因果响应决策定义主动性，配套数据集、指标、推理harness与训练流程，实现流式视频理解中的SOTA主动性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时交互系统需要主动响应：证据充分即作答，同时避免过早、冗余或错误响应。然而现有工作缺乏对"主动性"统一定义、标注数据、可测指标与系统性训练/推理方案的整合。

Method: 1) 构建BLIP-Live-780K数据集，含人工标注的主动响应内容与时机；2) 提出PAUC-F指标，将响应及时性度量与触发精度（firing precision）结合；3) 研究模型无关的推理harness（滑动视觉窗口、在线交互记忆、结构化响应输出）以及相应的训练流程，以激发离线VLM的流式主动能力。

Result: 滑动视觉窗口、在线交互记忆与结构化输出的组合在推理与训练两种设置下均取得最强主动性表现；BLIP-Live-Qwen在四个主动流式基准上达到SOTA，较基线最高提升44%，并优于多个闭源API模型。

Conclusion: 将主动性形式化为"响应 vs 沉默"的有效因果决策，并围绕统一定义整合数据、指标、harness与训练，是提升流式视频主动理解的有效路径；代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding is becoming increasingly important for real-world interactive systems. Such systems must be proactive for natural and useful human interaction: they should respond as soon as sufficient evidence becomes available while avoiding premature, redundant, or incorrect responses. We present BLIP-Live, a framework unifying data, metrics, harness, and training around a shared formulation of proactivity as effective causal response decisions balancing the two competing objectives of responding vs silence. First, we construct BLIP-Live-780K dataset containing human annotations of proactive response content and timing, which defines this desired behavior. We then propose PAUC-F, a metric to effectively measure our desired proactive behavior. PAUC-F combines existing response timeliness metrics with firing precision to reward early useful responses without rewarding excessive firing. With our desired behavior defined and measurable, we finally investigate model-agnostic inference harnesses that draw out proactive streaming capabilities of offline vision language models (VLMs), together with training pipelines that learn to further improve their proactive responsiveness. Across both settings, we find that combinations of sliding visual windows, online interaction memory, and structured response outputs consistently yield strongest proactive performance. Our experiments result in two models, with our BLIP-Live-Qwen model achieving state-of-the-art performance across four proactive streaming benchmarks, with gains up to 44% over baselines and outperforming multiple closed API models. Our code and data will be released publicly.

</details>


### [280] [Reference Forcing: Long Video Generation with Region-Guided Drift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21559)
*Sheng Zhao,Haitian Zheng,Weikai Lin,Junjie Zhao,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Reference Forcing，以首帧为外观参考，通过教师-学生蒸馏与 KV 缓存 value 校正，缓解长视频自回归生成中主体与背景漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成需同时保持主体/背景一致性与丰富自然运动。现有自回归模型仅在短窗口上用分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）训练，缺乏长程一致性约束，导致主体与背景随时间漂移；而维护 KV 缓存的方法所保留的历史本身可能已漂移，无法从根源解决。

Method: 1) Reference Forcing：以初始帧作为外观参考。2) 构建教师模型，用注意力掩码强制新帧各区域关注初始帧中对应区域，区域由现成分割模型给出。3) 将该行为蒸馏到学生模型的独立注意力通路中，推理时无需外部模型。4) 引入 value correction，将 KV 缓存中已漂移的值向无漂移初始帧的匹配值校正。

Result: 在多种基础模型上，长视频方法中主体与背景一致性达到 SOTA，且优势随视频长度增加而扩大；消融显示两个组件缺一不可——单独使用会牺牲运动或无法获得一致性。

Conclusion: 以首帧为锚点的参考注意力蒸馏与 KV 缓存 value 校正能有效抑制长程漂移，在不损失运动的前提下提升长视频一致性，且可即插即用于不同基座模型。

Abstract: Long video generation should keep the subject and background consistent while supporting rich, natural motion. However, autoregressive models are often distilled with distribution matching distillation (DMD) over short windows, which does not train them to keep this consistency over long ranges. As a result, the subject and background can drift over time. Methods that manage the key-value (KV) cache retain or retrieve history to reduce drift. Yet this history may itself have drifted. We propose Reference Forcing, which uses the initial frames as an appearance reference. We first build a teacher that uses attention masks to force each region of a new frame to attend to the same region in the initial frames. The teacher finds these regions with off-the-shelf segmentation models. We then distill this behavior into a separate attention path of the student, which needs no external models at inference. We further introduce value correction, which corrects drifted values in the KV cache toward the matching values of drift-free initial frames. Experiments show that Reference Forcing achieves state-of-the-art subject and background consistency among long-video methods on different base models, and its advantage grows with video length. Ablations show both components are needed to gain consistency without losing motion. More visualizations and code are available at https://zhaosheng-thu.github.io/reference-force/

</details>


### [281] [Wildfire-SSR: Geolocation-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Structure-Level Wildfire Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21563)
*Yu-Hsuan Ho,Yiming Xiao,Hao-En Lai,Ali Mostafavi,Samuel D. Brody*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Wildfire-SSR多模态框架，结合街景与卫星影像做结构级野火脆弱性评估，在2025年洛杉矶两场火灾约3.7万栋建筑上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时兼顾地块级（parcel-scale）点火路径刻画与社区级（community-scale）地理覆盖，缺乏统一的跨尺度评估手段。

Method: 视觉语言模型从灾前街景全景中提取建筑与场地属性并转为结构化语义文本；通过解耦交叉注意力融合解码器与卫星影像空间对齐；冻结的卫星预训练DINOv3编码器提取俯视上下文；比较多种地理定位嵌入策略，采用旋转位置编码（RoPE）实现文本特征在卫星图块内的锚定。标签由灾后相对损伤模式反推的野火风险等级。

Result: 在约37,000栋受影响建筑上，RoPE取得最佳空间锚定效果：建筑定位F1=0.6735，五类像素级风险分类调和平均F1=0.2476，优于单模态基线与无锚定多模态配置；对高风险类别呈保守偏置，减少严重风险低估；单GPU完成全区域端到端评估耗时37分钟。

Conclusion: 将跨视角融合重构为局部化语义对齐，为减灾优先级排序、韧性规划与面向保险的野火风险评估提供可规模化工具。

Abstract: Structure-level wildfire vulnerability assessment is critical for reducing losses in wildland-urban interface communities, yet existing methods often fail to combine parcel-scale ignition pathways with community-scale geographic coverage. This study introduces Wildfire-SSR (Satellite and Street-view for wildfire Risk), a geolocation-aware multimodal framework that integrates macro-scale satellite context with micro-scale street-view evidence. A Vision-Language Model extracts wildfire-relevant building and site attributes from pre-event street-view panoramas and converts them into structured semantic text. These features are spatially aligned with satellite imagery through a decoupled cross-attention fusion decoder, while a frozen satellite-pretrained DINOv3 encoder captures overhead visual context. Alternative geolocation embedding strategies are evaluated to anchor text-derived features within each satellite patch. The framework is tested on approximately 37,000 structures affected by the 2025 Eaton and Palisades fires in Los Angeles, using wildfire risk labels derived from relative post-event damage patterns to better represent inherent structural vulnerability. Rotary Positional Embedding provides the strongest spatial grounding, achieving a building localization F1 score of 0.6735 and a harmonic mean F1 score of 0.2476 for five-class pixel-level risk classification, outperforming unimodal baselines and an unanchored multimodal configuration. The model also shows a conservative bias in higher-risk classes, reducing severe-risk underestimation, and completes end-to-end assessment of the study area in 37 minutes on a single GPU. Wildfire-SSR reframes cross-view fusion as localized semantic alignment and provides a scalable tool for mitigation prioritization, resilience planning, and insurance-oriented wildfire risk assessment.

</details>


### [282] [When Do Histology Foundation Models Need Geometric Test-Time Augmentation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21565)
*Lars Chen,Valentino F. Foit,Dorry Segev,Michal Mankowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统评估了D4测试时增强（TTA）在组织病理学基础模型上是否仍有价值，发现其收益随模型专用性增强而递减，并提出熵路由以降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织学图像缺乏标准朝向，D4 TTA（对8种旋转与翻转视图的预测进行池化）一直是计算病理学的默认做法；但具有内在旋转不变性的自监督组织学基础模型（FMs）兴起，使D4 TTA的持续有效性受到质疑。

Method: 在四个patch级与两个WSI数据集上，基准测试9个组织学FMs与6个通用模型；比较D4 TTA的增益，并测试熵路由策略（仅对不确定性高的预测施加TTA），以消除计算开销。

Result: D4 TTA仍是可靠的事后改进，但收益从通用骨干的+1.98个百分点（pp）降至组织学FMs在TCGA-UT上的+0.50 pp，并在最大模型上进一步缩小；熵路由仅对约13%样本施加TTA即可恢复大部分增益；patch级TTA改进无法迁移至slide级多实例学习（MIL）。

Conclusion: D4 TTA对通用模型仍有显著价值，但对专用组织学基础模型增益有限；熵路由可作为一种低开销替代方案；patch级与slide级TTA效果不具可迁移性，需分别评估。

Abstract: Test-time augmentation (TTA) aggregates model predictions across multiple transformed views of an input during inference. Because histology images lack a canonical orientation, $D_4$ TTA---pooling across the 8 rotations and reflections of a square---is a standard default in computational pathology. However, the rise of self-supervised histology foundation models (FMs) with inherent rotational invariance calls its continued utility into question. We benchmark 9 histology FMs and 6 general-purpose models across four patch-level and two whole-slide image (WSI) datasets. We find that $D_4$ TTA remains a reliable post hoc improvement, but benefits diminish from $+1.98$ percentage points (pp) for general-purpose backbones to $+0.50$ pp for histology FMs on TCGA-UT, and shrink further for the largest models. To eliminate computational overhead, our experiments demonstrate that entropy routing---applying TTA selectively to uncertain predictions---recovers most of the gain while augmenting only ${\sim}13\%$ of samples. Finally, we show that patch-level TTA improvements do not transfer to slide-level multiple instance learning (MIL). Code: https://github.com/lars-chen/histology-tta

</details>


### [283] [How VLMs Plan What's Next](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21600)
*Rohit Gandikota,Arnab Sen Sharma,Adam Belfki,David Bau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示并验证了VLM在生成图像描述时"先定位、后命名"的顺序规划机制，将其分解为binding、cursor进度信号、select与gaze四个环节，并通过因果patching在Qwen3-VL上系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM描述图像时逐个命名对象且很少重复，但其如何规划这一顺序（"看之前决定看哪里"）的内在机制并不清楚。作者希望从层级与token粒度上打开这一黑箱，并为研究模型在长程多步任务中的规划提供方法路径。

Method: 提出四部分机制：(1) binding heads将文本token与图像匹配，解析为指向图像区域的指针；(2) 同一批binding heads把已写出的答案绑定回其来源区域，形成"cursor"——列表中已写到的图像位置，作为进度信号；(3) select步骤选择cursor之后最近的未写区域或停止；(4) gaze heads执行所选指针并返回该处概念，由language heads与后段MLP生成问题语言中的词。在合成视觉检索任务上用因果patching在Qwen3-VL上逐环节验证，并与替代假设对照。

Result: 因果patching支持该机制的每一环节并排除替代假设；机制在多种输入格式、自然照片与多语言下均成立；binding、relay与gaze heads在2B至32B各规模模型中均存在。

Conclusion: VLM通过上述机制将问题与图像转化为有序答案序列，可逐层、逐token地分析；该框架为研究模型在复杂多步、长rollout任务中的规划提供了可行途径。

Abstract: When a vision-language model describes an image, it names one object at a time and it rarely repeats itself, yet how the model plans this order is far from obvious. We characterize the mechanism a VLM uses to decide where to look before it looks. The mechanism has four main parts. First, binding heads match the relevant text tokens against the image and resolve them into pointers to the matched image region. Second, the same binding heads bind each written answer to the region it came from, and the model turns it into a progress signal: a cursor, the position in the image up to which the list has been written. Third, a select step chooses the nearest unwritten region after the cursor, or stops. Finally, gaze heads execute the chosen pointer and return a concept of what is drawn there, which language heads and the late MLPs turn into a word in the language of the question. We validate each part of this mechanism through causal patching in Qwen3-VL models on a synthetic visual retrieval task, and test our mechanisms against alternative hypotheses. We further show that the mechanism holds across input formats, natural photographs, and languages, and that its binding, relay, and gaze heads appear at every model size from 2B to 32B. This work allows us to investigate how a VLM turns a question and an image into a sequence of answers, layer by layer and token by token. More broadly, it opens a way to study how models plan over complex multi-step tasks with long rollouts. Our project is available at https://plan.baulab.info/

</details>


### [284] [FaceJev: Direct Visual Decisions for Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21604)
*Siran Peng,Tianshuo Zhang,Yibo Xue,Ajian Liu,Haoyuan Zhang,Weisong Zhao,Shuhuan Chen,Xiangyu Zhu,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FaceJev：基于MLLM对8个人脸部件做独立“异常/正常”视觉判断，直接从原生答案logit读取异常概率，任一部件异常即判为伪造，并构建PartForensics（约16万图）数据集与FS-GRPO训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸伪造检测方法在已见篡改类型上表现好，但对未见篡改泛化差；基于MLLM的方法多以文本解释表达部件判断，未将单个部件的决策作为显式学习目标，导致检测结果与部件判断缺乏直接关联。

Method: 沿用Jev形式化，共享图像编码后对8个面部部件回答8个独立问题，从原生答案logit读取异常概率；采用“任一部件异常即判伪造”的判定规则，使检测结果直接由部件决策导出；构建PartForensics数据集（约160k图像，含8个面部部件的二值异常标签）进行监督；提出FS-GRPO，通过与备选答案的精确奖励比较对每个部件决策做优化，奖励同时包含检测奖励与部件奖励。

Result: 在未见篡改类型上，FaceJev较最强基线提升7.25个百分点；消融实验验证了设计选择与主要模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 将人脸部件级异常判断作为显式学习目标并直接由部件决策导出检测结论，可显著提升对未见篡改的泛化能力，数据集与强化学习式优化策略是其中的关键支撑。

Abstract: Face forgery detection methods often perform well on seen manipulations but generalize poorly to unseen ones. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can recognize facial parts and assess their visual plausibility. Existing MLLM-based methods typically express these judgments through textual explanations, but seldom treat individual part decisions as explicit learning targets. We present FaceJev, an MLLM-based method for face forgery detection through direct visual decisions. Following the Jev formulation, FaceJev answers eight independent questions about facial parts using a shared image encoding and reads anomaly probabilities from the native answer logits. A face is classified as forged if any part is predicted to be abnormal, directly linking the detection result to the part decisions. We construct PartForensics, a dataset of approximately 160k images with binary anomaly targets for the eight facial parts, to supervise these decisions. We further propose FS-GRPO, which optimizes each part decision through an exact reward comparison with its alternative, using both detection and part rewards. FaceJev outperforms the strongest baseline by 7.25 percentage points on unseen manipulations. Extensive ablation studies validate our design choices and the effectiveness of the main components.

</details>


### [285] [ViRA: Toward Ultra-High-Resolution Visual Agents via Cross-Input Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21605)
*Huawei Lin,Jinting Liu,Tongyan Wang,Xuyang Chen,Ruihua Shi,Zhichao Xu,Jiaxin Qin,Guoshenghui Zhao,Baijian Yang,Yingjie Victor Chen,Tan Yu,Weijie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CrossRPO强化学习方法，利用对比集训练视觉Agent，迫使模型真正读取高清图像中的证据而非依赖常识作答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超高清图像视觉Agent在回答时常凭常识猜测而非读取图像证据；仅基于最终答案的强化学习无法纠正，因为猜测与真实读取获得相同奖励。

Method: CrossRPO在对比集上训练：同一问题对多张仅在含证据的小区域上有差异、答案不同的图像版本作答，奖励解出整个集合，并证明其不会强化凭常识作答的集合。构建ViRA-Train、ViRA-Bench（编辑2100万–1.2亿像素图像中的小目标）及ViRA-Bench-Real（真实拍摄变化）；以约3500条数据训练ViRA-9B和ViRA-27B。

Result: 最大分辨率下ViRA-Bench集合准确率63.3%，优于GRPO的59.3%；ViRA-9B在同规模开源模型中全部基准最优；ViRA-27B在多数基准上超越最高思考级别的Claude Haiku 5.5与GPT-6-Luna。

Conclusion: 对比集奖励设计可有效抑制常识捷径、强化证据阅读能力，使视觉推理Agent在超高清图像任务上取得领先性能，且数据效率高。

Abstract: Visual agents answer questions about ultra-high-resolution photos by zooming into the relevant regions. However, existing agents often answer from common sense instead of reading the evidence. Reinforcement learning (RL) on the final answer cannot correct this, because a guess earns the same reward as reading. We propose Cross-Input Relative Policy Optimization (CrossRPO), which trains agents on contrast sets. A contrast set asks one question about several versions of an image that differ only in a small region with the evidence and have different answers, so only reading the evidence solves the whole set. CrossRPO rewards solving the whole set, and we prove that it does not reinforce a set answered from common sense. We build contrast sets for training (ViRA-Train) and evaluation (ViRA-Bench) by editing one small object in images of 21 to 120 megapixels, and we photograph further sets with real changes (ViRA-Bench-Real) to exclude editing traces. We train two Visual Reasoning Agents, ViRA-9B and ViRA-27B, with CrossRPO on only about 3,500 items of ViRA-Train each. CrossRPO achieves 63.3% set accuracy on ViRA-Bench at the maximum resolution, against 59.3% for GRPO. ViRA-9B is the most accurate open model of its size on all benchmarks, and ViRA-27B outperforms Claude Haiku 5.5 and GPT-6-Luna, both at their highest thinking level, on most benchmarks.

</details>
